namespace AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Plot
{
	public class GraphAxes : IGraphAxes, IPlotRender, I3DPlotRender
	{
		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private Dictionary<PlotAxisID, IGraphAxis> _axes = new Dictionary<PlotAxisID, IGraphAxis>();

		private PlotAxesStyle _style;

		private PlotAxisID _zoomableAxes;

		private RGraphArea _graphArea = RGraphArea.Undefined;

		private bool _refreshRequired;

		private bool _stretchRequired = true;

		private double _axesFontScaling = 1.0;

		private AxesVisual _visual;

		private double _axisFontScaling = 1.0;

		private Rect _plotArea;

		private Rect _calculatedPlotArea;

		private ModelVisual3D _visual3D;

		private IGraph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as IGraph;
			}
		}

		public double AxesFontScaling
		{
			get
			{
				return _axesFontScaling;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_axesFontScaling != value)
				{
					_axesFontScaling = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool ValueRangesChanged
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
				{
					if (value.ValueRangesChanged)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public PlotAxesStyle Style => _style;

		public PlotAxisID ZoomableAxes
		{
			get
			{
				return _zoomableAxes;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_zoomableAxes == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.None;
				foreach (PlotAxisID key in _axes.Keys)
				{
					_zoomableAxes |= value & key;
				}
			}
		}

		public IPlotRender Renderer => this;

		public bool StretchRequired
		{
			get
			{
				return _stretchRequired;
			}
			set
			{
				_stretchRequired = value;
			}
		}

		public RGraphArea GraphArea
		{
			get
			{
				return _graphArea;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_graphArea != value)
				{
					_graphArea = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<IGraphAxis> AxisCollection => _axes.Values;

		public Canvas Visual
		{
			get
			{
				if (_visual == null)
				{
					_visual = new AxesVisual();
					_visual.GraphAxes = this;
				}
				return _visual;
			}
		}

		protected bool AddAxes
		{
			get
			{
				if (_plotArea.Height > PlotSupport.MinPlotSize)
				{
					return _plotArea.Width > PlotSupport.MinPlotSize;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private GraphAxes()
		{
		}

		public GraphAxes(IGraph graph, PlotAxesStyle style)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
			_style = style;
			_axes.Add(PlotAxisID.X, null);
			_axes.Add(PlotAxisID.Y, null);
			switch (_style)
			{
			case PlotAxesStyle.RectXY:
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.X | PlotAxisID.Y;
				break;
			case PlotAxesStyle.RectX2Y:
				_axes.Add(PlotAxisID.Y2, null);
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y2, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y2].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.YMask | PlotAxisID.X;
				break;
			case PlotAxesStyle.Rect2XY:
				_axes.Add(PlotAxisID.X2, null);
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X2, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X2].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.XMask | PlotAxisID.Y;
				break;
			case PlotAxesStyle.Rect2X2Y:
				_axes.Add(PlotAxisID.X2, null);
				_axes.Add(PlotAxisID.Y2, null);
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X2, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y2, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X2].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y2].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.XMask | PlotAxisID.YMask;
				break;
			case PlotAxesStyle.RectXMultiY:
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new MultiGraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				(_axes[PlotAxisID.Y] as IMultiGraphAxis).ChildAxisLayout = ChildAxisLayout.Overlayed;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.X | PlotAxisID.Y;
				break;
			case PlotAxesStyle.RectXStackedY:
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new StackedGraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.X;
				break;
			case PlotAxesStyle.RectXMergedY:
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new MergedGraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.X | PlotAxisID.Y;
				break;
			case PlotAxesStyle.TriangXYZ:
			case PlotAxesStyle.ThreeDXYZ:
				_axes.Add(PlotAxisID.Z, null);
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.X, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				SetAxis(PlotAxisID.Z, new GraphAxis(graph, new LinearAxisAnnotator()));
				_axes[PlotAxisID.X].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Y].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_axes[PlotAxisID.Z].LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				_zoomableAxes = PlotAxisID.X | PlotAxisID.Y | PlotAxisID.Z;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, bool visibility)
		{
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, visibility, viceVersa: false);
		}

		public void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, bool visibility, bool viceVersa)
		{
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
			{
				value.SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, visibility, viceVersa);
			}
		}

		public void SetAxis(PlotAxisID axisId, IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			if (_axes.ContainsKey(axisId))
			{
				_axes[axisId] = axis;
				axis.Id = axisId;
				_refreshRequired = true;
				return;
			}
			throw new ArgumentException("Invalid axis Id", "axisId");
		}

		public IGraphAxis GetAxis(PlotAxisID axisId)
		{
			if (_axes.ContainsKey(axisId))
			{
				return _axes[axisId];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void BeginDataRangeUpdate()
		{
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
			{
				value.BeginDataRangeUpdate();
			}
		}

		public void EndDataRangeUpdate()
		{
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
			{
				value.EndDataRangeUpdate();
			}
		}

		public void SetZoom(Rect zoomArea)
		{
			if (zoomArea.Right < zoomArea.Left || zoomArea.Bottom < zoomArea.Top)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Invalid zoom region", "zoomArea");
			}
			PlotAxesStyle style = _style;
			if ((uint)(style - 7) <= 1u)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
			{
				if ((_zoomableAxes & value.Id) != PlotAxisID.None)
				{
					ValueRange valueRange = new ValueRange();
					switch (value.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
						valueRange.SetAbsolute(zoomArea.Left, zoomArea.Right, validate: true);
						value.ZoomRange = valueRange;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
						valueRange.SetAbsolute(1.0 - zoomArea.Top, 1.0 - zoomArea.Bottom, validate: true);
						value.ZoomRange = valueRange;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void ZoomReset()
		{
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
			{
				value.ZoomReset();
			}
		}

		public void RenderInitialise()
		{
			IDataSeriesCollection dataSeriesCollection = _graph.DataSeriesCollection;
			foreach (IDataSeries item in dataSeriesCollection)
			{
				_stretchRequired = _stretchRequired || item.DataSource.Updated;
			}
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
			{
				_stretchRequired = _stretchRequired || value.StretchRequired;
			}
			if (_stretchRequired)
			{
				StretchAxes(dataSeriesCollection);
				_stretchRequired = false;
			}
			foreach (IGraphAxis value2 in _axes.Values)
			{
				_refreshRequired = _refreshRequired || value2.VisualElementsChanged;
				value2.Renderer.RenderInitialise();
			}
		}

		private void StretchAxes(IDataSeriesCollection dataSeries)
		{
			BeginDataRangeUpdate();
			try
			{
				foreach (IDataSeries item in dataSeries)
				{
					item.StretchAxes();
				}
				if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout != BarChartLayout.Stack)
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
				{
					if (value.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel || (value.Scaling & PlotAxisScaling.Auto) != PlotAxisScaling.Auto)
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool flag = false;
					double num = double.MaxValue;
					double num2 = double.NaN;
					foreach (IDataSeries item2 in dataSeries)
					{
						if (item2.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar && item2.AxisRanges.ContainsKey(value.Id))
						{
							IValueRange valueRange = item2.AxisRanges[value.Id];
							if (valueRange != null)
							{
								flag = true;
								num = Math.Min(num, valueRange.Minimum.Value);
								num2 = ((!double.IsNaN(num2)) ? (num2 + valueRange.Maximum.Value) : valueRange.Maximum.Value);
							}
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						value.DataRange.Stretch(num);
						value.DataRange.Stretch(num2);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				EndDataRangeUpdate();
			}
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			PlotCanvasBase plotCanvasBase = Visual as PlotCanvasBase;
			refreshAll = refreshAll || _refreshRequired;
			_refreshRequired = false;
			if (refreshAll)
			{
				plotCanvasBase.RenderArea = renderArea;
				plotCanvasBase.Children.Clear();
				renderArea.X = 0.0;
				renderArea.Y = 0.0;
				_plotArea = renderArea;
				_axisFontScaling = AxesFontScaling;
				try
				{
					if (AddAxes)
					{
						for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
						{
							foreach (IGraphAxis value in _axes.Values)
							{
								value.AxisFontScaling = _axisFontScaling;
								Size renderSize = value.RenderSize;
								switch (value.Id)
								{
								case PlotAxisID.X:
									_plotArea.Height -= renderSize.Height;
									if (_plotArea.Left - renderArea.Left < renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Width -= renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0 - (_plotArea.Left - renderArea.Left);
										_plotArea.X = renderArea.Left + renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0;
									}
									if (renderArea.Right - _plotArea.Right < renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Width -= renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0 - (renderArea.Right - _plotArea.Right);
									}
									break;
								case PlotAxisID.X2:
									_plotArea.Y += renderSize.Height;
									_plotArea.Height -= renderSize.Height;
									if (_plotArea.Left - renderArea.Left < renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Width -= renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0 - (_plotArea.Left - renderArea.Left);
										_plotArea.X = renderArea.Left + renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0;
									}
									if (renderArea.Right - _plotArea.Right < renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Width -= renderSize.Width / 2.0 + 5.0 - (renderArea.Right - _plotArea.Right);
									}
									break;
								case PlotAxisID.Y:
									if (renderSize.Width > _plotArea.Left - renderArea.Left)
									{
										_plotArea.Width -= Math.Min(_plotArea.Width, renderSize.Width - (_plotArea.Left - renderArea.Left));
										_plotArea.X = renderArea.Left + renderSize.Width;
									}
									if (_plotArea.Top - renderArea.Top < renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Height -= renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0 - (_plotArea.Top - renderArea.Top);
										_plotArea.Y = renderArea.Top + renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0;
									}
									if (renderArea.Bottom - _plotArea.Bottom < renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Height -= renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0 - (renderArea.Bottom - _plotArea.Bottom);
									}
									break;
								case PlotAxisID.Y2:
									if (renderSize.Width > renderArea.Right - _plotArea.Right)
									{
										_plotArea.Width -= renderSize.Width - (renderArea.Right - _plotArea.Right);
									}
									if (_plotArea.Top - renderArea.Top < renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Height -= renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0 - (_plotArea.Top - renderArea.Top);
										_plotArea.Y = renderArea.Top + renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0;
									}
									if (renderArea.Bottom - _plotArea.Bottom < renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0)
									{
										_plotArea.Height -= renderSize.Height / 2.0 + 5.0 - (renderArea.Bottom - _plotArea.Bottom);
									}
									break;
								}
							}
							if (!AddAxes)
							{
								_axisFontScaling = 0.8 * AxesFontScaling;
								_plotArea = renderArea;
								continue;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				_calculatedPlotArea = _plotArea;
				if (!double.IsNaN(_graphArea.Top))
				{
					_plotArea.Height = Math.Max(0.0, _plotArea.Height - (_graphArea.Top - _plotArea.Top));
					_plotArea.Y = _graphArea.Top;
				}
				if (!double.IsNaN(_graphArea.Left))
				{
					_plotArea.Width = Math.Max(0.0, _plotArea.Width - (_graphArea.Left - _plotArea.Left));
					_plotArea.X = _graphArea.Left;
				}
				if (!double.IsNaN(_graphArea.Bottom))
				{
					_plotArea.Height = Math.Max(0.0, _graphArea.Bottom - _plotArea.Top);
				}
				if (!double.IsNaN(_graphArea.Right))
				{
					_plotArea.Width = Math.Max(0.0, _graphArea.Right - _plotArea.Left);
				}
			}
			foreach (IGraphAxis value2 in _axes.Values)
			{
				value2.AxisFontScaling = _axisFontScaling;
				Rect axisArea = _plotArea;
				switch (value2.Id)
				{
				case PlotAxisID.X:
					axisArea.Height = Math.Max(1.0, renderArea.Bottom - _calculatedPlotArea.Bottom);
					axisArea.Y = Math.Min(_plotArea.Bottom, renderArea.Bottom - axisArea.Height);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.X2:
					axisArea.Height = Math.Max(1.0, _calculatedPlotArea.Top - renderArea.Top);
					axisArea.Y = Math.Max(_plotArea.Top - axisArea.Height, renderArea.Top);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y:
					axisArea.Width = Math.Max(1.0, _calculatedPlotArea.Left - renderArea.Left);
					axisArea.X = Math.Max(_plotArea.Left - axisArea.Width, renderArea.Left);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y2:
					axisArea.Width = Math.Max(1.0, renderArea.Right - _calculatedPlotArea.Right);
					axisArea.X = Math.Min(_plotArea.Right, renderArea.Right - axisArea.Width);
					break;
				}
				IAxisRender axisRender = value2 as IAxisRender;
				(axisRender.Visual as PlotCanvasBase).RenderArea = renderArea;
				axisRender.Draw(ref _plotArea, ref axisArea, refreshAll);
				if (!plotCanvasBase.Children.Contains(axisRender.Visual))
				{
					plotCanvasBase.AddCanvas(axisRender.Visual);
				}
			}
			(Visual as AxesVisual).PlotArea = (renderArea = _plotArea);
		}

		public Visual3D Draw3D(R3DCuboid volume, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (Style != PlotAxesStyle.ThreeDXYZ)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Axes style is not 3D.");
			}
			if (_visual3D == null)
			{
				_visual3D = new ModelVisual3D();
			}
			refreshAll = refreshAll || _refreshRequired;
			_refreshRequired = false;
			if (refreshAll)
			{
				_visual3D.Content = null;
				_visual3D.Children.Clear();
			}
			foreach (IGraphAxis value2 in _axes.Values)
			{
				value2.AxisFontScaling = AxesFontScaling;
				Visual3D value = ((value2 as I3DPlotRender) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Axis doesn't support 3D.")).Draw3D(volume, refreshAll);
				if (!_visual3D.Children.Contains(value))
				{
					_visual3D.Children.Add(value);
				}
			}
			return _visual3D;
		}
	}
	public enum TickType
	{
		None,
		Minor,
		Major
	}
	public struct RAxisAnnotation : IComparable
	{
		public object dataValue;

		public double LinearPos;

		public string Label;

		public TickType TickType;

		public double LabelOffset;

		public int CompareTo(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is RAxisAnnotation rAxisAnnotation)
			{
				if (rAxisAnnotation.LinearPos > LinearPos)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (rAxisAnnotation.LinearPos < LinearPos)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				return 0;
			}
			return -1;
		}
	}
	public abstract class GraphAxisAnnotator : IGraphAxisAnnotator, IAxisRender, IPlotRender, I3DPlotRender, IGraphAxisAccessible
	{
		private List<RAxisAnnotation> _annotations = new List<RAxisAnnotation>();

		protected RAxisAnnotatorState State;

		protected bool Initialised;

		protected Size RenderSize;

		protected IGraphAxis Axis;

		protected object AnnotationsLock = new object();

		private PlotCanvasBase _axisVisual = new PlotCanvasBase();

		private PlotCanvasBase _labelsVisual = new PlotCanvasBase
		{
			Background = null
		};

		private PlotCanvasBase _gridVisual = new PlotCanvasBase();

		private AxisAnnotatorVisual _memberVisual;

		private PlotCanvasBase _axisTitleVisual = new PlotCanvasBase();

		private ModelVisual3D _visual3D;

		private ModelVisual3D _labels3D;

		public abstract string AnnotatorType { get; }

		public abstract PlotAxisDataType SupportedDataTypes { get; }

		public abstract PlotAxisDataType DataType { get; set; }

		public virtual string RecommendedFormatString => "g4";

		public RAxisAnnotatorState AnnotatorState => State;

		public IAxisRender Renderer => this;

		public List<RAxisAnnotation> Annotations => _annotations;

		private AxisAnnotatorVisual _visual
		{
			get
			{
				if (_memberVisual == null)
				{
					_memberVisual = new AxisAnnotatorVisual
					{
						AxisAnnotator = this,
						Background = null
					};
				}
				return _memberVisual;
			}
		}

		public Canvas Visual => _visual;

		IGraphAxis IGraphAxisAccessible.Axis => Axis;

		public Size EnsureRenderSize(IGraphAxis axis, Size desiredSize)
		{
			if (!desiredSize.IsEmpty)
			{
				RenderSize = desiredSize;
			}
			else
			{
				RenderSize = new Size(1.0, 1.0);
				if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks))
				{
					switch (axis.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
						RenderSize.Height += 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
						RenderSize.Width += 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks))
				{
					switch (axis.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
						RenderSize.Height += 5.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
						RenderSize.Width += 5.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels))
				{
					switch (axis.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
						RenderSize.Height += 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
						RenderSize.Width += 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						break;
					}
					Size maxLabelExtent = GetMaxLabelExtent(axis);
					switch (axis.LabelOrientation)
					{
					case LabelOrientation.Vertical:
					{
						double height = maxLabelExtent.Height;
						maxLabelExtent.Height = maxLabelExtent.Width;
						maxLabelExtent.Width = height;
						break;
					}
					case LabelOrientation.HorizontalSlant:
					case LabelOrientation.VerticalSlant:
					{
						double num = ((axis.LabelOrientation != LabelOrientation.VerticalSlant) ? 20.0 : 70.0);
						num = num * Math.PI / 180.0;
						double height = maxLabelExtent.Height;
						double width = maxLabelExtent.Width;
						maxLabelExtent.Height = width * Math.Sin(num) + height * Math.Cos(num);
						maxLabelExtent.Width = width * Math.Cos(num) + height * Math.Sin(num);
						break;
					}
					}
					RenderSize.Height += maxLabelExtent.Height;
					RenderSize.Width += maxLabelExtent.Width;
				}
				if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(axis.Title))
				{
					Size labelExtent = PlotSupport.GetLabelExtent(axis.Title, axis.TitleFontFamily, Axis.TitleFontSize, Axis.TitleFontStyle, Axis.TitleFontWeight, Axis.TitleTextDecorations);
					switch (axis.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
						RenderSize.Height += 4.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						RenderSize.Height += labelExtent.Height;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
						RenderSize.Width += 4.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
						RenderSize.Width += labelExtent.Height;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return RenderSize;
		}

		public virtual Size GetMaxLabelExtent(IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			Size result = new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				if (Annotations.Count > 0)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < Annotations.Count; i++)
					{
						Size labelExtent = PlotSupport.GetLabelExtent(Annotations[i].Label, axis.LabelFontFamily, axis.LabelFontSize, Axis.LabelFontStyle, Axis.LabelFontWeight, Axis.LabelTextDecorations);
						result.Height = Math.Max(result.Height, labelExtent.Height);
						result.Width = Math.Max(result.Width, labelExtent.Width);
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual void Initialise(IGraphAxis axis, bool reinitialise)
		{
			if (!(!Initialised || reinitialise) && Axis == axis)
			{
				return;
			}
			Axis = axis;
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				Annotations.Clear();
			}
			int majorSteps = 0;
			int minorSteps = 0;
			int gridLineSteps = 1;
			if (axis.GridCallback != null)
			{
				axis.GridCallback.AxisGridRangeCallback(axis, ref majorSteps, ref minorSteps, ref gridLineSteps);
			}
			bool valid;
			object minView = AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, axis.LinearRange.Minimum.Value, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				minView = axis.LinearRange.Minimum.Value;
			}
			object maxView = AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, axis.LinearRange.Maximum.Value, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				maxView = axis.LinearRange.Maximum.Value;
			}
			object minExtent;
			if (minView.GetType().IsValueType)
			{
				minExtent = minView;
			}
			else
			{
				minExtent = AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, axis.LinearRange.Minimum.Value, out valid);
				if (!valid)
				{
					minExtent = minView;
				}
			}
			object maxExtent;
			if (maxView.GetType().IsValueType)
			{
				maxExtent = maxView;
			}
			else
			{
				maxExtent = AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, axis.LinearRange.Maximum.Value, out valid);
				if (!valid)
				{
					maxExtent = maxView;
				}
			}
			if (axis.LabelCallback != null)
			{
				axis.LabelCallback.AxisLabelRangeCallback(axis, ref minView, ref maxView, ref minExtent, ref maxExtent, ref gridLineSteps);
			}
			double num = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, minView, out valid);
			double num2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, maxView, out valid);
			State.MajorStep = majorSteps;
			State.MinorStep = minorSteps;
			State.GridLineSteps = gridLineSteps;
			State.MinVisible = (State.MinView = num);
			State.MaxVisible = (State.MaxView = num2);
			State.MinExtent = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, minExtent, out valid);
			State.MaxExtent = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, maxExtent, out valid);
			if (axis.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
			{
				State.MinVisible = (State.MinView -= 0.5);
				State.MaxVisible = (State.MaxView += 0.5);
				State.MinExtent -= 0.5;
				State.MaxExtent += 0.5;
				return;
			}
			double num3 = 1E-06;
			if (num != 0.0 && num2 != 0.0)
			{
				num3 = Math.Min(Math.Abs(num * 1E-06), 1E-06);
			}
			if (Math.Abs(num2 - num) <= num3)
			{
				if (axis.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.DateTime || axis.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.ElapsedTime)
				{
					State.MinVisible = (State.MinView -= 1.0);
					State.MaxVisible = (State.MaxView += 1.0);
					State.MinExtent -= 1.0;
					State.MaxExtent += 1.0;
				}
				else
				{
					State.MinVisible = (State.MinView -= 0.01);
					State.MaxVisible = (State.MaxView += 0.01);
					State.MinExtent -= 0.01;
					State.MaxExtent += 0.01;
				}
			}
		}

		public abstract double AxisLinearMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid);

		public abstract object AxisLinearUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid);

		public double AxisFractionMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			double num = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, value, out valid);
			if (valid)
			{
				double num2 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MinVisible, out valid);
				double num3 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MaxVisible, out valid);
				num = (num - num2) / (num3 - num2);
			}
			if (double.IsInfinity(num) || double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				valid = false;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public object AxisFractionUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid)
		{
			double num = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num2 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MaxVisible, out valid);
			double point2 = point * (num2 - num) + num;
			return AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, point2, out valid);
		}

		public string AxisFractionToValueString(IGraphAxis axis, double point)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			if (axis != null)
			{
				bool valid;
				object arg = AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
				result = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t= {1:" + axis.DataLabelFormatString + "}", string.IsNullOrEmpty(axis.Title) ? axis.Id.ToString() : axis.Title, arg);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual bool ValueIsValid(IGraphAxis axis, object value)
		{
			AxisLinearMapValue(axis, value, out var valid);
			return valid;
		}

		public abstract void BuildLabelList(IGraphAxis axis);

		protected virtual void DrawAxis(IGraphAxis axis, PlotCanvasBase visual, Rect axisRenderArea, Rect gridRenderArea)
		{
			bool flag = axisRenderArea.Height > 4.0 && axisRenderArea.Width > 4.0;
			if (!visual.Children.Contains(_gridVisual))
			{
				visual.AddChild(_gridVisual, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(gridRenderArea));
			}
			if (!visual.Children.Contains(_axisVisual))
			{
				visual.AddChild(_axisVisual, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(axisRenderArea));
			}
			if (!visual.Children.Contains(_labelsVisual))
			{
				visual.AddChild(_labelsVisual, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(axisRenderArea));
			}
			if (axis.Id == PlotAxisID.X || axis.Id == PlotAxisID.X2)
			{
				PlotSelection.SetElementType(_axisVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.XAxisScales);
				PlotSelection.SetElementType(_labelsVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.XAxisScales);
			}
			else if (axis.Id == PlotAxisID.Y || axis.Id == PlotAxisID.Y2)
			{
				PlotSelection.SetElementType(_axisVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.YAxisScales);
				PlotSelection.SetElementType(_labelsVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.YAxisScales);
			}
			_axisVisual.Children.Clear();
			_axisVisual.RenderArea = axisRenderArea;
			_labelsVisual.Children.Clear();
			_labelsVisual.RenderArea = axisRenderArea;
			_labelsVisual.Clip = null;
			_gridVisual.Children.Clear();
			_gridVisual.RenderArea = gridRenderArea;
			double num = (axisRenderArea.Y = 0.0);
			double num3 = (axisRenderArea.X = num);
			double x = (gridRenderArea.Y = num3);
			gridRenderArea.X = x;
			PlotPen pen = PlotSupport.MakePen(axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge), LineStyle.Solid, 1.0);
			if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge))
			{
				switch (axis.Id)
				{
				case PlotAxisID.X:
					_axisVisual.AddLine(pen, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(axisRenderArea), PlotSupport.RectTopRight(axisRenderArea));
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.X2:
					_axisVisual.AddLine(pen, PlotSupport.RectBottomLeft(axisRenderArea), PlotSupport.RectBottomRight(axisRenderArea));
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y:
					_axisVisual.AddLine(pen, PlotSupport.RectTopRight(axisRenderArea), PlotSupport.RectBottomRight(axisRenderArea));
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y2:
					_axisVisual.AddLine(pen, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(axisRenderArea), PlotSupport.RectBottomLeft(axisRenderArea));
					break;
				}
			}
			double num6 = 0.0;
			switch (axis.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
			case PlotAxisID.X2:
				num6 = axisRenderArea.Width;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
			case PlotAxisID.Y2:
				num6 = axisRenderArea.Height;
				break;
			}
			double num7 = 4.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
			bool valid;
			double num8 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num9 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MaxVisible, out valid);
			double num10 = num6 / (num9 - num8);
			PlotPen pen2 = PlotSupport.MakePen(axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks), LineStyle.Solid, 1.0);
			PlotPen pen3 = PlotSupport.MakePen(axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks), LineStyle.Solid, 1.0);
			PlotPen pen4 = PlotSupport.MakePen(axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines), LineStyle.Solid, 1.0);
			PlotPen pen5 = PlotSupport.MakePen(axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines), LineStyle.Solid, 1.0);
			num7 = ((!axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks)) ? (num7 + 5.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling) : (num7 + 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling));
			Rect lastLabelBounds = new Rect(double.NaN, double.NaN, 0.0, 0.0);
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				foreach (RAxisAnnotation annotation in Annotations)
				{
					double num11 = (annotation.LinearPos - num8) * num10;
					if (num11 < 0.0 || num11 > num6 || double.IsNaN(num11) || double.IsInfinity(num11))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (annotation.TickType == TickType.Minor)
					{
						if (flag)
						{
							DrawMinorTick(axis, _axisVisual, axisRenderArea, num11, pen3);
						}
						if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines))
						{
							DrawGrid(axis, _gridVisual, gridRenderArea, annotation.dataValue, pen5);
						}
						continue;
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						DrawMajorTick(axis, _axisVisual, axisRenderArea, num11, pen2);
					}
					if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines))
					{
						DrawGrid(axis, _gridVisual, gridRenderArea, annotation.dataValue, pen4);
					}
					if (flag && axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels))
					{
						lastLabelBounds = DrawLabel(axis, annotation, _labelsVisual, axisRenderArea, num11, num7, lastLabelBounds);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected static Rect DrawLabel(IGraphAxis axis, RAxisAnnotation annotation, PlotCanvasBase dc, Rect axisRenderArea, double pos, double labelOffset, Rect lastLabelBounds)
		{
			TextBlock textBlock = PlotCanvasBase.MakeTextBlock(annotation.Label, axis.LabelFontFamily, axis.LabelFontSize, axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels), axis.LabelFontStyle, axis.LabelFontWeight, axis.LabelTextDecorations);
			Size textSize = WpfSilverlightSupport.GetTextSize(textBlock);
			bool flag = !double.IsNaN(lastLabelBounds.X) || !double.IsNaN(lastLabelBounds.Y);
			RotateTransform rotateTransform;
			double num;
			double num2;
			switch (axis.LabelOrientation)
			{
			case LabelOrientation.Vertical:
				rotateTransform = new RotateTransform
				{
					Angle = -90.0
				};
				num = textSize.Height;
				num2 = textSize.Width;
				break;
			case LabelOrientation.VerticalSlant:
				rotateTransform = new RotateTransform
				{
					Angle = -70.0
				};
				num = textSize.Height;
				num2 = textSize.Width;
				break;
			case LabelOrientation.HorizontalSlant:
				rotateTransform = new RotateTransform
				{
					Angle = -20.0
				};
				num = textSize.Height;
				num2 = textSize.Width;
				break;
			default:
				rotateTransform = null;
				num = textSize.Width;
				num2 = textSize.Height;
				break;
			}
			switch (axis.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
			{
				double x;
				double num3;
				switch (axis.LabelOrientation)
				{
				case LabelOrientation.Vertical:
					x = pos - num / 2.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Top + labelOffset + num2;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num;
					break;
				case LabelOrientation.VerticalSlant:
					x = pos - num2 - num / 3.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x + num2)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = labelOffset;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x + num2;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num;
					break;
				case LabelOrientation.HorizontalSlant:
					x = pos - num2;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = labelOffset;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num2;
					rotateTransform.CenterX = x + num2;
					rotateTransform.CenterY = num3;
					break;
				default:
					x = pos - num / 2.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = labelOffset;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num;
					break;
				}
				textBlock.RenderTransform = rotateTransform;
				dc.AddChild(textBlock, new Point(x, num3));
				break;
			}
			case PlotAxisID.X2:
			{
				double x;
				double num3;
				switch (axis.LabelOrientation)
				{
				case LabelOrientation.Vertical:
					x = pos - num / 2.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - labelOffset;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num;
					break;
				case LabelOrientation.VerticalSlant:
					x = pos + num / 3.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - labelOffset - num;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num;
					break;
				case LabelOrientation.HorizontalSlant:
					x = pos;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - labelOffset - num;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num2;
					rotateTransform.CenterX = x;
					rotateTransform.CenterY = num3 - num;
					break;
				default:
					x = pos - num / 2.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Right + 2.0 >= x)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - labelOffset - num2;
					lastLabelBounds.X = x;
					lastLabelBounds.Width = num2;
					break;
				}
				textBlock.RenderTransform = rotateTransform;
				dc.AddChild(textBlock, new Point(x, num3));
				break;
			}
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
			{
				double num3;
				double x;
				switch (axis.LabelOrientation)
				{
				case LabelOrientation.Vertical:
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - pos + num2 / 2.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Top - 2.0 <= num3 + num && lastLabelBounds.Bottom + 2.0 >= num3)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					x = axisRenderArea.Width - labelOffset - num;
					lastLabelBounds.Y = num3 - num2;
					lastLabelBounds.Height = num2;
					break;
				case LabelOrientation.VerticalSlant:
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - pos - num;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Top - 2.0 <= num3 + num && lastLabelBounds.Bottom + 2.0 >= num3)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					x = axisRenderArea.Width - labelOffset - num2;
					lastLabelBounds.Y = num3 - num2;
					lastLabelBounds.Height = num2;
					break;
				case LabelOrientation.HorizontalSlant:
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - pos - num / 2.0;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Top - 2.0 <= num3 + num && lastLabelBounds.Bottom + 2.0 >= num3)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					x = axisRenderArea.Width - labelOffset - num2;
					lastLabelBounds.Y = num3;
					lastLabelBounds.Height = num;
					break;
				default:
					num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - pos - num2 / 2.0 - annotation.LabelOffset;
					if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Top - 2.0 <= num3 + num2 && lastLabelBounds.Bottom + 2.0 >= num3)
					{
						return lastLabelBounds;
					}
					x = axisRenderArea.Width - labelOffset - num;
					lastLabelBounds.Y = num3;
					lastLabelBounds.Height = num2;
					break;
				}
				textBlock.RenderTransform = rotateTransform;
				dc.AddChild(textBlock, new Point(x, num3));
				break;
			}
			case PlotAxisID.Y2:
			{
				double num3 = axisRenderArea.Height - pos - num2 / 2.0;
				if (flag && lastLabelBounds.Top - 2.0 <= num3 + num2 && lastLabelBounds.Bottom + 2.0 >= num3)
				{
					return lastLabelBounds;
				}
				dc.AddChild(textBlock, new Point(labelOffset, axisRenderArea.Height - pos - num2 / 2.0));
				lastLabelBounds.Y = num3;
				break;
			}
			}
			return lastLabelBounds;
		}

		protected void DrawMajorTick(IGraphAxis axis, PlotCanvasBase dc, Rect renderArea, double pos, PlotPen pen)
		{
			if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks))
			{
				DrawTick(axis, dc, renderArea, pos, pen, 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling);
			}
		}

		protected void DrawMinorTick(IGraphAxis axis, PlotCanvasBase dc, Rect renderArea, double pos, PlotPen pen)
		{
			if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks))
			{
				DrawTick(axis, dc, renderArea, pos, pen, 5.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DrawTick(IGraphAxis axis, PlotCanvasBase dc, Rect renderArea, double pos, PlotPen pen, double tickLength)
		{
			switch (axis.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				dc.AddLine(pen, new Point(pos + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Top), new Point(pos + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Top + tickLength));
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.X2:
				dc.AddLine(pen, new Point(pos + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Bottom), new Point(pos + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Bottom - tickLength));
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				dc.AddLine(pen, new Point(renderArea.Right, renderArea.Bottom - pos), new Point(renderArea.Right - tickLength, renderArea.Bottom - pos));
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y2:
				dc.AddLine(pen, new Point(renderArea.Left, renderArea.Bottom - pos), new Point(renderArea.Left + tickLength, renderArea.Bottom - pos));
				break;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DrawGrid(IGraphAxis axis, PlotCanvasBase dc, Rect renderArea, object value, PlotPen pen)
		{
			IGraphAxis linkedAxis = axis.LinkedAxis;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 1.0;
			double num3;
			double num4;
			if ((axis.Id & PlotAxisID.XMask) != PlotAxisID.None)
			{
				num3 = renderArea.Width;
				num4 = renderArea.Height;
			}
			else
			{
				num3 = renderArea.Height;
				num4 = renderArea.Width;
			}
			bool valid = true;
			if (linkedAxis != null)
			{
				double num5 = 1E-06;
				if (linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Minimum.Value != 0.0 && linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Maximum.Value != 0.0)
				{
					num5 = Math.Min(Math.Abs(linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Minimum.Value * 1E-06), 1E-06);
				}
				if (Math.Abs(linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Maximum.Value - linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Minimum.Value) > num5)
				{
					num = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Minimum.Value, out valid);
					num2 = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Maximum.Value, out valid);
				}
				else
				{
					num = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
					num2 = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
				}
			}
			double num6 = ((State.GridLineSteps == 0.0) ? (num2 - num) : ((num2 - num) / State.GridLineSteps));
			double num7 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num8 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MaxVisible, out valid);
			double num9 = num3 / (num8 - num7);
			double num10 = num4 / (num2 - num);
			object gridFrom = ((linkedAxis == null) ? ((object)num) : linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(linkedAxis, num, out valid));
			object position = value;
			if (axis.GridCallback != null)
			{
				axis.GridCallback.AxisGridCallback(axis, value, gridFrom, ref position, ref gridFrom, ref gridFrom, ref valid);
			}
			for (double num11 = linkedAxis?.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, gridFrom, out valid) ?? Convert.ToDouble(gridFrom, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture); num11 < num2; num11 += num6)
			{
				object gridFrom2;
				object gridTo;
				if (linkedAxis != null)
				{
					gridFrom2 = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(linkedAxis, num11, out valid);
					gridTo = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(linkedAxis, num11 + num6, out valid);
				}
				else
				{
					gridFrom2 = num11;
					gridTo = num11 + num6;
				}
				object position2 = position;
				valid = true;
				if (axis.GridCallback != null)
				{
					axis.GridCallback.AxisGridCallback(axis, value, gridTo, ref position2, ref gridFrom2, ref gridTo, ref valid);
				}
				if (valid)
				{
					Point start = default(Point);
					Point finish = default(Point);
					double num12 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, position, out valid);
					if (valid)
					{
						double num13 = linkedAxis?.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, gridFrom2, out valid) ?? Convert.ToDouble(gridFrom2, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
						double num14 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, position2, out valid);
						if (valid)
						{
							double num15 = linkedAxis?.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, gridTo, out valid) ?? Convert.ToDouble(gridFrom2, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
							if (valid)
							{
								switch (axis.Id)
								{
								case PlotAxisID.X:
								case PlotAxisID.X2:
									start.X = renderArea.Left + (num12 - num7) * num9;
									start.Y = renderArea.Bottom - (num13 - num) * num10;
									finish.X = renderArea.Left + (num14 - num7) * num9;
									finish.Y = renderArea.Bottom - (num15 - num) * num10;
									break;
								case PlotAxisID.Y:
								case PlotAxisID.Y2:
									start.Y = renderArea.Bottom - (num12 - num7) * num9;
									start.X = renderArea.Left + (num13 - num) * num10;
									finish.Y = renderArea.Bottom - (num14 - num7) * num9;
									finish.X = renderArea.Left + (num15 - num) * num10;
									break;
								}
								if (num12 != num14 || num12 != num7 || linkedAxis == null || !linkedAxis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge))
								{
									dc.AddLine(pen, start, finish);
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				position = position2;
			}
		}

		public void RenderInitialise()
		{
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (refreshAll)
			{
				Size renderSize = RenderSize;
				if (renderArea.Height < PlotSupport.MinPlotSize || renderArea.Width < PlotSupport.MinPlotSize)
				{
					renderSize.Height = 1.0;
					renderSize.Width = 1.0;
				}
				Rect axisArea = renderArea;
				switch (Axis.Id)
				{
				case PlotAxisID.X:
					axisArea.Y = renderArea.Bottom - renderSize.Height;
					axisArea.Height = renderSize.Height;
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.X2:
					axisArea.Height = renderSize.Height;
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y:
					axisArea.Width = renderSize.Width;
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y2:
					axisArea.X = renderArea.Right - renderSize.Width;
					axisArea.Width = renderSize.Width;
					break;
				}
				Draw(ref renderArea, ref axisArea, refreshAll);
			}
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, ref Rect axisArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (!refreshAll)
			{
				return;
			}
			_visual.Children.Clear();
			DrawAxis(Axis, _visual, axisArea, renderArea);
			if (axisArea.Width > 4.0 && axisArea.Height > 4.0 && Axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Axis.Title))
			{
				PlotSupport.MakeLabelVisual(maxTextWidth: ((Axis.Id & PlotAxisID.Y) == 0 && (Axis.Id & PlotAxisID.Y2) == 0) ? axisArea.Width : axisArea.Height, label: Axis.Title, typeface: Axis.TitleFontFamily, emSize: Axis.TitleFontSize, colour: Axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title), renderTransform: null, useVisual: _axisTitleVisual, maxLineCount: 1, fontStyle: Axis.TitleFontStyle, fontWeight: Axis.TitleFontWeight, textDecorations: Axis.TitleTextDecorations);
				Rect contentBounds = _axisTitleVisual.ContentBounds;
				switch (Axis.Id)
				{
				case PlotAxisID.X:
					PlotSelection.SetElementType(_axisTitleVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.XAxisTitle);
					_axisTitleVisual.RenderArea = new Rect(axisArea.Left + (axisArea.Width - _axisTitleVisual.ContentBounds.Width) / 2.0, axisArea.Bottom - _axisTitleVisual.ContentBounds.Height - 4.0 * Axis.AxisFontScaling, contentBounds.Width, contentBounds.Height);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.X2:
					PlotSelection.SetElementType(_axisTitleVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.XAxisTitle);
					_axisTitleVisual.RenderArea = new Rect(axisArea.Left + (axisArea.Width - _axisTitleVisual.ContentBounds.Width) / 2.0, axisArea.Top + 4.0 * Axis.AxisFontScaling, contentBounds.Width, contentBounds.Height);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y:
					PlotSelection.SetElementType(_axisTitleVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.YAxisTitle);
					_axisTitleVisual.RenderTransform = new RotateTransform
					{
						Angle = -90.0
					};
					_axisTitleVisual.RenderArea = new Rect(axisArea.Left + 4.0 * Axis.AxisFontScaling, axisArea.Top + (axisArea.Height + _axisTitleVisual.ContentBounds.Width) / 2.0, contentBounds.Width, contentBounds.Height);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y2:
					PlotSelection.SetElementType(_axisTitleVisual, PlotSelectedElementType.YAxisTitle);
					_axisTitleVisual.RenderTransform = new RotateTransform
					{
						Angle = 90.0
					};
					_axisTitleVisual.RenderArea = new Rect(axisArea.Right - 4.0 * Axis.AxisFontScaling, axisArea.Top + (axisArea.Height - _axisTitleVisual.ContentBounds.Width) / 2.0, contentBounds.Width, contentBounds.Height);
					break;
				}
				if (!_visual.Children.Contains(_axisTitleVisual))
				{
					_visual.AddCanvas(_axisTitleVisual);
				}
			}
		}

		public Visual3D Draw3D(R3DCuboid volume, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (_labels3D == null)
			{
				_labels3D = new ModelVisual3D();
			}
			if (_visual3D == null)
			{
				_visual3D = new ModelVisual3D();
			}
			else if (!refreshAll)
			{
				return _visual3D;
			}
			PlotSupport.Clear3DVisual(_visual3D);
			PlotSupport.Clear3DVisual(_labels3D);
			Draw3DAxis(Axis, volume, _visual3D, _labels3D);
			if (Axis.Graph.GraphLayout.Renderer is I3DGraphRender i3DGraphRender)
			{
				if (!i3DGraphRender.LabelVisuals.Children.Contains(_labels3D))
				{
					i3DGraphRender.LabelVisuals.Children.Add(_labels3D);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_visual3D.Children.Add(_labels3D);
			}
			return _visual3D;
		}

		protected virtual void Draw3DAxis(IGraphAxis axis, R3DCuboid volume, ModelVisual3D parentVisual, ModelVisual3D labelsVisual)
		{
			WireLines wireLines = new WireLines
			{
				Lines = new Point3DCollection()
			};
			WireLines wireLines2 = new WireLines
			{
				Lines = new Point3DCollection()
			};
			WireLines wireLines3 = new WireLines
			{
				Lines = new Point3DCollection()
			};
			WireLines wireLines4 = new WireLines
			{
				Lines = new Point3DCollection()
			};
			WireLines wireLines5 = new WireLines
			{
				Lines = new Point3DCollection()
			};
			parentVisual.Children.Add(wireLines);
			parentVisual.Children.Add(wireLines2);
			parentVisual.Children.Add(wireLines4);
			parentVisual.Children.Add(wireLines5);
			parentVisual.Children.Add(wireLines3);
			wireLines.Color = axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge);
			wireLines.Thickness = 1.0;
			double num = 0.0;
			int num4;
			int num3;
			int num2;
			switch (axis.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				num4 = 1;
				num3 = (num2 = 0);
				num = volume.Width;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				num3 = 1;
				num4 = (num2 = 0);
				num = volume.Height;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				num2 = -1;
				num3 = (num4 = 0);
				num = volume.Depth;
				break;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException("3D graphs only support X, Y & Z axes");
			}
			if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge))
			{
				wireLines.Lines.Add(new Point3D(volume.X, volume.Y, 0.0 - volume.Z));
				wireLines.Lines.Add(new Point3D(volume.X + (double)num4 * volume.Width, volume.Y + (double)num3 * volume.Height, 0.0 - volume.Z + (double)num2 * volume.Depth));
			}
			double num5 = 4.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling;
			bool valid;
			double num6 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num7 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MaxVisible, out valid);
			double num8 = num / (num7 - num6);
			wireLines2.Color = axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks);
			wireLines3.Color = axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks);
			wireLines4.Color = axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines);
			wireLines5.Color = axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines);
			num5 = ((!axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks)) ? (num5 + 5.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling) : (num5 + 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling));
			Rect labelBounds = new Rect(0.0, double.MaxValue, 0.0, 0.0);
			Size size = default(Size);
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				foreach (RAxisAnnotation annotation in Annotations)
				{
					if (annotation.LinearPos < num6 || annotation.LinearPos > num7)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num9 = (annotation.LinearPos - num6) * num8;
					if (double.IsNaN(num9) || double.IsInfinity(num9))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (annotation.TickType == TickType.Minor)
					{
						Draw3DMinorTick(axis, volume, wireLines2, num9);
						if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines))
						{
							Draw3DGrid(axis, volume, wireLines4, annotation.dataValue);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						Draw3DMajorTick(axis, volume, wireLines3, num9);
						if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines))
						{
							Draw3DGrid(axis, volume, wireLines5, annotation.dataValue);
						}
						if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels))
						{
							labelBounds = Draw3DLabel(axis, annotation, volume, labelsVisual, num9, num5, labelBounds);
						}
					}
					size.Width = Math.Max(labelBounds.Width, size.Width);
					size.Height = Math.Max(labelBounds.Height, size.Height);
				}
			}
			if (Axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Axis.Title))
			{
				num5 = 8.0 * Axis.AxisFontScaling;
				num5 = ((!Axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks)) ? (num5 + 5.0 * Axis.AxisFontScaling) : (num5 + 8.0 * Axis.AxisFontScaling));
				AxisAngleRotation3D normalRotation = null;
				if (axis.Graph.GraphLayout is IGraphLayout3D graphLayout3D)
				{
					normalRotation = graphLayout3D.NormalRotation;
				}
				switch (Axis.Id)
				{
				case PlotAxisID.X:
					num5 += size.Height;
					labelsVisual.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(Axis.Title, Axis.TitleFontFamily, Axis.TitleFontSize, Axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title), double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, new Point3D(volume.X + volume.Width / 2.0, volume.Y - num5, 0.0 - volume.Z), HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment.Top, Axis.Id, back: false, normalRotation, out labelBounds, Axis.TitleFontStyle, Axis.TitleFontWeight, Axis.TitleTextDecorations));
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y:
					num5 += size.Width;
					labelsVisual.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(Axis.Title, Axis.TitleFontFamily, Axis.TitleFontSize, Axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title), double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, new Point3D(volume.X - num5, volume.Y + volume.Height / 2.0, 0.0 - volume.Z), HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment.Bottom, Axis.Id, back: false, normalRotation, out labelBounds, Axis.TitleFontStyle, Axis.TitleFontWeight, Axis.TitleTextDecorations));
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Z:
					num5 += size.Height;
					labelsVisual.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(Axis.Title, Axis.TitleFontFamily, Axis.TitleFontSize, Axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title), double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, new Point3D(volume.X, volume.Y - num5, volume.Z + volume.Depth / 2.0), HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment.Top, PlotAxisID.X, back: false, normalRotation, out labelBounds, Axis.TitleFontStyle, Axis.TitleFontWeight, Axis.TitleTextDecorations));
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected void Draw3DMajorTick(IGraphAxis axis, R3DCuboid volume, WireLines ticks, double pos)
		{
			if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks))
			{
				Draw3DTick(axis, volume, ticks, pos, 8.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling);
			}
		}

		protected void Draw3DMinorTick(IGraphAxis axis, R3DCuboid volume, WireLines ticks, double pos)
		{
			if (axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks))
			{
				Draw3DTick(axis, volume, ticks, pos, 5.0 * axis.AxisFontScaling);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void Draw3DTick(IGraphAxis axis, R3DCuboid volume, WireLines wires, double pos, double tickLength)
		{
			int num3;
			int num2;
			int num;
			switch (axis.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				num3 = 1;
				num2 = (num = 0);
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				num2 = 1;
				num3 = (num = 0);
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				num = -1;
				num2 = (num3 = 0);
				break;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException("3D graphs only support X, Y & Z axes");
			}
			wires.Lines.Add(new Point3D((double)num3 * pos + volume.X, (double)num2 * pos + volume.Y, (double)num * pos - volume.Z));
			wires.Lines.Add(new Point3D((double)num3 * pos - (double)num2 * tickLength + volume.X, (double)num2 * pos - (double)(num3 + -num) * tickLength + volume.Y, (double)num * pos - volume.Z));
		}

		protected virtual void Draw3DGrid(IGraphAxis axis, R3DCuboid volume, WireLines wires, object value)
		{
			IGraphAxis linkedAxis = axis.LinkedAxis;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 1.0;
			double num3;
			int num6;
			int num5;
			int num4;
			switch (axis.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				num3 = volume.Width;
				num6 = 1;
				num5 = (num4 = 0);
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				num3 = volume.Height;
				num5 = 1;
				num6 = (num4 = 0);
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				num3 = volume.Depth;
				num4 = -1;
				num5 = (num6 = 0);
				break;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException("3D graphs only support X, Y & Z axes");
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
			{
				double num7 = 0.0;
				double num8;
				int num11;
				int num10;
				int num9;
				switch (axis.Id)
				{
				case PlotAxisID.X:
					if (i == 0)
					{
						linkedAxis = axis.Graph.Axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.Z);
						num8 = volume.Depth;
						num11 = -1;
						num10 = (num9 = 0);
					}
					else
					{
						linkedAxis = axis.Graph.Axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y);
						num8 = volume.Height;
						num10 = 1;
						num11 = (num9 = 0);
						num7 = 0.0 - volume.Depth;
					}
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y:
					if (i == 0)
					{
						linkedAxis = axis.Graph.Axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.Z);
						num8 = volume.Depth;
						num11 = -1;
						num10 = (num9 = 0);
					}
					else
					{
						linkedAxis = axis.Graph.Axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.X);
						num8 = volume.Width;
						num9 = 1;
						num11 = (num10 = 0);
						num7 = 0.0 - volume.Depth;
					}
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Z:
					if (i == 0)
					{
						linkedAxis = axis.Graph.Axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.X);
						num8 = volume.Width;
						num9 = 1;
						num10 = (num11 = 0);
					}
					else
					{
						linkedAxis = axis.Graph.Axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y);
						num8 = volume.Height;
						num10 = 1;
						num11 = (num9 = 0);
					}
					break;
				default:
					throw new InvalidOperationException("3D graphs only support X, Y & Z axes");
				}
				num = linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Minimum.Value;
				num2 = linkedAxis.VisibleRange.Maximum.Value;
				bool valid = true;
				double num12 = ((State.GridLineSteps == 0.0) ? (num2 - num) : ((num2 - num) / State.GridLineSteps));
				double num13 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MinVisible, out valid);
				double num14 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, State.MaxVisible, out valid);
				double num15 = num3 / (num14 - num13);
				double num16 = num8 / (num2 - num);
				object gridFrom = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(linkedAxis, num, out valid);
				object position = value;
				if (axis.GridCallback != null)
				{
					axis.GridCallback.AxisGridCallback(axis, value, gridFrom, ref position, ref gridFrom, ref gridFrom, ref valid);
				}
				for (double num17 = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, gridFrom, out valid); num17 < num2; num17 += num12)
				{
					if (num12 == 0.0)
					{
						break;
					}
					object gridFrom2 = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(linkedAxis, num17, out valid);
					object gridTo = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(linkedAxis, num17 + num12, out valid);
					object position2 = position;
					valid = true;
					if (axis.GridCallback != null)
					{
						axis.GridCallback.AxisGridCallback(axis, value, gridTo, ref position2, ref gridFrom2, ref gridTo, ref valid);
					}
					if (valid)
					{
						Point3D value2 = default(Point3D);
						Point3D value3 = default(Point3D);
						double num18 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, position, out valid);
						if (valid)
						{
							num18 = (num18 - num13) * num15;
							double num19 = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, gridFrom2, out valid);
							num19 = (num19 - num) * num16;
							double num20 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, position2, out valid);
							if (valid)
							{
								num20 = (num20 - num13) * num15;
								double num21 = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(linkedAxis, gridTo, out valid);
								if (valid)
								{
									num21 = (num21 - num) * num16;
									value2.X = volume.X + num18 * (double)num6 + num19 * (double)num9;
									value2.Y = volume.Y + num18 * (double)num5 + num19 * (double)num10;
									value2.Z = 0.0 - volume.Z + num7 + num18 * (double)num4 + num19 * (double)num11;
									value3.X = volume.X + num20 * (double)num6 + num21 * (double)num9;
									value3.Y = volume.Y + num20 * (double)num5 + num21 * (double)num10;
									value3.Z = 0.0 - volume.Z + num7 + num20 * (double)num4 + num21 * (double)num11;
									if (num18 != num20 || num18 != num13 || linkedAxis == null || !linkedAxis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge))
									{
										wires.Lines.Add(value2);
										wires.Lines.Add(value3);
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					position = position2;
				}
			}
		}

		protected Rect Draw3DLabel(IGraphAxis axis, RAxisAnnotation annotation, R3DCuboid volume, ModelVisual3D v3D, double pos, double labelOffset, Rect lastLabelBounds)
		{
			Point3D origin = default(Point3D);
			AxisAngleRotation3D normalRotation = null;
			if (axis.Graph.GraphLayout is IGraphLayout3D graphLayout3D)
			{
				normalRotation = graphLayout3D.NormalRotation;
			}
			switch (axis.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				origin.X = volume.X + pos;
				origin.Y = volume.Y - labelOffset;
				origin.Z = 0.0 - volume.Z;
				v3D.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(annotation.Label, Axis.LabelFontFamily, Axis.LabelFontSize, Axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels), double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, origin, HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment.Top, Axis.Id, back: false, normalRotation, out lastLabelBounds, Axis.LabelFontStyle, Axis.LabelFontWeight, Axis.LabelTextDecorations));
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				origin.X = volume.X - labelOffset;
				origin.Y = volume.Y + pos;
				origin.Z = 0.0 - volume.Z;
				v3D.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(annotation.Label, Axis.LabelFontFamily, Axis.LabelFontSize, Axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels), double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, origin, HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment.Center, PlotAxisID.X, back: false, normalRotation, out lastLabelBounds, Axis.LabelFontStyle, Axis.LabelFontWeight, Axis.LabelTextDecorations));
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				origin.X = volume.X;
				origin.Y = volume.Y - labelOffset;
				origin.Z = 0.0 - volume.Z - pos;
				v3D.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(annotation.Label, Axis.LabelFontFamily, Axis.LabelFontSize, Axis.GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels), double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, origin, HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment.Top, PlotAxisID.X, back: false, normalRotation, out lastLabelBounds, Axis.LabelFontStyle, Axis.LabelFontWeight, Axis.LabelTextDecorations));
				break;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException("3D graphs only support X, Y & Z axes");
			}
			return lastLabelBounds;
		}
	}
	public class LinearAxisAnnotator : GraphAxisAnnotator
	{
		private PlotAxisDataType _dataType;

		public override string AnnotatorType => "Linear";

		public override PlotAxisDataType SupportedDataTypes => PlotAxisDataType.Number | PlotAxisDataType.DateTime | PlotAxisDataType.ElapsedTime | PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel;

		public override PlotAxisDataType DataType
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataType;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & SupportedDataTypes) != value)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Data type not supported by axis annotator");
				}
				_dataType = value;
			}
		}

		public override string RecommendedFormatString
		{
			get
			{
				string result = base.RecommendedFormatString;
				if (State.MinVisible < State.MaxVisible)
				{
					int val = (int)Math.Log10(Math.Max(Math.Abs(State.MaxVisible), Math.Abs(State.MinVisible)) / (State.MaxVisible - State.MinVisible)) + 2;
					result = "g" + (Math.Max(3, val) + 1).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public override double AxisLinearMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearMapCallback(axis, value, out valid);
			}
			return PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
		}

		public override object AxisLinearUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearUnmapCallback(axis, point, out valid);
			}
			return PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axis, point, out valid);
		}

		public override void BuildLabelList(IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				base.Annotations.Clear();
				double num = Math.Min(State.MinView, State.MaxView);
				double num2 = num - State.MajorStep;
				double num3 = Math.Max(State.MaxVisible, State.MinVisible);
				double num4 = Math.Min(State.MaxVisible, State.MinVisible);
				if (num < num4)
				{
					num += State.MajorStep;
				}
				while ((num2 - num3) / State.MajorStep <= 1E-06)
				{
					if ((num2 - num4) / State.MajorStep >= -1E-06)
					{
						RAxisAnnotation item = default(RAxisAnnotation);
						double num5 = num2;
						string labelText = "";
						if (Math.Abs(num5) / Math.Max(Math.Abs(State.MinVisible), Math.Abs(State.MaxVisible)) < 1E-08)
						{
							num5 = 0.0;
						}
						bool valid;
						object position = AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, num5, out valid);
						if (valid)
						{
							TickType tickType;
							if (Math.Abs((num2 - num) / State.MajorStep) < 1E-06)
							{
								labelText = string.Format(format: (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(axis.DataLabelFormatString)) ? ("{0:" + axis.DataLabelFormatString + "}") : "{0:g4}", provider: CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, arg0: position);
								num += State.MajorStep;
								tickType = TickType.Major;
							}
							else
							{
								tickType = TickType.Minor;
							}
							if (axis.LabelCallback != null)
							{
								axis.LabelCallback.AxisLabelCallback(axis, position, ref labelText, ref position, ref valid);
							}
							if (valid)
							{
								item.LinearPos = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, position, out valid);
								if (valid)
								{
									item.dataValue = position;
									item.Label = labelText;
									item.TickType = tickType;
									base.Annotations.Add(item);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					double num6 = num2;
					num2 += State.MinorStep;
					int num7 = 10;
					while (num2 == num6)
					{
						num2 += State.MinorStep * (double)num7;
						num7 *= 10;
					}
				}
				base.Annotations.Sort();
			}
		}

		public override void Initialise(IGraphAxis axis, bool reinitialise)
		{
			if (!(!Initialised || reinitialise))
			{
				return;
			}
			base.Initialise(axis, reinitialise);
			double minView = State.MinView;
			double maxView = State.MaxView;
			if (State.MajorStep > 0.0 || State.MinorStep > 0.0)
			{
				if (State.MajorStep > 0.0)
				{
					State.MajorStep = (State.MaxExtent - State.MinExtent) / State.MajorStep;
				}
				else
				{
					State.MajorStep = 1.0;
				}
				if (State.MinorStep + 1.0 > 0.0)
				{
					State.MinorStep = State.MajorStep / (State.MinorStep + 1.0);
				}
				else
				{
					State.MinorStep = 0.1;
				}
			}
			else if (axis.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
			{
				State.MajorStep = 1.0;
				State.MinorStep = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				double num = ((maxView > minView) ? Math.Pow(10.0, Math.Floor(Math.Log10(maxView - minView))) : ((!(maxView < minView)) ? 0.0 : Math.Pow(10.0, Math.Floor(Math.Log10(minView - maxView)))));
				double num2 = ((num == 0.0) ? 1.0 : ((maxView - minView) / num));
				switch ((!(num2 > 7.0)) ? ((num2 > 5.0) ? 1 : ((num2 > 3.0) ? 2 : ((!(num2 > 2.0)) ? 4 : 3))) : 0)
				{
				case 0:
					State.MajorStep = 2.0 * num;
					State.MinorStep = 0.5 * num;
					break;
				case 1:
					State.MajorStep = 1.0 * num;
					State.MinorStep = 0.5 * num;
					break;
				case 2:
					State.MajorStep = 0.5 * num;
					State.MinorStep = 0.25 * num;
					break;
				case 3:
					State.MajorStep = 0.5 * num;
					State.MinorStep = 0.1 * num;
					break;
				case 4:
					State.MajorStep = 0.2 * num;
					State.MinorStep = 0.1 * num;
					break;
				default:
					State.MajorStep = 1.0;
					State.MinorStep = 0.1;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (State.MajorStep != 0.0)
			{
				State.MinView = State.MajorStep * Math.Ceiling(minView / State.MajorStep);
				State.MinExtent = State.MajorStep * Math.Ceiling(State.MinExtent / State.MajorStep);
				State.MaxView = State.MajorStep * Math.Floor(maxView / State.MajorStep);
				State.MaxExtent = State.MajorStep * Math.Floor(State.MaxExtent / State.MajorStep);
			}
			else
			{
				State.MajorStep = (State.MinorStep = 1.0);
				State.MinView = 0.0;
				State.MinExtent = 0.0;
				State.MaxView = 1.0;
				State.MaxExtent = 1.0;
			}
			State.MinDetail = State.MinVisible;
			State.MaxDetail = State.MaxVisible;
			BuildLabelList(axis);
			Initialised = true;
		}
	}
	public class LogarithmicAxisAnnotator : GraphAxisAnnotator
	{
		private const int MaxLabels = 10;

		public override string AnnotatorType => "Logarithmic";

		public override PlotAxisDataType SupportedDataTypes => PlotAxisDataType.Number;

		public override PlotAxisDataType DataType
		{
			get
			{
				return PlotAxisDataType.Number;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & SupportedDataTypes) != value)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Data type not supported by axis annotator");
				}
			}
		}

		public override double AxisLinearMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearMapCallback(axis, value, out valid);
			}
			return PlotSupport.ValueToLogDouble(axis, value, out valid);
		}

		public override object AxisLinearUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearUnmapCallback(axis, point, out valid);
			}
			return PlotSupport.LogDoubleToValue(axis, point, out valid);
		}

		public override void BuildLabelList(IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				base.Annotations.Clear();
				double num = Math.Floor(State.MajorStep / 10.0) * 10.0;
				double num2 = State.MajorStep - num;
				num = Math.Floor(State.MinorStep / 10.0) * 10.0;
				double num3 = State.MinorStep - num;
				double num4 = State.MinExtent;
				bool flag = false;
				while (num4 <= State.MaxExtent)
				{
					double num5 = 1.0;
					RAxisAnnotation item = default(RAxisAnnotation);
					for (; num5 <= 9.99 && num4 * num5 <= State.MaxExtent; num5 += num3)
					{
						double num6 = num4 * num5;
						string labelText = "";
						object position = num6;
						TickType tickType;
						if (num5 == 1.0 || Math.Abs(num5 / num2 - Math.Round(num5 / num2)) < 1E-06)
						{
							tickType = TickType.Major;
							labelText = string.Format(format: (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(axis.DataLabelFormatString)) ? ("{0:" + axis.DataLabelFormatString + "}") : "{0:g4}", provider: CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, arg0: num6);
						}
						else
						{
							tickType = TickType.Minor;
						}
						flag = true;
						if (axis.LabelCallback != null)
						{
							axis.LabelCallback.AxisLabelCallback(axis, position, ref labelText, ref position, ref flag);
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							item.LinearPos = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, position, out flag);
							if (flag)
							{
								item.dataValue = position;
								item.Label = labelText;
								item.TickType = tickType;
								base.Annotations.Add(item);
							}
						}
					}
					num4 *= num;
				}
				base.Annotations.Sort();
			}
		}

		public override void Initialise(IGraphAxis axis, bool reinitialise)
		{
			if (!(!Initialised || reinitialise))
			{
				return;
			}
			base.Initialise(axis, reinitialise);
			double minView = State.MinView;
			double maxView = State.MaxView;
			State.MajorStep = 0.0;
			State.MinorStep = 0.0;
			double y = Math.Floor(Math.Log10(minView));
			double num = Math.Pow(10.0, y);
			int num2;
			int num3;
			double num4;
			double num5;
			if (State.MajorStep == 0.0 && State.MinorStep == 0.0)
			{
				State.MajorStep = 10.0;
				State.MinorStep = 10.0;
				num2 = 1;
				num3 = 1;
				num4 = 0.0;
				num5 = 0.0;
			}
			else
			{
				double num6 = Math.Floor(State.MajorStep / 10.0) * 10.0;
				num4 = State.MajorStep - num6;
				State.MajorStep = num6;
				num6 = Math.Floor(State.MinorStep / 10.0) * 10.0;
				num5 = State.MinorStep - num6;
				State.MinorStep = num6;
				num2 = 0;
				num3 = 1;
			}
			while (num3 != 0)
			{
				double num7 = 0.0;
				switch (num2)
				{
				case 1:
					num4 = 0.1;
					num5 = 0.02;
					break;
				case 2:
					num4 = 0.2;
					num5 = 0.05;
					break;
				case 3:
					num4 = 0.5;
					num5 = 0.1;
					break;
				case 4:
					num4 = 1.0;
					num5 = 0.2;
					break;
				case 5:
					num4 = 2.0;
					num5 = 0.5;
					break;
				case 6:
					num4 = 5.0;
					num5 = 1.0;
					break;
				case 7:
					num4 = 9.9;
					num5 = 2.0;
					break;
				case 8:
					num4 = 9.9;
					num5 = 9.9;
					State.MajorStep = 100.0;
					State.MinorStep = 10.0;
					break;
				default:
					State.MajorStep *= 10.0;
					State.MinorStep *= 10.0;
					break;
				case 0:
					break;
				}
				if (num4 > 1.1 && num4 < 9.0)
				{
					num7 = -1.0;
				}
				int num8 = 0;
				num3 = 0;
				double num6 = num;
				while (num6 <= maxView && num3 == 0)
				{
					double num9 = num7;
					double num10;
					for (num10 = 1.0; num10 <= 9.99; num10 += num4)
					{
						double num11 = num6 * num10;
						if (num11 >= minView && num11 <= maxView)
						{
							if (num8 >= 10)
							{
								if (num2 > 0)
								{
									num3 = 1;
								}
								break;
							}
							num8++;
						}
						if (num11 >= maxView)
						{
							break;
						}
						num10 += num9;
						num9 = 0.0;
					}
					num6 *= State.MajorStep;
				}
				if (num3 != 0 && num2 < 9)
				{
					num2++;
				}
			}
			State.MajorStep += num4;
			State.MinorStep += num5;
			State.MinView = num;
			if (State.MinView < State.MinVisible)
			{
				State.MinView = State.MinVisible;
			}
			State.MaxView = maxView;
			if (State.MaxView > State.MaxVisible)
			{
				State.MaxView = State.MaxVisible;
			}
			State.MinExtent = Math.Pow(10.0, Math.Floor(Math.Log10(State.MinExtent)));
			State.MinDetail = State.MinView;
			State.MaxDetail = State.MaxView;
			BuildLabelList(axis);
			Initialised = true;
		}
	}
	public class ProbabilityAxisAnnotator : GraphAxisAnnotator
	{
		internal struct ProbPointRec
		{
			internal double Value;

			internal int Level;

			internal ProbPointRec(double value, int level)
			{
				Value = value;
				Level = level;
			}
		}

		private ProbPointRec[] ProbPoint = new ProbPointRec[25]
		{
			new ProbPointRec(0.0001, 4),
			new ProbPointRec(0.9999, 4),
			new ProbPointRec(0.0005, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.9995, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.001, 3),
			new ProbPointRec(0.999, 3),
			new ProbPointRec(0.002, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.998, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.005, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.995, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.01, 3),
			new ProbPointRec(0.99, 3),
			new ProbPointRec(0.02, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.98, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.05, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.95, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.1, 4),
			new ProbPointRec(0.9, 4),
			new ProbPointRec(0.2, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.8, 2),
			new ProbPointRec(0.3, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.7, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.4, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.6, 1),
			new ProbPointRec(0.5, 4)
		};

		private int _category;

		public override string AnnotatorType => "Probability";

		public override PlotAxisDataType SupportedDataTypes => PlotAxisDataType.Number;

		public override PlotAxisDataType DataType
		{
			get
			{
				return PlotAxisDataType.Number;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & SupportedDataTypes) != value)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Data type not supported by axis annotator");
				}
			}
		}

		public override double AxisLinearMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearMapCallback(axis, value, out valid);
			}
			double num = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return double.NaN;
			}
			if (num <= 0.0 || num >= 1.0)
			{
				valid = false;
				return double.NaN;
			}
			if (num < 1E-05)
			{
				num = 1E-05;
			}
			else if (num > 0.99999)
			{
				num = 0.99999;
			}
			double num2 = ((!(num > 0.5)) ? num : (1.0 - num));
			double num3 = ((num2 == 0.0) ? 0.0 : Math.Sqrt(Math.Log(1.0 / (num2 * num2))));
			num3 -= (2.515517 + num3 * (0.802853 + num3 * 0.010328)) / (1.0 + num3 * (1.432788 + num3 * (0.189269 + num3 * 0.001308)));
			if (num <= 0.5)
			{
				return 0.0 - num3;
			}
			return num3;
		}

		public override object AxisLinearUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearUnmapCallback(axis, point, out valid);
			}
			double num = 0.5;
			double num2 = 0.99999;
			double num3 = 1E-05;
			for (int i = 0; i <= 40; i++)
			{
				num = (num2 + num3) / 2.0;
				double num4 = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, num, out valid);
				if (Math.Abs(num4 - point) < 5E-05)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (num4 < point)
				{
					num3 = num;
				}
				else
				{
					num2 = num;
				}
			}
			return PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axis, num, out valid);
		}

		public override void BuildLabelList(IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				base.Annotations.Clear();
				for (int i = 0; i < ProbPoint.Length; i++)
				{
					double value = ProbPoint[i].Value;
					bool valid = value >= State.MinVisible && value <= State.MaxVisible;
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					object obj = PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axis, value, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					RAxisAnnotation item = new RAxisAnnotation
					{
						LinearPos = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, obj, out valid)
					};
					if (valid)
					{
						item.dataValue = obj;
						string label;
						if (ProbPoint[i].Level >= _category)
						{
							item.TickType = TickType.Major;
							label = string.Format(format: (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(axis.DataLabelFormatString)) ? ("{0:" + axis.DataLabelFormatString + "}") : "{0:g4}", provider: CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, arg0: obj);
						}
						else
						{
							item.TickType = TickType.Minor;
							label = string.Empty;
						}
						item.Label = label;
						base.Annotations.Add(item);
					}
				}
				base.Annotations.Sort();
			}
		}

		public override void Initialise(IGraphAxis axis, bool reinitialise)
		{
			if (!(!Initialised || reinitialise))
			{
				return;
			}
			base.Initialise(axis, reinitialise);
			State.MinVisible = State.MinView;
			State.MaxVisible = State.MaxView;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = ProbPoint.Length - 1;
			for (int i = 1; i < ProbPoint.Length; i++)
			{
				if (State.MinVisible < ProbPoint[i].Value)
				{
					num = i - 1;
					break;
				}
			}
			for (int num3 = ProbPoint.Length - 1; num3 >= 0; num3--)
