			{
				if (State.MaxVisible > ProbPoint[num3].Value)
				{
					num2 = num3 + 1;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (num2 - num >= 20)
			{
				_category = 3;
			}
			else if (num2 - num >= 13)
			{
				_category = 2;
			}
			else if (num2 - num >= 8)
			{
				_category = 1;
			}
			else
			{
				_category = 0;
			}
			if (_category > 3)
			{
				_category = 3;
			}
			BuildLabelList(axis);
			Initialised = true;
		}
	}
	public static class PlotAxisConstants
	{
		public const double MajorTickLength = 8.0;

		public const double MinorTickLength = 5.0;

		public const double LabelSize = 10.0;

		public const double TitleSize = 12.0;

		public const double Spacing = 4.0;
	}
	public class GraphAxis : IGraphAxis, IAxisRender, IPlotRender, I3DPlotRender
	{
		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private IGraphAxisAnnotator _axisAnnotator;

		private IAxisMapCallback _mapCallback;

		private IAxisGridCallback _gridCallback;

		private IAxisLabelCallback _labelCallback;

		private PlotAxisID _id;

		private PlotAxisID _linkedAxisId;

		private string _title = string.Empty;

		private FontFamily _titleTypeface;

		private double _titleFontSize = 12.0;

		private FontStyle? _titleFontStyle;

		private FontWeight? _titleFontWeight;

		private TextDecorationCollection _titleTextDecorations;

		private FontFamily _labelTypeface;

		private double _labelFontSize = 10.0;

		private FontStyle? _labelFontStyle;

		private FontWeight? _labelFontWeight;

		private TextDecorationCollection _labelTextDecorations;

		private LabelOrientation _labelOrientation;

		private string _displayUom;

		private PlotAxisDataType _axisDataType = PlotAxisDataType.Number;

		private string _dataFormatString = string.Empty;

		private string _scooterValueFormat = string.Empty;

		private List<string> _valueLabels;

		private Dictionary<PlotAxisVisualElements, bool> _visualElementsVisibility = new Dictionary<PlotAxisVisualElements, bool>();

		private Dictionary<PlotAxisVisualElements, Color> _visualElementsColour = new Dictionary<PlotAxisVisualElements, Color>();

		private IValueRange _dataRange = new ValueRange();

		private IValueRange _visibleRange = new ValueRange();

		private IValueRange _linearRange = new ValueRange();

		private IValueRange _zoomRange = new ValueRange();

		private IValueRange _initialZoomRange = new ValueRange();

		private PlotAxisScaling _scaling = PlotAxisScaling.AutoMargin;

		private bool _zoomChanged;

		private bool _visualElementsChanged = true;

		private bool _stretchRequired = true;

		private Size _renderSize = Size.Empty;

		private bool _rangeChanged = true;

		private double _dataRangeMinimum;

		private double _dataRangeMaximum;

		private double _bottomLabelOffset;

		private double _centerLabelOffset;

		private double _topLabelOffset;

		private double _axisFontScaling = 1.0;

		private bool _redrawAll;

		private IGraph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as IGraph;
			}
		}

		public bool ValueRangesChanged
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_rangeChanged)
				{
					return _zoomChanged;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public IValueRange DataRange => _dataRange;

		public IValueRange VisibleRange => _visibleRange;

		public IValueRange LinearRange => _linearRange;

		public virtual IValueRange ZoomRange
		{
			get
			{
				return _zoomRange;
			}
			set
			{
				double num = value.Minimum.Value * (_zoomRange.Maximum.Value - _zoomRange.Minimum.Value) + _zoomRange.Minimum.Value;
				double num2 = value.Maximum.Value * (_zoomRange.Maximum.Value - _zoomRange.Minimum.Value) + _zoomRange.Minimum.Value;
				if (num < 0.0)
				{
					num2 += 0.0 - num;
				}
				if (num2 > 1.0)
				{
					num -= num2 - 1.0;
				}
				num = Math.Max(num, 0.0);
				num2 = Math.Min(num2, 1.0);
				_zoomRange.SetAbsolute(num, num2, validate: true);
				_zoomChanged = true;
			}
		}

		public virtual IValueRange InitialZoomRange
		{
			get
			{
				return _initialZoomRange;
			}
			set
			{
				_initialZoomRange.SetAbsolute(value.Minimum.Value, value.Maximum.Value, validate: true);
			}
		}

		public virtual PlotAxisDataType DataType
		{

			{
				if (_axisAnnotator != null)

					_axisDataType = _axisAnnotator.DataType;
					return _axisAnnotator.DataType;
				}
				return _axisDataType;
			}
			set

					DataRange.AboveZero = !_axisAnnotator.ValueIsValid(this, 0.0);
					PlotSupport.RegisterAxisAnnotator(_axisAnnotator);

						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private GraphAxis()
		{
		}

		public GraphAxis(IGraph graph, IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
			AxisAnnotator = axisAnnotator ?? new LinearAxisAnnotator();
			_zoomRange.SetAbsolute(0.0, 1.0, validate: true);
			_initialZoomRange.SetAbsolute(0.0, 1.0, validate: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title, value: true);
			_visualElementsColour.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge, Colors.Black);
			_visualElementsColour.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, Colors.Black);
			_visualElementsColour.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines, Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, 250, 128, 114));
			_visualElementsColour.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks, Colors.Black);
			_visualElementsColour.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines, Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, byte.MaxValue, 218, 185));
			_visualElementsColour.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks, Colors.Black);
			_visualElementsColour.Add(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title, Colors.Black);
			_zoomRange.ValueChanged += ZoomRange_ValueChanged;
			_initialZoomRange.ValueChanged += InitialZoomRange_ValueChanged;
			_dataRange.ValueChanged += DataRange_ValueChanged;
		}

		protected virtual void InitialZoomRange_ValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ZoomReset();
		}

		protected virtual void DataRange_ValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_rangeChanged = true;
		}

		protected virtual void ZoomRange_ValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_zoomChanged = true;
		}

		public virtual void BeginDataRangeUpdate()
		{
			_dataRangeMinimum = DataRange.Minimum.Value;
			_dataRangeMaximum = DataRange.Maximum.Value;
			if (_scaling != PlotAxisScaling.None)
			{
				DataRange.Invalidate();
			}
			_rangeChanged = false;
		}

		public virtual void EndDataRangeUpdate()
		{
			if (_dataRangeMinimum != DataRange.Minimum.Value || _dataRangeMaximum != DataRange.Maximum.Value)
			{
				_rangeChanged = true;
			}
			_dataRangeMinimum = DataRange.Minimum.Value;
			_dataRangeMaximum = DataRange.Maximum.Value;
		}

		public virtual void ZoomReset()
		{
			_zoomRange.SetAbsolute(_initialZoomRange.Minimum.Value, _initialZoomRange.Maximum.Value, validate: true);
			_zoomChanged = true;
		}

		public virtual void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, bool visibility)
		{
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, visibility, viceVersa: false);
		}

		public virtual void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, bool visibility, bool viceVersa)
		{
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge, visibility, viceVersa);
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, visibility, viceVersa);
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines, visibility, viceVersa);
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks, visibility, viceVersa);
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines, visibility, viceVersa);
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks, visibility, viceVersa);
			SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.Title, visibility, viceVersa);
			_visualElementsChanged = true;
		}

		private void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, PlotAxisVisualElements elementToSet, bool visibility, bool viceVersa)
		{
			if ((elements & elementToSet) == elementToSet)
			{
				_visualElementsVisibility[elementToSet] = visibility;
			}
			else if (viceVersa)
			{
				_visualElementsVisibility[elementToSet] = !visibility;
			}
		}

		private void SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, PlotAxisVisualElements elementToSet, Color colour)
		{
			if ((elements & elementToSet) == elementToSet)
			{
				_visualElementsColour[elementToSet] = colour;
			}
		}

		public virtual void SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, Color colour)
		{
			SetVisualElementColour(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge, colour);
			SetVisualElementColour(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, colour);
			SetVisualElementColour(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines, colour);
			SetVisualElementColour(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks, colour);
			SetVisualElementColour(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines, colour);
			SetVisualElementColour(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks, colour);
			SetVisualElementColour(elements, PlotAxisVisualElements.Title, colour);
			_visualElementsChanged = true;
		}

		public virtual bool GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements element)
		{
			return _visualElementsVisibility[element];
		}

		public virtual Color GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements element)
		{
			return _visualElementsColour[element];
		}

		public virtual void Stretch(object value)
		{
			Stretch(value, null);
		}

		public virtual void Stretch(object value, IDataSeries dataSeries)
		{
			if ((_scaling & PlotAxisScaling.Auto) == PlotAxisScaling.Auto)
			{
				bool valid;
				double value2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(this, value, out valid);
				if (valid && AxisAnnotator.ValueIsValid(this, value))
				{
					DataRange.Stretch(value2);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetLinearRange()
		{
			bool valid;
			double num = _axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(this, _dataRange.Minimum.Value, out valid);
			double num2 = _axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(this, _dataRange.Maximum.Value, out valid);
			if ((Scaling & PlotAxisScaling.AutoMargin) == PlotAxisScaling.AutoMargin)
			{
				double num3 = 0.05 * (num2 - num);
				if (num3 == 0.0 || num == num - num3 || num2 == num2 + num3)
				{
					num3 = 0.05 * Math.Abs(num);
					if (num3 == 0.0)
					{
						num3 = 0.05;
					}
				}
				num -= num3;
				num2 += num3;
			}
			double num4 = num + (num2 - num) * _zoomRange.Minimum.Value;
			double num5 = num + (num2 - num) * _zoomRange.Maximum.Value;
			double minimum = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(this, _axisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(this, num4, out valid), out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				minimum = _dataRange.Minimum.Value;
				num4 = num;
			}
			double maximum = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(this, _axisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(this, num5, out valid), out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				maximum = _dataRange.Maximum.Value;
				num5 = num2;
			}
			_linearRange.SetAbsolute(num4, num5, validate: true);
			_visibleRange.SetAbsolute(minimum, maximum, validate: true);
		}

		public virtual void RenderInitialise()
		{
			_redrawAll = _redrawAll || _rangeChanged || _zoomChanged || _visualElementsChanged;
			if (_redrawAll)
			{
				SetLinearRange();
				AxisAnnotator.Initialise(this, reinitialise: true);
				AxisAnnotator.Renderer.RenderInitialise();
				AxisAnnotator.EnsureRenderSize(this, _renderSize);
			}
			_visualElementsChanged = (_stretchRequired = false);
		}

		public virtual void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			AxisAnnotator.Renderer.Draw(ref renderArea, refreshAll || _redrawAll);
			ResetChangeFlags();
		}

		public virtual void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, ref Rect axisArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			AxisAnnotator.Renderer.Draw(ref renderArea, ref axisArea, refreshAll || _redrawAll);
			ResetChangeFlags();
		}

		public Visual3D Draw3D(R3DCuboid volume, bool refreshAll)
		{
			Visual3D result = ((AxisAnnotator.Renderer as I3DPlotRender) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Axis annotator does not support 3D.")).Draw3D(volume, refreshAll || _redrawAll);
			ResetChangeFlags();
			return result;
		}

		protected void ResetChangeFlags()
		{
			_rangeChanged = (_zoomChanged = (_redrawAll = false));
		}
	}
	public class DataSeriesCollection : List<IDataSeries>, IDataSeriesCollection, IList<IDataSeries>, ICollection<IDataSeries>, IEnumerable<IDataSeries>, IEnumerable
	{
		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private Graph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as Graph;
			}
		}

		private DataSeriesCollection()
		{
		}

		public DataSeriesCollection(Graph graph)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
		}

		public new void Add(IDataSeries item)
		{
			base.Add(item);
			Graph graph = _graph;
			bool refreshRequired = (_graph.Axes.StretchRequired = true);
			graph.RefreshRequired = refreshRequired;
		}

		public new bool Remove(IDataSeries item)
		{
			Graph graph = _graph;
			bool refreshRequired = (_graph.Axes.StretchRequired = true);
			graph.RefreshRequired = refreshRequired;
			return base.Remove(item);
		}

		public new void Clear()
		{
			base.Clear();
			Graph graph = _graph;
			bool refreshRequired = (_graph.Axes.StretchRequired = true);
			graph.RefreshRequired = refreshRequired;
		}
	}
	public class AxisRanges : Dictionary<PlotAxisID, IValueRange>, IAxisRanges, IDictionary<PlotAxisID, IValueRange>, ICollection<KeyValuePair<PlotAxisID, IValueRange>>, IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<PlotAxisID, IValueRange>>, IEnumerable
	{
	}
	public class DataSeries : IDataSeries, ISurfaceDataSeries, IPlotRender, I3DPlotRender
	{
		[Flags]
		private enum ClipPoints
		{
			None = 0,
			XMin = 1,
			XMax = 2,
			YMin = 4,
			YMax = 8,
			ZMin = 0x10,
			ZMax = 0x20
		}

		private struct PointAndIndex
		{
			public Point3D point;

			public int index;
		}

		private IPlotDataSource _dataSource;

		private string _label;

		private FontFamily _labelTypeface;

		private FontStyle? _labelFontStyle;

		private FontWeight? _labelFontWeight;

		private TextDecorationCollection _labelTextDecorations;

		private bool _visible = true;

		private Dictionary<PlotSeriesVisualElements, bool> _visualElementsVisibility = new Dictionary<PlotSeriesVisualElements, bool>();

		private PlotAxisID _xMapId = PlotAxisID.X;

		private PlotAxisID _yMapId = PlotAxisID.Y;

		private PlotAxisID _zMapId = PlotAxisID.Z;

		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private bool _visualElementsChanged = true;

		private object _axisGroup;

		private object _tag;

		private Dictionary<object, IDataSeriesStyle> _styles;

		private DataSeriesStyle _defaultStyle;

		private IDataPoint _extensionPoint = RDataPoint.EmptyDataPoint;

		private AxisRanges _axisRanges = new AxisRanges();

		private int _lastIntersectIndex;

		private PlotAxisID _contouredFillAxis;

		private PlotAxisID _xFillMap = PlotAxisID.X;

		private PlotAxisID _yFillMap = PlotAxisID.Z;

		private object _minFillX;

		private object _minFillY;

		private object _maxFillX;

		private object _maxFillY;

		private PlotCanvasBase _visual = new DataSeriesVisual();

		private Dictionary<object, double> _stackOffsets = new Dictionary<object, double>();

		private ModelVisual3D _visual3D;

		private ModelVisual3D _labels3D;

		private AxisAngleRotation3D _normalRotation;

		private R3DCuboid _volume;

		private Dictionary<Visual3D, IDataPoint[]> _visual3DDataPoints = new Dictionary<Visual3D, IDataPoint[]>();

		private IGraph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as IGraph;
			}
		}

		internal bool VisualElementsChanged
		{
			get

				}
			}
		}

				throw new InvalidOperationException((string)PlotSupport.Resources["dataSourceTypeError"]);
			}
		}

		internal IDataPoint LastDataPoint
		{
			get
			{
				IDataPoint extensionPoint = ExtensionPoint;
				IDataPoint dataPoint = ((DataSource.Count > 0) ? DataSource[DataSource.Count - 1] : null);
				if (extensionPoint == null || extensionPoint.IsEmptyDataPoint)
				{
					return dataPoint;
				}
				return new RDataPoint
				{
					X = ((extensionPoint.X == null && dataPoint != null) ? dataPoint.X : extensionPoint.X),
					Y = ((extensionPoint.Y == null && dataPoint != null) ? dataPoint.Y : extensionPoint.Y),
					Z = ((extensionPoint.Z == null && dataPoint != null) ? dataPoint.Z : extensionPoint.Z),
					Label = ((string.IsNullOrEmpty(extensionPoint.Label) && dataPoint != null) ? dataPoint.Label : extensionPoint.Label),
					NewSegment = extensionPoint.NewSegment,
					StyleKey = ((extensionPoint.StyleKey == null && dataPoint != null) ? dataPoint.StyleKey : extensionPoint.StyleKey),
					HideSymbol = extensionPoint.HideSymbol
				};
			}
		}

		private bool UseContouredFill
		{
			get
			{
				if (_contouredFillAxis == _yFillMap)
				{
					return _contouredFillAxis != PlotAxisID.None;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private Brush ContouredFill
		{
			get
			{
				if (!UseContouredFill)
				{
					return FillBrush;
				}
				LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush = new LinearGradientBrush();
				GradientStopCollection gradientStopCollection;
				if (FillBrush is GradientBrush gradientBrush)
				{
					gradientStopCollection = new GradientStopCollection();
					foreach (GradientStop gradientStop in gradientBrush.GradientStops)
					{
						gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
						{
							Color = gradientStop.Color,
							Offset = gradientStop.Offset
						});
					}
					linearGradientBrush.GradientStops = gradientStopCollection;
					if (gradientBrush is LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush2)
					{
						linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = linearGradientBrush2.StartPoint;
						linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = linearGradientBrush2.EndPoint;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					Color color = Colors.Transparent;
					Color color2 = Colors.Transparent;
					if (FillBrush is SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush)
					{
						if (!ContoursVisible)
						{
							return solidColorBrush;
						}
						color = (color2 = solidColorBrush.Color);
					}
					linearGradientBrush.GradientStops = new GradientStopCollection();
					linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
					{
						Color = color,
						Offset = 0.0
					});
					linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
					{
						Color = color2,
						Offset = 1.0
					});
					linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.5, 0.0);
					linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.5, 1.0);
				}
				gradientStopCollection = new GradientStopCollection();
				IGraphAxis axis = Graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yFillMap);
				IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axis.AxisAnnotator;
				bool visualElementVisibility = axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines);
				bool visualElementVisibility2 = axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines);
				IValueRange valueRange = _axisRanges[axis.Id];
				bool valid;
				double num = ((_minFillY != null) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, _minFillY, out valid) : valueRange.Minimum.Value);
				double num2 = ((_maxFillY != null) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, _maxFillY, out valid) : valueRange.Maximum.Value);
				double value = valueRange.Minimum.Value;
				double value2 = valueRange.Maximum.Value;
				double num3 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, value, out valid);
				double num4 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, value2, out valid);
				double num5 = (ContoursVisible ? (1.0 / 800.0) : 0.0);
				gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
				{
					Color = PlotSupport.GetColorAtPoint(linearGradientBrush, new Point(0.5, 0.0)),
					Offset = 0.0
				});
				for (int num6 = axisAnnotator.Annotations.Count - 1; num6 >= 0; num6--)
				{
					RAxisAnnotation rAxisAnnotation = axisAnnotator.Annotations[num6];
					double num7 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, axisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, rAxisAnnotation.LinearPos, out valid), out valid);
					if (!(num7 <= value) && !(num7 >= value2))
					{
						num7 = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, 1.0 - (num7 - num) / (num2 - num)));
						double num8 = 1.0 - (rAxisAnnotation.LinearPos - num3) / (num4 - num3);
						if (!double.IsNaN(num7) && !double.IsInfinity(num7))
						{
							if (rAxisAnnotation.TickType == TickType.Minor)
							{
								gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
								{
									Color = PlotSupport.GetColorAtPoint(linearGradientBrush, new Point(0.5, num7 - num5)),
									Offset = num8 - num5
								});
								if (ContoursVisible && visualElementVisibility)
								{
									gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
									{
										Color = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml("#FF606060"),
										Offset = num8 - num5
									});
									gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
									{
										Color = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml("#FF606060"),
										Offset = num8 + num5
									});
									gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
									{
										Color = PlotSupport.GetColorAtPoint(linearGradientBrush, new Point(0.5, num7 + num5)),
										Offset = num8 + num5
									});
								}
							}
							else if (rAxisAnnotation.TickType == TickType.Major)
							{
								gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
								{
									Color = PlotSupport.GetColorAtPoint(linearGradientBrush, new Point(0.5, num7 - num5)),
									Offset = num8 - num5
								});
								if (ContoursVisible && visualElementVisibility2)
								{
									gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
									{
										Color = Colors.Black,
										Offset = num8 - num5
									});
									gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
									{
										Color = Colors.Black,
										Offset = num8 + num5
									});
									gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
									{
										Color = PlotSupport.GetColorAtPoint(linearGradientBrush, new Point(0.5, num7 + num5)),
										Offset = num8 + num5
									});
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
				{
					Color = PlotSupport.GetColorAtPoint(linearGradientBrush, new Point(0.5, 1.0)),
					Offset = 1.0
				});
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops = gradientStopCollection;
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.5, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.5, 1.0);
				return linearGradientBrush;
			}
		}

		private DataSeries()

		public DataSeries(IGraph graph)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.Label, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.Legend, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.Symbol, value: true);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelStart, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelEnd, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoLine, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoTicks, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentXValueEnd, value: false);
			_visualElementsVisibility.Add(PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentYValueEnd, value: false);
			_styles = new Dictionary<object, IDataSeriesStyle>();
			_defaultStyle = new DataSeriesStyle(this);
			(_visual as DataSeriesVisual).DataSeries = this;
		}

		private void SetDefaultVisibilities(PlotDataSourceType dataSourceType)
		{
			_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Label] = false;
			_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line] = true;
			_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Symbol] = true;
			_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelStart] = false;
			_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelEnd] = false;
			_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill] = dataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar || dataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Histogram || dataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Surface || dataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.SurfaceGrid;
			Dictionary<PlotSeriesVisualElements, bool> visualElementsVisibility = _visualElementsVisibility;
			bool value = (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoTicks] = dataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.HiLo || dataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.HiLoAvg);
			visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoLine] = value;
		}

			this.ValueToTextFormat?.Invoke(this, e);
		}

			this.PointDrag?.Invoke(this, e);

		private bool SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotSeriesVisualElements elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements elementToSet, bool visibility)
		{
			if ((elements & elementToSet) == elementToSet && _visualElementsVisibility[elementToSet] != visibility)
			{
				_visualElementsVisibility[elementToSet] = visibility;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotSeriesVisualElements elements, bool visibility)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Label, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Legend, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Symbol, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelStart, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelEnd, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoLine, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoTicks, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentXValueEnd, visibility) || flag;
			flag = SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentYValueEnd, visibility) || flag;
			VisualElementsChanged |= flag;
		}

		private bool GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotSeriesVisualElements elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements elementToGet)
		{
			if ((elements & elementToGet) == elementToGet)
			{
				return _visualElementsVisibility[elementToGet];
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotSeriesVisualElements elements)
		{
			if (!GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Label) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Legend) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Symbol) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelStart) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelEnd) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoLine) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoTicks) && !GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentXValueEnd))
			{
				return GetVisualElementVisibility(elements, PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentYValueEnd);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void SetAxisMapping(PlotAxisID xMapId, PlotAxisID yMapId, PlotAxisID zMapId)
		{
			if (_xMapId != xMapId || _yMapId != yMapId || _zMapId != zMapId)
			{
				_xMapId = xMapId;
				_yMapId = yMapId;
				_zMapId = zMapId;
				VisualElementsChanged = true;
			}
		}

		public void GetAxisMapping(out PlotAxisID xMapId, out PlotAxisID yMapId, out PlotAxisID zMapId)
		{
			xMapId = _xMapId;
			yMapId = _yMapId;
			zMapId = _zMapId;
		}

		public IDataSeriesStyle NewStyle(object key)
		{
			if (_styles.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				return _styles[key];
			}
			DataSeriesStyle dataSeriesStyle = _defaultStyle.Clone();
			_styles.Add(key, dataSeriesStyle);
			return dataSeriesStyle;
		}

		public IDataSeriesStyle GetStyle(object key)
		{
			if (key != null && _styles.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				return _styles[key];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void RemoveStyle(object key)
		{
			_styles.Remove(key);
		}

		public void ClearStyles()
		{
			_styles.Clear();
		}

		public void StretchAxes()
		{
			if (!_visible)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId);
			IGraphAxis axis2 = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId);
			IGraphAxis axis3 = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_zMapId);
			_axisRanges.Clear();
			foreach (RDataPoint item in DataSource)
			{
				if (axis != null)
				{
					StretchAxis(axis, item.X);
				}
				if (axis2 != null)
				{
					StretchAxis(axis2, item.Y);
				}
				if (axis3 != null)
				{
					StretchAxis(axis3, item.Z);
				}
			}
		}

		private void StretchAxis(IGraphAxis axis, object value)
		{
			axis.Stretch(value, this);
			bool valid;
			double value2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
			if (valid && axis.AxisAnnotator.ValueIsValid(axis, value))
			{
				IValueRange valueRange = (_axisRanges.ContainsKey(axis.Id) ? _axisRanges[axis.Id] : (_axisRanges[axis.Id] = new ValueRange()));
				valueRange.Stretch(value2);
			}
		}

		public object GetIntersect(object value, bool getNextIntersect, PlotAxisID valueAxisId, PlotAxisID intersectAxisId)
		{
			return GetIntersect(value, getNextIntersect, valueAxisId, intersectAxisId, ignoreOffsets: false, ignoreSegmentBreaks: false);
		}

		public object GetIntersect(object value, bool getNextIntersect, PlotAxisID valueAxisId, PlotAxisID intersectAxisId, bool ignoreOffsets)
		{
			return GetIntersect(value, getNextIntersect, valueAxisId, intersectAxisId, ignoreOffsets, ignoreSegmentBreaks: false);
		}

		public object GetIntersect(object value, bool getNextIntersect, PlotAxisID valueAxisId, PlotAxisID intersectAxisId, bool ignoreOffsets, bool ignoreSegmentBreaks)
		{
			if (!getNextIntersect)
			{
				_lastIntersectIndex = DataSource.DataSize - 1;
			}
			int num;
			switch (DataSourceType)
			{
			case PlotDataSourceType.Bar:
				if (!getNextIntersect)
				{
					_lastIntersectIndex = -1;
				}
				num = _lastIntersectIndex + DataSource.DataSize;
				break;
			case PlotDataSourceType.Bubble:
				num = _lastIntersectIndex + DataSource.DataSize - 1;
				break;
			default:
				num = _lastIntersectIndex + DataSource.DataSize;
				break;
			}
			IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(valueAxisId);
			IGraphAxis axis2 = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(intersectAxisId);
			bool valid;
			double num2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return null;
			}
			while (num <= DataSource.Count)
			{
				int num3 = num - DataSource.DataSize;
				if (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar)
				{
					num3++;
				}
				IDataPoint dataPoint;
				if (num == DataSource.Count)
				{
					if (ExtensionPoint.IsEmptyDataPoint)
					{
						break;
					}
					dataPoint = LastDataPoint;
				}
				else
				{
					dataPoint = DataSource[num];
				}
				if (dataPoint == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				IDataPoint dataPoint2 = DataSource[num3];
				object value2;
				object value3;
				if (valueAxisId == _xMapId)
				{
					value2 = dataPoint.X;
					value3 = dataPoint2.X;
				}
				else if (valueAxisId == _yMapId)
				{
					value2 = dataPoint.Y;
					value3 = dataPoint2.Y;
				}
				else
				{
					value2 = dataPoint.Z;
					value3 = dataPoint2.Z;
				}
				object key;
				object key2;
				if (intersectAxisId == _xMapId)
				{
					key = dataPoint.X;
					key2 = dataPoint2.X;
				}
				else if (intersectAxisId == _yMapId)
				{
					key = dataPoint.Y;
					key2 = dataPoint2.Y;
				}
				else
				{
					key = dataPoint.Z;
					key2 = dataPoint2.Z;
				}
				double num4 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value2, out valid);
				double num5 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value3, out valid);
				if (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar)
				{
					if (_stackOffsets.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						num4 += _stackOffsets[key];
					}
					if (_stackOffsets.ContainsKey(key2))
					{
						num5 += _stackOffsets[key2];
					}
				}
				if ((num4 >= num2 && (num5 < num2 || (!getNextIntersect && num5 == num2))) || (num4 <= num2 && (num5 > num2 || (!getNextIntersect && num5 == num2))))
				{
					_lastIntersectIndex = num;
					if (!ignoreSegmentBreaks && dataPoint.NewSegment)
					{
						return null;
					}
					object value4;
					object value5;
					if (intersectAxisId == _xMapId)
					{
						value4 = dataPoint.X;
						value5 = dataPoint2.X;
					}
					else if (intersectAxisId == _yMapId)
					{
						value4 = dataPoint.Y;
						value5 = dataPoint2.Y;
					}
					else
					{
						value4 = dataPoint.Z;
						value5 = dataPoint2.Z;
					}
					double num6 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
					double num7 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis2, value4, out valid);
					double num8 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis2, value5, out valid);
					double num9;
					if (DataSourceType != PlotDataSourceType.Bar)
					{
						num9 = ((DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.SteppedLine) ? num8 : ((num4 != num5) ? ((num6 - num5) * (num7 - num8) / (num4 - num5) + num8) : ((num7 + num8) / 2.0)));
					}
					else
					{
						num9 = ((!(Math.Abs(num7) < Math.Abs(num8))) ? num7 : num8);
						if (!ignoreOffsets && _stackOffsets.ContainsKey(value))
						{
							num9 += _stackOffsets[value];
						}
					}
					return PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axis2, num9, out valid);
				}
				num += DataSource.DataSize;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void FillMapping(PlotAxisID xMap, PlotAxisID yMap, object minX, object maxX, object minY, object maxY)
		{
			if (DataSourceType != PlotDataSourceType.Surface && DataSourceType != PlotDataSourceType.SurfaceGrid)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot set fill mapping on a DataSource that is not a surface.");
			}
			if (_xFillMap != xMap || _yFillMap != yMap || _minFillX != minX || _maxFillX != maxX || _minFillY != minY || _maxFillY != maxY)
			{
				_xFillMap = xMap;
				_yFillMap = yMap;
				_minFillX = minX;
				_maxFillX = maxX;
				_minFillY = minY;
				_maxFillY = maxY;
				VisualElementsChanged = true;
			}
		}

		internal void GetFillMapping(out PlotAxisID xMap, out PlotAxisID yMap, out object minX, out object maxX, out object minY, out object maxY)
		{
			xMap = _xFillMap;
			yMap = _yFillMap;
			minX = _minFillX;
			maxX = _maxFillX;
			minY = _minFillY;
			maxY = _maxFillY;
		}

		public void RenderInitialise()
		{
			if (_graph.DataSeriesCollection.IndexOf(this) == 0)
			{
				_graph.StackedBarOffsets.Clear();
				_graph.StackedBarOffsets3D.Clear();
			}
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (!refreshAll && !_visualElementsChanged && !DataSource.Updated)
			{
				return;
			}
			_stackOffsets = new Dictionary<object, double>();
			if (((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.XMask) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.YMask) == 0) && ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.YMask) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.XMask) == 0))
			{
				return;
			}
			VisualElementsChanged = false;
			_dataSource.Updated = false;
			double extentX = 0.0;
			double extentY = 0.0;
			switch (_xMapId)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
			case PlotAxisID.X2:
				extentX = renderArea.Width;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
			case PlotAxisID.Y2:
				extentX = renderArea.Height;
				break;
			}
			switch (_yMapId)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
			case PlotAxisID.X2:
				extentY = renderArea.Width;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
			case PlotAxisID.Y2:
				extentY = renderArea.Height;
				break;
			}
			_visual.Children.Clear();
			_visual.RenderArea = renderArea;
			if (_visible)
			{
				double x = (renderArea.Y = 0.0);
				renderArea.X = x;
				switch (_dataSource.DataSourceType)
				{
				case PlotDataSourceType.Line:
				case PlotDataSourceType.SteppedLine:
					renderArea = DrawLineSeries(renderArea, extentX, extentY, _visual);
					break;
				case PlotDataSourceType.Bar:
				case PlotDataSourceType.Histogram:
					renderArea = DrawBarSeries(renderArea, extentX, extentY, _visual);
					break;
				case PlotDataSourceType.Bubble:
					renderArea = DrawBubbleSeries(renderArea, extentX, extentY, _visual);
					break;
				case PlotDataSourceType.HiLo:
				case PlotDataSourceType.HiLoAvg:
					renderArea = DrawHiLoSeries(renderArea, extentX, extentY, _visual);
					break;
				}
			}
			_visual.SetChildClips();
		}

		private Rect DrawBarSeries(Rect renderArea, double extentX, double extentY, PlotCanvasBase canvas)
		{
			if (!_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill] && !_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line])
			{
				return renderArea;
			}
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null)
			{
				return renderArea;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double num = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num2 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double num3 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num4 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			IDataSeriesStyle defaultStyle = _defaultStyle;
			for (int i = 0; i < _dataSource.Count; i += 2)
			{
				defaultStyle = GetStyle(_dataSource[i].StyleKey) ?? _defaultStyle;
				PlotPen linePen = defaultStyle.LinePen;
				linePen.IsFillVisible = _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill];
				linePen.IsStrokeVisible = _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line] && defaultStyle.LineStyle != LineStyle.None;
				double num5 = 0.0;
				double num6 = 0.0;
				double num7 = 0.0;
				double num8 = 0.0;
				IDataPoint dataPoint = _dataSource[i];
				IDataPoint dataPoint2 = _dataSource[i + 1];
				Point dataPointLinearPostion;
				Point dataPointLinearPostion2;
				if (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar && _graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.Stack)
				{
					double num9 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint.X, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num10 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint.Y, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num11 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint2.X, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num12 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint2.Y, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Dictionary<object, double> stackedBarOffsets = _graph.StackedBarOffsets;
					if (axisForSeries.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						object x = dataPoint.X;
						if (stackedBarOffsets.ContainsKey(x))
						{
							num10 += stackedBarOffsets[x];
							num12 += stackedBarOffsets[x];
							_stackOffsets[x] = stackedBarOffsets[x];
						}
						stackedBarOffsets[x] = num12;
					}
					else if (axisForSeries2.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						object y = dataPoint.Y;
						if (stackedBarOffsets.ContainsKey(y))
						{
							num9 += stackedBarOffsets[y];
							num11 += stackedBarOffsets[y];
							_stackOffsets[y] = stackedBarOffsets[y];
						}
						stackedBarOffsets[y] = num11;
					}
					dataPointLinearPostion = GetDataPointLinearPostion(num9, num10, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					dataPointLinearPostion2 = GetDataPointLinearPostion(num11, num12, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					dataPointLinearPostion = GetDataPointLinearPostion(dataPoint, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					dataPointLinearPostion2 = GetDataPointLinearPostion(dataPoint2, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
				}
				if (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar)
				{
					int num13 = 0;
					int num14 = 0;
					foreach (IDataSeries item in _graph.DataSeriesCollection)
					{
						if (item.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar && item.Visible)
						{
							if (item == this)
							{
								num14 = num13;
							}
							num13++;
						}
					}
					if (axisForSeries.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.SideBySide)
						{
							double num15 = 0.9;
							dataPointLinearPostion.X += (0.0 - num15) / 2.0 + num15 * (double)num14 / (double)num13;
							dataPointLinearPostion2.X = dataPointLinearPostion.X + num15 / (double)num13;
						}
						else if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.StaggeredOverlay)
						{
							double num16 = Math.Min(0.1, 0.45 / (double)num13);
							double num17 = 0.9 - num16 * (double)(num13 - 1);
							dataPointLinearPostion.X += -0.45 + num16 * (double)(num13 - 1 - num14);
							dataPointLinearPostion2.X = dataPointLinearPostion.X + num17;
						}
						else
						{
							dataPointLinearPostion.X -= 0.45;
							dataPointLinearPostion2.X += 0.45;
						}
					}
					else if (axisForSeries2.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.SideBySide)
						{
							double num18 = 0.9;
							dataPointLinearPostion.Y += (0.0 - num18) / 2.0 + num18 * (double)num14 / (double)num13;
							dataPointLinearPostion2.Y = dataPointLinearPostion.Y + num18 / (double)num13;
						}
						else if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.StaggeredOverlay)
						{
							double num19 = Math.Min(0.1, 0.45 / (double)num13);
							double num20 = 0.9 - num19 * (double)(num13 - 1);
							dataPointLinearPostion.Y += -0.45 + num19 * (double)num14;
							dataPointLinearPostion2.Y = dataPointLinearPostion.Y + num20;
						}
						else
						{
							dataPointLinearPostion.Y -= 0.45;
							dataPointLinearPostion2.Y += 0.45;
						}
					}
				}
				num5 = extentX * (dataPointLinearPostion.X - num) / (num2 - num);
				num6 = extentY * (dataPointLinearPostion.Y - num3) / (num4 - num3);
				num7 = extentX * (dataPointLinearPostion2.X - num) / (num2 - num);
				num8 = extentY * (dataPointLinearPostion2.Y - num3) / (num4 - num3);
				bool flag = false;
				if ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.YMask) != PlotAxisID.None && (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.XMask) != PlotAxisID.None)
				{
					double num21 = num5;
					num5 = num6;
					num6 = num21;
					double num22 = num7;
					num7 = num8;
					num8 = num22;
					flag = true;
				}
				dataPointLinearPostion = new Point(num5 + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Bottom - num6);
				dataPointLinearPostion2 = new Point(num7 + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Bottom - num8);
				canvas.AddPath(linePen, PlotSupport.CreateRectangleGeometry(new Rect(dataPointLinearPostion, dataPointLinearPostion2)));
				if (IsUserInteractive)
				{
					if ((flag && dataPoint2.X is string) || dataPoint2.Y is string)
					{
						DrawInteractionSymbol(dataPoint2, new Point(dataPointLinearPostion2.X, dataPointLinearPostion.Y), canvas, defaultStyle);
					}
					else
					{
						DrawInteractionSymbol(dataPoint2, new Point(dataPointLinearPostion.X, dataPointLinearPostion2.Y), canvas, defaultStyle);
					}
					DrawInteractionSymbol(dataPoint2, dataPointLinearPostion2, canvas, defaultStyle);
				}
			}
			return renderArea;
		}

		private static Point GetDataPointLinearPostion(IDataPoint dataPoint, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, out bool valid)
		{
			double x = axisX.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisX, dataPoint.X, out valid);
			double y = axisY.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisY, dataPoint.Y, out valid);
			return new Point(x, y);
		}

		private static Point GetDataPointLinearPostion(double xValue, double yValue, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, out bool valid)
		{
			double x = axisX.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisX, xValue, out valid);
			double y = axisY.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisY, yValue, out valid);
			return new Point(x, y);
		}

		private Rect DrawHiLoSeries(Rect renderArea, double extentX, double extentY, PlotCanvasBase canvas)
		{
			IDataSeriesStyle defaultStyle = _defaultStyle;
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null)
			{
				return renderArea;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double num = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num2 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double num3 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num4 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			Point point = new Point(double.NaN, double.NaN);
			Point point2;
			Point value = (point2 = point);
			for (int i = 0; i < _dataSource.Count; i += _dataSource.DataSize)
			{
				IDataPoint dataPoint = _dataSource[i];
				IDataPoint dataPoint2 = _dataSource[i + 1];
				defaultStyle = GetStyle(dataPoint.StyleKey) ?? _defaultStyle;
				double linearX;
				double linearY;
				Point point3 = DataPointToScreen(renderArea, dataPoint, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, extentX, extentY, num, num2, num3, num4, out linearX, out linearY, out valid);
				if (!valid)
				{
					continue;
				}
				point2 = DataPointToScreen(renderArea, dataPoint2, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, extentX, extentY, num, num2, num3, num4, out var linearX2, out var linearY2, out valid);
				if (!valid || ((point3.X - value.X) * (point3.X - value.X) + (point3.Y - value.Y) * (point3.Y - value.Y) < 0.25 && (point2.X - point.X) * (point2.X - point.X) + (point2.Y - point.Y) * (point2.Y - point.Y) < 0.25))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (!dataPoint.NewSegment && !dataPoint2.NewSegment && defaultStyle.FillBrush != null && _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill] && ((Math.Max(point3.X, point2.X) >= renderArea.Left && Math.Min(value.X, point.X) <= renderArea.Right) || (Math.Min(point3.X, point2.X) <= renderArea.Right && Math.Max(value.X, point.X) >= renderArea.Left)) && ((Math.Max(point3.Y, point2.Y) >= renderArea.Top && Math.Min(value.Y, point.Y) <= renderArea.Bottom) || (Math.Min(point3.Y, point2.Y) <= renderArea.Bottom && Math.Max(value.Y, point.Y) >= renderArea.Top)))
				{
					PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
					pointCollection.Add(point);
					pointCollection.Add(point2);
					pointCollection.Add(point3);
					pointCollection.Add(value);
					canvas.AddPolygon(null, pointCollection, FillRule.Nonzero);
				}
				if (!dataPoint.HideSymbol)
				{
					double num5 = point3.X - point2.X;
					double num6 = point3.Y - point2.Y;
					Point orientationVector;
					if (num5 == 0.0)
					{
						orientationVector = new Point(5.0, 0.0);
					}
					else if (num6 == 0.0)
					{
						orientationVector = new Point(0.0, 5.0);
					}
					else
					{
						double num7 = 5.0 / Math.Sqrt(1.0 + num5 * num5 / (num6 * num6));
						orientationVector = new Point(num7, Math.Sqrt(25.0 - num7 * num7));
					}
					if (linearX >= num && linearX <= num2 && linearY >= num3 && linearY <= num4)
					{
						DrawTick(dataPoint, point3, canvas, orientationVector, defaultStyle);
					}
					if (linearX2 >= num && linearX2 <= num2 && linearY2 >= num3 && linearY2 <= num4)
					{
						DrawTick(dataPoint2, point2, canvas, orientationVector, defaultStyle);
					}
					if (!dataPoint.NewSegment && !dataPoint2.NewSegment && defaultStyle.LineStyle != LineStyle.None && _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoLine] && ((point3.X >= renderArea.Left && point2.X <= renderArea.Right) || (point3.X <= renderArea.Right && point2.X >= renderArea.Left)) && ((point3.Y >= renderArea.Top && point2.Y <= renderArea.Bottom) || (point3.Y <= renderArea.Bottom && point2.Y >= renderArea.Top)))
					{
						canvas.AddLine(defaultStyle.LinePen, point3, point2);
					}
				}
				value = point3;
				point = point2;
			}
			if (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.HiLoAvg)
			{
				return DrawLineSeries(renderArea, extentX, extentY, canvas);
			}
			return renderArea;
		}

		private Point DataPointToScreen(Rect renderArea, IDataPoint dataPoint, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, double extentX, double extentY, double xMinVisible, double xMaxVisible, double yMinVisible, double yMaxVisible, out double linearX, out double linearY, out bool valid)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			linearY = double.NaN;
			linearX = axisX.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisX, dataPoint.X, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point);
			}
			linearY = axisY.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisY, dataPoint.Y, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point);
			}
			num = extentX * (linearX - xMinVisible) / (xMaxVisible - xMinVisible);
			num2 = extentY * (linearY - yMinVisible) / (yMaxVisible - yMinVisible);
			if ((_xMapId == PlotAxisID.Y || _xMapId == PlotAxisID.Y2) && (_yMapId == PlotAxisID.X || _yMapId == PlotAxisID.X2))
			{
				double num3 = num;
				num = num2;
				num2 = num3;
			}
			return new Point(num + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Bottom - num2);
		}

		private bool DrawTick(IDataPoint dataPoint, Point screenPoint, PlotCanvasBase canvas, Point orientationVector, IDataSeriesStyle style)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoTicks])
			{
				canvas.AddLine(start: new Point(screenPoint.X - orientationVector.X, screenPoint.Y - orientationVector.Y), finish: new Point(screenPoint.X + orientationVector.X, screenPoint.Y + orientationVector.Y), pen: style.SymbolPen);
				flag = true;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return DrawPointLabel(dataPoint, screenPoint, canvas);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool DrawPoint(IDataPoint dataPoint, Point screenPoint, PlotCanvasBase canvas, IDataSeriesStyle style)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (!dataPoint.HideSymbol && _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Symbol])
			{
				PlotSupport.Draw2DSymbol(canvas, screenPoint, style.SymbolStyle, style.SymbolPen, style.SymbolScale, IsUserInteractive ? dataPoint : null);
				flag = true;
			}
			else if (IsUserInteractive)
			{
				DrawInteractionSymbol(dataPoint, screenPoint, canvas, style);
			}
			return DrawPointLabel(dataPoint, screenPoint, canvas) || flag;
		}

		private static void DrawInteractionSymbol(IDataPoint dataPoint, Point screenPoint, PlotCanvasBase canvas, IDataSeriesStyle style)
		{
			Panel.SetZIndex(PlotSupport.Draw2DSymbol(canvas, screenPoint, SymbolStyle.Circle, new PlotPen(Colors.Transparent, 0.0, LineStyle.None, WpfSilverlightSupport.Brushes.Transparent), style.SymbolScale, dataPoint), 1000);
		}

		private bool DrawPointLabel(IDataPoint dataPoint, Point screenPoint, PlotCanvasBase canvas)
		{
			if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Label] && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataPoint.Label))
			{
				canvas.AddText(dataPoint.Label, LabelFontFamily, 8.5, Colors.Black, PlotSupport.TransparentBrush, screenPoint, LabelFontStyle, LabelFontWeight, LabelTextDecorations);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private Rect DrawLineSeries(Rect renderArea, double extentX, double extentY, PlotCanvasBase canvas)
		{
			IDataSeriesStyle defaultStyle = _defaultStyle;
			IDataSeriesStyle dataSeriesStyle = _defaultStyle;
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			if (Graph.AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.RectXMergedY)
			{
				axisForSeries2 = MergedGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			}
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null)
			{
				return renderArea;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double num = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num2 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double num3 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);

				Point point = new Point(double.NaN, double.NaN);

				for (int j = _dataSource.DataSize - 1; j < _dataSource.Count + 1; j += _dataSource.DataSize)

					}

								goto IL_03a2;

							{
								if (point2.Y >= renderArea.Top && point.Y <= renderArea.Bottom)
								{
									num8 = 1;
									goto IL_03a2;
								}

								}

							else
							{
								num8 = 0;
							}
						}

						}

					}
					goto IL_080f;
					IL_03a2:

					goto IL_03ac;
					IL_03ac:

					{

								canvas.AddPath(dataSeriesStyle.LinePen, pathGeometry);
							}
							pathFigure = null;
							pathGeometry = null;
						}
					}
					if (num8 != 0)

						if (pathFigure == null)
						{
							point.X = Math.Min(1000000.0, Math.Max(point.X, -1000000.0));
							point.Y = Math.Min(1000000.0, Math.Max(point.Y, -1000000.0));
							pathFigure = new PathFigure();
							pathFigure.StartPoint = point;
							if (flag4)
							{
								pathFigure2 = new PathFigure();

								pathFigure2.IsFilled = true;
								LineSegment lineSegment = new LineSegment();
								lineSegment.Point = point;
								lineSegment.IsSmoothJoin = true;
								pathFigure2.Segments.Add(lineSegment);
							}
						}
						if (pathGeometry == null)
						{
							pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
						}
						if (flag4 && pathGeometry2 == null)
						{
							pathGeometry2 = new PathGeometry();
						}
						point2.X = Math.Min(1000000.0, Math.Max(point2.X, -1000000.0));

						lineSegment2.Point = point2;
						lineSegment2.IsSmoothJoin = true;

				}
				if (pathGeometry != null)

					}
					pathGeometry.Freeze();
					canvas.AddPath(dataSeriesStyle.LinePen, pathGeometry);
					System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = canvas.AddPath(dataSeriesStyle.LinePen, pathGeometry);
					path.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new Binding("StrokeThickness")
					{
						Converter = new AddValueConverter

							Addition = 2.0
						}
					});

					PlotSelection.SetElementType(path, PlotSelectedElementType.DataSeries);
					path.Fill = Brushes.Transparent;
					path.Stroke = Brushes.Transparent;
					Panel.SetZIndex(path, 9999);

			}
