		}

		private void DrawLastDataPointLabel(Rect renderArea, PlotCanvasBase canvas, Point p1, Point p2, IDataPoint lastPoint)
		{
			if (!_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentXValueEnd] && !_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentYValueEnd])
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(Graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			string xValueText = string.Format("{0:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}", lastPoint.X);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(Graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			string yValueText = string.Format("{0:" + axisForSeries2.DataLabelFormatString + "}", lastPoint.Y);
			ValueTextFormatEventArgs e = new ValueTextFormatEventArgs(lastPoint.X, lastPoint.Y, null, xValueText, yValueText, null);
			PerformValueToTextFormat(e);
			if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentXValueEnd])
			{
				if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.CurrentYValueEnd])
				{
					DrawLineLabel(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "({0}, {1})", e.XValueText, e.YValueText), renderArea, p2, p1, canvas);
				}
				else
				{
					DrawLineLabel(e.XValueText, renderArea, p2, p1, canvas);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DrawLineLabel(e.YValueText, renderArea, p2, p1, canvas);
			}
		}

		private void DrawLineLabel(string label, Rect renderArea, Point p1, Point p2, PlotCanvasBase canvas)
		{
			TextBlock textBlock = PlotCanvasBase.MakeTextBlock(label, LabelFontFamily, 8.5, LineColour, LabelFontStyle, LabelFontWeight, LabelTextDecorations);
			Size textSize = WpfSilverlightSupport.GetTextSize(textBlock);
			if (double.IsNaN(p1.X) || double.IsNaN(p1.Y))
			{
				p1 = p2;
			}
			double num = ((p2.X == p1.X) ? p2.X : ((p1.Y < renderArea.Top) ? (p1.X + (p2.X - p1.X) * (renderArea.Top - p1.Y) / (p2.Y - p1.Y)) : ((!(p1.Y > renderArea.Bottom)) ? p1.X : (p1.X + (p2.X - p1.X) * (renderArea.Bottom - p1.Y) / (p2.Y - p1.Y)))));
			if (double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				num = p1.X;
			}
			num = Math.Max(renderArea.Left, Math.Min(num, renderArea.Right - textSize.Width));
			double num2 = ((p2.Y == p1.Y) ? p2.Y : ((p1.X < renderArea.Left) ? (p1.Y + (p2.Y - p1.Y) * (renderArea.Left - p1.X) / (p2.X - p1.X)) : ((!(p1.X > renderArea.Right)) ? p1.Y : (p1.Y + (p2.Y - p1.Y) * (renderArea.Right - p1.X) / (p2.X - p1.X)))));
			if (p2.Y > p1.Y)
			{
				num2 -= textSize.Height;
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(num2))
			{
				num2 = p1.Y;
			}
			num2 = Math.Max(renderArea.Top, Math.Min(num2, renderArea.Bottom - textSize.Height));
			canvas.AddChild(textBlock, new Point(num, num2));
		}

		private Rect DrawBubbleSeries(Rect renderArea, double extentX, double extentY, PlotCanvasBase dc)
		{
			IDataSeriesStyle defaultStyle = _defaultStyle;
			if (!_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill] && !_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line])
			{
				return renderArea;
			}
			Point point = default(Point);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null)
			{
				return renderArea;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double num = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num2 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double num3 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double num4 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			for (int i = 0; i < _dataSource.Count; i += 2)
			{
				double num5 = 0.0;
				double num6 = 0.0;
				double num7 = 0.0;
				double num8 = 0.0;
				double num9 = 0.0;
				double num10 = 0.0;
				PathGeometry pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
				PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure();
				pathFigure.IsClosed = true;
				IDataPoint dataPoint = _dataSource[i];
				IDataPoint dataPoint2 = _dataSource[i + 1];
				if (dataPoint.HideSymbol)
				{
					continue;
				}
				defaultStyle = GetStyle(dataPoint.StyleKey) ?? _defaultStyle;
				if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill])
				{
					_ = defaultStyle.FillBrush;
				}
				PlotPen pen = ((_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line] && defaultStyle.LineStyle != LineStyle.None) ? defaultStyle.LinePen : null);
				Point dataPointLinearPostion = GetDataPointLinearPostion(dataPoint, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, out valid);
				num5 = extentX * (dataPointLinearPostion.X - num) / (num2 - num);
				num6 = extentY * (dataPointLinearPostion.Y - num3) / (num4 - num3);
				double num11 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint.X, out valid);
				double num12 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint.Y, out valid);
				double num13 = Math.Max(0.0, PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint2.X, out valid));
				if (!valid)
				{
					num13 = 0.0;
				}
				double num14 = Math.Max(0.0, PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint2.Y, out valid));
				if (!valid)
				{
					num14 = 0.0;
				}
				Point dataPointLinearPostion2 = GetDataPointLinearPostion(new RDataPoint
				{
					X = PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axisForSeries, num11 + num13, out valid),
					Y = PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axisForSeries2, num12 + num14, out valid)
				}, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, out valid);
				num7 = extentX * (dataPointLinearPostion2.X - num) / (num2 - num);
				num8 = extentY * (dataPointLinearPostion2.Y - num3) / (num4 - num3);
				if (!valid)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
				{
					int num15 = 0;
					int num16 = 0;
					switch (j)
					{
					case 0:
					case 4:
						num15 = 0;
						num16 = 1;
						break;
					case 1:
						num15 = 1;
						num16 = 0;
						break;
					case 2:
						num15 = 0;
						num16 = -1;
						break;
					case 3:
						num15 = -1;
						num16 = 0;
						break;
					}
					Point dataPointLinearPostion3 = GetDataPointLinearPostion(new RDataPoint
					{
						X = PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axisForSeries, num11 + (double)num15 * num13, out valid),
						Y = PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axisForSeries2, num12 + (double)num16 * num14, out valid)
					}, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, out valid);
					if (valid)
					{
						num9 = ((num13 != 0.0) ? (extentX * (dataPointLinearPostion3.X - num) / (num2 - num)) : (num5 + (double)num15 * Math.Abs(num8 - num6)));
						num10 = ((num14 != 0.0) ? (extentY * (dataPointLinearPostion3.Y - num3) / (num4 - num3)) : (num6 + (double)num15 * Math.Abs(num7 - num5)));
						if ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.YMask) != PlotAxisID.None && (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.XMask) != PlotAxisID.None)
						{
							double num17 = num9;
							num9 = num10;
							num10 = num17;
						}
						dataPointLinearPostion3 = new Point(num9 + renderArea.Left, renderArea.Bottom - num10);
						if (j == 0)
						{
							point = (pathFigure.StartPoint = dataPointLinearPostion3);
							continue;
						}
						pathFigure.Segments.Add(PlotSupport.CreateArcSegment(dataPointLinearPostion3, new Size(Math.Abs(point.X - dataPointLinearPostion3.X), Math.Abs(point.Y - dataPointLinearPostion3.Y)), 0.0, isLargeArc: false, SweepDirection.Clockwise));
						point = dataPointLinearPostion3;
					}
				}
				pathGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure);
				dc.AddPath(pen, pathGeometry);
			}
			return renderArea;
		}

		private static double GetAxisExtent(R3DCuboid volume, PlotAxisID axisId)
		{
			return axisId switch
			{
				PlotAxisID.X => volume.Width, 
				PlotAxisID.Y => volume.Height, 
				PlotAxisID.Z => volume.Depth, 
				_ => 0.0, 
			};
		}

		public Visual3D Draw3D(R3DCuboid volume, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (_visual3D == null)
			{
				_visual3D = new ModelVisual3D();
			}
			if (_labels3D == null)
			{
				_labels3D = new ModelVisual3D();
			}
			if (!refreshAll && !_visualElementsChanged && !DataSource.Updated)
			{
				return _visual3D;
			}
			if (((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.X) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.Y) == 0 || (_zMapId & PlotAxisID.Z) == 0) && ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.X) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.Z) == 0 || (_zMapId & PlotAxisID.Y) == 0) && ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.Y) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.X) == 0 || (_zMapId & PlotAxisID.Z) == 0) && ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.Y) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.Z) == 0 || (_zMapId & PlotAxisID.X) == 0) && ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.Z) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.Y) == 0 || (_zMapId & PlotAxisID.X) == 0) && ((_xMapId & PlotAxisID.Z) == 0 || (_yMapId & PlotAxisID.X) == 0 || (_zMapId & PlotAxisID.Y) == 0))
			{
				return _visual3D;
			}
			VisualElementsChanged = false;
			_dataSource.Updated = false;
			_volume = volume;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			num = GetAxisExtent(volume, _xMapId);
			num2 = GetAxisExtent(volume, _yMapId);
			num3 = GetAxisExtent(volume, _zMapId);
			_normalRotation = null;
			if (_graph.GraphLayout is IGraphLayout3D graphLayout3D)
			{
				_normalRotation = graphLayout3D.NormalRotation;
			}
			PlotSupport.Clear3DVisual(_visual3D);
			PlotSupport.Clear3DVisual(_labels3D);
			_visual3DDataPoints.Clear();
			if (_visible)
			{
				switch (_dataSource.DataSourceType)
				{
				case PlotDataSourceType.Line:
				case PlotDataSourceType.SteppedLine:
					Draw3DLineSeries(volume, num, num2, num3);
					break;
				case PlotDataSourceType.Bar:
				case PlotDataSourceType.Histogram:
					Draw3DBarSeries(volume, num, num2, num3);
					break;
				case PlotDataSourceType.Surface:
					Draw3DSurface(volume, num, num2, num3);
					break;
				case PlotDataSourceType.SurfaceGrid:
					Draw3DSurfaceGrid(volume, num, num2, num3);
					break;
				}
			}
			if (Graph.GraphLayout.Renderer is I3DGraphRender i3DGraphRender)
			{
				if (!i3DGraphRender.LabelVisuals.Children.Contains(_labels3D))
				{
					i3DGraphRender.LabelVisuals.Children.Add(_labels3D);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_visual3D.Children.Add(_labels3D);
			}
			return _visual3D;
		}

		private static Point3D GetDataPoint3DLinearPostion(IDataPoint dataPoint, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, IGraphAxis axisZ, out bool valid)
		{
			double num = double.NaN;
			double num2 = double.NaN;
			double x = axisX.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisX, dataPoint.X, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point3D);
			}
			num = axisY.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisY, dataPoint.Y, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point3D);
			}
			num2 = axisZ.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisZ, dataPoint.Z, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point3D);
			}
			return new Point3D(x, num, num2);
		}

		private static Point3D GetDataPoint3DLinearPostion(double xValue, double yValue, double zValue, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, IGraphAxis axisZ, out bool valid)
		{
			double num = double.NaN;
			double num2 = double.NaN;
			double x = axisX.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisX, xValue, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point3D);
			}
			num = axisY.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisY, yValue, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point3D);
			}
			num2 = axisZ.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisZ, zValue, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point3D);
			}
			return new Point3D(x, num, num2);
		}

		private static Point3D DataPointTo3D(R3DCuboid volume, IDataPoint dataPoint, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, IGraphAxis axisZ, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ, double xMinVisible, double xMaxVisible, double yMinVisible, double yMaxVisible, double zMinVisible, double zMaxVisible, out Point3D linearPosition, out bool valid)
		{
			linearPosition = GetDataPoint3DLinearPostion(dataPoint, axisX, axisY, axisZ, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return default(Point3D);
			}
			Point3D result = LinearPointTo3D(volume, axisX, axisY, axisZ, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, linearPosition, out valid);
			valid = valid && ValidDouble(result.X) && ValidDouble(result.Y) && ValidDouble(result.Z);
			return result;
		}

		internal bool Point3DToXYZ(Point3D point3D, out object x, out object y, out object z)
		{
			x = (y = (z = null));
			if (!ValidDouble(point3D.X) || !ValidDouble(point3D.Y) || !ValidDouble(point3D.Z))
			{
				return false;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			num = GetAxisExtent(_volume, _xMapId);
			num2 = GetAxisExtent(_volume, _yMapId);
			num3 = GetAxisExtent(_volume, _zMapId);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_zMapId);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null || axis == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double xMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double xMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator2 = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double yMinVisible = axisAnnotator2.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator2.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double yMaxVisible = axisAnnotator2.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator2.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axis.AxisAnnotator;
			double zMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double zMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			Point3D point3D2 = Point3DToLinearPoint(_volume, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, num, num2, num3, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, point3D);
			x = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(axisForSeries, point3D2.X, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return false;
			}
			y = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(axisForSeries2, point3D2.Y, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return false;
			}
			z = axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, point3D2.Z, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static bool ValidDouble(double x)
		{
			if (!double.IsInfinity(x) && !double.IsNaN(x) && !double.IsNegativeInfinity(x))
			{
				return !double.IsPositiveInfinity(x);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static Point3D LinearPointTo3D(R3DCuboid volume, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, IGraphAxis axisZ, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ, double xMinVisible, double xMaxVisible, double yMinVisible, double yMaxVisible, double zMinVisible, double zMaxVisible, Point3D linearPosition, out bool valid)
		{
			ClipPoints clipPoints;
			return LinearPointTo3D(volume, axisX, axisY, axisZ, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, linearPosition, out valid, clipToExtent: false, out clipPoints);
		}

		private static Point3D LinearPointTo3D(R3DCuboid volume, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, IGraphAxis axisZ, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ, double xMinVisible, double xMaxVisible, double yMinVisible, double yMaxVisible, double zMinVisible, double zMaxVisible, Point3D linearPosition, out bool valid, bool clipToExtent, out ClipPoints clipPoints)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			valid = false;
			clipPoints = ClipPoints.None;
			double num4 = linearPosition.X;
			double num5 = linearPosition.Y;
			double num6 = linearPosition.Z;
			if (clipToExtent)
			{
				num4 = Math.Min(xMaxVisible, Math.Max(xMinVisible, num4));
				clipPoints |= (ClipPoints)(((num4 > linearPosition.X) ? 1 : 0) | ((num4 < linearPosition.X) ? 2 : 0));
				num5 = Math.Min(yMaxVisible, Math.Max(yMinVisible, num5));
				clipPoints |= (ClipPoints)(((num5 > linearPosition.Y) ? 4 : 0) | ((num5 < linearPosition.Y) ? 8 : 0));
				num6 = Math.Min(zMaxVisible, Math.Max(zMinVisible, num6));
				clipPoints |= (ClipPoints)(((num6 > linearPosition.Z) ? 16 : 0) | ((num6 < linearPosition.Z) ? 32 : 0));
			}
			valid = num4 >= xMinVisible && num4 <= xMaxVisible && num5 >= yMinVisible && num5 <= yMaxVisible && num6 >= zMinVisible && num6 <= zMaxVisible;
			num4 = extentX * (num4 - xMinVisible) / (xMaxVisible - xMinVisible);
			num5 = extentY * (num5 - yMinVisible) / (yMaxVisible - yMinVisible);
			num6 = extentZ * (num6 - zMinVisible) / (zMaxVisible - zMinVisible);
			switch (axisX.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				num2 = num4;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				num3 = num4;
				break;
			default:
				num = num4;
				break;
			}
			switch (axisY.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				num = num5;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				num3 = num5;
				break;
			default:
				num2 = num5;
				break;
			}
			switch (axisZ.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				num = num6;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				num2 = num6;
				break;
			default:
				num3 = num6;
				break;
			}
			return new Point3D(volume.X + num, volume.Y + num2, 0.0 - volume.Z - num3);
		}

		private static Point3D Point3DToLinearPoint(R3DCuboid volume, IGraphAxis axisX, IGraphAxis axisY, IGraphAxis axisZ, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ, double xMinVisible, double xMaxVisible, double yMinVisible, double yMaxVisible, double zMinVisible, double zMaxVisible, Point3D point3D)
		{
			point3D.X -= volume.X;
			point3D.Y -= volume.Y;
			point3D.Z = 0.0 - volume.Z - point3D.Z;
			double x = 0.0;
			double y = 0.0;
			double z = 0.0;
			switch (axisX.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				y = point3D.X * (xMaxVisible - xMinVisible) / extentX + xMinVisible;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				z = point3D.X * (xMaxVisible - xMinVisible) / extentX + xMinVisible;
				break;
			default:
				x = point3D.X * (xMaxVisible - xMinVisible) / extentX + xMinVisible;
				break;
			}
			switch (axisY.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				x = point3D.Y * (yMaxVisible - yMinVisible) / extentY + yMinVisible;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				z = point3D.Y * (yMaxVisible - yMinVisible) / extentY + yMinVisible;
				break;
			default:
				y = point3D.Y * (yMaxVisible - yMinVisible) / extentY + yMinVisible;
				break;
			}
			switch (axisZ.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				x = point3D.Z * (zMaxVisible - zMinVisible) / extentZ + zMinVisible;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				y = point3D.Z * (zMaxVisible - zMinVisible) / extentZ + zMinVisible;
				break;
			default:
				z = point3D.Z * (zMaxVisible - zMinVisible) / extentZ + zMinVisible;
				break;
			}
			return new Point3D(x, y, z);
		}

		private void Draw3DLineSeries(R3DCuboid volume, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ)
		{
			IDataSeriesStyle defaultStyle = _defaultStyle;
			IDataSeriesStyle dataSeriesStyle = _defaultStyle;
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_zMapId);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null || axis == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double xMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double xMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator2 = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double yMinVisible = axisAnnotator2.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator2.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double yMaxVisible = axisAnnotator2.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator2.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axis.AxisAnnotator;
			double zMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double zMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = true;
			WireLines wireLines = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
			{
				Point3D point3D = new Point3D(double.NaN, double.NaN, double.NaN);
				Point3D point3D2 = point3D;
				double y = volume.Y;
				for (int j = _dataSource.DataSize - 1; j < _dataSource.Count; j += _dataSource.DataSize)
				{
					IDataPoint dataPoint = _dataSource[j];
					defaultStyle = GetStyle(dataPoint.StyleKey) ?? _defaultStyle;
					point3D2 = DataPointTo3D(volume, dataPoint, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, out var _, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						flag3 = false;
						continue;
					}
					if (defaultStyle == dataSeriesStyle && (point3D2.X - point3D.X) * (point3D2.X - point3D.X) + (point3D2.Y - point3D.Y) * (point3D2.Y - point3D.Y) + (point3D2.Z - point3D.Z) * (point3D2.Z - point3D.Z) < 0.25)
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num4;
					if (i == 0)
					{
						bool num = (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Line || DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.SteppedLine) && defaultStyle.FillBrush != null && defaultStyle.FillBrush != PlotSupport.TransparentBrush && GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill);
						y = volume.Y;
						if (num)
						{
							double num2 = Math.Min(axisAnnotator2.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, Math.Max(axisAnnotator2.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, 0.0));
							double num3 = axisAnnotator2.AxisFractionMapValue(axisForSeries2, num2, out valid);
							if (valid)
							{
								y -= num3 * extentY;
							}
						}
						if (flag3 && !dataPoint.NewSegment && !double.IsNaN(point3D.X) && defaultStyle.LineStyle != LineStyle.None)
						{
							num4 = (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line] ? 1 : 0);
							if (num4 != 0 && dataSeriesStyle == defaultStyle)
							{
								goto IL_033c;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							num4 = 0;
						}
						if (wireLines != null)
						{
							_visual3D.Children.Add(wireLines);
						}
						wireLines = null;
						goto IL_033c;
					}
					DrawPoint3D(dataPoint, point3D2, defaultStyle);
					if (!flag)
					{
						flag = true;
						if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelStart])
						{
							_labels3D.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(Label, LabelFontFamily, 8.5, LineColour, double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, point3D2, HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment.Center, PlotAxisID.X, back: false, _normalRotation, out var _, LabelFontStyle, LabelFontWeight, LabelTextDecorations));
						}
					}
					goto IL_040f;
					IL_033c:
					if (num4 != 0)
					{
						if (wireLines == null)
						{
							wireLines = new WireLines
							{
								Color = defaultStyle.LineColour,
								Thickness = defaultStyle.LineWidth,
								Lines = new Point3DCollection()
							};
						}
						wireLines.Lines.Add(point3D);
						wireLines.Lines.Add(point3D2);
					}
					goto IL_040f;
					IL_040f:
					flag3 = true;
					dataSeriesStyle = defaultStyle;
					point3D = point3D2;
				}
				if (flag && !flag2)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelEnd])
					{
						_labels3D.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(Label, LabelFontFamily, 8.5, LineColour, double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, point3D2, HorizontalAlignment.Left, VerticalAlignment.Center, PlotAxisID.X, back: false, _normalRotation, out var _, LabelFontStyle, LabelFontWeight, LabelTextDecorations));
					}
				}
				if (wireLines != null)
				{
					_visual3D.Children.Add(wireLines);
					_visual3DDataPoints.Add(wireLines, null);
					wireLines = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private void DrawPoint3D(IDataPoint dataPoint, Point3D position, IDataSeriesStyle style)
		{
			if (!dataPoint.HideSymbol && _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Symbol])
			{
				Visual3D visual3D = PlotSupport.Draw3DSymbol(_visual3D, position, style.SymbolStyle, style.SymbolColour, style.SymbolScale, _normalRotation);
				if (visual3D != null)
				{
					_visual3DDataPoints.Add(visual3D, new IDataPoint[1] { dataPoint });
				}
			}
			DrawPointLabel3D(dataPoint, position);
		}

		private void DrawPointLabel3D(IDataPoint dataPoint, Point3D position)
		{
			if (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Label] && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataPoint.Label))
			{
				_labels3D.Children.Add(PlotSupport.Make3DLabel(dataPoint.Label, LabelFontFamily, 8.5, Colors.Black, double.PositiveInfinity, 1.0, position, HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment.Center, PlotAxisID.X, back: false, _normalRotation, out var _, LabelFontStyle, LabelFontWeight, LabelTextDecorations));
			}
		}

		private void Draw3DBarSeries(R3DCuboid volume, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ)
		{
			if (!_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill] && !_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line])
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_zMapId);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null || axis == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double xMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double xMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double yMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double yMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axis.AxisAnnotator;
			double zMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double zMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			IDataSeriesStyle defaultStyle = _defaultStyle;
			for (int i = 0; i < _dataSource.Count; i += 2)
			{
				defaultStyle = GetStyle(_dataSource[i].StyleKey) ?? _defaultStyle;
				Brush fill = (_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill] ? defaultStyle.FillBrush : null);
				PlotPen line = ((_visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line] && defaultStyle.LineStyle != LineStyle.None) ? defaultStyle.LinePen : null);
				IDataPoint dataPoint = _dataSource[i];
				IDataPoint dataPoint2 = _dataSource[i + 1];
				Point3D dataPoint3DLinearPostion;
				Point3D dataPoint3DLinearPostion2;
				if (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar && _graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.Stack)
				{
					double num = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint.X, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint.Y, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num3 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, dataPoint.Z, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num4 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint2.X, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num5 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint2.Y, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num6 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, dataPoint2.Z, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Dictionary<object, Dictionary<object, double>> stackedBarOffsets3D = _graph.StackedBarOffsets3D;
					Dictionary<object, double> dictionary = null;
					PlotAxisID plotAxisID = PlotAxisID.X | PlotAxisID.Y | PlotAxisID.Z;
					object obj = null;
					if (axisForSeries.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						plotAxisID &= ~PlotAxisID.X;
						obj = dataPoint.X;
						dictionary = (stackedBarOffsets3D.ContainsKey(obj) ? stackedBarOffsets3D[obj] : (stackedBarOffsets3D[obj] = new Dictionary<object, double>()));
					}
					if (axisForSeries2.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						plotAxisID &= ~PlotAxisID.Y;
						obj = dataPoint.Y;
						if (dictionary == null)
						{
							dictionary = (stackedBarOffsets3D.ContainsKey(obj) ? stackedBarOffsets3D[obj] : (stackedBarOffsets3D[obj] = new Dictionary<object, double>()));
						}
					}
					if (axis.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						plotAxisID &= ~PlotAxisID.Z;
						obj = dataPoint.Z;
						if (dictionary == null)
						{
							dictionary = (stackedBarOffsets3D.ContainsKey(obj) ? stackedBarOffsets3D[obj] : (stackedBarOffsets3D[obj] = new Dictionary<object, double>()));
						}
					}
					if (obj == null)
					{
						throw new InvalidOperationException("Must have two label axes for 3-D bar chart.");
					}
					switch (plotAxisID)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
						if (dictionary.ContainsKey(obj))
						{
							num += dictionary[obj];
							num4 += dictionary[obj];
						}
						dictionary[obj] = num4;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
						if (dictionary.ContainsKey(obj))
						{
							num2 += dictionary[obj];
							num5 += dictionary[obj];
						}
						dictionary[obj] = num5;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Z:
						if (dictionary.ContainsKey(obj))
						{
							num3 += dictionary[obj];
							num6 += dictionary[obj];
						}
						dictionary[obj] = num6;
						break;
					default:
						throw new InvalidOperationException("Must have two label axes for 3-D bar chart.");
					}
					dataPoint3DLinearPostion = GetDataPoint3DLinearPostion(num, num2, num3, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					dataPoint3DLinearPostion2 = GetDataPoint3DLinearPostion(num4, num5, num3, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					dataPoint3DLinearPostion = GetDataPoint3DLinearPostion(dataPoint, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					dataPoint3DLinearPostion2 = GetDataPoint3DLinearPostion(dataPoint2, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
				}
				if (DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar)
				{
					if (axisForSeries.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.SideBySide)
						{
							int num7 = _graph.DataSeriesCollection.IndexOf(this);
							int count = _graph.DataSeriesCollection.Count;
							double num8 = 0.9;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X += (0.0 - num8) / 2.0 + num8 * (double)num7 / (double)count;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X = dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X + num8 / (double)count;
						}
						else
						{
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X -= 0.45;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X += 0.45;
						}
					}
					if (axisForSeries2.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.SideBySide)
						{
							int num9 = _graph.DataSeriesCollection.IndexOf(this);
							int count2 = _graph.DataSeriesCollection.Count;
							double num10 = 0.9;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y += (0.0 - num10) / 2.0 + num10 * (double)num9 / (double)count2;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y = dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y + num10 / (double)count2;
						}
						else
						{
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y -= 0.45;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y += 0.45;
						}
					}
					if (axis.DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						if (_graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.Overlay || _graph.GraphLayout.BarLayout == BarChartLayout.StaggeredOverlay)
						{
							int num11 = _graph.DataSeriesCollection.IndexOf(this);
							int count3 = _graph.DataSeriesCollection.Count;
							double num12 = 0.9;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z += (0.0 - num12) / 2.0 + num12 * (double)num11 / (double)count3;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z = dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z + num12 / (double)count3;
						}
						else
						{
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z -= 0.45;
							dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z += 0.45;
						}
					}
				}
				dataPoint3DLinearPostion = LinearPointTo3D(volume, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, dataPoint3DLinearPostion, out valid, clipToExtent: true, out var clipPoints);
				if (valid)
				{
					dataPoint3DLinearPostion2 = LinearPointTo3D(volume, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2, out valid, clipToExtent: true, out var clipPoints2);
					if (valid && (clipPoints & clipPoints2) == 0)
					{
						IDataPoint[] dataPoints = new IDataPoint[2] { dataPoint, dataPoint2 };
						Point3D topLeft = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						Point3D point3D = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						Point3D bottomLeft = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						Point3D point3D2 = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						_visual3D.Children.Add(MakeFilledRectangle3D(fill, line, topLeft, point3D, bottomLeft, point3D2, miss4thLine: true, dataPoints));
						topLeft = point3D;
						bottomLeft = point3D2;
						point3D = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						point3D2 = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						_visual3D.Children.Add(MakeFilledRectangle3D(fill, line, topLeft, point3D, bottomLeft, point3D2, miss4thLine: true, dataPoints));
						topLeft = point3D;
						bottomLeft = point3D2;
						point3D = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						point3D2 = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						_visual3D.Children.Add(MakeFilledRectangle3D(fill, line, topLeft, point3D, bottomLeft, point3D2, miss4thLine: true, dataPoints));
						topLeft = point3D;
						bottomLeft = point3D2;
						point3D = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						point3D2 = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						_visual3D.Children.Add(MakeFilledRectangle3D(fill, line, topLeft, point3D, bottomLeft, point3D2, miss4thLine: true, dataPoints));
						topLeft = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						point3D = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						bottomLeft = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						point3D2 = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						_visual3D.Children.Add(MakeFilledRectangle3D(fill, null, topLeft, point3D, bottomLeft, point3D2, miss4thLine: false, dataPoints));
						topLeft = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						point3D = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						bottomLeft = new Point3D(Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						point3D2 = new Point3D(Math.Max(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.X, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.X), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Y, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Y), Math.Min(dataPoint3DLinearPostion.Z, dataPoint3DLinearPostion2.Z));
						_visual3D.Children.Add(MakeFilledRectangle3D(fill, null, topLeft, point3D, bottomLeft, point3D2, miss4thLine: false, dataPoints));
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private Visual3D MakeFilledRectangle3D(Brush fill, PlotPen line, Point3D topLeft, Point3D topRight, Point3D bottomLeft, Point3D bottomRight, bool miss4thLine, IDataPoint[] dataPoints)
		{
			ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D = new ModelVisual3D();
			if (fill != null)
			{
				Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
				Int32Collection triangleIndices = PlotSupport.MakeInt32Collection(new int[6] { 0, 2, 1, 1, 2, 3 });
				PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 1.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 1.0));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(topLeft.X, topLeft.Y, topLeft.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(topRight.X, topRight.Y, topRight.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(bottomLeft.X, bottomLeft.Y, bottomLeft.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(bottomRight.X, bottomRight.Y, bottomRight.Z));
				Visual3DCollection children = modelVisual3D.Children;
				ModelVisual3D obj = new ModelVisual3D
				{
					Content = new GeometryModel3D
					{
						Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
						{
							TriangleIndices = triangleIndices,
							Positions = point3DCollection,
							TextureCoordinates = pointCollection
						},
						Material = new DiffuseMaterial(fill)
					}
				};
				ModelVisual3D key = obj;
				children.Add(obj);
				if (dataPoints != null && dataPoints.Length != 0)
				{
					_visual3DDataPoints[key] = dataPoints;
				}
			}
			if (line != null)
			{
				WirePolyline wirePolyline = new WirePolyline();
				wirePolyline.Points = new Point3DCollection();
				wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(topLeft.X, topLeft.Y, topLeft.Z));
				if (topLeft != topRight)
				{
					wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(topRight.X, topRight.Y, topRight.Z));
				}
				if (bottomRight != topRight)
				{
					wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(bottomRight.X, bottomRight.Y, bottomRight.Z));
				}
				if (bottomRight != bottomLeft)
				{
					wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(bottomLeft.X, bottomLeft.Y, bottomLeft.Z));
				}
				if (bottomLeft != topLeft && !miss4thLine)
				{
					wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(topLeft.X, topLeft.Y, topLeft.Z));
				}
				wirePolyline.Color = line.StrokeColour;
				wirePolyline.Thickness = line.StrokeThickness;
				modelVisual3D.Children.Add(wirePolyline);
				if (dataPoints != null && dataPoints.Length != 0)
				{
					_visual3DDataPoints[wirePolyline] = dataPoints;
				}
			}
			return modelVisual3D;
		}

		private void Draw3DSurfaceGrid(R3DCuboid volume, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ)
		{
			bool flag = _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line] && LineStyle != LineStyle.None;
			bool flag2 = _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill];
			if (!flag && !flag2)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_zMapId);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null || axis == null || _dataSource.Count < 3)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double xMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double xMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double yMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double yMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axis.AxisAnnotator;
			double zMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double zMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			_ = _defaultStyle;
			Point3D point3D = default(Point3D);
			Point3D point3D2 = default(Point3D);
			double num = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, _dataSource[0].X, out valid);
			double num2 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, _dataSource[0].Z, out valid);
			double num3 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, _dataSource[1].X, out valid);
			bool flag3 = num == num3;
			int num4 = 0;
			for (int i = 1; i < _dataSource.Count; i++)
			{
				num3 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, _dataSource[i].X, out valid);
				double num5 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, _dataSource[i].Z, out valid);
				if ((flag3 && num3 != num) || (!flag3 && num5 != num2))
				{
					num4 = i;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (num4 < 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num6 = _dataSource.Count / num4;
			if (num6 < 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
			Int32Collection int32Collection = new Int32Collection();
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			Dictionary<IDataPoint, PointAndIndex> dictionary = new Dictionary<IDataPoint, PointAndIndex>();
			Dictionary<Point3D, IDataPoint> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<Point3D, IDataPoint>();
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection2 = new Point3DCollection();
			int num7 = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < num6; j++)
			{
				for (int k = 0; k < num4; k++)
				{
					int index;
					IDataPoint dataPoint = (((index = num4 * j + k) < _dataSource.Count) ? _dataSource[index] : RDataPoint.EmptyDataPoint);
					if (dataPoint.IsEmptyDataPoint)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Point3D linearPosition;
					Point3D point3D3 = DataPointTo3D(volume, dataPoint, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, out linearPosition, out valid);
					if (!valid || dictionary.ContainsKey(dataPoint))
					{
						continue;
					}
					PointAndIndex value = new PointAndIndex
					{
						point = point3D3,
						index = num7++
					};
					dictionary.Add(dataPoint, value);
					point3DCollection.Add(point3D3);
					dictionary2[point3D3] = dataPoint;
					if (j <= 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IDataPoint dataPoint2 = (((index = num4 * (j - 1) + k) < _dataSource.Count) ? _dataSource[index] : RDataPoint.EmptyDataPoint);
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(dataPoint2))
					{
						continue;
					}
					PointAndIndex pointAndIndex = dictionary[dataPoint2];
					point3D = pointAndIndex.point;
					IDataPoint dataPoint3;
					if (k > 0 && !dataPoint.NewSegment)
					{
						dataPoint3 = _dataSource[num4 * j + k - 1];
						if (dictionary.ContainsKey(dataPoint3))
						{
							PointAndIndex pointAndIndex2 = dictionary[dataPoint3];
							point3D2 = pointAndIndex2.point;
							if (!dataPoint2.IsEmptyDataPoint && !dataPoint3.IsEmptyDataPoint && !dataPoint.NewSegment && !dataPoint3.NewSegment)
							{
								if (flag2)
								{
									int32Collection.Add(pointAndIndex2.index);
									int32Collection.Add(pointAndIndex.index);
									int32Collection.Add(value.index);
								}
								if (flag)
								{
									point3DCollection2.Add(point3D2);
									point3DCollection2.Add(point3D3);
									point3DCollection2.Add(point3D3);
									point3DCollection2.Add(point3D);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (k >= num4 - 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					dataPoint3 = (((index = num4 * (j - 1) + k + 1) < _dataSource.Count) ? _dataSource[index] : RDataPoint.EmptyDataPoint);
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(dataPoint3))
					{
						continue;
					}
					point3D2 = dictionary[dataPoint3].point;
					if (!dataPoint2.IsEmptyDataPoint && !dataPoint3.IsEmptyDataPoint && !dataPoint.NewSegment && !dataPoint3.NewSegment)
					{
						if (flag2)
						{
							int32Collection.Add(point3DCollection.IndexOf(point3D3));
							int32Collection.Add(point3DCollection.IndexOf(point3D));
							int32Collection.Add(point3DCollection.IndexOf(point3D2));
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							point3DCollection2.Add(point3D3);
							point3DCollection2.Add(point3D);
							point3DCollection2.Add(point3D);
							point3DCollection2.Add(point3D2);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				int val = int.MaxValue;
				int val2 = int.MinValue;
				int val3 = int.MaxValue;
				int val4 = int.MinValue;
				IGraphAxis axis2 = Graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xFillMap);
				IGraphAxis axis3 = Graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yFillMap);
				bool useContouredFill = UseContouredFill;
				double num8 = axis2.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis2, (useContouredFill || _minFillX == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_xFillMap].Minimum.Value) : _minFillX, out valid);
				double num9 = axis2.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis2, (useContouredFill || _maxFillX == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_xFillMap].Maximum.Value) : _maxFillX, out valid);
				double num10 = axis3.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis3, (useContouredFill || _minFillY == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_yFillMap].Minimum.Value) : _minFillY, out valid);
				double num11 = axis3.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis3, (useContouredFill || _maxFillY == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_yFillMap].Maximum.Value) : _maxFillY, out valid);
				for (int l = 0; l < point3DCollection.Count; l++)
				{
					val = Math.Min((int)point3DCollection[l].X, val);
					val2 = Math.Max((int)point3DCollection[l].X, val2);
					val3 = Math.Min((int)point3DCollection[l].Y, val3);
					val4 = Math.Max((int)point3DCollection[l].Y, val4);
					IDataPoint dataPoint4 = dictionary2[point3DCollection[l]];
					double num12 = ((_xFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint4.Y, out valid) : ((_xFillMap != PlotAxisID.Z) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint4.X, out valid) : PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, dataPoint4.Z, out valid)));
					double num13 = ((_yFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint4.Y, out valid) : ((_yFillMap != PlotAxisID.Z) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint4.X, out valid) : PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, dataPoint4.Z, out valid)));
					num12 = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, (axis2.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis2, num12, out valid) - num8) / (num9 - num8)));
					if (_xFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y)
					{
						num12 = 1.0 - num12;
					}
					num13 = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, (axis3.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis3, num13, out valid) - num10) / (num11 - num10)));
					if (_yFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y)
					{
						num13 = 1.0 - num13;
					}
					pointCollection.Add(new Point(num12, num13));
				}
				Brush contouredFill = ContouredFill;
				ModelVisual3D key;
				_visual3D.Children.Add(key = new ModelVisual3D
				{
					Content = new GeometryModel3D
					{
						Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
						{
							TriangleIndices = int32Collection,
							Positions = point3DCollection,
							TextureCoordinates = pointCollection
						},
						Material = new DiffuseMaterial(contouredFill),
						BackMaterial = new DiffuseMaterial(contouredFill)
					}
				});
				_visual3DDataPoints.Add(key, null);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				_visual3D.Children.Add(new WireLines
				{
					Lines = point3DCollection2,
					Color = LineColour
				});
			}
		}

		private void Draw3DSurface(R3DCuboid volume, double extentX, double extentY, double extentZ)
		{
			bool flag = _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line] && LineStyle != LineStyle.None;
			bool flag2 = _visualElementsVisibility[PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill];
			if (!flag && !flag2)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapId), this);
			IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_zMapId);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null || axis == null || _dataSource.Count < 3)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator;
			bool valid;
			double xMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double xMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator;
			double yMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double yMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axisForSeries2, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			axisAnnotator = axis.AxisAnnotator;
			double zMinVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MinVisible, out valid);
			double zMaxVisible = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis, axisAnnotator.AnnotatorState.MaxVisible, out valid);
			_ = _defaultStyle;
			Point3D point3D = default(Point3D);
			Point3D point3D2 = default(Point3D);
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
			Int32Collection int32Collection = new Int32Collection();
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection2 = new Point3DCollection();
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			Dictionary<IDataPoint, Point3D> dictionary = new Dictionary<IDataPoint, Point3D>();
			Dictionary<Point3D, IDataPoint> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<Point3D, IDataPoint>();
			for (int i = 0; i < _dataSource.Count; i += _dataSource.DataSize)
			{
				IDataPoint dataPoint = _dataSource[i];
				if (dataPoint.IsEmptyDataPoint)
				{
					continue;
				}
				Point3D point3D3;
				Point3D linearPosition;
				if (dictionary.ContainsKey(dataPoint))
				{
					point3D3 = dictionary[dataPoint];
				}
				else
				{
					point3D3 = DataPointTo3D(volume, dataPoint, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, out linearPosition, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					point3DCollection.Add(point3D3);
					dictionary.Add(dataPoint, point3D3);
					dictionary2.Add(point3D3, dataPoint);
				}
				IDataPoint dataPoint2 = _dataSource[i + 1];
				if (dictionary.ContainsKey(dataPoint2))
				{
					point3D = dictionary[dataPoint2];
				}
				else
				{
					point3D = DataPointTo3D(volume, dataPoint2, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, out linearPosition, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					point3DCollection.Add(point3D);
					dictionary.Add(dataPoint2, point3D);
					dictionary2.Add(point3D, dataPoint2);
				}
				IDataPoint dataPoint3 = _dataSource[i + 2];
				if (dictionary.ContainsKey(dataPoint3))
				{
					point3D2 = dictionary[dataPoint3];
				}
				else
				{
					point3D2 = DataPointTo3D(volume, dataPoint3, axisForSeries, axisForSeries2, axis, extentX, extentY, extentZ, xMinVisible, xMaxVisible, yMinVisible, yMaxVisible, zMinVisible, zMaxVisible, out linearPosition, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					point3DCollection.Add(point3D2);
					dictionary.Add(dataPoint3, point3D2);
					dictionary2.Add(point3D2, dataPoint3);
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					int32Collection.Add(point3DCollection.IndexOf(point3D3));
					int32Collection.Add(point3DCollection.IndexOf(point3D));
					int32Collection.Add(point3DCollection.IndexOf(point3D2));
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					point3DCollection2.Add(point3D3);
					point3DCollection2.Add(point3D);
					point3DCollection2.Add(point3D);
					point3DCollection2.Add(point3D2);
					point3DCollection2.Add(point3D2);
					point3DCollection2.Add(point3D3);
				}
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				int val = int.MaxValue;
				int val2 = int.MinValue;
				int val3 = int.MaxValue;
				int val4 = int.MinValue;
				IGraphAxis axis2 = Graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xFillMap);
				IGraphAxis axis3 = Graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yFillMap);
				bool useContouredFill = UseContouredFill;
				double num = axis2.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis2, (useContouredFill || _minFillX == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_xFillMap].Minimum.Value) : _minFillX, out valid);
				double num2 = axis2.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis2, (useContouredFill || _maxFillX == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_xFillMap].Maximum.Value) : _maxFillX, out valid);
				double num3 = axis3.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis3, (useContouredFill || _minFillY == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_yFillMap].Minimum.Value) : _minFillY, out valid);
				double num4 = axis3.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis3, (useContouredFill || _maxFillY == null) ? ((object)_axisRanges[_yFillMap].Maximum.Value) : _maxFillY, out valid);
				for (int j = 0; j < point3DCollection.Count; j++)
				{
					val = Math.Min((int)point3DCollection[j].X, val);
					val2 = Math.Max((int)point3DCollection[j].X, val2);
					val3 = Math.Min((int)point3DCollection[j].Y, val3);
					val4 = Math.Max((int)point3DCollection[j].Y, val4);
					IDataPoint dataPoint4 = dictionary2[point3DCollection[j]];
					double num5 = ((_xFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint4.Y, out valid) : ((_xFillMap != PlotAxisID.Z) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint4.X, out valid) : PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, dataPoint4.Z, out valid)));
					double num6 = ((_yFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries2, dataPoint4.Y, out valid) : ((_yFillMap != PlotAxisID.Z) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axisForSeries, dataPoint4.X, out valid) : PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, dataPoint4.Z, out valid)));
					num5 = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, (axis2.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis2, num5, out valid) - num) / (num2 - num)));
					if (_xFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y)
					{
						num5 = 1.0 - num5;
					}
					num6 = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, (axis3.AxisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(axis3, num6, out valid) - num3) / (num4 - num3)));
					if (_yFillMap == PlotAxisID.Y)
					{
						num6 = 1.0 - num6;
					}
					pointCollection.Add(new Point(num5, num6));
				}
				Brush contouredFill = ContouredFill;
				ModelVisual3D key;
				_visual3D.Children.Add(key = new ModelVisual3D
				{
					Content = new GeometryModel3D
					{
						Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
						{
							TriangleIndices = int32Collection,
							Positions = point3DCollection,
							TextureCoordinates = pointCollection
						},
						Material = new DiffuseMaterial(contouredFill),
						BackMaterial = new DiffuseMaterial(contouredFill)
					}
				});
				_visual3DDataPoints.Add(key, null);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				_visual3D.Children.Add(new WireLines
				{
					Lines = point3DCollection2,
					Color = LineColour
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class DataSeriesStyle : IDataSeriesStyle
	{
		private WeakReference _dataSeriesWR;

		private SymbolStyle _symbolStyle = SymbolStyle.Diamond;

		private double _symbolScale = 1.0;

		private PlotPen _linePen;

		private PlotPen _symbolPen;

		private object _tag;

		private DataSeries _dataSeries
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dataSeriesWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _dataSeriesWR.Target as DataSeries;
			}
		}

		public LineStyle LineStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return LinePen.StrokeLineStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				LinePen.StrokeLineStyle = value;
			}
		}

		public SymbolStyle SymbolStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _symbolStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_symbolStyle != value)
				{
					_symbolStyle = value;
					SymbolPen.IsFillVisible = _symbolStyle == SymbolStyle.Dot || (_symbolStyle & SymbolStyle.Filled) == SymbolStyle.Filled;
					_dataSeries.VisualElementsChanged = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public double LineWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return LinePen.StrokeThickness;
			}
			set
			{
				LinePen.StrokeThickness = value;
				SymbolPen.StrokeThickness = Math.Max(1.0, value);
			}
		}

		public Color LineColour
		{
			get
			{
				return LinePen.StrokeColour;
			}
			set
			{
				LinePen.StrokeColour = value;
			}
		}

		public Color SymbolColour
		{
			get
			{
				return SymbolPen.StrokeColour;
			}
			set
			{
				SymbolPen.StrokeColour = value;
				SymbolPen.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(value);
			}
		}

		public Brush FillBrush
		{
			get
			{
				return LinePen.Fill;
			}
			set
			{
				LinePen.Fill = value;
			}
		}

		public double SymbolScale
		{
			get
			{
				return _symbolScale;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_symbolScale != value)
				{
					_symbolScale = value;
					_dataSeries.VisualElementsChanged = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public PlotPen LinePen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_linePen == null)
				{
					_linePen = PlotSupport.MakePen(Colors.Black, LineStyle.Solid, 1.5);
					_linePen.NonVisibleFill = null;
				}
				return _linePen;
			}
		}

		public PlotPen SymbolPen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_symbolPen == null)
				{
					_symbolPen = PlotSupport.MakePen(Colors.Black, LineStyle.Solid, 1.5);
					_symbolPen.IsFillVisible = _symbolStyle == SymbolStyle.Dot || (_symbolStyle & SymbolStyle.Filled) == SymbolStyle.Filled;
				}
				return _symbolPen;
			}
		}

		public object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return _tag;
			}
			set
			{
				_tag = value;
			}
		}

		private DataSeriesStyle()
		{
		}

		internal DataSeriesStyle(DataSeries dataSeries)
		{
			_dataSeriesWR = new WeakReference(dataSeries);
		}

		internal DataSeriesStyle Clone()
		{
			return new DataSeriesStyle(_dataSeries)
			{
				FillBrush = FillBrush,
				LineColour = LineColour,
				LineStyle = LineStyle,
				LineWidth = LineWidth,
				SymbolColour = SymbolColour,
				SymbolScale = SymbolScale,
				SymbolStyle = SymbolStyle
			};
		}
	}
	public class DataStore : List<IDataPoint>, IPlotDataStore, IPlotDataSource, IEnumerable<IDataPoint>, IEnumerable, IList<IDataPoint>, ICollection<IDataPoint>
	{
		private string _xUom = string.Empty;

		private string _yUom = string.Empty;

		private string _zUom = string.Empty;

		private PlotDataSourceType _dataSourceType;

		private bool _updated;

		public new IDataPoint this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return base[index];
			}
			set
			{
				if (base[index] != value)
				{
					base[index] = value;
					_updated = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public PlotDataSourceType DataSourceType
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataSourceType;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_dataSourceType != value)
				{
					_dataSourceType = value;
					_updated = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool LiveDataSource => false;

		public int DataSize
		{
			get
			{
				switch (_dataSourceType)
				{
				case PlotDataSourceType.Surface:
				case PlotDataSourceType.HiLoAvg:
					return 3;
				case PlotDataSourceType.Bar:
				case PlotDataSourceType.Histogram:
				case PlotDataSourceType.Bubble:
				case PlotDataSourceType.HiLo:
					return 2;
				default:
					return 1;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Updated
		{
			get
			{
				return _updated;
			}
			set
			{
				_updated = value;
			}
		}

		public new void Add(IDataPoint item)
		{
			base.Add(item);
			_updated = true;
		}

		public new void Insert(int index, IDataPoint item)
		{
			base.Insert(index, item);
			_updated = true;
		}

		public new void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			base.RemoveAt(index);
			_updated = true;
		}

		public new void Clear()
		{
			base.Clear();
			_updated = true;
		}

		public int AddPoint(object x, object y)
		{
			return AddPoint(x, y, 0, string.Empty, newSegment: false);
		}

		public int AddPoint(object x, object y, object z)
		{
			return AddPoint(x, y, z, string.Empty, newSegment: false);
		}

		public int AddPoint(object x, object y, string label, bool newSegment)
		{
			return AddPoint(x, y, 0, label, newSegment);
		}

		public int AddPoint(object x, object y, object z, string label, bool newSegment)
		{
			return AddPoint(x, y, z, label, newSegment, null, hideSymbol: false);
		}

		public int AddPoint(object x, object y, string label, bool newSegment, object styleKey, bool hideSymbol)
		{
			return AddPoint(x, y, 0, label, newSegment, styleKey, hideSymbol);
		}

		public int AddPoint(object x, object y, object z, string label, bool newSegment, object styleKey, bool hideSymbol)
		{
			RDataPoint rDataPoint = new RDataPoint();
			rDataPoint.X = x;
			rDataPoint.Y = y;
			rDataPoint.Z = z;
			rDataPoint.Label = label;
			rDataPoint.NewSegment = newSegment;
			rDataPoint.StyleKey = styleKey;
			rDataPoint.HideSymbol = hideSymbol;
			Add(rDataPoint);
			return base.Count - 1;
		}

		public void GetDataUom(out string xUom, out string yUom, out string zUom)
		{
			xUom = _xUom;
			yUom = _yUom;
			zUom = _zUom;
		}

		public void SetDataUom(string xUom, string yUom, string zUom)
		{
			if (xUom != _xUom || yUom != _yUom || zUom != _zUom)
			{
				_xUom = xUom;
				_yUom = yUom;
				_zUom = zUom;
				_updated = true;
			}
		}
	}
	public class DateTimeAxisAnnotator : GraphAxisAnnotator
	{
		private DateTimeAxisRecommendations _recommendations;

		public override string AnnotatorType => "DateTime";

		public override PlotAxisDataType SupportedDataTypes => PlotAxisDataType.DateTime;

		public override PlotAxisDataType DataType
		{
			get
			{
				return PlotAxisDataType.DateTime;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((value & SupportedDataTypes) != value)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Data type not supported by axis annotator");
				}
			}
		}

		public override string RecommendedFormatString
		{
			get
			{
				if (_recommendations != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_recommendations.Format))
				{
					return _recommendations.Format;
				}
				return "g";
			}
		}

		public override double AxisLinearMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearMapCallback(axis, value, out valid);
			}
			return PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
		}

		public override object AxisLinearUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis.MapCallback != null)
			{
				return axis.MapCallback.AxisLinearUnmapCallback(axis, point, out valid);
			}
			return PlotSupport.DoubleToValue(axis, point, out valid);
		}

		public override void BuildLabelList(IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			lock (AnnotationsLock)
			{
				base.Annotations.Clear();
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = 0;
				DateTime dateTime = default(DateTime);
				DateTime dateTime2 = default(DateTime);
				string label = string.Empty;
				bool flag = true;
				bool flag2 = true;
				while (flag || flag2)
				{
					if (num < _recommendations.Minor.Length)
					{
						dateTime = _recommendations.Minor[num];
					}
					else
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					if (num2 < _recommendations.Major.Length)
					{
						dateTime2 = _recommendations.Major[num2];
						label = _recommendations.Annotation[num2];
					}
					else
					{
						flag2 = false;
					}
					RAxisAnnotation item = default(RAxisAnnotation);
					bool valid;
					if (flag && (dateTime < dateTime2 || !flag2))
					{
						item.LinearPos = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, dateTime, out valid);
						if (valid)
						{
							item.TickType = TickType.Minor;
							item.Label = string.Empty;
							item.dataValue = dateTime;
							base.Annotations.Add(item);
						}
						num++;
					}
					else if (flag2)
					{
						item.LinearPos = AxisLinearMapValue(axis, dateTime2, out valid);
						if (valid)
						{
							item.TickType = TickType.Major;
							item.Label = label;
							item.dataValue = dateTime2;
							base.Annotations.Add(item);
						}
						num2++;
					}
				}
				base.Annotations.Sort();
			}
		}

		public override void Initialise(IGraphAxis axis, bool reinitialise)
		{
			if (!Initialised || reinitialise)
			{
				base.Initialise(axis, reinitialise);
				double minView = State.MinView;
				double maxView = State.MaxView;
				try
				{
					bool valid;
					DateTime start = (DateTime)AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, minView, out valid);
					DateTime end = (DateTime)AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, maxView, out valid);
					DateTimeRange range = new DateTimeRange(start, end);
					_recommendations = DateTimeAxisHelpers.RecommendAxis(range, 300);
					State.MinDetail = State.MinVisible;
					State.MaxDetail = State.MaxVisible;
					BuildLabelList(axis);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				Initialised = true;
			}
		}
	}

		void ScrollTo(double horizontalValue, double verticalValue);
	}

					_distance = Math.Sqrt(_camera.Position.X * _camera.Position.X + _camera.Position.Y * _camera.Position.Y + _camera.Position.Z * _camera.Position.Z);
					Set3DProperties();
				}
			}
		}

		private ScrollBar HScroll
		{
			get
			{
				if (_hScroll == null)

					_hScroll = new ScrollBar();

					if (!Is3D)
					{
						_hScroll.ViewportSize = 1.0;
						_hScroll.Scroll += _hScroll_Scroll;
					}
				}
				return _hScroll;
			}
		}

		{

					}

		private IValueRange HorizontalZoom
		{
			get
			{
				IValueRange valueRange = new ValueRange();
				valueRange.SetAbsolute(0.0, 1.0, validate: true);
				IGraphAxis axis;
				if ((axis = _axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.X)) != null)
				{
					valueRange = axis.ZoomRange;
				}
				if ((axis = _axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.X2)) != null)
				{
					valueRange.SetAbsolute(Math.Max(valueRange.Minimum.Value, axis.ZoomRange.Minimum.Value), Math.Min(valueRange.Maximum.Value, axis.ZoomRange.Maximum.Value), validate: true);
				}
				return valueRange;
			}
		}

		private IValueRange VerticalZoom
		{
			get
			{
				IValueRange valueRange = new ValueRange();
				valueRange.SetAbsolute(0.0, 1.0, validate: true);
				IGraphAxis axis;
				if ((axis = _axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y)) != null)
				{
					valueRange = axis.ZoomRange;
				}
				if ((axis = _axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.Y2)) != null)
				{
					valueRange.SetAbsolute(Math.Max(valueRange.Minimum.Value, axis.ZoomRange.Minimum.Value), Math.Min(valueRange.Maximum.Value, axis.ZoomRange.Maximum.Value), validate: true);
				}
				return valueRange;
			}
		}

		ScrollBar IScrollableGraph.HorizontalScrollBar
		{
			get
			{
				if (!Is3D)
				{
					return ConfigureScrollBar(HScroll, HorizontalZoom, ResolvedHorizontalScrollBarVisibility);
				}
				return HScroll;
			}
		}

		{
			get
			{
				if (!Is3D)
				{
					return ConfigureScrollBar(VScroll, VerticalZoom, ResolvedVerticalScrollBarVisibility);
				}
				return VScroll;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ChildYAxisEventArgs> ChildYAxisGenerated;

		event EventHandler<GraphScrollEventArgs> IScrollableGraph.GraphScroll
		{
			add
			{
				_graphScroll = (EventHandler<GraphScrollEventArgs>)Delegate.Combine(_graphScroll, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_graphScroll = (EventHandler<GraphScrollEventArgs>)Delegate.Remove(_graphScroll, value);
			}
		}

		public Graph()
		{
			_dataSeries = new DataSeriesCollection(this);
			_scooterCollection = new GraphScooterCollection(this);
			_annotationCollection = new GraphAnnotationCollection(this);
			_sliceCollection = new GraphSliceCollection(this);
		}

		internal void RaiseChildYAxisGenerated(IGraphAxis childYAxis)
		{
			if (this.ChildYAxisGenerated != null)
			{
				this.ChildYAxisGenerated(this, new ChildYAxisEventArgs(childYAxis));
			}
		}
