		{
			return BaseAnnotator.AxisLinearUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
		}

		double IGraphAxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis == this)
			{
				return BaseAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axis, value, out valid);
			}
			return axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axis, value, out valid);
		}

		object IGraphAxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis == this)
			{
				return BaseAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
			}
			return axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
		}

		string IGraphAxisAnnotator.AxisFractionToValueString(IGraphAxis axis, double point)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (axis == this)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				foreach (IGraphAxis renderableAxis in _renderableAxes)
				{
					bool valid;
					object arg = renderableAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(renderableAxis, point, out valid);
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("\n");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t= {1:" + renderableAxis.DataLabelFormatString + "}", renderableAxis.Title, arg));
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return BaseAnnotator.AxisFractionToValueString(axis, point);
		}

		Size IGraphAxisAnnotator.GetMaxLabelExtent(IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			return BaseAnnotator.GetMaxLabelExtent(axis);
		}

		bool IGraphAxisAnnotator.ValueIsValid(IGraphAxis axis, object value)
		{
			return BaseAnnotator.ValueIsValid(axis, value);
		}

		Size IGraphAxisAnnotator.EnsureRenderSize(IGraphAxis axis, Size desiredSize)
		{
			Size result = default(Size);
			_axisSpacing = new Dictionary<IGraphAxis, double>();
			Size renderSize;
			if (SubAxisLayout != ChildAxisLayout.None)
			{
				foreach (IDataSeries key in _axes.Keys)
				{
					if (!key.Visible)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IGraphAxis graphAxis = _axes[key];
					if (_axisSpacing.ContainsKey(graphAxis))
					{
						continue;
					}
					_axisSpacing.Add(graphAxis, 0.0);
					renderSize = graphAxis.RenderSize;
					if ((base.Id & PlotAxisID.X) != PlotAxisID.None)
					{
						ChildAxisLayout subAxisLayout = SubAxisLayout;
						if ((uint)(subAxisLayout - 2) <= 1u)
						{
							result.Height += renderSize.Height;
							_axisSpacing[graphAxis] = renderSize.Height;
						}
						else
						{
							result.Height = Math.Max(renderSize.Height, result.Height);
						}
						result.Width = Math.Max(renderSize.Width, result.Width);
					}
					else
					{
						ChildAxisLayout subAxisLayout = SubAxisLayout;
						if ((uint)(subAxisLayout - 2) <= 1u)
						{
							result.Width += renderSize.Width;
							_axisSpacing[graphAxis] = renderSize.Width;
						}
						else
						{
							result.Width = Math.Max(renderSize.Width, result.Width);
						}
						result.Height = Math.Max(renderSize.Height, result.Height);
					}
				}
			}
			renderSize = BaseAnnotator.EnsureRenderSize(axis, desiredSize);
			if ((base.Id & PlotAxisID.X) != PlotAxisID.None)
			{
				result.Height += renderSize.Height;
				result.Width = Math.Max(renderSize.Width, result.Width);
				_axisSpacing.Add(this, renderSize.Height);
			}
			else
			{
				result.Width += renderSize.Width;
				result.Height = Math.Max(renderSize.Height, result.Height);
				_axisSpacing.Add(this, renderSize.Width);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override void RenderInitialise()
		{
			_stretching = false;
			_visibleCount = 0;
			_renderableAxes = new List<IGraphAxis>();
			if (Visual != null)
			{
				foreach (IDataSeries item in _axes.Keys.ToList())
				{
					IGraphAxis graphAxis = _axes[item];
					if (item.Visible)
					{
						if (!_renderableAxes.Contains(graphAxis))
						{
							SetAxisFromDataSeries(item, graphAxis);
							graphAxis.Renderer.RenderInitialise();
							_visibleCount++;
							_renderableAxes.Add(graphAxis);
						}
					}
					else if (Visual.Children.Contains(graphAxis.Renderer.Visual))
					{
						Visual.Children.Remove(graphAxis.Renderer.Visual);
					}
				}
			}
			base.RenderInitialise();
		}

		public override void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			base.Draw(ref renderArea, refreshAll);
			if (Visual == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (IGraphAxis renderableAxis in _renderableAxes)
			{
				IPlotRender renderer = renderableAxis.Renderer;
				renderer.Draw(ref renderArea, refreshAll);
				if (!Visual.Children.Contains(renderer.Visual))
				{
					Visual.Children.Add(renderer.Visual);
				}
			}
		}

		public override void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, ref Rect axisArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			base.Draw(ref renderArea, ref axisArea, refreshAll);
			double num = 0.0;
			if (SubAxisLayout == ChildAxisLayout.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (base.Id)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				axisArea.Height = Math.Max(0.0, axisArea.Height - _axisSpacing[this]);
				num = axisArea.Top;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.X2:
				axisArea.Y += _axisSpacing[this];
				axisArea.Height = Math.Max(0.0, axisArea.Height - _axisSpacing[this]);
				num = axisArea.Top;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				axisArea.X += _axisSpacing[this];
				axisArea.Width = Math.Max(0.0, axisArea.Width - _axisSpacing[this]);
				num = axisArea.Left;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y2:
				axisArea.Width = Math.Max(0.0, axisArea.Width - _axisSpacing[this]);
				num = axisArea.Left;
				break;
			}
			if (Visual == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Rect renderArea2 = (Visual as PlotCanvasBase).RenderArea;
			double x = (renderArea2.Y = 0.0);
			renderArea2.X = x;
			foreach (IGraphAxis renderableAxis in _renderableAxes)
			{
				IAxisRender renderer = renderableAxis.Renderer;
				(renderer.Visual as PlotCanvasBase).RenderArea = renderArea2;
				if ((SubAxisLayout == ChildAxisLayout.SideBySide || SubAxisLayout == ChildAxisLayout.TitledSideBySide) && _axisSpacing.ContainsKey(renderableAxis))
				{
					switch (base.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
						axisArea.Y = num;
						axisArea.Height = Math.Max(0.0, _axisSpacing[renderableAxis]);
						num = axisArea.Bottom;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
						axisArea.X = num;
						axisArea.Width = Math.Max(0.0, _axisSpacing[renderableAxis]);
						num = axisArea.Right;
						break;
					}
				}
				renderer.Draw(ref renderArea, ref axisArea, refreshAll);
				if (!Visual.Children.Contains(renderer.Visual))
				{
					Visual.Children.Add(renderer.Visual);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class Plot : IPlot, IPlotRender
	{
		private List<IPlotFrame> _frames = new List<IPlotFrame>();

		private ReadOnlyCollection<IPlotFrame> _readOnlyFrames;

		private Brush _backgroundColour = PlotSupport.TransparentBrush;

		private PlotCanvas _visual = new PlotCanvas();

		private Rect _renderArea;

		private IPlotFrame _selectedFrame;

		private PlotLayoutMode _layoutMode;

		private bool _clearVisuals;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<IPlotFrame> Frames
		{
			get
			{
				if (_readOnlyFrames == null)
				{
					_readOnlyFrames = new ReadOnlyCollection<IPlotFrame>(_frames);
				}
				return _readOnlyFrames;
			}
		}

		public int MaximisedFrameIndex
		{
			get
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _frames.Count; i++)
				{
					if (_frames[i].Maximised)
					{
						return i;
					}
				}
				return -1;
			}
			set
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _frames.Count; i++)
				{
					_frames[i].Maximised = i == value;
				}
				_clearVisuals = true;
			}
		}

		public IPlotFrame MaximisedFrame
		{
			get
			{
				int maximisedFrameIndex = MaximisedFrameIndex;
				if (maximisedFrameIndex == -1)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _frames[maximisedFrameIndex];
			}
			set
			{
				MaximisedFrameIndex = ((value != null) ? _frames.IndexOf(value) : (-1));
			}
		}

		public Brush BackgroundColour
		{
			get
			{
				return _backgroundColour;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _backgroundColour)
				{
					_backgroundColour = value;
					_visual.Background = _backgroundColour;
				}
			}
		}

		public PlotLayoutMode LayoutMode
		{
			get
			{
				return _layoutMode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _layoutMode)
				{
					_layoutMode = value;
					_clearVisuals = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public IPlotFrame SelectedFrame
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedFrame;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					if (_frames.Count > 0)
					{
						_selectedFrame = _frames[0];
					}
					else
					{
						_selectedFrame = null;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (!_frames.Contains(value))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException((string)PlotSupport.Resources["unknownFrameError"]);
					}
					if (_selectedFrame != value)
					{
						_selectedFrame = value;
					}
				}
				IPlotFrame maximisedFrame = MaximisedFrame;
				if (maximisedFrame != null && _selectedFrame != maximisedFrame)
				{
					MaximisedFrame = _selectedFrame;
				}
			}
		}

		public IPlotRender Renderer => this;

		public Canvas Visual
		{
			get
			{
				if (_visual.Plot != this)
				{
					_visual.Plot = this;
				}
				return _visual;
			}
		}

		public IPlotFrame CreateFrame(Rect frameArea, IGraphLayout graphLayout)
		{
			IPlotFrame plotFrame = new PlotFrame(this);
			plotFrame.FrameArea = frameArea;
			if (graphLayout == null)
			{
				graphLayout = new Graph().GraphLayout;
			}
			plotFrame.GraphLayout = graphLayout;
			_frames.Add(plotFrame);
			if (_selectedFrame == null)
			{
				_selectedFrame = plotFrame;
			}
			return plotFrame;
		}

		public void DeleteFrame(IPlotFrame plotFrame)
		{
			if (_frames.Contains(plotFrame))
			{
				if (plotFrame.Renderer.Visual is PlotFrameVisual plotFrameVisual)
				{
					plotFrameVisual.Children.Clear();
					plotFrameVisual.PlotFrame = null;
					plotFrameVisual.HScroll = null;
					plotFrameVisual.VScroll = null;
				}
				_frames.Remove(plotFrame);
				plotFrame.GraphLayout = null;
				_clearVisuals = true;
				if (_selectedFrame == plotFrame)
				{
					SelectedFrame = null;
				}
				return;
			}
			throw new ArgumentException((string)PlotSupport.Resources["unrecognisedIPlotFrameError"], "plotFrame");
		}

		public void ClearFrames()
		{
			for (int num = _frames.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				DeleteFrame(_frames[num]);
			}
			_selectedFrame = null;
			_clearVisuals = true;
		}

		public void RenderInitialise()
		{
			foreach (IPlotFrame frame in _frames)
			{
				frame.Renderer.RenderInitialise();
			}
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			bool refreshAll2 = refreshAll || _clearVisuals;
			if (renderArea != _renderArea)
			{
				_renderArea = renderArea;
				_visual.RenderArea = renderArea;
				refreshAll2 = true;
				_visual.Children.Clear();
			}
			if (_clearVisuals)
			{
				_visual.Children.Clear();
			}
			_clearVisuals = false;
			IPlotFrame maximisedFrame;
			if ((maximisedFrame = MaximisedFrame) == null)
			{
				foreach (IPlotFrame frame in _frames)
				{
					DrawFrame(frame, ref renderArea, refreshAll2);
				}
				return;
			}
			DrawFrame(maximisedFrame, ref renderArea, refreshAll2);
		}

		private Rect GetGraphCoords(int position, int count)
		{
			Rect result = default(Rect);
			if (position < 0 || position >= count)
			{
				return result;
			}
			switch (_layoutMode)
			{
			case PlotLayoutMode.AutoRows:
				result.X = 0.0;
				result.Width = 1.0;
				result.Y = (float)position / (float)count;
				result.Height = (double)((float)(position + 1) / (float)count) - result.Y;
				return result;
			case PlotLayoutMode.AutoColumns:
				result.X = (float)position / (float)count;
				result.Width = (double)((float)(position + 1) / (float)count) - result.X;
				result.Y = 0.0;
				result.Height = 1.0;
				return result;
			default:
			{
				int num = (int)Math.Ceiling(Math.Sqrt(count));
				int num2 = (int)Math.Ceiling((double)count / (double)num);
				int num3 = position % num2;
				int num4 = position / num2;
				result.X = (float)num3 / (float)num2;
				result.Width = (double)((float)(num3 + 1) / (float)num2) - result.X;
				result.Y = (float)num4 / (float)num;
				result.Height = (double)((float)(num4 + 1) / (float)num) - result.Y;
				return result;
			}
			}
		}

		private void DrawFrame(IPlotFrame frame, ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			Rect renderArea2;
			if (!frame.Maximised)
			{
				Rect rect = frame.FrameArea;
				if ((_layoutMode & PlotLayoutMode.AutoMask) == PlotLayoutMode.AutoMask)
				{
					rect = GetGraphCoords(_frames.IndexOf(frame), _frames.Count);
				}
				renderArea2 = new Rect(renderArea.Width * rect.Left, renderArea.Height * rect.Top, renderArea.Width * rect.Width, renderArea.Height * rect.Height);
			}
			else
			{
				renderArea2 = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, renderArea.Width, renderArea.Height);
			}
			if (!(renderArea2.Width <= 0.0) && !(renderArea2.Height <= 0.0))
			{
				IPlotRender renderer = frame.Renderer;
				PlotFrameVisual plotFrameVisual = renderer.Visual as PlotFrameVisual;
				plotFrameVisual.RenderArea = renderArea2;
				PlotPen pen = PlotSupport.UnselectedBorderPen;
				if (frame == _selectedFrame && _frames.Count > 1)
				{
					pen = PlotSupport.SelectedBorderPen;
				}
				plotFrameVisual.DrawFrameBorder(pen);
				if (!frame.GraphLayout.Graph.Is3D && frame.GraphLayout is IScrollableGraph scrollableGraph)
				{
					plotFrameVisual.HScroll = scrollableGraph.HorizontalScrollBar;
					plotFrameVisual.VScroll = scrollableGraph.VerticalScrollBar;
				}
				renderer.Draw(ref renderArea2, refreshAll);
				if (!_visual.Children.Contains(plotFrameVisual))
				{
					_visual.AddCanvas(plotFrameVisual);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class PlotEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private readonly Point _position;

		private readonly IPlotFrame _frame;

		private PlotCtrl _plotCtrl;

		public Point MousePosition => _position;

		public IPlotFrame SelectedFrame => _frame;

		public PlotCtrl PlotControl
		{
			get
			{
				return _plotCtrl;
			}
			internal set
			{
				_plotCtrl = value;
			}
		}

		public PlotEventArgs(Point position, IPlotFrame frame)
		{
			_position = position;
			_frame = frame;
		}
	}
	public class PlotTrackEventArgs : PlotEventArgs
	{
		private bool _visible;

		private readonly Dictionary<PlotAxisID, object> _values;

		private readonly Dictionary<PlotAxisID, object> _tolerances;

		private string _text;

		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				return _text;
			}
			set
			{
				_text = value;
			}
		}

		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return _visible;
			}
			set
			{
				_visible = value;
			}
		}

		public PlotTrackEventArgs(bool visible, Point position, Dictionary<PlotAxisID, object> values, Dictionary<PlotAxisID, object> tolerances, IPlotFrame frame, string text)
			: base(position, frame)
		{
			_visible = visible;
			_values = values;
			_tolerances = tolerances;
			_text = text;
		}

		public object GetAxisValue(PlotAxisID axisId)
		{
			if (_values.ContainsKey(axisId))
			{
				return _values[axisId];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void SetAxisValue(PlotAxisID axisId, object value)
		{
			if (_values.ContainsKey(axisId))
			{
				if (value.GetType() != _values[axisId].GetType())
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Type mismatch", "value");
				}
				_values[axisId] = value;
				return;
			}
			throw new ArgumentException("Axis Id not recognised", "axisId");
		}

		public object GetAxisTolerance(PlotAxisID axisId)
		{
			if (_tolerances.ContainsKey(axisId))
			{
				return _tolerances[axisId];
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	public class PlotElementSelectionChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{

		private PlotElementInfo NewElementInfo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_newElementInfo == null)

					_newElementInfo = new PlotElementInfo(NewSelectedElement);

			}
		}

		public FrameworkElement OldSelectedElement { get; private set; }

		public FrameworkElement NewSelectedElement { get; private set; }

		public IPlotFrame NewSelectedElementLinkedPlotFrame => NewElementInfo.TargetElementLinkedPlotFrame;

		public PlotSelectedElementType NewSelectedElementType => NewElementInfo.TargetElementType;

		public IGraphAxis NewSelectedElementLinkedAxis => NewElementInfo.TargetElementLinkedAxis;

		public IDataSeries NewSelectedElementLinkedDataSeries => NewElementInfo.TargetElementLinkedDataSeries;

		public IGraphAnnotation NewSelectedElementLinkedGraphAnnotation => NewElementInfo.TargetElementLinkedGraphAnnotation;

	public class PlotElementInfo
	{
		private IPlotFrame _plotFrame;

		private IGraphAxis _linkedAxis;

		private IDataSeries _linkedDataSeries;

		private IGraphAnnotation _linkedGraphAnnotation;

		public FrameworkElement TargetElement { get; private set; }

		public IPlotFrame TargetElementLinkedPlotFrame
		{
			get
			{
				if (TargetElement == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (_plotFrame == null)
				{
					_plotFrame = PlotSelection.ResolvePlotFrame(TargetElement);
				}
				return _plotFrame;
			}
		}

		public PlotSelectedElementType TargetElementType
		{
			get
			{
				if (TargetElement != null)
				{
					return PlotSelection.GetElementType(TargetElement);
				}
				return PlotSelectedElementType.Unknown;
			}
		}

		public IGraphAxis TargetElementLinkedAxis
		{
			get
			{
				if (TargetElement == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (_linkedAxis == null)
				{
					_linkedAxis = PlotSelection.ResolveGraphAxis(TargetElement);
				}
				return _linkedAxis;
			}
		}

		public IDataSeries TargetElementLinkedDataSeries
		{
			get
			{
				if (TargetElement == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (_linkedDataSeries == null)
				{
					_linkedDataSeries = PlotSelection.ResolveDataSeries(TargetElement);
				}
				return _linkedDataSeries;
			}
		}

		public IGraphAnnotation TargetElementLinkedGraphAnnotation
		{
			get
			{
				if (TargetElement == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (_linkedGraphAnnotation == null)
				{
					_linkedGraphAnnotation = PlotSelection.ResolveGraphAnnotation(TargetElement);
				}
				return _linkedGraphAnnotation;
			}
		}

		public PlotElementInfo(FrameworkElement targetElement)
		{
			TargetElement = targetElement;
		}

		public static PlotElementInfo CreateFrom(MouseEventArgs mouseEventArgs, PlotCtrl container = null)
		{
			if (mouseEventArgs == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("mouseEventArgs");
			}
			return new PlotElementInfo(PlotSelection.GetAncestor(mouseEventArgs.OriginalSource as DependencyObject, container, (FrameworkElement x) => PlotSelection.GetElementType(x) != PlotSelectedElementType.Unknown));
		}
	}

	public class PlotFrame : IPlotFrame, IPlotRender
	{
		private IPlot _parentPlot;

		private IGraphLayout _graphLayout;

		private bool _refreshRequired = true;

		private Rect _frameArea = Rect.Empty;

		private bool _maximised;

		private PlotFrameVisual _visual = new PlotFrameVisual();

		public IGraphLayout GraphLayout
		{
			get
			{
				return _graphLayout;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _graphLayout)
				{
					if (_graphLayout != null)
					{
						(_graphLayout as Graph).ClearVisuals();
					}
					_graphLayout = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public IPlotRender Renderer => this;

		public Rect FrameArea
		{
			get
			{
				return _frameArea;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_frameArea != value)
				{
					_frameArea = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Maximised
		{
			get
			{
				return _maximised;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _maximised)
				{
					_maximised = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
					if (_parentPlot != null)
					{
						_parentPlot.MaximisedFrame = (value ? this : null);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public Canvas Visual => _visual;

		public PlotFrame(IPlot plot)
		{
			_parentPlot = plot;
		}

		~PlotFrame()
		{
		}

		public void RenderInitialise()
		{
			_visual.PlotFrame = this;
			_visual.PlotFrame.GraphLayout.Renderer.RenderInitialise();
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (_refreshRequired)
			{
				_visual.Children.Clear();
				_refreshRequired = false;
			}
			renderArea = _visual.ChildRenderArea;
			GraphLayout.Renderer.Draw(ref renderArea, refreshAll);
			if (!_visual.Children.Contains(GraphLayout.Renderer.Visual))
			{
				_visual.AddCanvas(GraphLayout.Renderer.Visual);
			}
		}
	}
	public class PlotPen : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private bool _isStrokeVisible = true;

		private bool _isFillVisible = true;

		private Brush _fill = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Transparent);

		private Brush _nonVisibleFill = WpfSilverlightSupport.Brushes.Transparent;

		private PlotPen _fillOnlyPen;

		private SolidColorBrush _stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Black);

		private double _stokeThickness;

		private LineStyle _strokeLineStyle = LineStyle.Solid;

		public bool IsStrokeVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return _isStrokeVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isStrokeVisible != value)
				{
					_isStrokeVisible = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("IsStrokeVisible");
					RaisePropertyChanged("VisibleStroke");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsFillVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return _isFillVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isFillVisible != value)
				{
					_isFillVisible = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("IsFillVisible");
					RaisePropertyChanged("VisibleFill");
				}
			}
		}

		public Brush Fill
		{
			get
			{
				return _fill;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_fill != value)
				{
					_fill = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Fill");
					RaisePropertyChanged("VisibleFill");
					if (_fillOnlyPen != null)
					{
						_fillOnlyPen.Fill = value;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public Brush NonVisibleFill
		{
			get
			{
				return _nonVisibleFill;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_nonVisibleFill != value)
				{
					_nonVisibleFill = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("NonVisibleFill");
					RaisePropertyChanged("VisibleFill");
				}
			}
		}

		public Brush VisibleFill
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_isFillVisible)
				{
					return NonVisibleFill;
				}
				return _fill;
			}
		}

		public PlotPen FillOnlyPen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_fillOnlyPen == null)
				{
					_fillOnlyPen = new PlotPen(Colors.Transparent, 0.0, LineStyle.None, _fill)
					{
						IsStrokeVisible = false
					};
				}
				return _fillOnlyPen;
			}
		}

		public Color StrokeColour
		{
			get
			{
				return _stroke.Color;
			}
			set
			{
				if (StrokeColour != value)
				{
					_stroke.Color = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("StrokeColour");
				}
			}
		}

		public Brush VisibleStroke
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_isStrokeVisible)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _stroke;
			}
		}

		public double StrokeThickness
		{
			get
			{
				return _stokeThickness;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_stokeThickness != value)
				{
					_stokeThickness = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("StrokeThickness");
				}
			}
		}

		public LineStyle StrokeLineStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _strokeLineStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_strokeLineStyle != value)
				{
					_strokeLineStyle = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("StrokeLineStyle");
					RaisePropertyChanged("StrokeDashArray");
				}
			}
		}

		public DoubleCollection StrokeDashArray
		{
			get
			{
				DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
				switch (_strokeLineStyle)
				{
				case LineStyle.Dot:
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					break;
				case LineStyle.Dash:
					doubleCollection.Add(2.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					break;
				case LineStyle.DashDot:
					doubleCollection.Add(2.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					break;
				case LineStyle.DashDotDot:
					doubleCollection.Add(2.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(1.0);
					break;
				case LineStyle.None:
					doubleCollection.Add(0.0);
					break;
				}
				return doubleCollection;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public PlotPen()
			: this(Colors.Black)
		{
		}

		public PlotPen(Color colour)
			: this(colour, 1.0)
		{
		}

		public PlotPen(Color colour, double thickness)
			: this(colour, thickness, LineStyle.Solid)
		{
		}

		public PlotPen(Color colour, double thickness, LineStyle lineStyle)
			: this(colour, thickness, lineStyle, WpfSilverlightSupport.Brushes.Transparent)
		{
		}

		public PlotPen(Color colour, double thickness, LineStyle lineStyle, Brush fill)
		{
			StrokeColour = colour;
			StrokeThickness = thickness;
			StrokeLineStyle = lineStyle;
			Fill = fill;
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}
	}
	public static class PlotSupport
	{
		private static Color[] _colourSet;

		private static LocalSupport _local;

		private static double _minPlotSize;

		private static ResourceDictionary _resources;

		private static double _symbolSize;

		private static Rect _zeroRectangle;

		private static Dictionary<string, Type> _registeredAxisAnnotators;

		private static Dictionary<string, PlotAxisDataType> _registeredAxisAnnotatorsSupportedDataTypes;

		private static FontFamily _systemMessageTypeface;

		private static FontFamily _systemCaptionTypeface;

		public static Color[] ColourSet => _colourSet;

		public static double MinPlotSize
		{
			get
			{
				return _minPlotSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value >= 0.0)
				{
					_minPlotSize = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public static SolidColorBrush TransparentBrush => Brush(Colors.Transparent);

			get
			{

					_resources = XamlHelpers.LoadResourceDictionaryFromAssembly(typeof(PlotSupport).Assembly, "component/PlotResources1.xaml");
				}
				return _resources;
			}

		public static Rect ZeroRectangle => _zeroRectangle;

		internal static Dictionary<string, Type> RegisteredAxisAnnotators
		{
			get
			{
				if (_registeredAxisAnnotators == null)
				{
					_registeredAxisAnnotators = new Dictionary<string, Type>();
					_registeredAxisAnnotatorsSupportedDataTypes = new Dictionary<string, PlotAxisDataType>();
					RegisterAxisAnnotator(new LinearAxisAnnotator());
					RegisterAxisAnnotator(new LogarithmicAxisAnnotator());
					RegisterAxisAnnotator(new ProbabilityAxisAnnotator());
					RegisterAxisAnnotator(new DateTimeAxisAnnotator());
				}
				return _registeredAxisAnnotators;
			}
		}

		static PlotSupport()
		{
			_colourSet = new Color[16]
			{
				Colors.Black,
				Colors.Maroon,
				Colors.Green,
				Colors.Olive,
				Colors.Navy,
				Colors.Purple,
				Colors.Teal,
				Colors.DarkGray,
				Colors.Red,
				Colors.Lime,
				Colors.Yellow,
				Colors.Blue,
				Colors.Fuchsia,
				Colors.Aqua,
				Colors.Gray,
				Colors.Yellow
			};
			_local = LocalSupport.GetAssemblyLocalSupport(VisualisationControlsLocalisation.Behaviour, Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			_minPlotSize = 100.0;
			_symbolSize = 5.0;
			_zeroRectangle = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			LabelScale3D = 0.75;
		}

		public static SolidColorBrush Brush(Color colour)

		}

			string text = Resources[key].ToString();
			if (_local != null)
			{
				text = _local.Localise(typeof(PlotCtrl), key, text);
			}
			return text;
		}

		}

		public static EllipseGeometry CreateEllipseGeometry(Point centre, double radiusX, double radiusY)
		{
			return new EllipseGeometry
			{
				Center = centre,
				RadiusX = radiusX,
				RadiusY = radiusY
			};
		}

		public static RectangleGeometry CreateRectangleGeometry(Rect rect)
		{
			return new RectangleGeometry
			{
				Rect = rect
			};
		}

		public static ArcSegment CreateArcSegment(Point point, Size size, double rotationAngle, bool isLargeArc, SweepDirection sweepDirection)
		{
			return new ArcSegment
			{
				Point = point,
				Size = size,
				RotationAngle = rotationAngle,
				IsLargeArc = isLargeArc,
				SweepDirection = sweepDirection
			};
		}

		public static double MathCeiling(double a)
		{
			return (double)Math.Ceiling((decimal)a);
		}

		public static Shape Draw2DSymbol(PlotCanvasBase canvas, Point pos, SymbolStyle symbol, PlotPen pen, double symbolScale, IDataPoint dataPoint)
		{
			double num = symbolScale * _symbolSize;
			Point centre = new Point(num, num);
			pos = new Point(pos.X - num, pos.Y - num);
			Shape shape = null;
			switch (symbol & SymbolStyle.ShapeMask)
			{
			case SymbolStyle.Circle:
				shape = canvas.AddPath(pen, CreateEllipseGeometry(centre, num, num), pos);
				break;
			case SymbolStyle.Cross:
			{
				GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
				geometryGroup.Children.Add(new LineGeometry
				{
					StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0),
					EndPoint = new Point(2.0 * num, 2.0 * num)
				});
				geometryGroup.Children.Add(new LineGeometry
				{
					StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 2.0 * num),
					EndPoint = new Point(2.0 * num, 0.0)
				});
				shape = canvas.AddPath(pen, geometryGroup, pos);
				break;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Diamond:
			{
				PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(num, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(2.0 * num, num));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(num, 2.0 * num));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, num));
				shape = canvas.AddPolygon(pen, pointCollection, FillRule.Nonzero, pos);
				break;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Dot:
				shape = canvas.AddPath(pen, CreateEllipseGeometry(centre, num / 2.0, num / 2.0), pos);
				break;
			case SymbolStyle.Plus:
			{
				GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
				geometryGroup.Children.Add(new LineGeometry
				{
					StartPoint = new Point(0.0, num),
					EndPoint = new Point(2.0 * num, num)
				});
				geometryGroup.Children.Add(new LineGeometry
				{
					StartPoint = new Point(num, 0.0),
					EndPoint = new Point(num, 2.0 * num)
				});
				shape = canvas.AddPath(pen, geometryGroup, pos);
				break;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Square:
				shape = canvas.AddPath(pen, CreateRectangleGeometry(new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), new Point(2.0 * num, 2.0 * num))), pos);
				break;
			case SymbolStyle.TriangleDown:
			{
				PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(num, 2.0 * num));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(2.0 * num, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
				shape = canvas.AddPolygon(pen, pointCollection, FillRule.Nonzero, pos);
				break;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.TriangleUp:
			{
				PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(num, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(2.0 * num, 2.0 * num));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 2.0 * num));
				shape = canvas.AddPolygon(pen, pointCollection, FillRule.Nonzero, pos);
				break;
			}
			}
			if (shape != null)
			{
				shape.Tag = dataPoint;
			}
			return shape;
		}

		public static double ValueToDouble(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			try
			{
				double num;
				if (value is double)
				{
					num = (double)value;
				}
				else
				{
					switch (axis.DataType)
					{
					case PlotAxisDataType.DateTime:
					case PlotAxisDataType.ElapsedTime:
					{
						num = ((value is DateTime dateTime) ? ((double)dateTime.Ticks) : ((!(value is string)) ? 0.0 : ((!DateTime.TryParse((string)value, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out var result)) ? 0.0 : ((double)result.Ticks))));
						break;
					}
					case PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel:
						num = axis.LabelValues.IndexOf((string)value);
						if (num < 0.0)
						{
							num = double.NaN;
						}
						break;
					default:
						num = Convert.ToDouble(value, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
						break;
					}
				}
				valid = !double.IsNaN(num);
				return num;
			}
			catch
			{
				valid = false;
				return double.NaN;
			}
		}

		public static object DoubleToValue(IGraphAxis axis, double value, out bool valid)
		{
			try
			{
				object obj = null;
				switch (axis.DataType)
				{
				case PlotAxisDataType.DateTime:
				case PlotAxisDataType.ElapsedTime:
					obj = (((long)value <= DateTime.MinValue.Ticks) ? ((object)new DateTime(DateTime.MinValue.Ticks)) : ((object)new DateTime((long)value)));
					break;
				case PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel:
				{
					int num = (int)Math.Floor(value + 0.5);
					if (num >= 0 && num < axis.LabelValues.Count)
					{
						obj = axis.LabelValues[num];
					}
					break;
				}
				default:
					obj = value;
					break;
				}
				valid = obj != null;
				return obj;
			}
			catch
			{
				valid = false;
				return null;
			}
		}

		public static object DoubleToDeltaValue(IGraphAxis axis, double value, out bool valid)
		{
			try
			{
				object obj = null;
				switch (axis.DataType)
				{
				case PlotAxisDataType.DateTime:
				case PlotAxisDataType.ElapsedTime:
					obj = new TimeSpan((long)value);
					break;
				case PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel:
					obj = string.Empty;
					break;
				default:
					obj = value;
					break;
				}
				valid = obj != null;
				return obj;
			}
			catch
			{
				valid = false;
				return null;
			}
		}

		public static double ValueToLogDouble(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			try
			{
				double num = double.NaN;
				num = ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
				if (!valid || double.IsNaN(num) || num <= 0.0)
				{
					valid = false;
					return double.NaN;
				}
				num = Math.Log10(num);
				valid = !double.IsNaN(num);
				return num;
			}
			catch
			{
				valid = false;
				return double.NaN;
			}
		}

		public static object LogDoubleToValue(IGraphAxis axis, double value, out bool valid)
		{
			try
			{
				value = Math.Pow(10.0, value);
				return DoubleToValue(axis, value, out valid);
			}
			catch
			{
				valid = false;
				return null;
			}
		}

		public static Size GetLabelExtent(string label, FontFamily typeface, double emSize, FontStyle? fontStyle = null, FontWeight? fontWeight = null, TextDecorationCollection textDecorations = null)
		{
			Size result = new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			Size textSize = WpfSilverlightSupport.GetTextSize(PlotCanvasBase.MakeTextBlock(label, typeface, emSize, Colors.Black, fontStyle, fontWeight, textDecorations));
			result.Height = Math.Max(result.Height, textSize.Height);
			result.Width = Math.Max(result.Width, textSize.Width);
			return result;
		}

		public static PlotCanvasBase MakeLabelVisual(string label, FontFamily typeface, double emSize, Color colour, Transform renderTransform)
		{
			return MakeLabelVisual(label, typeface, emSize, colour, renderTransform, null, double.PositiveInfinity, 0);
		}

		public static PlotCanvasBase MakeLabelVisual(string label, FontFamily typeface, double emSize, Color colour, Transform renderTransform, PlotCanvasBase useVisual, double maxTextWidth, int maxLineCount, FontStyle? fontStyle = null, FontWeight? fontWeight = null, TextDecorationCollection textDecorations = null)
		{
			if (useVisual == null)
			{
				useVisual = new PlotCanvasBase();
			}
			else
			{
				useVisual.Children.Clear();
			}
			TextBlock textBlock = PlotCanvasBase.MakeTextBlock(label, typeface, emSize, colour, fontStyle, fontWeight, textDecorations);
			if (maxLineCount == 1)
			{
				textBlock.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.NoWrap;
			}
			else
			{
				textBlock.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap;
			}
			textBlock.MaxWidth = maxTextWidth;
			textBlock.RenderTransform = renderTransform;
			useVisual.AddChild(textBlock, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			return useVisual;
		}

		public static void RegisterAxisAnnotator(IGraphAxisAnnotator annotator)
		{
			if (!RegisteredAxisAnnotators.ContainsValue(annotator.GetType()))
			{
				RegisteredAxisAnnotators.Add(annotator.AnnotatorType, annotator.GetType());
				_registeredAxisAnnotatorsSupportedDataTypes.Add(annotator.AnnotatorType, annotator.SupportedDataTypes);
			}
		}

		internal static Dictionary<string, Type> GetValidRegisteredAxisAnnotators(IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			Dictionary<string, Type> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, Type>();
			if (RegisteredAxisAnnotators.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string key in _registeredAxisAnnotatorsSupportedDataTypes.Keys)
				{
					if ((_registeredAxisAnnotatorsSupportedDataTypes[key] & axis.DataType) == axis.DataType)
					{
						dictionary.Add(key, RegisteredAxisAnnotators[key]);
					}
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		public static void WritePlotToXml(IPlot plot, XmlWriter writer)
		{
			WritePlotToXml(plot, writer, writeSettingsOnly: false);
		}

		public static void WritePlotSettingsToXml(IPlot plot, XmlWriter writer)
		{
			WritePlotToXml(plot, writer, writeSettingsOnly: true);
		}

		private static void WritePlotToXml(IPlot plot, XmlWriter writer, bool writeSettingsOnly)
		{
			if (plot == null || writer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			writer.WriteStartElement("plot");
			try
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString("layoutMode", plot.LayoutMode.ToString());
				WriteBrushToXml("backgroundBrush", plot.BackgroundColour, writer);
				foreach (IPlotFrame frame in plot.Frames)
				{
					writer.WriteStartElement("plotFrame");
					try
					{
						string value = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0},{1},{2},{3}", frame.FrameArea.Left, frame.FrameArea.Top, frame.FrameArea.Width, frame.FrameArea.Height);
						writer.WriteAttributeString("frameArea", value);
						IGraphLayout graphLayout = frame.GraphLayout;
						writer.WriteStartElement("graphScene");
						try
						{
							writer.WriteAttributeString("titleTypeface", graphLayout.TitleFontFamily.Source);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "titleFontStyle", graphLayout.TitleFontStyle);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "titleFontWeight", graphLayout.TitleFontWeight);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "titleTextDecorations", graphLayout.TitleTextDecorations);
							writer.WriteAttributeString("titlePointSize", graphLayout.TitlePointSize.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
							writer.WriteAttributeString("titleColour", graphLayout.TitleColour.ToString());
							writer.WriteAttributeString("titlePosition", graphLayout.TitlePosition.ToString());
							writer.WriteAttributeString("subtitleTypeface", graphLayout.SubtitleFontFamily.Source);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "subtitleFontStyle", graphLayout.SubtitleFontStyle);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "subtitleFontWeight", graphLayout.SubtitleFontWeight);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "subtitleTextDecorations", graphLayout.SubtitleTextDecorations);
							writer.WriteAttributeString("subtitlePointSize", graphLayout.SubtitlePointSize.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
							writer.WriteAttributeString("subtitleColour", graphLayout.SubtitleColour.ToString());
							writer.WriteAttributeString("legendTypeface", graphLayout.LegendFontFamily.Source);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "legendFontStyle", graphLayout.LegendFontStyle);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "legendFontWeight", graphLayout.LegendFontWeight);
							WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "legendTextDecorations", graphLayout.LegendTextDecorations);
							writer.WriteAttributeString("legendPointSize", graphLayout.LegendPointSize.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
							writer.WriteAttributeString("legendColour", graphLayout.LegendColour.ToString());
							writer.WriteAttributeString("legendPosition", graphLayout.LegendPosition.ToString());
							if (graphLayout.Graph.Is3D)
							{
								writer.WriteAttributeString("cameraDistance", (graphLayout.Graph as IGraphLayout3D).CameraDistance.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
								AxisAngleRotation3D sceneRotation = (graphLayout.Graph as IGraphLayout3D).SceneRotation;
								writer.WriteAttributeString("graphRotationQuaternion", new Quaternion(sceneRotation.Axis, sceneRotation.Angle).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
							}
							else
							{
								writer.WriteAttributeString("graphZoom", (graphLayout.Graph as IGraphLayout).GetZoom().ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
								writer.WriteAttributeString("graphHorizontalScrollOffset", (graphLayout.Graph as IScrollableGraph).HorizontalScrollBar.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
								writer.WriteAttributeString("graphVerticalScrollOffset", (graphLayout.Graph as IScrollableGraph).VerticalScrollBar.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
							}
							if ((graphLayout.LegendPosition | GraphElementPosition.Floating) == GraphElementPosition.Floating)
							{
								Point floatingLegendPosition = graphLayout.FloatingLegendPosition;
								value = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0},{1}", floatingLegendPosition.X, floatingLegendPosition.Y);
								writer.WriteAttributeString("floatingLegendPosition", value);
							}
							writer.WriteAttributeString("autoElementVisibility", graphLayout.AutoElementVisibility.ToString());
							writer.WriteAttributeString("scrollBarVisibility", graphLayout.ScrollBarVisibility.ToString());
							writer.WriteAttributeString("barChartLayout", graphLayout.BarLayout.ToString());
							WriteBrushToXml("backgroundBrush", graphLayout.BackgroundColour, writer);
							IGraph graph = graphLayout.Graph;
							writer.WriteStartElement("graph");
							try
							{
								writer.WriteAttributeString("title", graph.Title);
								writer.WriteAttributeString("subtitle", graph.Subtitle);
								writer.WriteAttributeString("axesStyle", graph.AxesStyle.ToString());
								IGraphAxes axes = graph.Axes;
								writer.WriteStartElement("axes");
								writer.WriteAttributeString("axesFontScaling", axes.AxesFontScaling.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
								try
								{
									foreach (IGraphAxis item in axes.AxisCollection)
									{
										writer.WriteStartElement("axis");
										try
										{
											writer.WriteAttributeString("axisId", item.Id.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("linkedAxisId", item.LinkedAxisId.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("title", item.Title);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("titleTypeface", item.TitleFontFamily.Source);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("titleFontSize", item.TitleFontSize.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "titleFontStyle", item.TitleFontStyle);
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "titleFontWeight", item.TitleFontWeight);
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "titleTextDecorations", item.TitleTextDecorations);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("labelTypeface", item.LabelFontFamily.Source);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("labelFontSize", item.LabelFontSize.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelFontStyle", item.LabelFontStyle);
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelFontWeight", item.LabelFontWeight);
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelTextDecorations", item.LabelTextDecorations);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("dataLabelFormat", item.DataLabelFormatString);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("displayUom", item.DisplayUom);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("scaling", item.Scaling.ToString());
											if (item.Scaling == PlotAxisScaling.None)
											{
												writer.WriteAttributeString("axisMinimum", item.DataRange.Minimum.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
												writer.WriteAttributeString("axisMaximum", item.DataRange.Maximum.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
											}
											if (graph.AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.RectXMergedY && item.Id == PlotAxisID.Y)
											{
												writer.WriteAttributeString("numberOfIntervals", (item as MergedGraphAxis).NumberOfIntervals.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
											}
											writer.WriteAttributeString("dataType", item.DataType.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("annotatorType", item.AxisAnnotator.AnnotatorType);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("initialZoomValue", string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0},{1}", item.InitialZoomRange.Minimum.Value, item.InitialZoomRange.Maximum.Value));
											WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(item, PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge, writer);
											WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(item, PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, writer);
											WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(item, PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines, writer);
											WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(item, PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks, writer);
											WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(item, PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines, writer);
											WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(item, PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks, writer);
											WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(item, PlotAxisVisualElements.Title, writer);
											if (item.LabelValues == null || item.LabelValues.Count <= 0)
											{
												continue;
											}
											writer.WriteStartElement("labelValues");
											try
											{
												foreach (string labelValue in item.LabelValues)
												{
													writer.WriteStartElement("label");
													try
													{
														writer.WriteAttributeString("value", labelValue);
													}
													finally
													{
														writer.WriteEndElement();
													}
												}
											}
											finally
											{
												writer.WriteEndElement();
											}
										}
										finally
										{
											writer.WriteEndElement();
										}
									}
								}
								finally
								{
									writer.WriteEndElement();
								}
								writer.WriteStartElement("dataSeriesCollection");
								try
								{
									foreach (IDataSeries item2 in graph.DataSeriesCollection)
									{
										writer.WriteStartElement("dataSeries");
										try
										{
											writer.WriteAttributeString("type", item2.DataSourceType.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("label", item2.Label);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("labelTypeface", item2.LabelFontFamily.Source);
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelFontStyle", item2.LabelFontStyle);
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelFontWeight", item2.LabelFontWeight);
											WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelTextDecorations", item2.LabelTextDecorations);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("lineStyle", item2.LineStyle.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("lineColour", item2.LineColour.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("lineWidth", item2.LineWidth.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
											writer.WriteAttributeString("symbolStyle", item2.SymbolStyle.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("symbolColour", item2.SymbolColour.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("symbolScale", item2.SymbolScale.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
											writer.WriteAttributeString("visible", item2.Visible.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("isUserInteractive", item2.IsUserInteractive.ToString());
											if (item2.AxisGroup != null)
											{
												if (item2.AxisGroup is string)
												{
													writer.WriteAttributeString("axisGroup", item2.AxisGroup.ToString());
												}
												else
												{
													writer.WriteAttributeString("axisGroup", item2.AxisGroup.GetHashCode().ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
												}
											}
											item2.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var zMapId);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("xMapId", xMapId.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("yMapId", yMapId.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("zMapId", zMapId.ToString());
											WriteBrushToXml("fillBrush", item2.FillBrush, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Fill, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Label, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Legend, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelEnd, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.LineLabelStart, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.Symbol, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoLine, writer);
											WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(item2, PlotSeriesVisualElements.HiLoTicks, writer);
											if (item2.ExtensionPoint != null && !item2.ExtensionPoint.IsEmptyDataPoint)
											{
												WriteDataPointToXml(writer, item2.ExtensionPoint, "extensionPoint");
											}
											if ((item2.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Surface || item2.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.SurfaceGrid) && item2 is ISurfaceDataSeries surfaceDataSeries)
											{
												writer.WriteStartElement("surfaceDataSeries");
												try
												{
													writer.WriteAttributeString("contouredFillAxis", surfaceDataSeries.ContouredFillAxis.ToString());
													if (surfaceDataSeries is DataSeries dataSeries)
													{
														dataSeries.GetFillMapping(out var xMap, out var yMap, out var minX, out var maxX, out var minY, out var maxY);
														writer.WriteAttributeString("fillXMap", xMap.ToString());
														writer.WriteAttributeString("fillYMap", yMap.ToString());
														writer.WriteAttributeString("fillXMin", ToXml(minX));
														writer.WriteAttributeString("fillXMax", ToXml(maxX));
														writer.WriteAttributeString("fillYMin", ToXml(minY));
														writer.WriteAttributeString("fillYMax", ToXml(maxY));
														if (minX != null)
														{
															writer.WriteAttributeString("xType", minX.GetType().ToString());
														}
														else if (maxX != null)
														{
															writer.WriteAttributeString("xType", maxX.GetType().ToString());
														}
														if (minY != null)
														{
															writer.WriteAttributeString("yType", minY.GetType().ToString());
														}
														else if (maxY != null)
														{
															writer.WriteAttributeString("yType", maxY.GetType().ToString());
														}
														writer.WriteAttributeString("contoursVisible", surfaceDataSeries.ContoursVisible.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
													}
												}
												finally
												{
													writer.WriteEndElement();
												}
											}
											if (item2.StyleKeys.Count > 0 && !writeSettingsOnly)
											{
												writer.WriteStartElement("styles");
												try
												{
													foreach (object styleKey in item2.StyleKeys)
													{
														IDataSeriesStyle style = item2.GetStyle(styleKey);
														if (style != null)
														{
															writer.WriteStartElement("style");
															try
															{
																writer.WriteAttributeString("key", styleKey.ToString());
																writer.WriteAttributeString("lineStyle", style.LineStyle.ToString());
																writer.WriteAttributeString("lineColour", style.LineColour.ToString());
																writer.WriteAttributeString("lineWidth", style.LineWidth.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
																writer.WriteAttributeString("symbolStyle", style.SymbolStyle.ToString());
																writer.WriteAttributeString("symbolColour", style.SymbolColour.ToString());
																writer.WriteAttributeString("symbolScale", style.SymbolScale.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
																WriteBrushToXml("fillBrush", style.FillBrush, writer);
															}
															finally
															{
																writer.WriteEndElement();
															}
														}
													}
												}
												finally
												{
													writer.WriteEndElement();
												}
											}
											if (item2.DataSource.Count <= 0 || writeSettingsOnly)
											{
												continue;
											}
											writer.WriteStartElement("dataPoints");
											try
											{
												writer.WriteAttributeString("dataType", item2.DataSource.DataSourceType.ToString());
												item2.DataSource.GetDataUom(out var xUom, out var yUom, out var zUom);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("xUom", xUom);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("yUom", yUom);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("zUom", zUom);
												foreach (IDataPoint item3 in item2.DataSource)
												{
													WriteDataPointToXml(writer, item3, "dataPoint");
												}
											}
											finally
											{
												writer.WriteEndElement();
											}
										}
										finally
										{
											writer.WriteEndElement();
										}
									}
								}
								finally
								{
									writer.WriteEndElement();
								}
								if (graph.ScooterCollection.Count > 0)
								{
									writer.WriteStartElement("scooterCollection");
									try
									{
										foreach (IGraphScooter item4 in graph.ScooterCollection)
										{
											writer.WriteStartElement("scooter");
											try
											{
												writer.WriteAttributeString("id", item4.Id);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("type", item4.ScooterType.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("position", ToXml(item4.ScooterPosition));
												writer.WriteAttributeString("dataType", item4.ScooterPosition.GetType().ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("linkedAxisId", item4.LinkedAxisId.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("label", item4.Label);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("labelTypeface", item4.LabelFontFamily.Source);
												WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelFontStyle", item4.LabelFontStyle);
												WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelFontWeight", item4.LabelFontWeight);
												WriteXmlAttribute(writer, "labelTextDecorations", item4.LabelTextDecorations);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("labelBackground", item4.LabelBackground.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("labelColour", item4.LabelColour.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("lineStyle", item4.LineStyle.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("lineColour", item4.LineColour.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("lineWidth", item4.LineWidth.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
												writer.WriteAttributeString("visible", item4.Visible.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("showTicks", item4.ShowIntersectTicks.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("showValues", item4.ShowIntersectValues.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("userCanDelete", item4.UserCanDelete.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("userCanFormat", item4.UserCanFormat.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("userMoveable", item4.UserMoveable.ToString());
											}
											finally
											{
												writer.WriteEndElement();
											}
										}
									}
									finally
									{
										writer.WriteEndElement();
									}
								}
								if (graph.SliceCollection.Count > 0)
								{
									writer.WriteStartElement("sliceCollection");
									try
									{
										foreach (IGraphSlice item5 in graph.SliceCollection)
										{
											writer.WriteStartElement("slice");
											try
											{
												writer.WriteAttributeString("id", item5.Id);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("dataType", item5.StartValue.GetType().ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("startPosition", ToXml(item5.StartValue));
												writer.WriteAttributeString("endPosition", ToXml(item5.EndValue));
												writer.WriteAttributeString("linkedAxisId", item5.LinkedAxisId.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("linkedDataSeries", item5.LinkedDataSeries.Label);
												writer.WriteAttributeString("lineStyle", item5.LineStyle.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("lineColour", item5.LineColour.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("lineWidth", item5.LineWidth.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
												writer.WriteAttributeString("visible", item5.IsVisible.ToString());
												WriteBrushToXml("fillBrush", item5.Fill, writer);
											}
											finally
											{
												writer.WriteEndElement();
											}
										}
									}
									finally
									{
										writer.WriteEndElement();
									}
								}
								if (graph.AnnotationCollection.Count <= 0)
								{
									continue;
								}
								writer.WriteStartElement("annotationCollection");
								try
								{
									foreach (IGraphAnnotation item6 in graph.AnnotationCollection)
									{
										writer.WriteStartElement("annotation");
										try
										{
											writer.WriteAttributeString("id", item6.Id);
											writer.WriteAttributeString("type", item6.PositionType.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("contentAnchor", item6.ContentAnchor.ToString());
											RAnnotationPositionInfo positionInfo = item6.PositionInfo;
											switch (item6.PositionType)
											{
											case AnnotationPositionType.Relative:
												writer.WriteAttributeString("relativePos", positionInfo.RelativePos.ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("relativeTo", item6.PositionInfo.RelativeTo.ToString());
												break;
											case AnnotationPositionType.Data:
												writer.WriteAttributeString("xDataType", positionInfo.XValue.GetType().ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("xValue", ToXml(positionInfo.XValue));
												writer.WriteAttributeString("yDataType", positionInfo.YValue.GetType().ToString());
												writer.WriteAttributeString("yValue", ToXml(positionInfo.YValue));
												if (positionInfo.DataSeries == null)
												{
													writer.WriteAttributeString("xMapping", positionInfo.XMapping.ToString());
													writer.WriteAttributeString("yMapping", positionInfo.YMapping.ToString());
												}
												else
												{
													writer.WriteAttributeString("dataSeries", positionInfo.DataSeries.Label);
												}
												break;
											}
											writer.WriteAttributeString("visible", item6.Visible.ToString());
											writer.WriteAttributeString("zOrder", item6.ZOrder.ToString());
											if (item6 is GraphTextAnnotation)
											{
												GraphTextAnnotation graphTextAnnotation = item6 as GraphTextAnnotation;
												writer.WriteStartElement("GraphTextAnnotation");
												writer.WriteString(graphTextAnnotation.Save());
												writer.WriteEndElement();
											}
											else
											{
												writer.WriteStartElement("content");
												try
												{
													XamlWriter.Save(item6, writer);
												}
												finally
												{
													writer.WriteEndElement();
												}
											}
											writer.WriteStartElement("contentDrawing");
											try
											{
											}
											finally
											{
												writer.WriteEndElement();
											}
										}
										finally
										{
											writer.WriteEndElement();
										}
									}
								}
								finally
								{
									writer.WriteEndElement();
								}
							}
							finally
							{
								writer.WriteEndElement();
							}
						}
						finally
						{
							writer.WriteEndElement();
						}
					}
					finally
					{
						writer.WriteEndElement();
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				writer.WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		private static void WriteDataPointToXml(XmlWriter writer, IDataPoint dataPoint, string elementName)
		{
			writer.WriteStartElement(elementName);
			try
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString("x", ToXml(dataPoint.X));
				writer.WriteAttributeString("y", ToXml(dataPoint.Y));
				writer.WriteAttributeString("z", ToXml(dataPoint.Z));
				writer.WriteAttributeString("label", dataPoint.Label);
				writer.WriteAttributeString("newSegment", dataPoint.NewSegment.ToString());
				if (dataPoint.X != null)
				{
					writer.WriteAttributeString("xType", dataPoint.X.GetType().ToString());
				}
				if (dataPoint.Y != null)
				{
					writer.WriteAttributeString("yType", dataPoint.Y.GetType().ToString());
				}
				if (dataPoint.Z != null)
				{
					writer.WriteAttributeString("zType", dataPoint.Z.GetType().ToString());
				}
				if (dataPoint.StyleKey != null)
				{
					writer.WriteAttributeString("styleKey", dataPoint.StyleKey.ToString());
					writer.WriteAttributeString("hideSymbol", dataPoint.HideSymbol.ToString());
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				writer.WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		private static void WriteBrushToXml(string elementName, Brush brush, XmlWriter writer)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(elementName) || brush == null || writer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			writer.WriteStartElement(elementName);
			try
			{
				XmlSupport.WriteBrush(brush, writer);
			}
			finally
			{
				writer.WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		private static void WriteAxisVisualElementToXml(IGraphAxis axis, PlotAxisVisualElements element, XmlWriter writer)
		{
			if (axis == null || writer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			writer.WriteStartElement("axisVisualElement");
			try
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString("element", element.ToString());
				writer.WriteAttributeString("visibility", axis.GetVisualElementVisibility(element).ToString());
				writer.WriteAttributeString("colour", axis.GetVisualElementColour(element).ToString());
			}
			finally
			{
				writer.WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		private static void WriteDataSeriesVisualElementToXml(IDataSeries dataSeries, PlotSeriesVisualElements element, XmlWriter writer)
		{
			if (dataSeries == null || writer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			writer.WriteStartElement("dataSeriesVisualElement");
			try
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString("element", element.ToString());
				writer.WriteAttributeString("visibility", dataSeries.GetVisualElementVisibility(element).ToString());
			}
			finally
			{
				writer.WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		public static void ReadFromXml(IPlot plot, XElement xml)
		{
			ReadFromXml(plot, xml, readSettingsOnly: false);
		}

		public static void ReadSettingsFromXml(IPlot plot, XElement xml)
		{
			ReadFromXml(plot, xml, readSettingsOnly: true);
		}

		private static void ReadFromXml(IPlot plot, XElement xml, bool readSettingsOnly)
		{
			if (plot == null || xml == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			while (xml != null && xml.Name != "plot")
			{
				xml = xml.Element("plot");
			}
			if (xml == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!readSettingsOnly)
			{
				plot.ClearFrames();
			}
			XElement xElement = xml.Element("backgroundBrush");
			if (xElement != null)
			{
				plot.BackgroundColour = ReadBrushFromXml(xElement);
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XElement item in xml.Elements("plotFrame"))
			{
				string text = item.AttributeValue("frameArea");
				Rect frameArea;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					string[] array = text.Split(',');
					frameArea = new Rect(double.Parse(array[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), double.Parse(array[1], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), double.Parse(array[2], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), double.Parse(array[3], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
				}
				else
				{
					frameArea = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0);
				}
				XElement xElement2 = item.Element("graphScene");
				if (xElement2 == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				XElement xElement3 = xElement2.Element("graph");
				if (xElement3 == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				IGraph graph;
				if (!readSettingsOnly)
				{
					graph = new Graph();
				}
				else
				{
					if (num >= plot.Frames.Count)
					{
						break;
					}
					graph = plot.Frames[num].GraphLayout.Graph;
					num++;
				}
				IGraphLayout graphLayout = graph.GraphLayout;
				graphLayout.TitleFontFamily = CreateTypeface(xElement2.AttributeValue((XName)"titleTypeface", (string)null), graphLayout.TitleFontFamily);
				graphLayout.TitleFontStyle = CreateFontStyle(xElement2.AttributeValue("titleFontStyle"));
				graphLayout.TitleFontWeight = CreateFontWeight(xElement2.AttributeValue("titleFontWeight"));
				graphLayout.TitleTextDecorations = CreateTextDecorations(xElement2.AttributeValue("titleTextDecorations"));
				graphLayout.TitlePointSize = double.Parse(xElement2.AttributeValue("titlePointSize"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				graphLayout.TitleColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(xElement2.AttributeValue("titleColour"));
				graphLayout.TitlePosition = (GraphTitlePosition)Enum.Parse(typeof(GraphTitlePosition), xElement2.AttributeValue("titlePosition"), ignoreCase: true);
				graphLayout.SubtitleFontFamily = CreateTypeface(xElement2.AttributeValue((XName)"subtitleTypeface", (string)null), graphLayout.TitleFontFamily);
				graphLayout.SubtitleFontStyle = CreateFontStyle(xElement2.AttributeValue("subtitleFontStyle"));
				graphLayout.SubtitleFontWeight = CreateFontWeight(xElement2.AttributeValue("subtitleFontWeight"));
				graphLayout.SubtitleTextDecorations = CreateTextDecorations(xElement2.AttributeValue("subtitleTextDecorations"));
				graphLayout.SubtitlePointSize = double.Parse(xElement2.AttributeValue("subtitlePointSize"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				graphLayout.SubtitleColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(xElement2.AttributeValue("subtitleColour"));
				graphLayout.LegendFontFamily = CreateTypeface(xElement2.AttributeValue("legendTypeface"), graphLayout.LegendFontFamily);
				graphLayout.LegendFontStyle = CreateFontStyle(xElement2.AttributeValue("legendFontStyle"));
				graphLayout.LegendFontWeight = CreateFontWeight(xElement2.AttributeValue("legendFontWeight"));
				graphLayout.LegendTextDecorations = CreateTextDecorations(xElement2.AttributeValue("legendTextDecorations"));
				graphLayout.LegendPointSize = double.Parse(xElement2.AttributeValue("legendPointSize"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				graphLayout.LegendColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(xElement2.AttributeValue("legendColour"));
				graphLayout.LegendPosition = (GraphElementPosition)Enum.Parse(typeof(GraphElementPosition), xElement2.AttributeValue("legendPosition"), ignoreCase: true);
				if ((graphLayout.LegendPosition | GraphElementPosition.Floating) == GraphElementPosition.Floating)
				{
					text = item.AttributeValue("floatingLegendPosition");
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						string[] array2 = text.Split(',');
						graphLayout.FloatingLegendPosition = new Point(double.Parse(array2[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), double.Parse(array2[1], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
					}
				}
				graphLayout.AutoElementVisibility = bool.Parse(xElement2.AttributeValue("autoElementVisibility"));
				graphLayout.ScrollBarVisibility = (ScrollBarVisibility)Enum.Parse(typeof(ScrollBarVisibility), xElement2.AttributeValue("scrollBarVisibility"), ignoreCase: true);
				graphLayout.BarLayout = (BarChartLayout)Enum.Parse(typeof(BarChartLayout), xElement2.AttributeValue((XName)"barChartLayout", "Overlay"), ignoreCase: true);
				xElement = xElement2.Element("backgroundBrush");
				if (xElement != null)
				{
					graphLayout.BackgroundColour = ReadBrushFromXml(xElement);
				}
				graph.Title = xElement3.AttributeValue("title");
				graph.Subtitle = xElement3.AttributeValue("subtitle");
				if (!readSettingsOnly)
				{
					graph.AxesStyle = (PlotAxesStyle)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxesStyle), xElement3.AttributeValue("axesStyle"), ignoreCase: true);
				}
				if (xElement2.Attribute("cameraDistance") != null && double.TryParse(xElement2.AttributeValue("cameraDistance"), NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result))
				{
					(graph as IGraphLayout3D).CameraDistance = result;
				}
				Rect zoom = Rect.Empty;
				if (xElement2.Attribute("graphZoom") != null)
				{
					try
					{
						zoom = Rect.Parse(xElement2.AttributeValue("graphZoom"));
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						zoom = Rect.Empty;
					}
				}
				double? num2 = null;
				double? num3 = null;
				if (xElement2.Attribute("graphHorizontalScrollOffset") != null && double.TryParse(xElement2.AttributeValue("graphHorizontalScrollOffset"), NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result2))
				{
					num2 = result2;
				}
				if (xElement2.Attribute("graphVerticalScrollOffset") != null && double.TryParse(xElement2.AttributeValue("graphVerticalScrollOffset"), NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result3))
				{
					num3 = result3;
				}
				if (xElement2.Attribute("graphRotationQuaternion") != null)
				{
					Quaternion delta = Quaternion.Parse(xElement2.AttributeValue("graphRotationQuaternion"));
					(graph as Graph).RotateScene(delta);
				}
				XElement xElement4 = xElement3.Element("axes");
				if (xElement4 != null)
				{
					double result4 = 0.0;
					if (!double.TryParse(xElement4.AttributeValue("axesFontScaling"), NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result4))
					{
						result4 = double.NaN;
					}
					foreach (XElement item2 in xElement4.Elements("axis"))
					{

						IGraphAxis axis = graph.Axes.GetAxis(axisId);
						if (axis == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						axis.LinkedAxisId = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), item2.AttributeValue("linkedAxisId"), ignoreCase: true);
						axis.Title = item2.AttributeValue("title");
						axis.TitleFontFamily = CreateTypeface(item2.AttributeValue("titleTypeface"), axis.TitleFontFamily);
						axis.TitleFontSize = CreateFontSize(item2.AttributeValue("titleFontSize"), 12.0);
						axis.TitleFontStyle = CreateFontStyle(item2.AttributeValue("titleFontStyle"));
						axis.TitleFontWeight = CreateFontWeight(item2.AttributeValue("titleFontWeight"));

						axis.DataLabelFormatString = item2.AttributeValue("dataLabelFormat");
						axis.DisplayUom = item2.AttributeValue("displayUom");
						axis.Scaling = (PlotAxisScaling)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisScaling), item2.AttributeValue("scaling"), ignoreCase: true);
						if (axis.Scaling == PlotAxisScaling.None)
						{

							double result6 = 0.0;
							if (double.TryParse(item2.AttributeValue((XName)"axisMinimum", "0"), NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result5) && double.TryParse(item2.AttributeValue((XName)"axisMaximum", "0"), NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result6))
							{
								axis.DataRange.SetAbsolute(result5, result6, validate: true);
							}

							(axis as MergedGraphAxis).NumberOfIntervals = Convert.ToInt16(item2.AttributeValue("numberOfIntervals"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						}
						text = item2.AttributeValue("annotatorType");
						if (RegisteredAxisAnnotators.ContainsKey(text))
						{
							axis.AxisAnnotator = Activator.CreateInstance(RegisteredAxisAnnotators[text]) as IGraphAxisAnnotator;
						}
						else
						{
							Dictionary<string, Type> validRegisteredAxisAnnotators = GetValidRegisteredAxisAnnotators(axis);
							if (validRegisteredAxisAnnotators.Count > 0)
							{
								string[] array3 = new string[validRegisteredAxisAnnotators.Count];
								validRegisteredAxisAnnotators.Keys.CopyTo(array3, 0);

							}
						}
						axis.DataType = (PlotAxisDataType)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisDataType), item2.AttributeValue("dataType"), ignoreCase: true);
						text = item2.AttributeValue("initialZoomRange");
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							string[] array4 = text.Split(',');
							if (array4.Length == 2)
							{
								axis.InitialZoomRange.SetAbsolute(XmlConvert.ToDouble(array4[0]), XmlConvert.ToDouble(array4[1]), validate: true);
							}
						}
						foreach (XElement item3 in item2.Elements("axisVisualElement"))
						{
							PlotAxisVisualElements elements = (PlotAxisVisualElements)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisVisualElements), item3.AttributeValue("element"), ignoreCase: true);
							axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, bool.Parse(item3.AttributeValue("visibility")));
							axis.SetVisualElementColour(elements, XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item3.AttributeValue("colour")));
						}
						if (readSettingsOnly)
						{
							continue;
						}
						XElement xElement5 = item2.Element("labelValues");
						if (xElement5 == null)
						{

					}
					if (!zoom.IsEmpty)

					}

						_ = (graphLayout.Graph as IScrollableGraph).VerticalScrollBar;
						(graphLayout.Graph as IScrollableGraph).ScrollTo(num2.Value, num3.Value);

				XElement xElement6 = xElement3.Element("dataSeriesCollection");
				int num4 = 0;

						dataSeries.LabelFontWeight = CreateFontWeight(item5.AttributeValue("labelFontWeight"));
						dataSeries.LabelTextDecorations = CreateTextDecorations(item5.AttributeValue("labelTextDecorations"));
						dataSeries.LineStyle = (LineStyle)Enum.Parse(typeof(LineStyle), item5.AttributeValue((XName)"lineStyle", ""), ignoreCase: true);
						dataSeries.LineColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item5.AttributeValue("lineColour"));
						dataSeries.LineWidth = double.Parse(item5.AttributeValue("lineWidth"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						dataSeries.SymbolStyle = (SymbolStyle)Enum.Parse(typeof(SymbolStyle), item5.AttributeValue("symbolStyle"), ignoreCase: true);
						dataSeries.SymbolColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item5.AttributeValue("symbolColour"));
						dataSeries.SymbolScale = double.Parse(item5.AttributeValue("symbolScale"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						dataSeries.Visible = bool.Parse(item5.AttributeValue("visible"));
						if (!readSettingsOnly)

							dataSeries.IsUserInteractive = bool.Parse(item5.AttributeValue("isUserInteractive"));
							string text2 = item5.AttributeValue("axisGroup");

								if (int.TryParse(text2, out var result7))

									dataSeries.AxisGroup = result7;
								}
								else
								{
									dataSeries.AxisGroup = text2;
								}
							}

							PlotAxisID yMapId = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), item5.AttributeValue("yMapId"), ignoreCase: true);
							PlotAxisID zMapId = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), item5.AttributeValue("zMapId"), ignoreCase: true);
							dataSeries.SetAxisMapping(xMapId, yMapId, zMapId);
						}
						xElement = item5.Element("fillBrush");
						if (xElement != null)
						{
							dataSeries.FillBrush = ReadBrushFromXml(xElement);
