							PlotSeriesVisualElements elements2 = (PlotSeriesVisualElements)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotSeriesVisualElements), item6.AttributeValue((XName)"element", ""), ignoreCase: true);
							dataSeries.SetVisualElementVisibility(elements2, bool.Parse(item6.AttributeValue("visibility")));
						}
						if (readSettingsOnly)
						{
							continue;
						}
						XElement xElement7 = item5.Element("extensionPoint");
						if (xElement7 != null)
						{
							dataSeries.ExtensionPoint = ReadDataPointFromXml(xElement7);
						}
						XElement xElement8 = item5.Element("surfaceDataSeries");
						if (xElement8 != null && (dataSeries.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Surface || dataSeries.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.SurfaceGrid) && dataSeries is ISurfaceDataSeries surfaceDataSeries)
						{
							surfaceDataSeries.ContouredFillAxis = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), xElement8.AttributeValue((XName)"contouredFillAxis", "None"), ignoreCase: true);
							PlotAxisID xMap = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), xElement8.AttributeValue((XName)"fillXMap", "X"), ignoreCase: true);
							PlotAxisID yMap = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), xElement8.AttributeValue((XName)"fillYMap", "Z"), ignoreCase: true);
							string value = xElement8.AttributeValue("fillXMin");
							string value2 = xElement8.AttributeValue("fillXMax");
							string value3 = xElement8.AttributeValue("fillYMin");
							string value4 = xElement8.AttributeValue("fillYMax");
							string type = xElement8.AttributeValue("xType");
							string type2 = xElement8.AttributeValue("yType");
							bool result8 = false;
							if (bool.TryParse(xElement8.AttributeValue("contoursVisible"), out result8))
							{
								surfaceDataSeries.ContoursVisible = result8;
							}
							surfaceDataSeries.FillMapping(xMap, yMap, GetInstance(type, value), GetInstance(type, value2), GetInstance(type2, value3), GetInstance(type2, value4));
						}
						XElement xElement9 = item5.Element("styles");
						if (xElement9 != null)
						{
							foreach (XElement item7 in xElement9.Elements("style"))
							{
								string key = item7.AttributeValue("key");
								IDataSeriesStyle dataSeriesStyle = dataSeries.NewStyle(key);
								dataSeriesStyle.LineStyle = (LineStyle)Enum.Parse(typeof(LineStyle), item7.AttributeValue((XName)"lineStyle", ""), ignoreCase: true);
								dataSeriesStyle.LineColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item7.AttributeValue("lineColour"));
								dataSeriesStyle.LineWidth = double.Parse(item7.AttributeValue("lineWidth"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
								dataSeriesStyle.SymbolStyle = (SymbolStyle)Enum.Parse(typeof(SymbolStyle), item7.AttributeValue((XName)"symbolStyle", ""), ignoreCase: true);
								dataSeriesStyle.SymbolColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item7.AttributeValue("symbolColour"));
								dataSeriesStyle.SymbolScale = double.Parse(item7.AttributeValue("symbolScale"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
								xElement = item7.Element("fillBrush");
								if (xElement != null)
								{
									dataSeriesStyle.FillBrush = ReadBrushFromXml(xElement);
								}
							}
						}
						XElement xElement10 = item5.Element("dataPoints");
						if (xElement10 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						string xUom = xElement10.AttributeValue("xUom");
						string yUom = xElement10.AttributeValue("yUom");
						string zUom = xElement10.AttributeValue("zUom");
						plotDataStore.SetDataUom(xUom, yUom, zUom);
						foreach (XElement item8 in xElement10.Elements("dataPoint"))
						{
							plotDataStore.Add(ReadDataPointFromXml(item8));
						}
					}
				}
				XElement xElement11 = xElement3.Element("scooterCollection");
				int num5 = 0;
				if (xElement11 != null)
				{
					foreach (XElement item9 in xElement11.Elements("scooter"))
					{
						IGraphScooter graphScooter;
						if (!readSettingsOnly)
						{
							graphScooter = graph.NewScooter();
						}
						else

						string value5 = item9.AttributeValue("position");
						string type3 = item9.AttributeValue("dataType");
						graphScooter.ScooterPosition = GetInstance(type3, value5);
						graphScooter.LinkedAxisId = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), item9.AttributeValue("linkedAxisId"), ignoreCase: true);
						graphScooter.Label = item9.AttributeValue("label");
						graphScooter.LabelFontFamily = new FontFamily(item9.AttributeValue("labelTypeface"));
						graphScooter.LabelFontStyle = CreateFontStyle(item9.AttributeValue("labelFontStyle"));
						graphScooter.LabelFontWeight = CreateFontWeight(item9.AttributeValue("labelFontWeight"));
						graphScooter.LabelTextDecorations = CreateTextDecorations(item9.AttributeValue("labelTextDecorations"));
						graphScooter.LabelBackground = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item9.AttributeValue("labelBackground"));
						graphScooter.LabelColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item9.AttributeValue("labelColour"));
						graphScooter.LineStyle = (LineStyle)Enum.Parse(typeof(LineStyle), item9.AttributeValue("lineStyle"), ignoreCase: true);
						graphScooter.LineColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item9.AttributeValue("lineColour"));
						graphScooter.LineWidth = double.Parse(item9.AttributeValue("lineWidth"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						graphScooter.Visible = bool.Parse(item9.AttributeValue("visible"));
						graphScooter.ShowIntersectTicks = bool.Parse(item9.AttributeValue("showTicks"));
						graphScooter.ShowIntersectValues = bool.Parse(item9.AttributeValue("showValues"));
						graphScooter.UserCanDelete = bool.Parse(item9.AttributeValue("userCanDelete"));
						graphScooter.UserCanFormat = bool.Parse(item9.AttributeValue("userCanFormat"));
						graphScooter.UserMoveable = bool.Parse(item9.AttributeValue("userMoveable"));
					}
				}
				XElement xElement12 = xElement3.Element("sliceCollection");
				num5 = 0;
				if (xElement12 != null)
				{
					foreach (XElement item10 in xElement12.Elements("slice"))
					{
						IGraphSlice graphSlice;
						if (!readSettingsOnly)
						{
							graphSlice = graph.NewSlice();
						}
						else
						{
							if (num5 >= graph.SliceCollection.Count)
							{
								break;
							}
							graphSlice = graph.SliceCollection[num5];
							num5++;
						}
						graphSlice.Id = item10.AttributeValue("id");
						string type4 = item10.AttributeValue("dataType");
						string value6 = item10.AttributeValue("startPosition");
						graphSlice.StartValue = GetInstance(type4, value6);
						value6 = item10.AttributeValue("endPosition");
						graphSlice.EndValue = GetInstance(type4, value6);
						graphSlice.LinkedAxisId = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), item10.AttributeValue("linkedAxisId"), ignoreCase: true);
						string label = item10.AttributeValue("linkedDataSeries");
						graphSlice.LinkedDataSeries = GetDataSeriesByLabel(graph.DataSeriesCollection, label);
						graphSlice.LineStyle = (LineStyle)Enum.Parse(typeof(LineStyle), item10.AttributeValue("lineStyle"), ignoreCase: true);
						graphSlice.LineColour = XmlSupport.ColourFromXml(item10.AttributeValue("lineColour"));
						graphSlice.LineWidth = double.Parse(item10.AttributeValue("lineWidth"), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						graphSlice.IsVisible = bool.Parse(item10.AttributeValue("visible"));
						xElement = item10.Element("fillBrush");
						if (xElement != null)
						{
							graphSlice.Fill = ReadBrushFromXml(xElement);
						}
					}
				}
				if (!readSettingsOnly)
				{
					XElement xElement13 = xElement3.Element("annotationCollection");
					if (xElement13 != null)
					{
						foreach (XElement item11 in xElement13.Elements("annotation"))
						{
							item11.AttributeValue("Id");
							AnnotationPositionType annotationPositionType = (AnnotationPositionType)Enum.Parse(typeof(AnnotationPositionType), item11.AttributeValue("type"), ignoreCase: true);
							_ = (GraphElementPosition)Enum.Parse(typeof(GraphElementPosition), item11.AttributeValue("contentAnchor"), ignoreCase: true);
							bool.Parse(item11.AttributeValue("visible"));
							_ = (AnnotationZOrder)Enum.Parse(typeof(AnnotationZOrder), item11.AttributeValue("zOrder"), ignoreCase: true);
							Point position = default(Point);
							AnnotationRelativeTo type5 = AnnotationRelativeTo.Axes;
							object xValue = null;
							object yValue = null;
							PlotAxisID xAxisMapping = PlotAxisID.None;
							PlotAxisID yAxisMapping = PlotAxisID.None;
							IDataSeries dataSeries2 = null;
							switch (annotationPositionType)
							{
							case AnnotationPositionType.Relative:
							{
								text = item11.AttributeValue("relativePos");
								string[] array5 = text.Split(',');
								position = new Point(XmlConvert.ToDouble(array5[0]), XmlConvert.ToDouble(array5[1]));
								type5 = (AnnotationRelativeTo)Enum.Parse(typeof(AnnotationRelativeTo), item11.AttributeValue("relativeTo"), ignoreCase: true);
								break;
							}
							case AnnotationPositionType.Data:
							{
								string type6 = item11.AttributeValue("xDataType");
								string value7 = item11.AttributeValue("xValue");
								xValue = GetInstance(type6, value7);
								string type7 = item11.AttributeValue("yDataType");
								value7 = item11.AttributeValue("yValue");
								yValue = GetInstance(type7, value7);
								string text3 = item11.AttributeValue("dataSeries");
								if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
								{
									xAxisMapping = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), item11.AttributeValue("xMapping"), ignoreCase: true);
									yAxisMapping = (PlotAxisID)Enum.Parse(typeof(PlotAxisID), item11.AttributeValue("yMapping"), ignoreCase: true);
								}
								else
								{
									dataSeries2 = GetDataSeriesByLabel(graph.DataSeriesCollection, text3);
								}
								break;
							}
							}
							XElement xElement14 = item11.Element("content");
							if (xElement14 != null)
							{
								IGraphAnnotation graphAnnotation = (IGraphAnnotation)ReadXaml(xElement14, readInnerXml: true);
								if (graphAnnotation == null)
								{
									continue;
								}
								graphAnnotation.Initialise(graph);
								item11.Element("contentDrawing");
								switch (annotationPositionType)
								{
								case AnnotationPositionType.Relative:
									graphAnnotation.SetPosition(position, type5);
									break;
								case AnnotationPositionType.Data:
									if (dataSeries2 == null)
									{
										graphAnnotation.SetPosition(xValue, yValue, xAxisMapping, yAxisMapping);
									}
									else
									{
										graphAnnotation.SetPosition(xValue, yValue, dataSeries2);
									}
									break;
								}
								graph.AnnotationCollection.Add(graphAnnotation);
								continue;
							}
							XElement xElement15 = item11.Element("GraphTextAnnotation");
							if (xElement15 == null)
							{
								continue;
							}
							IGraphAnnotation graphAnnotation2 = GraphTextAnnotation.Parse(xElement15.Value);
							graphAnnotation2.Initialise(graph);
							item11.Element("contentDrawing");
							switch (annotationPositionType)
							{
							case AnnotationPositionType.Relative:
								graphAnnotation2.SetPosition(position, type5);
								break;
							case AnnotationPositionType.Data:
								if (dataSeries2 == null)
								{
									graphAnnotation2.SetPosition(xValue, yValue, xAxisMapping, yAxisMapping);
								}
								else
								{
									graphAnnotation2.SetPosition(xValue, yValue, dataSeries2);
								}
								break;
							}
							graph.AnnotationCollection.Add(graphAnnotation2);
						}
					}
				}
				if (!readSettingsOnly)
				{
					plot.CreateFrame(frameArea, graphLayout);
				}
			}
		}

		private static IDataPoint ReadDataPointFromXml(XElement xDataPoint)
		{
			bool hideSymbol = false;
			string value = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("x");
			string value2 = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("y");
			string value3 = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("z");
			string label = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("label");
			bool newSegment = bool.Parse(xDataPoint.AttributeValue("newSegment"));
			string type = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("xType");
			string type2 = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("yType");
			string type3 = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("zType");
			string text = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("styleKey");
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return new RDataPoint
				{
					X = GetInstance(type, value),
					Y = GetInstance(type2, value2),
					Z = GetInstance(type3, value3),
					Label = label,
					NewSegment = newSegment,
					StyleKey = text,
					HideSymbol = hideSymbol
				};
			}
			string value4 = xDataPoint.AttributeValue("hideSymbol");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value4))
			{
				hideSymbol = bool.Parse(value4);
			}
			return new RDataPoint
			{
				X = GetInstance(type, value),
				Y = GetInstance(type2, value2),
				Z = GetInstance(type3, value3),
				Label = label,
				NewSegment = newSegment,
				StyleKey = text,
				HideSymbol = hideSymbol
			};
		}

		private static object ReadXaml(XElement node, bool readInnerXml)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (node != null)
			{
				try
				{
					using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
					XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
					xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
					using (XmlWriter writer = XmlWriter.Create(memoryStream, xmlWriterSettings))
					{
						if (readInnerXml)
						{
							if (node.FirstNode is XElement xElement)
							{
								xElement.WriteTo(writer);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							node.WriteTo(writer);
						}
					}
					memoryStream.Position = 0L;
					result = XamlReader.Load(memoryStream);
				}
				catch
				{
					result = null;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static IDataSeries GetDataSeriesByLabel(IDataSeriesCollection dataSeriesCollection, string label)
		{
			foreach (IDataSeries item in dataSeriesCollection)
			{
				if (string.Compare(item.Label, label, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static FontFamily CreateTypeface(string typefaceName, FontFamily defaultTypeface)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(typefaceName))
			{
				return defaultTypeface;
			}
			return new FontFamily(typefaceName);
		}

		private static double CreateFontSize(string fontSizeText, double defaultSize)
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fontSizeText) && double.TryParse(fontSizeText, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result) && result > 0.0)
			{
				return result;
			}
			return defaultSize;
		}

		internal static FontStyle? CreateFontStyle(string fontStyle)
		{
			try
			{
				return (FontStyle)new FontStyleConverter().ConvertFromString(fontStyle);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal static FontWeight? CreateFontWeight(string fontWeight)
		{
			try
			{
				return (FontWeight)new FontWeightConverter().ConvertFromString(fontWeight);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal static TextDecorationCollection CreateTextDecorations(string textDecorations)
		{
			try
			{
				return TextDecorationCollectionConverter.ConvertFromString(textDecorations);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal static string ConvertToString(FontStyle? fontStyle)
		{
			if (fontStyle.HasValue)
			{
				return new FontStyleConverter().ConvertToString(fontStyle.Value);
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static string ConvertToString(FontWeight? fontWeight)
		{
			if (fontWeight.HasValue)
			{
				return new FontWeightConverter().ConvertToString(fontWeight.Value);
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static string ConvertToString(TextDecorationCollection textDecorations)
		{
			if (textDecorations != null)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				foreach (TextDecoration textDecoration in textDecorations)
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(',');
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(textDecoration.Location.ToString());
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static void WriteXmlAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string attrName, FontStyle? fontStyle)
		{
			string value = ConvertToString(fontStyle);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(attrName, value);
			}
		}

		private static void WriteXmlAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string attrName, FontWeight? fontWeight)
		{
			string value = ConvertToString(fontWeight);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(attrName, value);
			}
		}

		private static void WriteXmlAttribute(XmlWriter writer, string attrName, TextDecorationCollection textDecorations)
		{
			string value = ConvertToString(textDecorations);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				writer.WriteAttributeString(attrName, value);
			}
		}

		private static Brush ReadBrushFromXml(XElement node)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (node.FirstNode is XElement element)
			{
				return XmlSupport.ReadBrush(element);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static object GetInstance(string type, string value)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(type) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				switch (type)
				{
				case "System.Boolean":
					return XmlConvert.ToBoolean(value);
				case "System.Byte":
					return XmlConvert.ToByte(value);
				case "System.Char":
					return XmlConvert.ToChar(value);
				case "System.DateTime":
					return XmlConvert.ToDateTime(value, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Utc);
				case "System.Decimal":
					return XmlConvert.ToDecimal(value);
				case "System.Double":
					return XmlConvert.ToDouble(value);
				case "System.Guid":
					return XmlConvert.ToGuid(value);
				case "System.Int16":
					return XmlConvert.ToInt16(value);
				case "System.Int32":
				case "System.Int":
					return XmlConvert.ToInt32(value);
				case "System.Int64":
					return XmlConvert.ToInt64(value);
				case "System.SByte":
					return XmlConvert.ToSByte(value);
				case "System.Single":
					return XmlConvert.ToSingle(value);
				case "System.String":
					return value;
				case "System.TimeSpan":
					return XmlConvert.ToTimeSpan(value);
				case "System.UInt16":
					return XmlConvert.ToUInt16(value);
				case "System.UInt32":
					return XmlConvert.ToUInt32(value);
				case "System.UInt64":
					return XmlConvert.ToUInt64(value);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static string ToXml(object value)
		{
			if (value is bool)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((bool)value);
			}
			if (value is byte)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((byte)value);
			}
			if (value is char)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((char)value);
			}
			if (value is DateTime)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((DateTime)value, XmlDateTimeSerializationMode.Utc);
			}
			if (value is decimal)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((decimal)value);
			}
			if (value is double)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((double)value);
			}
			if (value is Guid)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((Guid)value);
			}
			if (value is short)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((short)value);
			}
			if (value is int)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((int)value);
			}
			if (value is long)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((long)value);
			}
			if (value is sbyte)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((sbyte)value);
			}
			if (value is float)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((float)value);
			}
			if (value is string)
			{
				return (string)value;
			}
			if (value is TimeSpan)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((TimeSpan)value);
			}
			if (value is ushort)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((ushort)value);
			}
			if (value is uint)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((uint)value);
			}
			if (value is ulong)
			{
				return XmlConvert.ToString((ulong)value);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static Color GetColorAtPoint(LinearGradientBrush brush, Point thePoint)
		{
			double y = thePoint.Y;
			double x = thePoint.X;
			double x2 = brush.StartPoint.X;
			double y2 = brush.StartPoint.Y;
			Point po = new Point(x2, y2);
			double x3 = brush.EndPoint.X;
			double y3 = brush.EndPoint.Y;
			Point point = new Point(x3, y3);
			Point point2 = default(Point);
			if (y2 == y3)
			{
				point2 = new Point(x, y2);
			}
			else if (x2 == x3)
			{
				point2 = new Point(x2, y);
			}
			else
			{
				double num = (y3 - y2) / (x3 - x2);
				double num2 = -1.0 / num;
				double num3 = y2 - num * x2;
				double num4 = (y - num2 * x - num3) / (num - num2);
				double y4 = num * num4 + num3;
				point2 = new Point(num4, y4);
				point2 = new Point(x, y);
			}
			double num5 = dist(point2, po, point);
			double num6 = dist(point, po, point);
			double x4 = num5 / num6;
			double num7 = brush.GradientStops.Max((GradientStop n) => n.Offset);
			if (x4 > num7)
			{
				x4 = num7;
			}
			double num8 = brush.GradientStops.Min((GradientStop n) => n.Offset);
			if (x4 < num8)
			{
				x4 = num8;
			}
			GradientStop gradientStop = (from n in brush.GradientStops
				where n.Offset <= x4
				orderby n.Offset
				select n).Last();
			GradientStop gradientStop2 = (from n in brush.GradientStops
				where n.Offset >= x4
				orderby n.Offset
				select n).First();
			float num9 = 0f;
			if (gradientStop.Offset != gradientStop2.Offset)
			{
				num9 = (float)((x4 - gradientStop.Offset) / (gradientStop2.Offset - gradientStop.Offset));
			}
			Color color = default(Color);
			if (brush.ColorInterpolationMode == ColorInterpolationMode.ScRgbLinearInterpolation)
			{
				float a = (gradientStop2.Color.ScA - gradientStop.Color.ScA) * num9 + gradientStop.Color.ScA;
				float r = (gradientStop2.Color.ScR - gradientStop.Color.ScR) * num9 + gradientStop.Color.ScR;
				float g = (gradientStop2.Color.ScG - gradientStop.Color.ScG) * num9 + gradientStop.Color.ScG;
				float b = (gradientStop2.Color.ScB - gradientStop.Color.ScB) * num9 + gradientStop.Color.ScB;
				return Color.FromScRgb(a, r, g, b);
			}
			byte a2 = (byte)((float)(gradientStop2.Color.A - gradientStop.Color.A) * num9 + (float)(int)gradientStop.Color.A);
			byte r2 = (byte)((float)(gradientStop2.Color.R - gradientStop.Color.R) * num9 + (float)(int)gradientStop.Color.R);
			byte g2 = (byte)((float)(gradientStop2.Color.G - gradientStop.Color.G) * num9 + (float)(int)gradientStop.Color.G);
			byte b2 = (byte)((float)(gradientStop2.Color.B - gradientStop.Color.B) * num9 + (float)(int)gradientStop.Color.B);
			return Color.FromArgb(a2, r2, g2, b2);
		}

		private static double dist(Point px, Point po, Point pf)
		{
			double num = Math.Sqrt((px.Y - po.Y) * (px.Y - po.Y) + (px.X - po.X) * (px.X - po.X));
			if ((px.Y < po.Y && pf.Y > po.Y) || (px.Y > po.Y && pf.Y < po.Y) || (px.Y == po.Y && px.X < po.X && pf.X > po.X) || (px.Y == po.Y && px.X > po.X && pf.X < po.X))
			{
				num = 0.0 - num;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public static void Clear3DVisual(Visual3D visual3D)
		{
			if (visual3D is ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D)
			{
				modelVisual3D.Children.Clear();
				modelVisual3D.Content = null;
			}
		}

		public static Visual3D Make3DLabel(string text, FontFamily fontFamily, double fontSize, Color foreground, double maxWidth, double maxLineCount, Point3D origin, HorizontalAlignment horizontalAlignment, VerticalAlignment verticalAlignment, PlotAxisID axisId, bool back, AxisAngleRotation3D normalRotation, out Rect labelBounds, FontStyle? fontStyle = null, FontWeight? fontWeight = null, TextDecorationCollection textDecorations = null)
		{
			TextBlock textBlock = PlotCanvasBase.MakeTextBlock(text, fontFamily, LabelScale3D * fontSize, foreground);
			if (maxLineCount == 1.0)
			{
				textBlock.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.NoWrap;
			}
			else
			{
				textBlock.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap;
			}
			textBlock.MaxWidth = maxWidth;
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			labelBounds = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, textBlock.DesiredSize.Width, textBlock.DesiredSize.Height);
			Int32Collection triangleIndices;
			if (back)
			{
				triangleIndices = MakeInt32Collection(new int[6] { 1, 3, 0, 0, 3, 2 });
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 1.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 1.0));
			}
			else
			{
				triangleIndices = MakeInt32Collection(new int[6] { 0, 2, 1, 1, 2, 3 });
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 1.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 1.0));
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			Point3D center = origin;
			switch (horizontalAlignment)
			{
			case HorizontalAlignment.Center:
			case HorizontalAlignment.Stretch:
				num = (0.0 - labelBounds.Width) / 2.0;
				break;
			case HorizontalAlignment.Right:
				num = 0.0 - labelBounds.Width;
				break;
			}
			switch (verticalAlignment)
			{
			case VerticalAlignment.Center:
			case VerticalAlignment.Stretch:
				num2 = labelBounds.Height / 2.0;
				break;
			case VerticalAlignment.Bottom:
				num2 = labelBounds.Height;
				break;
			}
			switch (axisId)
			{
			case PlotAxisID.X:
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X + num, origin.Y + num2, origin.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X + num + labelBounds.Width, origin.Y + num2, origin.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X + num, origin.Y + num2 - labelBounds.Height, origin.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X + num + labelBounds.Width, origin.Y + num2 - labelBounds.Height, origin.Z));
				labelBounds.X = point3DCollection[2].X;
				labelBounds.Y = point3DCollection[2].Y;
				center.X = origin.X + num + labelBounds.Width / 2.0;
				center.Y = origin.Y + num2 - labelBounds.Height / 2.0;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Y:
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X - num2, origin.Y + num, origin.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X - num2, origin.Y + num + labelBounds.Width, origin.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X - num2 + labelBounds.Height, origin.Y + num, origin.Z));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X - num2 + labelBounds.Height, origin.Y + num + labelBounds.Width, origin.Z));
				labelBounds.X = point3DCollection[0].X;
				labelBounds.Y = point3DCollection[0].Y;
				center.X = origin.X + num + labelBounds.Width / 2.0;
				center.Y = origin.Y + num2 - labelBounds.Height / 2.0;
				break;
			case PlotAxisID.Z:
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X, origin.Y + num2, origin.Z + num));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X, origin.Y + num2, origin.Z + num + labelBounds.Width));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X, origin.Y + num2 - labelBounds.Height, origin.Z + num));
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(origin.X, origin.Y + num2 - labelBounds.Height, origin.Z + num + labelBounds.Width));
				labelBounds.X = point3DCollection[2].X;
				labelBounds.Y = point3DCollection[2].Y;
				center.Z = origin.Z + num + labelBounds.Width / 2.0;
				center.Y = origin.Y + num2 - labelBounds.Height / 2.0;
				break;
			default:
				labelBounds = default(Rect);
				break;
			}
			Viewport2DVisual3D viewport2DVisual3D = new Viewport2DVisual3D();
			viewport2DVisual3D.Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
			{
				Positions = point3DCollection,
				TextureCoordinates = pointCollection,
				TriangleIndices = triangleIndices
			};
			viewport2DVisual3D.Material = new DiffuseMaterial(new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Transparent));
			Viewport2DVisual3D.SetIsVisualHostMaterial(viewport2DVisual3D.Material, value: true);
			viewport2DVisual3D.Visual = textBlock;
			if (normalRotation != null)
			{
				viewport2DVisual3D.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, center);
			}
			return viewport2DVisual3D;
		}

		public static Visual3D Draw3DSymbol(ModelVisual3D visual3D, Point3D pos, SymbolStyle symbol, Color color, double symbolScale, AxisAngleRotation3D normalRotation)
		{
			bool flag = (symbol & SymbolStyle.Filled) == SymbolStyle.Filled;
			double num = symbolScale * _symbolSize;
			switch (symbol & SymbolStyle.ShapeMask)
			{
			case SymbolStyle.Circle:
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D4 = FilledCircle3D(pos, color, num);
					if (normalRotation != null)
					{
						modelVisual3D4.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
					}
					visual3D.Children.Add(modelVisual3D4);
					return modelVisual3D4;
				}
				WirePolyline wirePolyline3 = new WirePolyline();
				wirePolyline3.Points = new Point3DCollection();
				for (double num2 = 0.0; num2 <= 360.0; num2 += 10.0)
				{
					wirePolyline3.Points.Add(new Point3D(Math.Cos(num2 * Math.PI / 180.0) * num + pos.X, Math.Sin(num2 * Math.PI / 180.0) * num + pos.Y, pos.Z));
				}
				wirePolyline3.Color = color;
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					wirePolyline3.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(wirePolyline3);
				return wirePolyline3;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Cross:
			{
				WireLines wireLines2 = new WireLines();
				wireLines2.Lines = new Point3DCollection();
				wireLines2.Color = color;
				wireLines2.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wireLines2.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wireLines2.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wireLines2.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					wireLines2.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(wireLines2);
				return wireLines2;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Diamond:
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D5 = FilledDiamond3D(pos, color, num);
					if (normalRotation != null)
					{
						modelVisual3D5.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
					}
					visual3D.Children.Add(modelVisual3D5);
					return modelVisual3D5;
				}
				WirePolyline wirePolyline4 = new WirePolyline();
				wirePolyline4.Points = new Point3DCollection();
				wirePolyline4.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline4.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline4.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline4.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline4.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline4.Color = color;
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					wirePolyline4.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(wirePolyline4);
				return wirePolyline4;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Dot:
			{
				ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D3 = FilledCircle3D(pos, color, num / 2.0);
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					modelVisual3D3.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(modelVisual3D3);
				return modelVisual3D3;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Plus:
			{
				WireLines wireLines = new WireLines();
				wireLines.Lines = new Point3DCollection();
				wireLines.Color = color;
				wireLines.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y, pos.Z));
				wireLines.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y, pos.Z));
				wireLines.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wireLines.Lines.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					wireLines.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(wireLines);
				return wireLines;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.Square:
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D2 = FilledSquare3D(pos, color, num);
					if (normalRotation != null)
					{
						modelVisual3D2.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
					}
					visual3D.Children.Add(modelVisual3D2);
					return modelVisual3D2;
				}
				WirePolyline wirePolyline2 = new WirePolyline();
				wirePolyline2.Points = new Point3DCollection();
				wirePolyline2.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline2.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline2.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline2.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline2.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline2.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline2.Color = color;
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					wirePolyline2.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(wirePolyline2);
				return wirePolyline2;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.TriangleDown:
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D6 = FilledTriangle3D(pos, color, 0.0 - num);
					if (normalRotation != null)
					{
						modelVisual3D6.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
					}
					visual3D.Children.Add(modelVisual3D6);
					return modelVisual3D6;
				}
				WirePolyline wirePolyline5 = new WirePolyline();
				wirePolyline5.Points = new Point3DCollection();
				wirePolyline5.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline5.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline5.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline5.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline5.Color = color;
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					wirePolyline5.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(wirePolyline5);
				return wirePolyline5;
			}
			case SymbolStyle.TriangleUp:
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					ModelVisual3D modelVisual3D = FilledTriangle3D(pos, color, num);
					if (normalRotation != null)
					{
						modelVisual3D.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
					}
					visual3D.Children.Add(modelVisual3D);
					return modelVisual3D;
				}
				WirePolyline wirePolyline = new WirePolyline();
				wirePolyline.Points = new Point3DCollection();
				wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + num, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - num, pos.Y - num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline.Points.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y + num, pos.Z));
				wirePolyline.Color = color;
				if (normalRotation != null)
				{
					wirePolyline.Transform = new RotateTransform3D(normalRotation, pos);
				}
				visual3D.Children.Add(wirePolyline);
				return wirePolyline;
			}
			default:
				return null;
			}
		}

		public static ModelVisual3D FilledCircle3D(Point3D pos, Color color, double radius)
		{
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
			Int32Collection int32Collection = new Int32Collection();
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.5, 0.5));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + radius, pos.Y, pos.Z));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 0.5));
			int num = 2;
			for (double num2 = 10.0; num2 <= 360.0; num2 += 10.0)
			{
				point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(Math.Cos(num2 * Math.PI / 180.0) * radius + pos.X, Math.Sin(num2 * Math.PI / 180.0) * radius + pos.Y, pos.Z));
				pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.5 * (1.0 + Math.Cos(num2 * Math.PI / 180.0)), 0.5 * (1.0 - Math.Sin(num2 * Math.PI / 180.0))));
				int32Collection.Add(num);
				int32Collection.Add(0);
				int32Collection.Add(num - 1);
				num++;
			}
			return new ModelVisual3D
			{
				Content = new GeometryModel3D
				{
					Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
					{
						TriangleIndices = int32Collection,
						Positions = point3DCollection,
						TextureCoordinates = pointCollection
					},
					Material = new DiffuseMaterial(new SolidColorBrush(color))
				}
			};
		}

		public static Int32Collection MakeInt32Collection(int[] values)
		{
			Int32Collection int32Collection = new Int32Collection();
			foreach (int value in values)
			{
				int32Collection.Add(value);
			}
			return int32Collection;
		}

		public static ModelVisual3D FilledDiamond3D(Point3D pos, Color color, double size)
		{
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
			Int32Collection triangleIndices = MakeInt32Collection(new int[6] { 0, 1, 2, 2, 3, 0 });
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.5, 1.0));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 0.5));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.5, 0.0));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 0.5));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y - size, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + size, pos.Y, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y + size, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - size, pos.Y, pos.Z));
			return new ModelVisual3D
			{
				Content = new GeometryModel3D
				{
					Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
					{
						TriangleIndices = triangleIndices,
						Positions = point3DCollection,
						TextureCoordinates = pointCollection
					},
					Material = new DiffuseMaterial(new SolidColorBrush(color))
				}
			};
		}

		public static ModelVisual3D FilledTriangle3D(Point3D pos, Color color, double size)
		{
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
			Int32Collection triangleIndices = MakeInt32Collection(new int[3] { 0, 1, 2 });
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.5, 0.0));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 1.0));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 1.0));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X, pos.Y + size, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - size, pos.Y - size, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + size, pos.Y - size, pos.Z));
			return new ModelVisual3D
			{
				Content = new GeometryModel3D
				{
					Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
					{
						TriangleIndices = triangleIndices,
						Positions = point3DCollection,
						TextureCoordinates = pointCollection
					},
					Material = new DiffuseMaterial(new SolidColorBrush(color))
				}
			};
		}

		public static ModelVisual3D FilledSquare3D(Point3D pos, Color color, double size)
		{
			Point3DCollection point3DCollection = new Point3DCollection();
			Int32Collection triangleIndices = MakeInt32Collection(new int[6] { 0, 3, 2, 0, 1, 3 });
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 1.0));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 1.0));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			pointCollection.Add(new Point(1.0, 0.0));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - size, pos.Y - size, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + size, pos.Y - size, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X - size, pos.Y + size, pos.Z));
			point3DCollection.Add(new Point3D(pos.X + size, pos.Y + size, pos.Z));
			return new ModelVisual3D
			{
				Content = new GeometryModel3D
				{
					Geometry = new MeshGeometry3D
					{
						TriangleIndices = triangleIndices,
						Positions = point3DCollection,
						TextureCoordinates = pointCollection
					},
					Material = new DiffuseMaterial(new SolidColorBrush(color))
				}
			};
		}

		public static GradientStopCollection GenerateGradientStopCollection(int layers)
		{
			GradientStopCollection gradientStopCollection = new GradientStopCollection();
			if (layers == 1)
			{
				gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
				{
					Color = Colors.Blue,
					Offset = 0.0
				});
				gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
				{
					Color = Colors.Blue,
					Offset = 1.0
				});
				return gradientStopCollection;
			}
			double num = 1.0 / ((double)layers - 1.0);
			for (int i = 0; i < layers; i++)
			{
				gradientStopCollection.Add(new GradientStop
				{
					Color = GradientLayerColorConverter(i, layers),
					Offset = (double)i * num
				});
			}
			return gradientStopCollection;
		}

		private static Color GradientLayerColorConverter(int layer, int totalLayers)
		{
			int num = 1020;
			int num2 = num / (totalLayers - 1);
			int num3 = layer * num2;
			if (layer == totalLayers - 1)
			{
				num3 = num;
			}
			byte[] array = new byte[3] { 255, 0, 0 };
			int num4 = 0;
			int num5 = 1;
			while (num3 > 0)
			{
				if (array[num5] == 0)
				{
					if (num3 > 255)
					{
						array[num5] += byte.MaxValue;
						num3 -= 255;
					}
					else
					{
						array[num5] += (byte)num3;
						num3 = 0;
					}
				}
				else if (num3 > 255)
				{
					array[num4] -= byte.MaxValue;
					num3 -= 255;
				}
				else
				{
					array[num4] -= (byte)num3;
					num3 = 0;
				}
				if (array[num4] == 0)
				{
					num4 = num5;
					num5 = 2;
				}
			}
			return Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, array[0], array[1], array[2]);
		}
	}
	public sealed class PlotSelection : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ElementTypeProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ElementType", typeof(PlotSelectedElementType), typeof(PlotSelection), new PropertyMetadata(PlotSelectedElementType.Unknown, OnElementTypePropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedBackgroundProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("SelectedBackground", typeof(Brush), typeof(PlotSelection), new PropertyMetadata(Brushes.LightGray));

		private static readonly DependencyProperty LastEffectProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("LastEffect", typeof(object), typeof(PlotSelection), new PropertyMetadata(null));

		private static FrameworkElement _selectionTarget;

		public static void SetElementType(FrameworkElement target, PlotSelectedElementType value)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			target.SetValue(ElementTypeProperty, value);
		}

		public static PlotSelectedElementType GetElementType(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			return (PlotSelectedElementType)target.GetValue(ElementTypeProperty);
		}

		private static void OnElementTypePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		public static void SetSelectedBackground(FrameworkElement target, Brush value)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			target.SetValue(SelectedBackgroundProperty, value);
		}

		public static Brush GetSelectedBackground(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			return (Brush)target.GetValue(SelectedBackgroundProperty);
		}

		private static void SetLastEffect(FrameworkElement target, object value)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			target.SetValue(LastEffectProperty, value);
		}

		private static object GetLastEffect(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			if (target == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("target");
			}
			return target.GetValue(LastEffectProperty);
		}

		internal static void ProcessMouseDown(PlotCtrl pc, object originalSource)
		{
			if (pc.AllowElementSelection)
			{
				_selectionTarget = GetAncestor(originalSource as DependencyObject, pc, (FrameworkElement x) => GetElementType(x) != PlotSelectedElementType.Unknown);
			}
		}

		internal static void ProcessMouseUp(PlotCtrl pc, bool selectIt)
		{
			if (pc.AllowElementSelection)
			{
				if (selectIt)
				{
					pc.SelectedElement = _selectionTarget;
				}
				_selectionTarget = null;
			}
		}

		internal static void AddSelectedEffect(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			PlotSelectedElementType elementType = GetElementType(target);
			if (elementType != PlotSelectedElementType.Unknown)
			{
				if (GetLastEffect(target) == null)
				{
					SetLastEffect(target, GetBackground(target));
				}
				SetBackground(target, GetSelectedBackground(target));
				if (elementType == PlotSelectedElementType.XAxisScales || elementType == PlotSelectedElementType.YAxisScales || elementType == PlotSelectedElementType.DataSeries)
				{
					target.Opacity = 0.5;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static void RemoveSelectedEffect(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			PlotSelectedElementType elementType = GetElementType(target);
			if (elementType != PlotSelectedElementType.Unknown)
			{
				Brush background = GetLastEffect(target) as Brush;
				SetBackground(target, background);
				if (elementType == PlotSelectedElementType.XAxisScales || elementType == PlotSelectedElementType.YAxisScales || elementType == PlotSelectedElementType.DataSeries)
				{
					target.Opacity = 1.0;
				}
			}
		}

		internal static IPlotFrame ResolvePlotFrame(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			return GetAncestor<PlotFrameVisual>(target)?.PlotFrame;
		}

		internal static IGraphAxis ResolveGraphAxis(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			AxisAnnotatorVisual ancestor = GetAncestor<AxisAnnotatorVisual>(target);
			if (ancestor != null && ancestor.AxisAnnotator is IGraphAxisAccessible graphAxisAccessible)
			{
				return graphAxisAccessible.Axis;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal static IDataSeries ResolveDataSeries(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			return GetAncestor<DataSeriesVisual>(target)?.DataSeries;
		}

		internal static IGraphAnnotation ResolveGraphAnnotation(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			return GetAncestor<GraphAnnotationVisual>(target)?.GraphAnnotation;
		}

		private static Brush GetBackground(FrameworkElement target)
		{
			if (target is Control)
			{
				return ((Control)target).Background;
			}
			if (target is TextBlock)
			{
				return ((TextBlock)target).Background;
			}
			if (target is Border)
			{
				return ((Border)target).Background;
			}
			if (target is Panel)
			{
				return ((Panel)target).Background;
			}
			if (target is Shape)
			{
				return ((Shape)target).Stroke;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static void SetBackground(FrameworkElement target, Brush background)
		{
			if (target is Control)
			{
				target.SetValue(Control.BackgroundProperty, background);
			}
			else if (target is TextBlock)
			{
				target.SetValue(TextBlock.BackgroundProperty, background);
			}
			else if (target is Border)
			{
				target.SetValue(Border.BackgroundProperty, background);
			}
			else if (target is Shape)
			{
				target.SetValue(Shape.StrokeProperty, background);
			}
			else if (target is Panel)
			{
				target.SetValue(Panel.BackgroundProperty, background);
			}
		}

		internal static T GetAncestor<T>(DependencyObject descendant, DependencyObject upperLimit = null, Func<T, bool> filter = null) where T : DependencyObject
		{
			if (descendant != null)
			{
				DependencyObject dependencyObject = descendant;
				while (dependencyObject != null && dependencyObject != upperLimit)
				{
					if (dependencyObject is T val)
					{
						if (filter == null)
						{
							return val;
						}
						if (filter(val))
						{
							return val;
						}
					}
					dependencyObject = ((!(dependencyObject is Visual)) ? LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject) : VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject));
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public enum PlotSelectedElementType
	{
		Unknown,
		Title,
		Subtitle,
		Legend,
		XAxisTitle,
		XAxisScales,
		YAxisTitle,
		YAxisScales,
		DataSeries,
		Annotation
	}
	public class AddValueConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public double Addition { get; set; }

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value is double)
			{
				return (double)value + Addition;
			}
			return double.NaN;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value != null && value is double)
			{
				return (double)value - Addition;
			}
			return double.NaN;
		}
	}

	{

		}

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowElementSelectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AllowElementSelection", typeof(bool), typeof(PlotCtrl));

		private bool _mouseDragVisible;

		{
			Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed
		};

		private const int DOUBLE_CLICK_TIME = 175;

		private DateTime _lastClickTime = DateTime.MinValue;

		private bool _onDoubleClicked;

		private Size _oldSize = Size.Empty;

		public FrameworkElement SelectedElement
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(SelectedElementProperty);
			}
			internal set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedElementPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public bool AllowElementSelection
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AllowElementSelectionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AllowElementSelectionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ContextMenuItems ContextMenuItems
		{
			get
			{
				return _contextMenuItems;
			}
			set
			{
				_contextMenuItems = value;
			}
		}

		private double CurrentZoomFactor
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedFrameVisual == null)
				{
					return 100.0;
				}
				Rect zoom = _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame.GraphLayout.GetZoom();
				double num = ((!SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Is3D) ? (1.0 / Math.Min(zoom.Width, zoom.Height)) : Math.Max(0.01, 2.0 / Math.Min(zoom.Width, zoom.Height)));
				return 100.0 * num;
			}
		}

		public double ZoomFactor
		{
			get
			{
				return CurrentZoomFactor;
			}
			set
			{
				double currentZoomFactor = CurrentZoomFactor;
				if (currentZoomFactor != value && _selectedFrameVisual != null)
				{
					SetZoom(new Point(_selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Width / 2.0, _selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Height / 2.0), currentZoomFactor / value);
				}
			}
		}

		public PlotControlInteractions Interactions
		{
			get
			{
				return _interactions;
			}
			set
			{
				_interactions = value;
			}
		}

		public PlotCanvasBase SelectedVisual => _selectedVisual;

		private IPlotFrame SelectedFrame
		{
			get
			{
				if (_selectedFrameVisual == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame;
			}
		}

		public IPlot Plot => _plot;

		public double PlotBorder
		{
			get
			{
				return _plotBorder;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value >= 0.0 && value != _plotBorder)
				{
					_plotBorder = value;
					Refresh();
				}
			}
		}

		public MouseDragMode DragMode
		{
			get
			{
				if (_mouseDragMode == MouseDragMode.Rotate3D)
				{
					IPlotFrame plotFrame = ((_selectedFrameVisual != null) ? _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame : null);
					if (plotFrame == null || plotFrame.GraphLayout == null || plotFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.AxesStyle != PlotAxesStyle.ThreeDXYZ)
					{
						_mouseDragMode = DefaultDragMode;
					}
				}
				return _mouseDragMode;
			}
			set
			{
				_mouseDragMode = value;
			}
		}

		public MouseTrackMode TrackMode { get; set; }

		private MouseDragMode DefaultDragMode
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					if (SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Is3D)
					{
						return MouseDragMode.Rotate3D;
					}
					return MouseDragMode.Zoom;
				}
				catch (NullReferenceException)
				{
					return MouseDragMode.Zoom;
				}
			}
		}

		public ICommand ZoomIn { get; private set; }

		public ICommand ZoomOut { get; private set; }

		public ICommand ZoomReset { get; private set; }

		public ICommand Properties { get; private set; }
