		private static void OnSelectedElementPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			PlotCtrl obj = d as PlotCtrl;
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = e.OldValue as FrameworkElement;
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement2 = e.NewValue as FrameworkElement;
			if (frameworkElement != null)
			{
				PlotSelection.RemoveSelectedEffect(frameworkElement);
			}
			if (frameworkElement2 != null)
			{
				PlotSelection.AddSelectedEffect(frameworkElement2);
			}
			obj.OnElementSelectionChanged(new PlotElementSelectionChangedEventArgs(frameworkElement, frameworkElement2));
		}

		public PlotCtrl()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_plotVisual = _plot.Renderer.Visual;
			AddChildVisual(_plotVisual);
			_mouseDragVisual = new Rectangle();
			_dragPen = PlotSupport.MakePen(Colors.Blue, LineStyle.Dash, 1.0);
			_mouseDragVisual.Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Blue);
			_mouseDragVisual.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
			_mouseDragVisual.StrokeDashArray = _dragPen.StrokeDashArray;
			Color yellow = Colors.Yellow;
			yellow.A = 127;
			Brush background = new SolidColorBrush(yellow);
			_trackGrid.Background = background;
			GetContextActions += PlotCtrl_GetContextActions;
			_handleGraphScroll = scrollGraph_GraphScroll;
			SetupCommands();
		}

		private void _toolTip_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			SetToolTip(visible: false);
		}

		public void AddChildVisual(UIElement visual, double left, double top)
		{
			if (!mainCanvas.Children.Contains(visual))
			{
				mainCanvas.Children.Add(visual);
			}
			Canvas.SetLeft(visual, left);
			Canvas.SetTop(visual, top);
		}

		public void AddChildVisual(UIElement visual)
		{
			AddChildVisual(visual, 0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public void RemoveChildVisual(UIElement visual)
		{
			if (mainCanvas.Children.Contains(visual))
			{
				mainCanvas.Children.Remove(visual);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseMove(e);
			_mouseLocation = e.GetPosition(this);
			if (!_mouseDrag && (_contextMenu == null || !_contextMenu.IsVisible))
			{
				GetSelectableVisual(showSelection: false);
			}
			PerformMouseMove();
		}

		private void PerformMouseMove()
		{
			if (_interactions == PlotControlInteractions.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null && SelectedFrame != null)
			{
				AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				Point point = axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_mouseLocation);
				if ((point.X >= 0.0 && point.Y >= 0.0 && point.X <= axesVisual.PlotArea.Width && point.Y <= axesVisual.PlotArea.Height) || DragMode == MouseDragMode.Rotate3D)
				{
					IGraphScooter graphScooter = null;
					double num = double.NaN;
					switch (_mouseMode)
					{
					case MouseMode.Camera:
						base.Cursor = Cursors.ScrollAll;
						break;
					case MouseMode.Pan:
					case MouseMode.Zoom:
					case MouseMode.Properties:
						base.Cursor = Cursors.Cross;
						break;
					default:
						if (_selectedVisual is GraphScooterVisual graphScooterVisual && graphScooterVisual.GraphScooter.UserMoveable && DragMode == MouseDragMode.Scooter)
						{
							graphScooter = graphScooterVisual.GraphScooter;
							switch (graphScooter.LinkedAxisId)
							{
							case PlotAxisID.X:
							case PlotAxisID.X2:
								num = point.X / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
								base.Cursor = Cursors.SizeWE;
								break;
							case PlotAxisID.Y:
							case PlotAxisID.Y2:
								num = 1.0 - point.Y / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
								base.Cursor = Cursors.SizeNS;
								break;
							}
						}
						else if (_selectableVisual != null)
						{
							base.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
						}
						else
						{
							base.Cursor = Cursors.Cross;
						}
						break;
					}
					if (_mouseDrag && _mouseLocation != _mouseDragStart)
					{
						_mouseDragEnd = _mouseLocation;
						switch (DragMode)
						{
						case MouseDragMode.Scooter:
							if (_mouseMode == MouseMode.Pointer && graphScooter != null && !double.IsNaN(num))
							{
								graphScooter.SetFractionalPosition(num);
								Refresh();
								if (graphScooter is SliceScooter sliceScooter)
								{
									OnSliceMoved(new PlotSliceEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, sliceScooter.GraphSlice));
								}
								else
								{
									OnScooterMoved(new PlotScooterEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, graphScooter));
								}
							}
							break;
						case MouseDragMode.Zoom:
							if (_mouseMode == MouseMode.Pointer || _mouseMode == MouseMode.Zoom)
							{
								CalculateMouseDragRegion();
							}
							break;
						case MouseDragMode.Rotate3D:
							if (_mouseMode == MouseMode.Pointer || _mouseMode == MouseMode.Camera)
							{
								base.Cursor = Cursors.ScrollAll;
								double num2 = _mouseDragEnd.X - _mouseDragStart.X;
								double num3 = _mouseDragEnd.Y - _mouseDragStart.Y;
								_mouseDragStart = _mouseDragEnd;
								(SelectedFrame.GraphLayout as IGraphLayout3D).RotateCamera(num2 / 2.0, num3 / 2.0);
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SetToolTip((DragMode != MouseDragMode.Rotate3D || !_mouseDrag) && (_mouseMode == MouseMode.Pointer || _mouseMode == MouseMode.Properties));
				return;
			}
			SetToolTip(visible: false);
			base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
		}

		public void RotatePlot(IPlotFrame frame, Quaternion delta)
		{
			if (frame != null && _plot.Frames.Contains(frame))
			{
				(frame.GraphLayout as Graph).RotateScene(delta);
			}
		}

		public void SetInitialPlotCameraDistance(IPlotFrame frame, double value)
		{
			if (frame != null && _plot.Frames.Contains(frame))
			{
				(frame.GraphLayout as Graph)._defaultCameraDistance = value;
				(frame.GraphLayout as Graph).CameraDistance = value;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnScooterMoved(PlotScooterEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.ScooterMoved != null)
			{
				this.ScooterMoved(this, e);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSliceMoved(PlotSliceEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.SliceMoved != null)
			{
				this.SliceMoved(this, e);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementSelectionChanged(PlotElementSelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.ElementSelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.ElementSelectionChanged(this, e);
			}
		}

		private void CalculateMouseDragRegion()
		{
			AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
			Point point = axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_mouseDragStart);
			Point point2 = axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_mouseDragEnd);
			double num = point.X;
			double num2 = point.Y;
			double num3 = point2.X;
			double num4 = point2.Y;
			if ((Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift) == ModifierKeys.Shift)
			{
				if (Math.Abs(num3 - num) > Math.Abs(num4 - num2))
				{
					num4 = num2 + Math.Abs(num3 - num) * (double)Math.Sign(num4 - num2);
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = num + Math.Abs(num4 - num2) * (double)Math.Sign(num3 - num);
				}
			}
			if ((Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Control) == ModifierKeys.Control)
			{
				num -= num3 - num;
				num2 -= num4 - num2;
			}
			num = Math.Min(Math.Max(0.0, num), axesVisual.PlotArea.Width);
			num3 = Math.Min(Math.Max(0.0, num3), axesVisual.PlotArea.Width);
			num2 = Math.Min(Math.Max(0.0, num2), axesVisual.PlotArea.Height);
			num4 = Math.Min(Math.Max(0.0, num4), axesVisual.PlotArea.Height);
			if (num > num3)
			{
				double num5 = num3;
				num3 = num;
				num = num5;
			}
			if (num2 > num4)
			{
				double num6 = num4;
				num4 = num2;
				num2 = num6;
			}
			_selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea = new Rect(num / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width, num2 / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height, (num3 - num) / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width, (num4 - num2) / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height);
			SetMouseDragRegion();
			SetMouseDragVisual(_mouseDrag && (_interactions & PlotControlInteractions.Zoom) == PlotControlInteractions.Zoom && _mouseSelectRegion.Width > 4.0 && _mouseSelectRegion.Height > 4.0);
		}

		private void SetToolTip(bool visible)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (visible && _selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
				if (DragMode == MouseDragMode.Rotate3D)
				{
					IGraph graph = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph;
					Viewport3D viewport3D = (graph as Graph).Viewport3D;
					if (viewport3D != null)
					{
						Point point = TransformToVisual(viewport3D).Transform(_mouseLocation);
						bool found = false;
						foreach (IDataSeries dataSeries in graph.DataSeriesCollection)
						{
							Dictionary<Visual3D, IDataPoint[]> vis3DDPs = (dataSeries as DataSeries).Visual3DDataPoints;
							if (vis3DDPs != null && vis3DDPs.Count > 0)
							{
								VisualTreeHelper.HitTest(viewport3D, null, delegate(HitTestResult result)
								{
									if (result.VisualHit is Visual3D key)
									{
										dataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var zMapId);
										IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(graph.Axes.GetAxis(xMapId), dataSeries);
										IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(graph.Axes.GetAxis(yMapId), dataSeries);
										IGraphAxis axisForSeries3 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(graph.Axes.GetAxis(zMapId), dataSeries);
										if (vis3DDPs.TryGetValue(key, out var value2))
										{
											object x;
											object y;
											object z;
											if (value2 != null)
											{
												if (dataSeries.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar && value2.Length == 2)
												{
													string text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}", value2[0].X);
													if (value2[0].X != value2[1].X && value2[0].X.ToString() == "0")
													{
														text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}", value2[1].X);
													}
													string text3 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:" + axisForSeries2.DataLabelFormatString + "}", value2[0].Y);
													if (value2[0].Y != value2[1].Y && value2[0].Y.ToString() == "0")
													{
														text3 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:" + axisForSeries2.DataLabelFormatString + "}", value2[1].Y);
													}
													string text4 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:" + axisForSeries3.DataLabelFormatString + "}", value2[0].Z);
													if (value2[0].Z != value2[1].Z && value2[0].Z.ToString() == "0")
													{
														text4 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0:" + axisForSeries3.DataLabelFormatString + "}", value2[1].Z);
													}
													string label = value2[1].Label;
													if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(label))
													{
														label = value2[0].Label;
													}
													if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(label))
													{
														sb.AppendLine(label);
													}
													sb.Append(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t= {1}\n{2}\t= {3}\n{4}\t= {5}", axisForSeries.Title, text2, axisForSeries2.Title, text3, axisForSeries3.Title, text4));
												}
												else
												{
													IDataPoint[] array = value2;
													foreach (IDataPoint dataPoint in array)
													{
														string label2 = dataPoint.Label;
														if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(label2))
														{
															sb.Append(label2 + ", \n");
														}
														sb.AppendLine(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t= {1:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}, \n{2}\t= {3:" + axisForSeries2.DataLabelFormatString + "}, \n{4}\t= {5:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}", axisForSeries.Title, dataPoint.X, axisForSeries2.Title, dataPoint.Y, axisForSeries3.Title, dataPoint.Z));
													}
												}
											}
											else if ((dataSeries as DataSeries).Point3DToXYZ((result as RayMeshGeometry3DHitTestResult).PointHit, out x, out y, out z))
											{
												sb.Append(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t= {1:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}\n{2}\t= {3:" + axisForSeries2.DataLabelFormatString + "}\n{4}\t= {5:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}", axisForSeries.Title, x, axisForSeries2.Title, y, axisForSeries3.Title, z));
											}
										}
										found = true;
										return HitTestResultBehavior.Stop;
									}
									return HitTestResultBehavior.Continue;
								}, new PointHitTestParameters(point));
							}
							if (found)
							{
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (TrackMode == MouseTrackMode.SeriesValues)
					{
						foreach (IDataSeries item in SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.DataSeriesCollection)
						{
							if (item.Renderer.Visual is DataSeriesVisual dataSeriesVisual && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataSeriesVisual.MouseOverText))
							{
								if (sb.Length > 0)
								{
									sb.Append("\n");
								}
								sb.Append(dataSeriesVisual.MouseOverText);
							}
						}
					}
					AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
					Point point2 = axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_mouseLocation);
					foreach (IGraphAxis item2 in SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.AxisCollection)
					{
						double fractionTolerance = 1.0 / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
						PlotAxisID id = item2.Id;
						double num;
						if ((uint)(id - 1) > 1u)
						{
							if (id != PlotAxisID.Y && id != PlotAxisID.Y2)
							{
								continue;
							}
							num = 1.0 - point2.Y / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
						}
						else
						{
							num = point2.X / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
						}
						bool valid;
						object obj = item2.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(item2, num, out valid);
						if (!valid)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (TrackMode == MouseTrackMode.Coordinates)
						{
							text = item2.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionToValueString(item2, num);
							if (sb.Length > 0)
							{
								sb.Append("\n");
							}
							sb.Append(text);
						}
						if (_trackValues.ContainsKey(item2.Id))
						{
							if (_trackValues[item2.Id] != obj)
							{
								_trackValues[item2.Id] = obj;
								flag = true;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							_trackValues.Add(item2.Id, obj);
							flag = true;
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							object value = CalculateTolerance(item2, obj, num, fractionTolerance);
							if (_tolerances.ContainsKey(item2.Id))
							{
								_tolerances[item2.Id] = value;
							}
							else
							{
								_tolerances.Add(item2.Id, value);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				text = sb.ToString();
			}
			if (flag && _selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				PlotTrackEventArgs e = new PlotTrackEventArgs(visible, _mouseLocation, _trackValues, _tolerances, SelectedFrame, text)
				{
					PlotControl = this
				};
				OnPointTrack(e);
				text = e.Text;
				visible = visible && e.Visible && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text);
			}
			if (visible && (_interactions & PlotControlInteractions.Track) == PlotControlInteractions.Track)
			{
				_trackGrid.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				SetToolTipGrid(text);
				_trackGrid.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
				double num2 = _mouseLocation.X + 4.0;
				double num3 = _mouseLocation.Y + 4.0;
				double width = _trackGrid.DesiredSize.Width;
				double height = _trackGrid.DesiredSize.Height;
				_maxGridWidth = Math.Max(width, _maxGridWidth);
				if (num2 + width > base.RenderSize.Width)
				{
					num2 = base.RenderSize.Width - _maxGridWidth;
				}
				if (num3 + height > base.RenderSize.Height)
				{
					num3 -= height + 8.0;
				}
				AddChildVisual(_trackGrid, num2, num3);
			}
			else
			{
				_trackGrid.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				_maxGridWidth = 0.0;
				RemoveChildVisual(_trackGrid);
			}
		}

		private void SetToolTipGrid(string text)
		{
			string[] array = text.Split('\n');
			Grid trackGrid = _trackGrid;
			trackGrid.Children.Clear();
			List<TextBlock> list = new List<TextBlock>();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (trackGrid.RowDefinitions.Count == i)
				{
					RowDefinition rowDefinition = new RowDefinition();
					rowDefinition.Height = default(GridLength);
					trackGrid.RowDefinitions.Add(rowDefinition);
				}
				string[] array2 = array[i].Replace("\r", "").Split('\t');
				for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
				{
					if (trackGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Count == j)
					{
						trackGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition());
					}
					TextBlock textBlock = new TextBlock();
					textBlock.Text = array2[j];
					textBlock.TextWrapping = TextWrapping.NoWrap;
					textBlock.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					Grid.SetRow(textBlock, i);
					Grid.SetColumn(textBlock, j);
					textBlock.Padding = new Thickness(1.0);
					trackGrid.Children.Add(textBlock);
					if (array2.Length == 1)
					{
						list.Add(textBlock);
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (TextBlock item in list)
			{
				Grid.SetColumnSpan(item, trackGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Count);
			}
		}

		private static object CalculateTolerance(IGraphAxis axis, object value, double fraction, double fractionTolerance)
		{
			if (axis == null || value == null || double.IsNaN(fraction) || double.IsNaN(fractionTolerance))
			{
				return null;
			}
			bool valid;
			object value2 = axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, fraction + fractionTolerance, out valid);
			object value3 = axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, fraction - fractionTolerance, out valid);
			double num = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value, out valid);
			double num2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value2, out valid) - num;
			double num3 = num - PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(axis, value3, out valid);
			if (!double.IsNaN(num2) && !double.IsNaN(num3))
			{
				return PlotSupport.DoubleToDeltaValue(axis, Math.Max(num2, num3), out valid);
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(num2))
			{
				return PlotSupport.DoubleToDeltaValue(axis, num2, out valid);
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(num3))
			{
				return PlotSupport.DoubleToDeltaValue(axis, num3, out valid);
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual void OnPointTrack(PlotTrackEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.PointTrack != null)
			{
				this.PointTrack(this, e);
			}
		}

		private void SetMouseDragVisual(bool showDragRegion)
		{
			if (showDragRegion)
			{
				_mouseDragVisual.Width = _mouseSelectRegion.Width;
				_mouseDragVisual.Height = _mouseSelectRegion.Height;
				AddChildVisual(_mouseDragVisual, _mouseSelectRegion.Left, _mouseSelectRegion.Top);
			}
			else
			{
				RemoveChildVisual(_mouseDragVisual);
			}
			_mouseDragVisible = showDragRegion;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Focus();
			PlotSelection.ProcessMouseDown(this, e.OriginalSource);
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			if (_interactions == PlotControlInteractions.None)
			{
				return;
			}
			SetSelectedVisual();
			_mouseDrag = true;
			_mouseLocation = e.GetPosition(this);
			_mouseDragStart = _mouseLocation;
			if (_mouseMode == MouseMode.Pointer)
			{
				if (DragMode == MouseDragMode.Slice)
				{
					AddSlice();
				}
				if (_selectedVisual is GraphScooterVisual)
				{
					DragMode = MouseDragMode.Scooter;
				}
			}
			PerformMouseMove();
			CaptureMouse();
		}

		private void AddSlice()
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				IGraph graph = _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame.GraphLayout.Graph;
				IGraphAxis axis = graph.Axes.GetAxis(PlotAxisID.X);
				AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				double point = axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_mouseLocation).X / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
				IGraphSlice graphSlice = graph.NewSlice();
				graphSlice.LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				bool valid;
				object obj = axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
				if (valid)
				{
					graphSlice.StartValue = obj;
					graphSlice.EndValue = obj;
				}
				Color green = Colors.Green;
				green.A = 33;
				graphSlice.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(green);
				OnSliceAdded(new PlotSliceEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, graphSlice));
				Refresh();
				_selectedVisual = (graphSlice as GraphSlice).EndScooter.Renderer.Visual as PlotCanvasBase;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSliceAdded(PlotSliceEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.SliceAdded != null)
			{
				this.SliceAdded(this, e);
			}
		}

		private HitTestResultBehavior ClickHitTestCallback(HitTestResult result)
		{
			if (result != null && result.VisualHit is FrameworkElement { Parent: PlotCanvasBase parent })
			{
				OnVisualClick(parent);
			}
			return HitTestResultBehavior.Continue;
		}

		private void SetSelectedVisual()
		{
			if (_selectedVisual != null)
			{
				_selectedVisual.IsSelected = false;
			}
			_selectedVisual = _selectableVisual;
			if (_selectedVisual != null)
			{
				_selectedVisual.IsSelected = true;
			}
		}

		private void GetSelectableVisual(bool showSelection)
		{
			_selectableVisual = null;
			VisualTreeHelper.HitTest(this, null, SelectableHitTestCallback, new PointHitTestParameters(_mouseLocation));
			if (_selectableVisual != _selectedVisual && showSelection)
			{
				SetSelectedVisual();
			}
		}

		private HitTestResultBehavior SelectableHitTestCallback(HitTestResult result)
		{
			if (result != null && result.VisualHit is FrameworkElement { Parent: PlotCanvasBase { IsSelectable: not false } parent })
			{
				_selectableVisual = parent;
				return HitTestResultBehavior.Stop;
			}
			return HitTestResultBehavior.Continue;
		}

		protected virtual void OnPlotClick(PlotClickEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.PlotClick != null)
			{
				this.PlotClick(this, e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ReleaseMouseCapture();
			_lastClickTime = DateTime.Now;
			bool flag = false;
			bool selectIt = !_onDoubleClicked;
			DragMode = DefaultDragMode;
			if (!_onDoubleClicked)
			{
				PlotClickEventArgs e2 = new PlotClickEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame)
				{
					PlotControl = this
				};
				OnPlotClick(e2);
				flag = e2.Handled;
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			if (_mouseDrag)
			{
				_mouseDrag = false;
				if (_mouseDragVisible)
				{
					_mouseDragVisible = false;
					if (_selectedFrameVisual != null && _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea != PlotSupport.ZeroRectangle && (_interactions & PlotControlInteractions.Zoom) == PlotControlInteractions.Zoom)
					{
						SetZoom(_selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea);
					}
					selectIt = false;
				}
			}
			if (_onDoubleClicked)
			{
				_onDoubleClicked = false;
			}
			else if (!flag)
			{
				Point position = e.GetPosition(this);
				VisualTreeHelper.HitTest(this, null, ClickHitTestCallback, new PointHitTestParameters(position));
				if (_mouseMode == MouseMode.Properties)
				{
					ShowContextMenu(position, e);
					selectIt = false;
				}
			}
			PlotSelection.ProcessMouseUp(this, selectIt);
		}

		private void SetSelectedFrameVisual(PlotFrameVisual frameVisual)
		{
			if (frameVisual == _selectedFrameVisual)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				IPlotFrame plotFrame = _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame;
				if (plotFrame != null)
				{
					flag = plotFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.ThreeDXYZ;
				}
				if (_scrollGraph != null)
				{
					if (plotFrame == null)
					{
						flag = (_scrollGraph as IGraph).AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.ThreeDXYZ;
					}
					_scrollGraph.GraphScroll -= _handleGraphScroll;
				}
			}
			_selectedFrameVisual = frameVisual;
			_scrollGraph = null;
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				IPlotFrame plotFrame = _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame;
				if (plotFrame != null && (_scrollGraph = plotFrame.GraphLayout as IScrollableGraph) != null)
				{
					_scrollGraph.GraphScroll += _handleGraphScroll;
				}
				if (plotFrame == null || flag != (plotFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.ThreeDXYZ))
				{
					DragMode = DefaultDragMode;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DragMode = DefaultDragMode;
			}
		}

		private void OnVisualClick(PlotCanvasBase pc)
		{
			if (pc is PlotFrameVisual { PlotFrame: var plotFrame } && plotFrame != _plot.SelectedFrame)
			{
				_trackValues = new Dictionary<PlotAxisID, object>();
				_tolerances = new Dictionary<PlotAxisID, object>();
				_plot.SelectedFrame = plotFrame;
				Refresh();
				if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
				{
					SetMouseDragRegion();
					PerformMouseMove();
				}
				OnSelectAction(new PlotClickEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame)
				{
					PlotControl = this
				});
			}
		}

		private IList<PlotClickEventArgs> GetSelection()
		{
			return new SelectedItemCollection<PlotClickEventArgs>(new PlotClickEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame)
			{
				PlotControl = this
			});
		}

		protected override void OnMouseDoubleClick(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_onDoubleClicked = true;
			e.Handled = PerformDoubleClick();
			base.OnMouseDoubleClick(e);
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				ToggleMaximisedFrame();
			}
		}

		private bool PerformDoubleClick()
		{
			PlotClickEventArgs e = new PlotClickEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame)
			{
				PlotControl = this
			};
			OnDoubleClickAction(e);
			return e.Handled;
		}

		private void ToggleMaximisedFrame()
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IPlotFrame plotFrame = _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame;
			if (plotFrame != null)
			{
				if (_plot.MaximisedFrame == plotFrame)
				{
					_plot.MaximisedFrame = null;
				}
				else
				{
					_plot.MaximisedFrame = plotFrame;
				}
				Refresh();
				_mouseDrag = false;
				_mouseDragVisible = false;
				SetMouseDragRegion();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseWheel(MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseWheel(e);
			DoMouseWheel((double)e.Delta / 120.0);
		}

		private void DoMouseWheel(double delta)
		{
			if ((_interactions & PlotControlInteractions.Zoom) == PlotControlInteractions.Zoom && _selectedFrameVisual != null && delta != 0.0)
			{
				double zoomFactor = 1.0 / StandardValues.MouseWheelZoomFactor(2.0 * delta);
				Point centre = ((!SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Is3D) ? (SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual).PlotAreaToHost(_mouseLocation) : _selectedFrameVisual.ClientToHost(_mouseLocation));
				SetZoom(centre, zoomFactor);
			}
		}

		private void SetZoom(double zoomFactor)
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				SetZoom(new Point(_selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Width / 2.0, _selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Height / 2.0), zoomFactor);
			}
		}

		private void SetZoom(Point centre, double zoomFactor)
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				Rect rect;
				if (_selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Is3D)
				{
					rect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, _selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Width, _selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
					rect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, axesVisual.PlotArea.Width, axesVisual.PlotArea.Height);
				}
				double num = centre.X * (1.0 - zoomFactor);
				double num2 = centre.Y * (1.0 - zoomFactor);
				double num3 = zoomFactor * rect.Width;
				double num4 = zoomFactor * rect.Height;
				SetZoom(new Rect(num / rect.Width, num2 / rect.Height, num3 / rect.Width, num4 / rect.Height));
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			SetToolTip(visible: false);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
		}

		private void SetZoom(Rect zoomRegion)
		{
			SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.SetZoom(zoomRegion);
			Refresh();
			SetMouseDragRegion();
			SetToolTip(_trackGrid.Visibility == Visibility.Visible);
			OnGraphZoom(new PlotZoomEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, zoomRegion, isZoomReset: false)
			{
				PlotControl = this
			});
		}

		private void scrollGraph_GraphScroll(object sender, GraphScrollEventArgs e)
		{
			OnGraphZoom(new PlotZoomEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, e.ZoomArea, isZoomReset: false)
			{
				PlotControl = this
			});
		}

		private void SetMouseDragRegion()
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				Point point = axesVisual.HostToPlotArea(new Point(_selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Left * axesVisual.PlotArea.Width, _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Top * axesVisual.PlotArea.Height));
				_mouseSelectRegion.X = point.X;
				_mouseSelectRegion.Y = point.Y;
				_mouseSelectRegion.Width = _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Width * axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
				_mouseSelectRegion.Height = _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Height * axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
				SetMouseDragVisual(_mouseDragVisible);
			}
		}

		public void Refresh()
		{
			Refresh(base.RenderSize);
		}

		public void Refresh(Size size)
		{
			bool flag = size.Width != _oldSize.Width || size.Height != _oldSize.Height;
			double num = 10.0 + 2.0 * _plotBorder;
			if (size.Width >= num && size.Height >= num)
			{
				_plot.RenderInitialise();
				Rect renderArea = new Rect(_plotBorder, _plotBorder, size.Width - 2.0 * _plotBorder, size.Height - 2.0 * _plotBorder);
				_plot.Draw(ref renderArea, flag);
				if (_plot.SelectedFrame != null)
				{
					SetSelectedFrameVisual(_plot.SelectedFrame.Renderer.Visual as PlotFrameVisual);
				}
				else
				{
					SetSelectedFrameVisual(null);
				}
				_oldSize = size;
				mainCanvas.InvalidateArrange();
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SetToolTip(_trackGrid.Visibility == Visibility.Visible);
			}
		}

		private void UserControl_SizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Refresh(e.NewSize);
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				Point point = axesVisual.HostToPlotArea(new Point(axesVisual.Width * _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Left, axesVisual.Height * _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Top));
				_mouseDragStart.X = point.X;
				_mouseDragStart.Y = point.Y;
				_mouseDragEnd.X = _mouseDragStart.X + _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Width * axesVisual.Width;
				_mouseDragEnd.Y = _mouseDragStart.Y + _selectedFrameVisual.SelectedArea.Height * axesVisual.Height;
				SetMouseDragVisual(_mouseDragVisible);
			}
			else
			{
				SetMouseDragVisual(showDragRegion: false);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseRightButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Focus();
			base.OnMouseRightButtonDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseRightButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			VisualTreeHelper.HitTest(this, null, ClickHitTestCallback, new PointHitTestParameters(_mouseLocation));
			ShowContextMenu(_mouseLocation, e);
			base.OnMouseRightButtonUp(e);
		}

		private bool ShowContextMenu(Point location, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((_interactions & PlotControlInteractions.ContextMenu) == PlotControlInteractions.ContextMenu && this.GetContextActions != null)
			{
				location = PointToScreen(location);
				_contextMenu = ActionSupport<PlotClickEventArgs>.DoContextMenu(this.GetContextActions, this, null, GetSelection(), new Rect(location, location), e);
			}
			return e.Handled;
		}

		private void PlotCtrl_GetContextActions(object sender, GetActionsEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> actions)
		{
			BuildContextMenu(actions.Actions);
		}

		private void BuildContextMenu(IActionMenuCollection<PlotClickEventArgs> actions)
		{
			_contextMenuPosition = _mouseLocation;
			if (CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems.XmlExport))
			{
				actions.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("exportPlotMenu"), "export", miExportPlot_Click));
			}
			if (CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems.XmlImport))
			{
				actions.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("importPlotMenu"), "import", miImportPlot_Click));
			}
			if (SelectedFrame == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems.Copy))
			{
				ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs> actionMenu = new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("copyMenu"), "copy");
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("copyPlotMenu"), "copyPlot", miCopyPlot_Click));
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("copyGraphMenu"), "copyGraph", miCopyGraph_Click, _selectedFrameVisual != null));
				actions.Add(actionMenu);
			}
			if (CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems.Print))
			{
				ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs> actionMenu = new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("printMenu"), "print");
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("printPlotMenu"), "printPlot", miPrintPlot_Click));
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("printGraphMenu"), "printGraph", miPrintGraph_Click, _selectedFrameVisual != null));
				actions.Add(actionMenu);
			}
			if (CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems.ZoomReset))
			{
				actions.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("resetZoomMenu"), "resetZoom", miResetZoom_Click, _selectedFrameVisual != null));
				if (SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Is3D)
				{
					actions.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("resetViewMenu"), "resetView", miReset3DView_Click, _selectedFrameVisual != null));
				}
			}
			if (CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems.Scooters) && !SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Is3D)
			{
				GraphScooterVisual graphScooterVisual = _selectedVisual as GraphScooterVisual;
				actions.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>());
				ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs> actionMenu = new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("scootersMenu"), "scooters");
				AxesVisual axesVisual = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				Point point = axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_contextMenuPosition);
				double num = point.X / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("addVertScooterMenu"), "addVerticalScooter", miVerticalScooter_Click, num >= 0.0 && num <= 1.0));
				num = 1.0 - point.Y / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("addHorizScooterMenu"), "addHorizontalScooter", miHorizontalScooter_Click, num >= 0.0 && num <= 1.0));
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("deleteSelectedScooterMenu"), "deleteSelectedScooter", miDeleteSelectedScooter_Click, graphScooterVisual?.GraphScooter.UserCanDelete ?? false));
				actionMenu.Subitems.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("deleteAllScootersMenu"), "deleteAllScooter", miDeleteAllScooters_Click));
				actions.Add(actionMenu);
			}
			if (CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems.Properties))
			{
				actions.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>());
				actions.Add(new ActionMenu<PlotClickEventArgs>(PlotSupport.ResourceString("propertiesMenu"), "properties", miProperties_Click, _selectedFrameVisual != null));
			}
		}

		private bool CanAddMenuItem(ContextMenuItems item)
		{
			return (_contextMenuItems & item) == item;
		}

		private void miAddSlice_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			DragMode = MouseDragMode.Slice;
		}

		private void miVerticalScooter_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				IGraph graph = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph;
				AxesVisual axesVisual = graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				double num = axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_contextMenuPosition).X / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
				IGraphScooter graphScooter = graph.NewScooter();
				graphScooter.LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.X;
				graphScooter.ScooterPosition = num;
				bool flag = (graphScooter.UserCanDelete = true);
				bool userCanFormat = (graphScooter.UserMoveable = flag);
				graphScooter.UserCanFormat = userCanFormat;
				Refresh();
				OnScooterAdded(new PlotScooterEventArgs(_contextMenuPosition, SelectedFrame, graphScooter));
			}
		}

		private void miHorizontalScooter_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				IGraph graph = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph;
				AxesVisual axesVisual = graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				double num = 1.0 - axesVisual.PlotAreaToHost(_contextMenuPosition).Y / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
				IGraphScooter graphScooter = graph.NewScooter();
				graphScooter.LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.Y;
				graphScooter.ScooterPosition = num;
				bool flag = (graphScooter.UserCanDelete = true);
				bool userCanFormat = (graphScooter.UserMoveable = flag);
				graphScooter.UserCanFormat = userCanFormat;
				Refresh();
				OnScooterAdded(new PlotScooterEventArgs(_contextMenuPosition, SelectedFrame, graphScooter));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnScooterAdded(PlotScooterEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.ScooterAdded != null)
			{
				this.ScooterAdded(this, e);
			}
		}

		private void miDeleteAllScooters_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraph graph = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph;
			for (int num = graph.ScooterCollection.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				IGraphScooter graphScooter = graph.ScooterCollection[num];
				if (graphScooter.UserCanDelete)
				{
					graph.DeleteScooter(graphScooter);
					OnScooterDeleted(new PlotScooterEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, graphScooter));
				}
			}
			Refresh();
		}

		private void miDeleteSelectedScooter_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			if (_selectedVisual is GraphScooterVisual graphScooterVisual)
			{
				IGraph graph = SelectedFrame.GraphLayout.Graph;
				IGraphScooter graphScooter = graphScooterVisual.GraphScooter;
				if (graphScooter.UserCanDelete)
				{
					graph.DeleteScooter(graphScooter);
					_selectedVisual = null;
					Refresh();
					OnScooterDeleted(new PlotScooterEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, graphScooter));
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnScooterDeleted(PlotScooterEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.ScooterDeleted != null)
			{
				this.ScooterDeleted(this, e);
			}
		}

		{
			ShowPropertiesDialog();
		}

		{
			if (PlotPropertiesDlg.ShowProperties(this, _selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame.GraphLayout, _selectedVisual) == true)
			{
				Refresh();
			}
		}

				PlotControl = this
			});
		}

				this.PropertiesUpdated(this, e);
			}
		}

		private void miCopyPlot_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			CopyPlot((int)base.RenderSize.Width + 1, (int)base.RenderSize.Height + 1);
		}

		public void CopyPlot(int pixelWidth, int pixelHeight)
		{
			if (_plotVisual != null)
			{
				Clipboard.SetImage(RenderPlotToBitmap(pixelWidth, pixelHeight));
			}
		}

		public void CopyPlot()
		{
			if (_plotVisual != null)
			{
				Clipboard.SetImage(RenderPlotToBitmap());
			}
		}

		public RenderTargetBitmap RenderPlotToBitmap(int pixelWidth, int pixelHeight)
		{
			Size size = base.RenderSize;
			bool flag = !base.IsVisible;
			if (Math.Abs(size.Width - (double)pixelWidth) > 1.0 || Math.Abs(size.Height - (double)pixelHeight) > 1.0)
			{
				size = new Size(pixelWidth, pixelHeight);
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				Refresh(size);
				Measure(size);
				Arrange(new Rect(size));
				_plotVisual.UpdateLayout();
			}
			RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(pixelWidth, pixelHeight, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
			DrawingVisual drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
			using (DrawingContext drawingContext = drawingVisual.RenderOpen())
			{
				drawingContext.DrawRectangle(Brushes.White, null, new Rect(size));
			}
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(drawingVisual);
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(_plotVisual);
			return renderTargetBitmap;
		}

		public RenderTargetBitmap RenderPlotToBitmap()
		{
			Size renderSize = base.RenderSize;
			if (!base.IsVisible)
			{
				Refresh(renderSize);
				Measure(renderSize);
				Arrange(new Rect(renderSize));
				_plotVisual.UpdateLayout();
			}
			RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)renderSize.Width + 1, (int)renderSize.Height + 1, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
			DrawingVisual drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
			using (DrawingContext drawingContext = drawingVisual.RenderOpen())
			{
				drawingContext.DrawRectangle(Brushes.White, null, new Rect(renderSize));
			}
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(drawingVisual);
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(_plotVisual);
			return renderTargetBitmap;
		}

		private void miCopyGraph_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			CopyGraph(_selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame, (int)_selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Width + 1, (int)_selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Height + 1);
		}

		public static void CopyGraph(IPlotFrame plotFrame, int pixelWidth, int pixelHeight)
		{
			if (plotFrame != null)
			{
				PlotFrameVisual plotFrameVisual = null;
				if (plotFrame.Renderer.Visual.RenderSize == new Size(0.0, 0.0))
				{
					Size size = new Size(pixelWidth, pixelHeight);
					Rect renderArea = new Rect(size);
					plotFrameVisual = plotFrame.Renderer.Visual as PlotFrameVisual;
					plotFrameVisual.RenderArea = renderArea;
					plotFrame.Renderer.RenderInitialise();
					plotFrame.Renderer.Draw(ref renderArea, refreshAll: true);
					plotFrameVisual.Measure(size);
					plotFrameVisual.Arrange(new Rect(size));
				}
				else
				{
					plotFrameVisual = plotFrame.Renderer.Visual as PlotFrameVisual;
				}
				Rect rectangle = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, pixelWidth, pixelHeight);
				RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(pixelWidth, pixelHeight, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
				DrawingVisual drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
				using (DrawingContext drawingContext = drawingVisual.RenderOpen())
				{
					drawingContext.DrawRectangle(Brushes.White, null, rectangle);
				}
				renderTargetBitmap.Render(drawingVisual);
				renderTargetBitmap.Render(plotFrameVisual);
				Clipboard.SetImage(renderTargetBitmap);
			}
		}

		private void miReset3DView_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			Reset3DView();
		}

				if (_selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame.GraphLayout is IGraphLayout3D graphLayout3D)
				{
					graphLayout3D.ResetView();
				}
				Refresh();
			}
		}

		{
			ResetZoom();
		}

		private void ResetZoom()
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
			{
				_selectedFrameVisual.PlotFrame.GraphLayout.ZoomReset();
				Refresh();
				OnGraphZoom(new PlotZoomEventArgs(_mouseLocation, SelectedFrame, PlotSupport.ZeroRectangle, isZoomReset: true)
				{
					PlotControl = this
				});
			}
		}

		public virtual void OnGraphZoom(PlotZoomEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.GraphZoom != null)
			{
				this.GraphZoom(this, e);
			}
			RaisePropertyChanged("ZoomFactor");
		}

		private void miPrintPlot_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			PrintPlot();
		}

		public void PrintPlot()
		{
			if (_plotVisual == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			PrintDialog printDialog = new PrintDialog();
			if (printDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				if (_selectedFrameVisual != null)
				{
					_selectedFrameVisual.DrawFrameBorder(null);
				}
				PrintQueue printQueue = printDialog.PrintQueue;
				PlotCanvasBase plotCanvasBase = _plotVisual as PlotCanvasBase;
				VisualBrush visualBrush = new VisualBrush(_plotVisual);
				visualBrush.Stretch = Stretch.Uniform;
				DrawingVisual drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
				using (DrawingContext drawingContext = drawingVisual.RenderOpen())
				{
					drawingContext.DrawRectangle(visualBrush, null, new Rect(0.0, 0.0, plotCanvasBase.RenderSize.Width, plotCanvasBase.RenderSize.Height));
				}
				printDialog.PrintVisual(PerformTransform(drawingVisual, printQueue, drawingVisual.ContentBounds.Width, drawingVisual.ContentBounds.Height, Stretch.Uniform), "AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Plot.1");
				if (_selectedFrameVisual != null && _plot.Frames.Count > 1)
				{
					_selectedFrameVisual.DrawFrameBorder(PlotSupport.SelectedBorderPen);
				}
			}
		}

		private void miPrintGraph_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			PrintGraph();
		}

		public void PrintGraph()
		{
			if (_selectedFrameVisual == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			PrintDialog printDialog = new PrintDialog();
			if (printDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				_selectedFrameVisual.DrawFrameBorder(null);
				PrintQueue printQueue = printDialog.PrintQueue;
				VisualBrush visualBrush = new VisualBrush(_selectedFrameVisual);
				visualBrush.Stretch = Stretch.Uniform;
				DrawingVisual drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
				using (DrawingContext drawingContext = drawingVisual.RenderOpen())
				{
					drawingContext.DrawRectangle(visualBrush, null, new Rect(0.0, 0.0, _selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Width, _selectedFrameVisual.RenderArea.Height));
				}
				printDialog.PrintVisual(PerformTransform(drawingVisual, printQueue, drawingVisual.ContentBounds.Width, drawingVisual.ContentBounds.Height, Stretch.Uniform), "AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Plot.1");
				if (_plot.Frames.Count > 1)
				{
					_selectedFrameVisual.DrawFrameBorder(PlotSupport.SelectedBorderPen);
				}
			}
		}

		private void miPrintPreview_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
		}

		private static Visual PerformTransform(Visual v, PrintQueue pq, double width, double height, Stretch stretch)
		{
			ContainerVisual containerVisual = new ContainerVisual();
			double num = 96.0;
			double num2 = 96.0;
			PrintTicket userPrintTicket = pq.UserPrintTicket;
			double value = userPrintTicket.PageMediaSize.Width.Value;
			double value2 = userPrintTicket.PageMediaSize.Height.Value;
			double num3 = (value - num * 2.0) / width;
			double num4 = (value2 - num2 * 2.0) / height;
			switch (stretch)
			{
			case Stretch.None:
				num3 = 1.0;
				num4 = 1.0;
				break;
			case Stretch.Uniform:
				num4 = (num3 = Math.Min(num3, num4));
				break;
			case Stretch.UniformToFill:
				num4 = (num3 = Math.Max(num3, num4));
				break;
			}
			containerVisual.Children.Add(v);
			containerVisual.Transform = new MatrixTransform(num3, 0.0, 0.0, num4, num, num2);
			return containerVisual;
		}

		private void miExportPlot_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			SaveFileDialog saveFileDialog = new SaveFileDialog();
			saveFileDialog.DefaultExt = "xml";
			saveFileDialog.Filter = "Xml files|*.xml|All Files|*.*";
			saveFileDialog.Title = PlotSupport.ResourceString("exportPlotDialogTitle");
			if (saveFileDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
				xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
				xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = Encoding.UTF8;
				using XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(saveFileDialog.FileName, xmlWriterSettings);
				xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument(standalone: true);
				PlotSupport.WritePlotToXml(_plot, xmlWriter);
				xmlWriter.WriteEndDocument();
			}
		}

		private void miImportPlot_Click(object sender, DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs> e)
		{
			OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new OpenFileDialog();
			openFileDialog.Filter = "Xml files|*.xml|All Files|*.*";
			openFileDialog.Multiselect = false;
			openFileDialog.DefaultExt = "xml";
			openFileDialog.Title = PlotSupport.ResourceString("importPlotDialogTitle");
			if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
			{
				PlotSupport.ReadFromXml(_plot, XDocument.Load(openFileDialog.FileName).Root);
				Refresh();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDoubleClickAction(PlotClickEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.DoubleClickAction != null)
			{
				this.DoubleClickAction(this, new DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs>(e, default(Rect)));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectAction(PlotClickEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.SelectAction != null)
			{
				this.SelectAction(this, new DoActionEventArgs<PlotClickEventArgs>(e, default(Rect)));
			}
		}

		private void SetupCommands()
		{
			ZoomIn = new ActionCommand(delegate
			{
				SetZoom(1.0 / StandardValues.MouseWheelZoomFactor(2.0));
			}, () => _selectedFrameVisual != null);
			ZoomOut = new ActionCommand(delegate
			{
				SetZoom(1.0 / StandardValues.MouseWheelZoomFactor(-2.0));
			}, () => _selectedFrameVisual != null);
			ZoomReset = new ActionCommand(delegate
			{
				ResetZoom();
			}, () => _selectedFrameVisual != null);
			Properties = new ActionCommand(delegate
			{
				ShowPropertiesDialog();
			}, () => _selectedFrameVisual != null);
		}

		internal void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			RaiseCommandsChanged(propertyName);
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}

		private void RaiseCommandsChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == "ZoomFactor")
			{
				(ZoomIn as ActionCommand).RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
				(ZoomOut as ActionCommand).RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
				(ZoomReset as ActionCommand).RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			}
		}

		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "5.0.17.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.Visualisation.Controls.Plot.Wpf;V31.0.0.5;component/atplotterctrl01.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "5.0.17.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				((PlotCtrl)target).SizeChanged += UserControl_SizeChanged;
				break;
			case 2:
				mainCanvas = (Canvas)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

	public class PlotCanvasBase : Canvas
	{
		private WeakReference _plotElement;

		private bool _isSelected;

		protected object PlotElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (_plotElement == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _plotElement.Target;
			}
			set
			{
				_plotElement = ((value != null) ? new WeakReference(value) : null);
			}
		}

		public virtual bool IsSelectable => false;

		public virtual bool IsSelected
		{
			protected get
			{
				return _isSelected;
			}
			set
			{
				_isSelected = value;
			}
		}

		public virtual Rect RenderArea
		{
			get
			{
				return new Rect(Canvas.GetLeft(this), Canvas.GetTop(this), base.Width, base.Height);
			}
			set
			{
				if (base.Width != value.Width || base.Height != value.Height || Canvas.GetLeft(this) != value.Left || Canvas.GetTop(this) != value.Top)
				{
					base.Width = value.Width;
					base.Height = value.Height;
					Canvas.SetLeft(this, value.Left);
					Canvas.SetTop(this, value.Top);
					InvalidateMeasure();
				}
				SetClips();
			}
		}

		public virtual Rect ChildRenderArea => new Rect(0.0, 0.0, base.Width, base.Height);

		public Point Position
		{
			get
			{
				return new Point(Canvas.GetLeft(this), Canvas.GetTop(this));
			}
			set
			{
				Canvas.SetLeft(this, value.X);
				Canvas.SetTop(this, value.Y);
			}
		}

		private RectangleGeometry ClipArea => PlotSupport.CreateRectangleGeometry(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, base.Width, base.Height));

		public Rect ContentBounds
		{
			get
			{
				Rect result = Rect.Empty;
				foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
				{
					if (!child.IsMeasureValid)
					{
						child.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
					}
					Rect rect = default(Rect);
					if (child is Line line)
					{
						rect = default(Rect);
						rect.X = Math.Min(line.X1, line.X2);
						rect.Width = Math.Max(line.X1, line.X2) - rect.X;
						rect.Y = Math.Min(line.Y1, line.Y2);
						rect.Height = Math.Max(line.Y1, line.Y2) - rect.Y;
					}
					else
					{
						Point childPosition = GetChildPosition(child);
						rect = new Rect(childPosition.X, childPosition.Y, child.DesiredSize.Width, child.DesiredSize.Height);
					}
					if (result.IsEmpty)
					{
						result = rect;
					}
					else
					{
						result.Union(rect);
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public PlotCanvasBase()
		{
			base.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Transparent);
		}

		public Point ClientToHost(Point position)
		{
			Point result = position;
			for (PlotCanvasBase plotCanvasBase = this; plotCanvasBase != null; plotCanvasBase = plotCanvasBase.Parent as PlotCanvasBase)
			{
				Point position2 = plotCanvasBase.Position;
				result.X -= position2.X;
				result.Y -= position2.Y;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public Point HostToClient(Point position)
		{
			Point result = position;
			for (PlotCanvasBase plotCanvasBase = this; plotCanvasBase != null; plotCanvasBase = plotCanvasBase.Parent as PlotCanvasBase)
			{
				Point position2 = plotCanvasBase.Position;
				result.X += position2.X;
				result.Y += position2.Y;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SetClips()
		{
			base.Clip = ClipArea;
		}

		public void SetChildClips()
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				SetChildClip(child, (RectangleGeometry)base.Clip);
			}
		}

		private static void SetChildClip(UIElement element, RectangleGeometry clip)
		{
			element.Clip = clip;
		}

		public void RemoveChild(UIElement child)
		{
			if (base.Children.Contains(child))
			{
				base.Children.Remove(child);
			}
		}

		public void AddChild(UIElement child, Point pos)
		{
			if (!base.Children.Contains(child))
			{
				base.Children.Add(child);
			}
			SetChildPosition(child, pos);
		}

		public void SetChildPosition(UIElement child, Point pos)
		{
			Canvas.SetLeft(child, pos.X);
			Canvas.SetTop(child, pos.Y);
		}

		public void MoveChild(UIElement child, Point delta)
		{
			Point childPosition = GetChildPosition(child);
			SetChildPosition(child, new Point(childPosition.X + delta.X, childPosition.Y + delta.Y));
		}

		public void AddCanvas(Canvas canvas)
		{
			if (!base.Children.Contains(canvas))
			{
				base.Children.Add(canvas);
				SetChildClip(canvas, ClipArea);
			}
		}

		public Point GetChildPosition(UIElement child)
		{
			return new Point(Canvas.GetLeft(child), Canvas.GetTop(child));
		}

		public static void ModifyLinePen(Line line, PlotPen pen)
		{
			line.DataContext = pen;
			line.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeProperty, new Binding("VisibleStroke"));
			line.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new Binding("StrokeThickness"));
			line.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeDashArrayProperty, new Binding("StrokeDashArray"));
		}

		public void ModifyLine(Line line, PlotPen pen, Point start, Point finish)
		{
			ModifyLine(line, pen, start, finish, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
		}

		public void ModifyLine(Line line, PlotPen pen, Point start, Point finish, Point pos)
		{
			double num = Math.Min(start.X, finish.X);
			double num2 = Math.Min(start.Y, finish.Y);
			ModifyLinePen(line, pen);
			line.X1 = start.X - num;
			line.X2 = finish.X - num;
			line.Y1 = start.Y - num2;
			line.Y2 = finish.Y - num2;
			SetChildPosition(line, new Point(pos.X + num, pos.Y + num2));
		}

		public Line AddLine(PlotPen pen, Point start, Point finish)
		{
			return AddLine(pen, start, finish, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
		}

		public Line AddLine(PlotPen pen, Point start, Point finish, Point pos)
		{
			double num = Math.Min(start.X, finish.X);
			double num2 = Math.Min(start.Y, finish.Y);
			Line line = new Line();
			ModifyLine(line, pen, start, finish);
			AddChild(line, new Point(pos.X + num, pos.Y + num2));
			return line;
		}

		public Rectangle AddRectangle(PlotPen pen, Rect rect)
		{
			Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle();
			ModifyRectangle(rectangle, pen, rect);
			AddChild(rectangle, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(rect));
			return rectangle;
		}

		private void ModifyRectangle(Rectangle rectangle, PlotPen pen, Rect rect)
		{
			rectangle.DataContext = pen;
			rectangle.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, new Binding("VisibleFill"));
			rectangle.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeProperty, new Binding("VisibleStroke"));
			rectangle.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new Binding("StrokeThickness"));
			rectangle.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeDashArrayProperty, new Binding("StrokeDashArray"));
			rectangle.Width = rect.Width;
			rectangle.Height = rect.Height;
			SetChildPosition(rectangle, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(rect));
		}

		public static TextBlock MakeTextBlock(string text, FontFamily typeface, double emSize, Color colour, FontStyle? fontStyle = null, FontWeight? fontWeight = null, TextDecorationCollection textDecorations = null)
		{
			TextBlock obj = new TextBlock
			{
				HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left,
				VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
			};
			ConfigureTextBlock(obj, text, typeface, emSize, colour, fontStyle, fontWeight, textDecorations);
			return obj;
		}

		public static TextBlock ConfigureTextBlock(TextBlock textBlock, string text, FontFamily fontFamily, double emSize, Color colour, FontStyle? fontStyle = null, FontWeight? fontWeight = null, TextDecorationCollection textDecorations = null)
		{
			if (textBlock == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			textBlock.Text = text;
			textBlock.FontFamily = fontFamily;
			textBlock.FontSize = emSize;
			textBlock.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(colour);
			if (fontStyle.HasValue)
			{
				textBlock.FontStyle = fontStyle.Value;
			}
			if (fontWeight.HasValue)
			{
				textBlock.FontWeight = fontWeight.Value;
			}
			if (textDecorations != null)
			{
				textBlock.TextDecorations = textDecorations;
			}
			textBlock.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
			return textBlock;
		}

		public TextBlock AddText(string text, FontFamily typeface, double emSize, Color colour, Brush background, Point pos, FontStyle? fontStyle = null, FontWeight? fontWeight = null, TextDecorationCollection textDecorations = null)
		{
			TextBlock textBlock = MakeTextBlock(text, typeface, emSize, colour, fontStyle, fontWeight, textDecorations);
			AddChild(textBlock, pos);
			return textBlock;
		}

		public Polygon AddPolygon(PlotPen pen, PointCollection points, FillRule fillRule)
		{
			return AddPolygon(pen, points, fillRule, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
		}

		public Polygon AddPolygon(PlotPen pen, PointCollection points, FillRule fillRule, Point pos)
		{
			Polygon polygon = new Polygon();
			polygon.Points = points;
			polygon.FillRule = fillRule;
			polygon.DataContext = pen;
			polygon.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, new Binding("VisibleFill"));
			polygon.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeProperty, new Binding("VisibleStroke"));
			polygon.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new Binding("StrokeThickness"));
			polygon.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeDashArrayProperty, new Binding("StrokeDashArray"));
			AddChild(polygon, pos);
			return polygon;
		}

		public System.Windows.Shapes.Path AddPath(PlotPen pen, Geometry geometry)
		{
			return AddPath(pen, geometry, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
		}

		public System.Windows.Shapes.Path AddPath(PlotPen pen, Geometry geometry, Point pos)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			path.Data = geometry;
			path.DataContext = pen;
			path.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, new Binding("VisibleFill"));
			path.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeProperty, new Binding("VisibleStroke"));
			path.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new Binding("StrokeThickness"));
			path.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeDashArrayProperty, new Binding("StrokeDashArray"));
			AddChild(path, pos);
			return path;
		}

		public Ellipse AddEllipse(PlotPen pen, Point pos, double height, double width)
		{
			Ellipse ellipse = new Ellipse();
			ellipse.DataContext = pen;
			ellipse.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, new Binding("VisibleFill"));
			ellipse.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeProperty, new Binding("VisibleStroke"));
			ellipse.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new Binding("StrokeThickness"));
			ellipse.SetBinding(Shape.StrokeDashArrayProperty, new Binding("StrokeDashArray"));
			ellipse.Height = height;
			ellipse.Width = width;
			AddChild(ellipse, new Point(pos.X - width / 2.0, pos.Y - height / 2.0));
			return ellipse;
		}
	}
	public class PlotCanvas : PlotCanvasBase
	{
		public Plot Plot
		{
			get
			{
				return base.PlotElement as Plot;
			}
			set
			{
				base.PlotElement = value;
			}
		}
	}
	public class PlotFrameVisual : PlotCanvasBase
	{
		private RectangleGeometry _borderRectangle;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Path _borderPath;

		private Rect _selectedArea = PlotSupport.ZeroRectangle;

		private ScrollBar _vScroll;

		private ScrollBar _hScroll;

		public IPlotFrame PlotFrame
		{
			get
			{
				return base.PlotElement as IPlotFrame;
			}
			set
			{
				base.PlotElement = value;
			}
		}

		public override Rect RenderArea
		{
			get
			{
				return base.RenderArea;
			}
			set
			{
				base.RenderArea = value;
				LayoutScrollBars();
				LayoutBorder();
			}
		}

		public Rect GraphRenderArea => RenderArea;

		public Rect SelectedArea
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedArea;
			}
			set
			{
				_selectedArea = value;
			}
		}

		public ScrollBar VScroll
		{
			get
			{
				return _vScroll;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_vScroll != value)
				{
					base.Children.Remove(_vScroll);
					_vScroll = value;
				}
				LayoutScrollBars();
			}
		}

		public ScrollBar HScroll
		{
			get
			{
				return _hScroll;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_hScroll != value)
				{
					base.Children.Remove(_hScroll);
					_hScroll = value;
				}
				LayoutScrollBars();
			}
		}

		public override Rect ChildRenderArea
		{
			get
			{
				Rect childRenderArea = base.ChildRenderArea;
				if (_vScroll != null && _vScroll.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					childRenderArea.X += 18.0;
					childRenderArea.Width -= 18.0;
				}
				if (_hScroll != null && _hScroll.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					childRenderArea.Height -= 18.0;
				}
				return childRenderArea;
			}
		}

		public PlotFrameVisual()
		{
			_borderRectangle = new RectangleGeometry();
			_borderPath = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			Panel.SetZIndex(_borderPath, -1);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_borderPath, 0.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_borderPath, 0.0);
		}

		public void DrawFrameBorder(PlotPen pen)
		{
			_borderPath.Fill = PlotFrame.GraphLayout.BackgroundColour;
			if (pen != null)
			{
				_borderPath.StrokeThickness = pen.StrokeThickness;
				_borderPath.Stroke = pen.VisibleStroke;
			}
			else
			{
				_borderPath.Stroke = null;
			}
		}

		private void LayoutBorder()
		{
			Rect renderArea = RenderArea;
			_borderRectangle.Rect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, renderArea.Width, renderArea.Height);
			_borderPath.Data = _borderRectangle;
			if (!base.Children.Contains(_borderPath))
			{
				base.Children.Add(_borderPath);
			}
		}

		private void LayoutScrollBars()
		{
			if (_vScroll != null)
			{
				Canvas.SetLeft(_vScroll, 2.0);
				Canvas.SetTop(_vScroll, 2.0);
				Panel.SetZIndex(_vScroll, 100);
				double num = base.Height - 4.0;
				if (_hScroll != null && _hScroll.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					num -= 18.0;
				}
				_vScroll.Height = num;
				if (!base.Children.Contains(_vScroll))
				{
					base.Children.Add(_vScroll);
				}
			}
			if (_hScroll != null)
			{
				double num2 = 2.0;
				double num3 = base.Width - 4.0;
				if (_vScroll != null && _vScroll.Visibility != Visibility.Collapsed)
				{
					num2 += 18.0;
					num3 -= 18.0;
				}
				Canvas.SetLeft(_hScroll, num2);
				Canvas.SetTop(_hScroll, base.Height - 20.0);
				Panel.SetZIndex(_hScroll, 100);
				_hScroll.Width = num3;
				if (!base.Children.Contains(_hScroll))
				{
					base.Children.Add(_hScroll);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class GraphLayoutVisual : PlotCanvasBase
	{
		private Rect _visualRenderArea;

		public IGraphLayout GraphLayout
		{
			get
			{
				return base.PlotElement as IGraphLayout;
			}
			set
			{
				base.PlotElement = value;
			}
		}

		public Rect VisualRenderArea
		{
			get
			{
				return _visualRenderArea;
			}
			set
			{
				_visualRenderArea = value;
			}
		}
	}
	public class GraphScooterVisual : PlotCanvasBase
	{
		private Line _backgroundLine;

		private PlotPen _backgroundPen;

		private PlotPen _selectedPen;

		private Point _start;

		private Point _finish;

		private Ellipse _startAdorner;

		private Ellipse _endAdorner;

		public IGraphScooter GraphScooter
		{
			get
			{
				return base.PlotElement as IGraphScooter;
			}
			internal set
			{
				base.PlotElement = value;
			}
		}

		public override bool IsSelectable
		{
			get
			{
				if (GraphScooter != null)
				{
					return GraphScooter.UserMoveable;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public Point Start => _start;

		public Point Finish => _finish;

		public override bool IsSelected
		{
			set
			{
				base.IsSelected = value;
				SetBackgroundLine();
			}
		}

		public GraphScooterVisual()
		{
			base.Background = null;
		}

		public void AddScooterLine(Point start, Point finish, PlotPen linePen, PlotPen backgroundPen)
		{
			RemoveAdorners();
			_start = start;
			_finish = finish;
			_backgroundPen = backgroundPen;
			Color magenta = Colors.Magenta;
			magenta.A = 120;
			_selectedPen = new PlotPen(magenta, _backgroundPen.StrokeThickness + 1.0);
			_backgroundLine = AddLine(_backgroundPen, start, finish);
			SetBackgroundLine();
			AddLine(linePen, start, finish);
		}

		private void SetBackgroundLine()
		{
			if (_backgroundLine != null)
			{
				if (IsSelected)
				{
					AddAdorners();
					PlotCanvasBase.ModifyLinePen(_backgroundLine, _selectedPen);
				}
				else
				{
					RemoveAdorners();
					PlotCanvasBase.ModifyLinePen(_backgroundLine, _backgroundPen);
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddAdorners()
		{
			if (_startAdorner == null)
			{
				_startAdorner = AddEllipse(_selectedPen, _start, 10.0, 10.0);
			}
			if (_endAdorner == null)
			{
				_endAdorner = AddEllipse(_selectedPen, _finish, 10.0, 10.0);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveAdorners()
		{
			if (_startAdorner != null)
			{
				base.Children.Remove(_startAdorner);
				_startAdorner = null;
			}
			if (_endAdorner != null)
			{
				base.Children.Remove(_endAdorner);
				_endAdorner = null;
			}
		}
	}

		public Rect PlotArea { get; set; }

		public Point PlotAreaToHost(Point mouseLocation)
		{
			Point result = ClientToHost(mouseLocation);
			result.X -= PlotArea.X;
			result.Y -= PlotArea.Y;
			return result;
		}

		public Point HostToPlotArea(Point position)
		{
			Point result = HostToClient(position);
			result.X += PlotArea.X;
			result.Y += PlotArea.Y;
			return result;
		}
	}
