	public class DataSeriesVisual : PlotCanvasBase
	{
		private bool _isDragging;

		private Orientation _dragDirection;

		private bool _constrainedDrag;

		private bool _isMouseCaptured;

		private Point _mousePos;

		private Shape _hitTestShape;

		private string _mouseOverText;

		private PlotPen _oldDataContext;

		private Cursor _defaultCursor;

		private bool CanDragPoint
		{
			get
			{
				if (DataSeries != null && DataSeries.IsUserInteractive)
				{
					if (DataSeries.DataSourceType != PlotDataSourceType.Line && DataSeries.DataSourceType != PlotDataSourceType.SteppedLine)
					{
						return DataSeries.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public IDataSeries DataSeries { get; set; }

		public string MouseOverText => _mouseOverText;

		public DataSeriesVisual()
		{
			base.Background = null;
			base.MouseEnter += DataSeriesVisual_MouseEnter;
			base.MouseLeave += DataSeriesVisual_MouseLeave;
			base.MouseMove += DataSeriesVisual_MouseMove;
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += DataSeriesVisual_MouseDown;
			base.MouseLeftButtonUp += DataSeriesVisual_MouseUp;
		}

		private void DataSeriesVisual_MouseUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_isDragging)
			{
				IDataPoint obj = _hitTestShape.Tag as IDataPoint;
				AxesVisual relativeTo = DataSeries.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				Point position = e.GetPosition(relativeTo);
				Point position2 = e.GetPosition(this);
				IDataPoint dataPoint = CalculateNewDataPoint(position, position2, _mousePos);
				obj.X = dataPoint.X;
				obj.Y = dataPoint.Y;
				DataSeries.DataSource.Updated = true;
				IPlotRender renderer = DataSeries.Graph.GraphLayout.Renderer;
				Rect renderArea = (renderer.Visual as PlotCanvasBase).RenderArea;
				renderer.RenderInitialise();
				renderer.Draw(ref renderArea, refreshAll: false);
				if (_isMouseCaptured)
				{
					ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
				_isMouseCaptured = false;
				_isDragging = false;
				_constrainedDrag = false;
				Panel.SetZIndex(this, 0);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private IDataPoint CalculateNewDataPoint(Point mousePointAxes, Point mousePointSeries, Point mousePos)
		{
			RDataPoint rDataPoint = new RDataPoint();
			DataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var _);
			IGraphAxes axes = DataSeries.Graph.Axes;
			AxesVisual axesVisual = axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
			mousePos = new Point(mousePointAxes.X - (mousePointSeries.X - mousePos.X), mousePointAxes.Y - (mousePointSeries.Y - mousePos.Y));
			mousePos.X -= axesVisual.PlotArea.X;
			mousePos.Y -= axesVisual.PlotArea.Y;
			double point = mousePos.X / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axes.GetAxis(xMapId), DataSeries);
			rDataPoint.X = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axisForSeries, point, out var valid);
			point = 1.0 - mousePos.Y / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axes.GetAxis(yMapId), DataSeries);
			rDataPoint.Y = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axisForSeries2, point, out valid);
			return rDataPoint;
		}

		private Point CalculateNewPosition(IDataPoint dataPoint, Point mousePointAxes, Point mousePointSeries)
		{
			DataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var _);
			IGraphAxes axes = DataSeries.Graph.Axes;
			AxesVisual axesVisual = axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axes.GetAxis(xMapId), DataSeries);
			bool valid;
			double num = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axisForSeries, dataPoint.X, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return new Point(double.NaN, double.NaN);
			}
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axes.GetAxis(yMapId), DataSeries);
			double num2 = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axisForSeries2, dataPoint.Y, out valid);
			if (!valid)
			{
				return new Point(double.NaN, double.NaN);
			}
			double num3 = axesVisual.PlotArea.Width * num + axesVisual.PlotArea.X;
			double num4 = axesVisual.PlotArea.Height * (1.0 - num2) + axesVisual.PlotArea.Y;
			return new Point(num3 + (mousePointSeries.X - mousePointAxes.X), num4 + (mousePointSeries.Y - mousePointAxes.Y));
		}

		private void DataSeriesVisual_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (CanDragPoint && _hitTestShape != null)
			{
				_isDragging = true;
				_mousePos = e.GetPosition(this);
				_isMouseCaptured = CaptureMouse();
				Panel.SetZIndex(this, 1000);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void DataSeriesVisual_MouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_isDragging)
			{
				Point position = e.GetPosition(this);
				IDataPoint dataPoint = _hitTestShape.Tag as IDataPoint;
				if (DataSeries.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar)
				{
					_constrainedDrag = true;
					DataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var _);
					if (((xMapId & PlotAxisID.YMask) != PlotAxisID.None && (yMapId & PlotAxisID.XMask) != PlotAxisID.None && dataPoint.X is string) || dataPoint.Y is string)
					{
						_dragDirection = Orientation.Horizontal;
					}
					else
					{
						_dragDirection = Orientation.Vertical;
					}
				}
				else if ((Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift) == ModifierKeys.Shift)
				{
					if (!_constrainedDrag)
					{
						if (Math.Abs(position.X - _mousePos.X) < Math.Abs(position.Y - _mousePos.Y))
						{
							_dragDirection = Orientation.Vertical;
							_constrainedDrag = true;
						}
						else if (Math.Abs(position.X - _mousePos.X) > 0.0)
						{
							_dragDirection = Orientation.Horizontal;
							_constrainedDrag = true;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_constrainedDrag = false;
				}
				if (_constrainedDrag)
				{
					if (_dragDirection == Orientation.Vertical)
					{
						position.X = _mousePos.X;
					}
					else
					{
						position.Y = _mousePos.Y;
					}
				}
				AxesVisual relativeTo = DataSeries.Graph.Axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				Point position2 = e.GetPosition(relativeTo);
				Point position3 = e.GetPosition(this);
				IDataPoint dataPoint2 = CalculateNewDataPoint(position2, position3, position);
				PointDragEventArgs e2 = new PointDragEventArgs(dataPoint, dataPoint2);
				DataSeries.PerformPointDrag(e2);
				if (e2.Cancelled)
				{
					return;
				}
				position = CalculateNewPosition(dataPoint2, position2, position3);
				if (double.IsNaN(position.X) || double.IsNaN(position.Y))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (DataSeries.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar)
				{
					if (_dragDirection == Orientation.Horizontal)
					{
						position.Y = _mousePos.Y;
					}
					else
					{
						position.X = _mousePos.X;
					}
				}
				MoveChild(_hitTestShape, new Point(position.X - _mousePos.X, position.Y - _mousePos.Y));
				_mousePos = position;
				return;
			}
			CalculateMouseOverText(e);
			if (CanDragPoint)
			{
				Shape hitTestShape = _hitTestShape;
				_hitTestShape = null;
				VisualTreeHelper.HitTest(this, null, HitTestResultCallback, new PointHitTestParameters(e.GetPosition(this)));
				if (hitTestShape == _hitTestShape)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (hitTestShape != null && _oldDataContext != null)
				{
					hitTestShape.DataContext = _oldDataContext;
					DataSeries.PerformMouseLeavePoint(new PointEventArgs(hitTestShape.Tag as IDataPoint));
				}
				if (_hitTestShape != null)
				{
					base.Cursor = Cursors.Hand;
					_oldDataContext = _hitTestShape.DataContext as PlotPen;
					if (_oldDataContext != null)
					{
						Brush aspenMouseOver2Brush = AspenCommonResources.Instance.AspenMouseOver2Brush;
						_hitTestShape.DataContext = new PlotPen(_oldDataContext.StrokeColour, _oldDataContext.StrokeThickness, LineStyle.Solid, aspenMouseOver2Brush);
					}
					DataSeries.PerformMouseEnterPoint(new PointEventArgs(_hitTestShape.Tag as IDataPoint));
				}
				else
				{
					base.Cursor = _defaultCursor;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_hitTestShape = null;
			}
		}

		private HitTestResultBehavior HitTestResultCallback(HitTestResult result)
		{
			HitTestResultBehavior result2 = HitTestResultBehavior.Continue;
			if (result.VisualHit is Shape shape && shape.Tag is IDataPoint)
			{
				_hitTestShape = shape;
				result2 = HitTestResultBehavior.Stop;
			}
			return result2;
		}

		private void DataSeriesVisual_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Cursor = _defaultCursor;
			if (_hitTestShape != null && _oldDataContext != null)
			{
				_hitTestShape.DataContext = _oldDataContext;
				DataSeries.PerformMouseLeavePoint(new PointEventArgs(_hitTestShape.Tag as IDataPoint));
			}
			_hitTestShape = null;
			_oldDataContext = null;
			_mouseOverText = null;
		}

		private void DataSeriesVisual_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			_defaultCursor = base.Cursor;
			_oldDataContext = null;
			CalculateMouseOverText(e);
		}

		private object GetIntersectionValue(IDataSeries DataSeries, bool getNextIntersect, object value, PlotAxisID _linkedAxisId)
		{
			DataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var _);
			PlotAxisID intersectAxisId = ((_linkedAxisId != xMapId) ? xMapId : yMapId);
			return DataSeries.GetIntersect(value, getNextIntersect, _linkedAxisId, intersectAxisId);
		}

		private void CalculateMouseOverText(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (DataSeries != null)
			{
				object obj = null;
				bool flag = false;
				DataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var zMapId);
				IGraphAxes axes = DataSeries.Graph.Axes;
				AxesVisual axesVisual = axes.Renderer.Visual as AxesVisual;
				Point position = e.GetPosition(axesVisual);
				position.X -= axesVisual.PlotArea.X;
				position.Y -= axesVisual.PlotArea.Y;
				double point = position.X / axesVisual.PlotArea.Width;
				double point2 = 1.0 - position.Y / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
				if (DataSeries.DataSourceType == PlotDataSourceType.Bar && axes.GetAxis(xMapId).DataType != PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel && axes.GetAxis(yMapId).DataType == PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
				{
					zMapId = xMapId;
					xMapId = yMapId;
					yMapId = zMapId;
					point = 1.0 - position.Y / axesVisual.PlotArea.Height;
					flag = true;
				}
				IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axes.GetAxis(xMapId), DataSeries);
				IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axes.GetAxis(yMapId), DataSeries);
				if (DataSeries.Graph.AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.RectXMergedY)
				{
					axisForSeries2 = MergedGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axes.GetAxis(yMapId), DataSeries);
				}
				bool valid;
				object obj2 = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axisForSeries, point, out valid);
				object valueY = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axisForSeries2, point2, out valid);
				if (valid)
				{
					object obj3 = DataSeries.GetIntersect(obj2, getNextIntersect: false, xMapId, yMapId, ignoreOffsets: true, ignoreSegmentBreaks: true);
					if (axes.GetAxis(xMapId).DataType != PlotAxisDataType.TextLabel)
					{
						List<object> list = new List<object>();
						foreach (IDataPoint item in DataSeries.DataSource)
						{
							list.Add(item.X);
						}
						List<object> list2 = new List<object>();
						List<object> list3 = new List<object>();
						list2 = list.OrderBy((object xIncAxis) => xIncAxis).ToList();
						list3 = list.OrderByDescending((object xDescAxis) => xDescAxis).ToList();
						if (!list.SequenceEqual(list2) && !list.SequenceEqual(list3))
						{
							try
							{
								List<double> list4 = new List<double>();
								do
								{
									if ((obj = GetIntersectionValue(DataSeries, obj != null, obj2, xMapId)) != null)
									{
										list4.Add(Convert.ToDouble(obj));
									}
								}
								while (obj != null);
								obj3 = list4.OrderBy((double x) => Math.Abs(x - Convert.ToDouble(valueY))).First();
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
					}
					string text = string.Format("{0:" + axisForSeries.DataLabelFormatString + "}", obj2);
					string text2 = string.Format("{0:" + axisForSeries2.DataLabelFormatString + "}", obj3);
					if (flag)
					{
						ValueTextFormatEventArgs e2 = new ValueTextFormatEventArgs(obj3, obj2, null, text2, text, null);
						DataSeries.PerformValueToTextFormat(e2);
						text = e2.YValueText;
						text2 = e2.XValueText;
					}
					else
					{
						ValueTextFormatEventArgs e2 = new ValueTextFormatEventArgs(obj2, obj3, null, text, text2, null);
						DataSeries.PerformValueToTextFormat(e2);
						text = e2.XValueText;
						text2 = e2.YValueText;
					}
					if (obj3 == null)
					{
						text2 = string.Format("{0:" + axisForSeries2.DataLabelFormatString + "}", valueY);
					}
					text2 = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(DataSeries.Label)) ? string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:\n{1}\t= {2}\n{3}\t= {4}", DataSeries.Label, axisForSeries.Title, text, axisForSeries2.Title, text2) : string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t= {1}\n{2}\t= {3}", axisForSeries.Title, text, axisForSeries2.Title, text2));
					_mouseOverText = text2;
				}
				else
				{
					_mouseOverText = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_mouseOverText = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class LegendVisual : PlotCanvasBase
	{
		private bool _mouseCaptured;

		private bool _isDragging;

		private Point _mousePos;

		public bool IsFloating { get; set; }

		public Rect GraphArea { get; set; }

		public LegendVisual()
		{
			base.MouseEnter += LegendVisual_MouseEnter;
			base.MouseLeave += LegendVisual_MouseLeave;
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += LegendVisual_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			base.MouseLeftButtonUp += LegendVisual_MouseLeftButtonUp;
			base.MouseMove += LegendVisual_MouseMove;
			PlotSelection.SetElementType(this, PlotSelectedElementType.Legend);
		}

		private void LegendVisual_MouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsFloating && _isDragging)
			{
				if (e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed)
				{
					Point position = e.GetPosition(base.Parent as UIElement);
					double x = Math.Min(Math.Max(GraphArea.Left, RenderArea.Left + position.X - _mousePos.X), GraphArea.Right - RenderArea.Width);
					double y = Math.Min(Math.Max(GraphArea.Top, RenderArea.Top + position.Y - _mousePos.Y), GraphArea.Bottom - RenderArea.Height);
					RenderArea = new Rect(x, y, RenderArea.Width, RenderArea.Height);
					_mousePos = position;
				}
				else
				{
					_isDragging = false;
					ReleaseMouseCapture();
					_mouseCaptured = false;
					base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
				}
			}
		}

		private void LegendVisual_MouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsFloating && _isDragging)
			{
				_isDragging = false;
				ReleaseMouseCapture();
				_mouseCaptured = false;
				base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void LegendVisual_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsFloating)
			{
				base.Cursor = Cursors.ScrollAll;
				_mouseCaptured = CaptureMouse();
				_isDragging = true;
				_mousePos = e.GetPosition(base.Parent as UIElement);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void LegendVisual_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsFloating)
			{
				base.Background = WpfSilverlightSupport.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		private void LegendVisual_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsFloating)
			{
				base.Background = AspenCommonResources.Instance.AspenMouseOverOverlayBrush;
			}
		}
	}
	public class GraphScooterCollection : List<IGraphScooter>, IGraphScooterCollection, IList<IGraphScooter>, ICollection<IGraphScooter>, IEnumerable<IGraphScooter>, IEnumerable
	{
		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private Graph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as Graph;
			}
		}

		private GraphScooterCollection()
		{
		}

		public GraphScooterCollection(Graph graph)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
		}

		public new void Add(IGraphScooter item)
		{
			base.Add(item);
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
		}

		public new bool Remove(IGraphScooter item)
		{
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
			return base.Remove(item);
		}

		public new void Clear()
		{
			base.Clear();
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
		}
	}
	public class GraphScooter : IGraphScooter, IPlotRender
	{
		private ScooterType _scooterType = ScooterType.Axis;

		private object _scooterPosition = 0.5;

		private PlotAxisID _linkedAxisId;

		private Color _lineColour = Color.FromArgb(byte.MaxValue, 0, 128, 128);

		private LineStyle _lineStyle = LineStyle.Dash;

		private double _lineWidth = 1.0;

		private PlotPen _linePen;

		private PlotPen _transparentPen;

		private bool _visible = true;

		private bool _showIntersectValues = true;

		private bool _showIntersectTicks;

		private string _label = string.Empty;

		private string _id = string.Empty;

		private FontFamily _labelTypeface;

		private double _labelFontSize = 8.5;

		private FontStyle? _labelFontStyle;

		private FontWeight? _labelFontWeight;

		private TextDecorationCollection _labelTextDecorations;

		private Color _labelColour = Colors.Black;

		private bool _userMoveable;

		private bool _userCanDelete;

		private bool _refreshRequired;

		private bool _userCanFormat;

		private Color _labelBackground = Colors.Transparent;

		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private object _tag;

		private bool _snapToNearestDataPoint;

		private GraphScooterVisual _visual;

		private Graph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as Graph;
			}
		}

		public bool RefreshRequired => _refreshRequired;

		private PlotPen TransparentPen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_transparentPen == null)
				{
					_transparentPen = PlotSupport.MakePen(Colors.Transparent, LineStyle.Solid, _lineWidth);
				}
				return _transparentPen;
			}
		}

		public string Id
		{
			get
			{
				return _id;
			}
			set

				if (_id != value)
				{
					_id = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public ScooterType ScooterType
		{
			get
			{
				return _scooterType;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_scooterType == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (LinkedAxis != null)
				{
					bool valid;
					if (value == ScooterType.Data)
					{
						_scooterPosition = LinkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(LinkedAxis, (double)_scooterPosition, out valid);
					}
					else
					{
						_scooterPosition = LinkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(ActualLinkedAxis, _scooterPosition, out valid);
					}
				}
				_scooterType = value;
				_refreshRequired = true;
			}
		}

		public virtual object ScooterPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return _scooterPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((_scooterPosition == null || _scooterPosition.Equals(value)) && (value == null || value.Equals(_scooterPosition)))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_scooterType == ScooterType.Axis)
				{
					if (!(value is double))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Value must be of type double", "value");
					}
					if ((double)value < 0.0 || (double)value > 1.0)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "Value must be between 0 and 1");
					}
				}
				_scooterPosition = value;
				_refreshRequired = true;
			}
		}

			get

				return _linkedAxisId;

			set

					_linkedAxisId = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

				LinkedAxisId = value?.Id ?? PlotAxisID.None;
			}
		}

				{
					return StackedGraphAxis.GetAxisAtFraction(LinkedAxis, (double)_scooterPosition);

				return LinkedAxis;
			}
		}

		}

		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
		}

		public void SetFractionalPosition(double fractionalPosition)
		{
			if (_scooterType == ScooterType.Axis)
			{
				ScooterPosition = fractionalPosition;
				return;
			}
			bool valid;
			object scooterPosition = LinkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(LinkedAxis, fractionalPosition, out valid);
			if (valid)
			{
				ScooterPosition = scooterPosition;
			}
		}

		public object IntersectionValue(IDataSeries dataSeries, bool getNextIntersect)
		{
			bool valid;
			object value = ((_scooterType != ScooterType.Axis) ? _scooterPosition : LinkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(LinkedAxis, (double)_scooterPosition, out valid));
			return GetIntersectionValue(dataSeries, getNextIntersect, value);
		}

		private object GetIntersectionValue(IDataSeries dataSeries, bool getNextIntersect, object value)
		{
			dataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var _);
			return dataSeries.GetIntersect(intersectAxisId: (_linkedAxisId != xMapId) ? xMapId : yMapId, value: value, getNextIntersect: getNextIntersect, valueAxisId: _linkedAxisId);
		}

		public void RenderInitialise()
		{
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (refreshAll)
			{
				_refreshRequired = true;
			}
			if (!_refreshRequired)
			{
				return;
			}
			_refreshRequired = false;
			Visual.Children.Clear();
			_visual.RenderArea = renderArea;
			IGraphAxis linkedAxis = LinkedAxis;
			if (linkedAxis == null || !_visible)
			{
				return;
			}
			IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator = linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator;
			double num;
			object obj;
			bool valid;
			if (_scooterType == ScooterType.Axis)
			{
				num = (double)_scooterPosition;
				obj = axisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(linkedAxis, (double)_scooterPosition, out valid);
			}
			else
			{
				num = axisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(ActualLinkedAxis, _scooterPosition, out valid);
				if (!valid)
				{
					return;
				}
				obj = _scooterPosition;
			}
			if (_snapToNearestDataPoint)
			{
				IGraphAxis actualLinkedAxis = ActualLinkedAxis;
				object obj2 = null;
				double num2 = axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(actualLinkedAxis, obj, out valid);
				foreach (IDataSeries item2 in _graph.DataSeriesCollection)
				{
					item2.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var zMapId);
					foreach (IDataPoint item3 in item2.DataSource)
					{
						object obj3 = null;
						if (xMapId == actualLinkedAxis.Id)
						{
							obj3 = item3.X;
						}
						else if (yMapId == actualLinkedAxis.Id)
						{
							obj3 = item3.Y;
						}
						else if (zMapId == actualLinkedAxis.Id)
						{
							obj3 = item3.Z;
						}
						if (obj2 == null)
						{
							obj2 = obj3;
							continue;
						}
						double num3 = Math.Abs(axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(actualLinkedAxis, obj3, out valid) - num2);
						if (valid)
						{
							double num4 = Math.Abs(axisAnnotator.AxisLinearMapValue(actualLinkedAxis, obj2, out valid) - num2);
							if (!valid || num3 < num4)
							{
								obj2 = obj3;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				num = axisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(actualLinkedAxis, obj2, out valid);
				obj = obj2;
			}
			double width = renderArea.Width;
			double height = renderArea.Height;
			bool flag = false;
			Point point;
			Point point2;
			if ((_linkedAxisId & PlotAxisID.XMask) != PlotAxisID.None)
			{
				point = new Point(num * width, 0.0);
				point2 = new Point(num * width, renderArea.Height);
			}
			else
			{
				point = new Point(0.0, renderArea.Height - num * height);
				point2 = new Point(renderArea.Width, renderArea.Height - num * height);
				flag = true;
			}
			if (LineStyle != LineStyle.None)
			{
				_visual.AddScooterLine(point, point2, LinePen, TransparentPen);
			}
			Point point3 = new Point((point.X + point2.X) / 2.0, (point.Y + point2.Y) / 2.0);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_label) || _showIntersectValues)
			{
				string text = _label;
				if (_showIntersectValues)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						text += "\n";
					}
					text = (((linkedAxis is IStackedGraphAxis || !(linkedAxis is IMultiGraphAxis)) && (linkedAxis is IStackedGraphAxis || !(linkedAxis is IMergedGraphAxis))) ? (text + string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ResolveScooterFormat(linkedAxis.ScooterValueFormat), obj)) : (text + linkedAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionToValueString(linkedAxis, num).Replace('\t', ' ')));
				}
				TextBlock textBlock = _visual.AddText(text, LabelFontFamily, LabelFontSize, _labelColour, new SolidColorBrush(_labelBackground), point, LabelFontStyle, LabelFontWeight, LabelTextDecorations);
				Size textSize = WpfSilverlightSupport.GetTextSize(textBlock);
				Point suggestedPosition = default(Point);
				double num5 = 0.0;
				double num6 = 0.0;
				switch (_linkedAxisId)
				{
				case PlotAxisID.X:
					suggestedPosition = point2;
					num5 = 0.0 - (textSize.Width + 4.0);
					num6 = 0.0 - (textSize.Height + 4.0);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y:
					suggestedPosition = point;
					num5 = 0.0;
					num6 = 0.0 - (textSize.Height + 4.0);
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.X2:
					suggestedPosition = point;
					num5 = 0.0 - (textSize.Width + 4.0);
					num6 = 0.0;
					break;
				case PlotAxisID.Y2:
					suggestedPosition = point2;
					num5 = 0.0 - (textSize.Width + 4.0);
					num6 = 0.0 - (textSize.Height + 4.0);
					break;
				}
				suggestedPosition.X += num5 + 2.0;
				suggestedPosition.Y += num6 + 2.0;
				_visual.SetChildPosition(textBlock, FixScooterLabelPosition(renderArea, textSize, suggestedPosition));
			}
			if (!_showIntersectValues && !_showIntersectTicks)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<Rect> list = new List<Rect>();
			foreach (IDataSeries item4 in _graph.DataSeriesCollection)
			{
				if (!item4.Visible)
				{
					continue;
				}
				item4.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId2, out var yMapId2, out var _);
				PlotAxisID axisId;
				if (_linkedAxisId == xMapId2)
				{
					axisId = yMapId2;
				}
				else
				{
					if (_linkedAxisId != yMapId2)
					{
						continue;
					}
					axisId = xMapId2;
				}
				object obj4 = null;
				IGraphAxis axis = _graph.Axes.GetAxis(axisId);
				if (axis == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				Rect renderAreaForSeries = StackedGraphAxis.GetRenderAreaForSeries(axis, item4, renderArea);
				if (renderAreaForSeries == Rect.Empty)
				{
					continue;
				}
				renderAreaForSeries = StackedGraphAxis.GetRenderAreaForSeries(linkedAxis, item4, renderAreaForSeries);
				if (renderAreaForSeries == Rect.Empty || ((_linkedAxisId & PlotAxisID.YMask) != PlotAxisID.None && (renderAreaForSeries.Top > point3.Y || renderAreaForSeries.Bottom < point3.Y)))
				{
					continue;
				}
				axis = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axis, item4);
				if (_graph.AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.RectXMergedY)
				{
					axis = MergedGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(axis, item4);
				}
				if (axis == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string format = (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item4.ScooterValueFormat) ? axis.ScooterValueFormat : item4.ScooterValueFormat);
				format = ResolveScooterFormat(format);
				axisAnnotator = axis.AxisAnnotator;
				do
				{
					if (linkedAxis is IMultiGraphAxis && !(linkedAxis is IStackedGraphAxis))
					{
						IGraphAxis axis2 = (linkedAxis as IMultiGraphAxis).GetAxis(item4);
						if (axis2 != null)
						{
							obj = axis2.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis2, num, out valid);
						}
					}
					if (linkedAxis is IMergedGraphAxis && !(linkedAxis is IStackedGraphAxis))
					{
						IGraphAxis axis3 = (linkedAxis as IMergedGraphAxis).GetAxis(item4);
						if (axis3 != null)
						{
							obj = axis3.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis3, num, out valid);
						}
					}
					obj4 = GetIntersectionValue(item4, obj4 != null, obj);
					if (obj4 == null || (obj4 is double && double.IsNaN((double)obj4)))
					{
						continue;
					}
					double num7 = axisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axis, obj4, out valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						continue;
					}
					double val = (item4.GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotSeriesVisualElements.Line) ? item4.LineWidth : 0.0);
					val = Math.Max(val, _lineWidth);
					Point point4;
					Point start;
					Point finish;
					if (point.X == point2.X)
					{
						double num8 = renderAreaForSeries.Bottom - renderArea.Top;
						point4 = new Point(point.X + 2.0 + val, num8 - num7 * renderAreaForSeries.Height);
						start = new Point(point.X - 5.0 - val, num8 - num7 * renderAreaForSeries.Height);
						finish = new Point(point.X + 5.0 + val, num8 - num7 * renderAreaForSeries.Height);
					}
					else
					{
						point4 = new Point(num7 * renderAreaForSeries.Width, point.Y + 2.0 + val);
						start = new Point(num7 * renderAreaForSeries.Width, point.Y - 5.0 - val);
						finish = new Point(num7 * renderAreaForSeries.Width, point.Y + 5.0 + val);
					}
					if (_showIntersectValues)
					{
						string text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, format, obj4);
						TextBlock textBlock2 = _visual.AddText(text2, LabelFontFamily, LabelFontSize, item4.LineColour, new SolidColorBrush(_labelBackground), point4, LabelFontStyle, LabelFontWeight, LabelTextDecorations);
						Size textSize2 = WpfSilverlightSupport.GetTextSize(textBlock2);
						Rect item = new Rect(point4.X, point4.Y, textSize2.Width, textSize2.Height);
						for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
						{
							Rect rect = list[i];
							if (((rect.X <= item.X && rect.Right >= item.X) || (rect.X > item.X && rect.X <= item.Right)) && ((rect.Y <= item.Y && rect.Bottom >= item.Y) || (rect.Y > item.Y && rect.Y <= item.Bottom)))
							{
								if (flag)
								{
									item.Y = rect.Bottom + 2.0;
									point4.Y = rect.Bottom + 2.0;
									i = -1;
								}
								else
								{
									item.X = rect.Right + 2.0;
									point4.X = rect.Right + 2.0;
									i = -1;
								}
							}
						}
						_visual.SetChildPosition(textBlock2, FixScooterLabelPosition(renderArea, textSize2, point4));
						list.Add(item);
					}
					if (_showIntersectTicks)
					{
						_visual.AddLine(item4.LinePen, start, finish);
					}
				}
				while (obj4 != null);
			}
		}

		private static string ResolveScooterFormat(string format)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(format))
			{
				int num = format.IndexOf('{');
				int num2 = format.IndexOf('}');
				if (num >= 0 && num2 > num)
				{
					return format;
				}
				return "{0:" + format + "}";
			}
			return "{0}";
		}

		private static Point FixScooterLabelPosition(Rect renderArea, Size labelSize, Point suggestedPosition)
		{
			double num = suggestedPosition.X;
			double num2 = suggestedPosition.Y;
			if (num < 0.0)
			{
				num = 0.0;
			}
			else if (num > renderArea.Width - labelSize.Width)
			{
				num = renderArea.Width - labelSize.Width;
			}
			if (num2 < 0.0)
			{
				num2 = 0.0;
			}
			else if (num2 > renderArea.Height - labelSize.Height)
			{
				num2 = renderArea.Height - labelSize.Height;
			}
			return new Point(num, num2);
		}
	}
	internal class SliceScooter : GraphScooter
	{
		private GraphSlice _slice;

		private object _minPosition;

		private object _maxPosition;

		internal GraphSlice GraphSlice => _slice;

		internal object MinPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return _minPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((_minPosition == null || _minPosition.Equals(value)) && (value == null || value.Equals(_minPosition)))
				{
					return;
				}
				double num;
				double num2;
				double num3;
				if (base.ScooterType == ScooterType.Axis)
				{
					if (!(value is double))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Value must be of type double", "value");
					}
					if ((double)value < 0.0 || (double)value > 1.0)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "Value must be between 0 and 1");
					}
					num = ((_maxPosition != null) ? ((double)_maxPosition) : double.MaxValue);
					num2 = (double)ScooterPosition;
					num3 = (double)value;
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, value, out var valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Invalid minimum position value.", "value");
					}
					num = ((_maxPosition != null) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, _maxPosition, out valid) : double.MaxValue);
					num2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, ScooterPosition, out valid);
				}
				_minPosition = value;
				if (num3 > num)
				{
					_maxPosition = value;
				}
				if (num3 > num2)
				{
					ScooterPosition = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal object MaxPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return _maxPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				if ((_maxPosition == null || _maxPosition.Equals(value)) && (value == null || value.Equals(_maxPosition)))
				{
					return;
				}
				double num;
				double num2;
				double num3;
				if (base.ScooterType == ScooterType.Axis)
				{
					if (!(value is double))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Value must be of type double", "value");
					}
					if ((double)value < 0.0 || (double)value > 1.0)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "Value must be between 0 and 1");
					}
					num = ((_minPosition != null) ? ((double)_minPosition) : double.MinValue);
					num2 = (double)ScooterPosition;
					num3 = (double)value;
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, value, out var valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Invalid maximum position value.", "value");
					}
					num = ((_minPosition != null) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, _minPosition, out valid) : double.MinValue);
					num2 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, ScooterPosition, out valid);
				}
				_maxPosition = value;
				if (num3 < num)
				{
					_minPosition = value;
				}
				if (num3 < num2)
				{
					ScooterPosition = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public override object ScooterPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return base.ScooterPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				double num;
				double num2;
				double num3;
				if (base.ScooterType == ScooterType.Axis)
				{
					if (!(value is double))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Value must be of type double", "value");
					}
					if ((double)value < 0.0 || (double)value > 1.0)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "Value must be between 0 and 1");
					}
					num = ((_minPosition != null) ? ((double)_minPosition) : double.MinValue);
					num2 = ((_maxPosition != null) ? ((double)_maxPosition) : double.MaxValue);
					num3 = (double)value;
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, value, out var valid);
					if (!valid)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Invalid position value.", "value");
					}
					num = ((_minPosition != null) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, _minPosition, out valid) : double.MinValue);
					num2 = ((_maxPosition != null) ? PlotSupport.ValueToDouble(base.LinkedAxis, _maxPosition, out valid) : double.MaxValue);
				}
				if (num <= num3 && num3 <= num2)
				{
					base.ScooterPosition = value;
					if (_slice != null)
					{
						_slice.ScooterMoved(this);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected SliceScooter(Graph graph)
			: base(graph)
		{
		}

		internal SliceScooter(Graph graph, GraphSlice slice)
			: this(graph)
		{
			_slice = slice;
		}
	}
	public class StackedGraphAxis : MultiGraphAxis, IStackedGraphAxis, IMultiGraphAxis, IGraphAxis, IGraphAxisAnnotator
	{
		private Dictionary<IGraphAxis, Rect> _renderAreas = new Dictionary<IGraphAxis, Rect>();

		protected override ChildAxisLayout SubAxisLayout
		{
			get
			{
				return ChildAxisLayout.Stacked;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		public static Rect GetRenderAreaForSeries(IGraphAxis parentAxis, IDataSeries dataSeries, Rect defaultArea)
		{
			Rect result = defaultArea;
			if (parentAxis is IStackedGraphAxis stackedGraphAxis && dataSeries != null)
			{
				result = stackedGraphAxis.GetAxisRenderArea(dataSeries);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static IGraphAxis GetAxisAtFraction(IGraphAxis parentAxis, double fraction)
		{
			IGraphAxis result = parentAxis;
			if (parentAxis is IStackedGraphAxis stackedGraphAxis && !double.IsNaN(fraction))
			{
				result = stackedGraphAxis.GetAxis(fraction);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public StackedGraphAxis(IGraph graph, IGraphAxisAnnotator axisAnnotator)
			: base(graph, axisAnnotator)
		{
			SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks | PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks | PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines | PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, visibility: true);
		}

		protected override void SetChildAxis(IDataSeries dataSeries, IGraphAxis axis)
		{
			base.SetChildAxis(dataSeries, axis);
			axis.SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge, GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge));
			axis.SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels));
			axis.SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks, GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks));
			axis.SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks, GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks));
			axis.SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines, GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines));
			axis.SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines, GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines));
			axis.SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title, GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title));
			axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge, GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge));
			axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels, GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Labels));
			axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines, GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorGridLines));
			axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks, GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MajorTicks));
			axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines, GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorGridLines));
			axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks, GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.MinorTicks));
			axis.SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title, GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Title));
		}

		protected override void SetAxisFromDataSeries(IDataSeries dataSeries, IGraphAxis axis)
		{
		}

		private IGraphAxis GetAxis(double fraction)
		{
			int num = (int)Math.Min(base.RenderableAxes.Count - 1, Math.Max(0.0, (1.0 - fraction) * (double)base.VisibleCount));
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				return base.RenderableAxes[num];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public override void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (Visual != null)
			{
				foreach (IGraphAxis renderableAxis in base.RenderableAxes)
				{
					IPlotRender renderer = renderableAxis.Renderer;
					Rect renderArea2 = renderArea;
					double num2 = num / base.VisibleCount;
					switch (renderableAxis.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
						renderArea2.X = renderArea.X + renderArea.Width * num2;
						renderArea2.Width = renderArea.Width / (double)base.VisibleCount;
						break;
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
						renderArea2.Y = renderArea.Y + renderArea.Height * num2;
						renderArea2.Height = renderArea.Height / (double)base.VisibleCount;
						break;
					}
					SetAxisRenderArea(renderableAxis, renderArea2);
					renderer.Draw(ref renderArea2, refreshAll);
					if (!Visual.Children.Contains(renderer.Visual))
					{
						(Visual as PlotCanvasBase).AddCanvas(renderer.Visual);
					}
				}
			}
			ResetChangeFlags();
		}

		public override void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, ref Rect axisArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			int num = 0;
			PlotPen pen = PlotSupport.MakePen(GetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge), LineStyle.Solid, 1.0);
			if (Visual is PlotCanvasBase plotCanvasBase)
			{
				plotCanvasBase.Children.Clear();
				foreach (IGraphAxis renderableAxis in base.RenderableAxes)
				{
					IAxisRender renderer = renderableAxis.Renderer;
					Rect renderArea2 = renderArea;
					Rect axisArea2 = axisArea;
					double num2 = (double)num / (double)base.VisibleCount;
					switch (renderableAxis.Id)
					{
					case PlotAxisID.X:
					case PlotAxisID.X2:
					{
						double y = (axisArea2.X = renderArea.X + renderArea.Width * num2);
						renderArea2.X = y;
						y = (axisArea2.Width = renderArea.Width / (double)base.VisibleCount);
						renderArea2.Width = y;
						(renderableAxis.Renderer.Visual as PlotCanvasBase).RenderArea = new Rect(axisArea2.X, axisArea2.Y, axisArea2.Width, axisArea2.Height + renderArea2.Height);
						SetAxisRenderArea(renderableAxis, renderArea2);
						if (num < base.VisibleCount - 1 && GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge))
						{
							plotCanvasBase.AddLine(pen, PlotSupport.RectTopLeft(renderArea2), PlotSupport.RectBottomLeft(renderArea2));
						}
						y = (axisArea2.X = 0.0);
						renderArea2.X = y;
						break;
					}
					case PlotAxisID.Y:
					case PlotAxisID.Y2:
					{
						double y = (axisArea2.Y = renderArea.Y + renderArea.Height * num2);
						renderArea2.Y = y;
						y = (axisArea2.Height = renderArea.Height / (double)base.VisibleCount);
						renderArea2.Height = y;
						(renderableAxis.Renderer.Visual as PlotCanvasBase).RenderArea = new Rect(axisArea2.X, axisArea2.Y, axisArea2.Width + renderArea2.Width, axisArea2.Height);
						SetAxisRenderArea(renderableAxis, renderArea2);
						if (num < base.VisibleCount - 1 && GetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements.Edge))
						{
							plotCanvasBase.AddLine(pen, PlotSupport.RectBottomLeft(renderArea2), PlotSupport.RectBottomRight(renderArea2));
						}
						y = (axisArea2.Y = 0.0);
						renderArea2.Y = y;
						break;
					}
					}
					PlotAxisID id = renderableAxis.Id;
					if ((uint)(id - 1) > 1u && id != PlotAxisID.Y)
					{
						_ = 8;
					}
					renderer.Draw(ref renderArea2, ref axisArea2, refreshAll);
					num++;
					if (!plotCanvasBase.Children.Contains(renderer.Visual))
					{
						plotCanvasBase.AddCanvas(renderer.Visual);
					}
				}
			}
			ResetChangeFlags();
		}

		private void SetAxisRenderArea(IGraphAxis axis, Rect renderArea)
		{
			_renderAreas[axis] = renderArea;
		}

		public override void SetVisualElementColour(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, Color colour)
		{
			base.SetVisualElementColour(elements, colour);
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in base.Axes.Values)
			{
				value.SetVisualElementColour(elements, colour);
			}
		}

		public override void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, bool visibility)
		{
			base.SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, visibility);
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in base.Axes.Values)
			{
				value.SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, visibility);
			}
		}

		public override void SetVisualElementVisibility(PlotAxisVisualElements elements, bool visibility, bool viceVersa)
		{
			base.SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, visibility, viceVersa);
			foreach (IGraphAxis value in base.Axes.Values)
			{
				value.SetVisualElementVisibility(elements, visibility, viceVersa);
			}
		}

		IGraphAxis IStackedGraphAxis.GetAxis(double fraction)
		{
			return GetAxis(fraction);
		}

		Rect IStackedGraphAxis.GetAxisRenderArea(IDataSeries dataSeries)
		{
			Rect result = Rect.Empty;
			if (base.Axes.ContainsKey(dataSeries))
			{
				IGraphAxis key = base.Axes[dataSeries];
				if (_renderAreas.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					result = _renderAreas[key];
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		Size IGraphAxisAnnotator.EnsureRenderSize(IGraphAxis axis, Size desiredSize)
		{
			return base.BaseAnnotator.EnsureRenderSize(axis, desiredSize);
		}

		double IGraphAxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(IGraphAxis axis, object value, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis == this)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Cannot perform AxisFractionMapValue on a StackedGraphAxis object", "axis");
			}
			double num = axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axis, value, out valid);
			int num2 = base.RenderableAxes.IndexOf(axis);
			return 1.0 - ((1.0 - num) / (double)base.VisibleCount + (double)num2 / (double)base.VisibleCount);
		}

		object IGraphAxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(IGraphAxis axis, double point, out bool valid)
		{
			if (axis == this)
			{
				double num = 1.0 - point;
				int num2 = (int)Math.Min(base.RenderableAxes.Count - 1, Math.Max(0.0, num * (double)base.VisibleCount));
				if (num2 >= 0)
				{
					num = 1.0 - (num - (double)num2 / (double)base.VisibleCount) * (double)base.VisibleCount;
					IGraphAxis graphAxis = base.RenderableAxes[num2];
					return graphAxis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(graphAxis, num, out valid);
				}
				return base.BaseAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
			}
			return axis.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
		}

		string IGraphAxisAnnotator.AxisFractionToValueString(IGraphAxis axis, double point)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (axis == this)
			{
				bool valid;
				object arg = AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionUnmapValue(axis, point, out valid);
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\t= {1:" + axis.DataLabelFormatString + "}", axis.Title, arg);
			}
			return base.BaseAnnotator.AxisFractionToValueString(axis, point);
		}
	}
	public class ValueRange : IValueRange
	{
		private RRangeValue _minimum;

		private RRangeValue _maximum;

		private bool _aboveZero;

		public RRangeValue Minimum
		{
			get
			{
				return _minimum;
			}
			set
			{
				_minimum = value;
				OnValueChanged(new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public RRangeValue Maximum
		{
			get
			{
				return _maximum;
			}
			set
			{
				_maximum = value;
				OnValueChanged(new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public bool AboveZero
		{
			get
			{
				return _aboveZero;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_aboveZero != value)
				{
					_aboveZero = value;
					SetMinimum(_minimum.Value);
					SetMaximum(_maximum.Value);
					OnValueChanged(new EventArgs());
				}
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<EventArgs> ValueChanged;

		private bool SetMinimum(double value)
		{
			if (_aboveZero && value <= 0.0)
			{
				value = 1E-08;
			}
			bool result = false;
			if (_minimum.Value != value)
			{
				_minimum.Value = value;
				result = true;
			}
			if (_minimum.Valid != double.IsNaN(value))
			{
				_minimum.Valid = !double.IsNaN(value);
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool SetMaximum(double value)
		{
			if (_aboveZero && value <= 0.0)
			{
				value = 1E-08;
			}
			bool result = false;
			if (_maximum.Value != value)
			{
				_maximum.Value = value;
				result = true;
			}
			if (_maximum.Valid != double.IsNaN(value))
			{
				_maximum.Valid = !double.IsNaN(value);
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected void OnValueChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.ValueChanged != null)
			{
				this.ValueChanged(this, e);
			}
		}

		public void Invalidate()
		{
			_minimum.Valid = false;
			_maximum.Valid = false;
			OnValueChanged(new EventArgs());
		}

		public void Stretch(double value)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (!_minimum.Valid || _minimum.Value > value)
			{
				_minimum.Valid = true;
				flag |= SetMinimum(value);
			}
			if (!_maximum.Valid || _maximum.Value < value)
			{
				_maximum.Valid = true;
				flag |= SetMaximum(value);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				OnValueChanged(new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public void SetAbsolute(double minimum, double maximum, bool validate)
		{
			double minimum2;
			double maximum2;
			if (!validate || minimum <= maximum)
			{
				minimum2 = minimum;
				maximum2 = maximum;
			}
			else
			{
				minimum2 = maximum;
				maximum2 = minimum;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			flag = SetMinimum(minimum2) || flag;
			if (SetMaximum(maximum2) || flag)
			{
				OnValueChanged(new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public double ValueToFraction(double value)
		{
			if (Maximum.Value != Minimum.Value)
			{
				return (value - Minimum.Value) / (Maximum.Value - Minimum.Value);
			}
			return double.NaN;
		}

		public double FractionToValue(double value)
		{
			return Minimum.Value + value * (Maximum.Value - Minimum.Value);
		}
	}
	public class GraphAnnotationCollection : List<IGraphAnnotation>, IGraphAnnotationCollection, IList<IGraphAnnotation>, ICollection<IGraphAnnotation>, IEnumerable<IGraphAnnotation>, IEnumerable
	{
		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private Graph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as Graph;
			}
		}

		private GraphAnnotationCollection()
		{
		}

		public GraphAnnotationCollection(Graph graph)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
		}

		public new void Add(IGraphAnnotation item)
		{
			base.Add(item);
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
		}

		public new bool Remove(IGraphAnnotation item)
		{
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
			return base.Remove(item);
		}

		public new void Clear()
		{
			base.Clear();
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
		}
	}
	public class GraphAnnotation : IGraphAnnotation, IAxisRender, IPlotRender
	{
		private string _id;

		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private AnnotationPositionType _positionType;

		private AnnotationZOrder _zOrder = AnnotationZOrder.PostScooters;

		private GraphElementPosition _contentAnchor = GraphElementPosition.Left | GraphElementPosition.Top;

		private GraphAnnotationVisual _content;

		private bool _visible = true;

		private Point _relativePosition = new Point(0.5, 0.5);

		private RDataPoint _dataPosition = new RDataPoint();

		private IDataSeries _dataSeries;

		private PlotAxisID _xMapping = PlotAxisID.X;

		private PlotAxisID _yMapping = PlotAxisID.Y;

		private AnnotationRelativeTo _relativeTo;

		private bool _refreshRequired = true;

		private RAnnotationPositionInfo _positionInfo;

		private IGraph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as IGraph;
			}
		}

		protected bool RefreshRequired
		{
			get
			{
				return _refreshRequired;
			}
			set
			{
				_refreshRequired = value;
			}
		}

		public string Id
		{
			get
			{
				return _id;
			}
			set
			{
				_id = value;
			}
		}

		public IGraph Graph => _graph;

		public AnnotationPositionType PositionType => _positionType;

		public AnnotationZOrder ZOrder
		{
			get
			{
				return _zOrder;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _zOrder)
				{
					_zOrder = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public GraphElementPosition ContentAnchor
		{
			get
			{
				return _contentAnchor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_contentAnchor != value)
				{
					_contentAnchor = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public GraphAnnotationVisual Content => _content;

		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return _visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_visible != value)
				{
					_visible = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public RAnnotationPositionInfo PositionInfo => _positionInfo;

		public IAxisRender Renderer => this;

		public Canvas Visual
		{
			get
			{
				if (_positionType == AnnotationPositionType.Undefined)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _content;
			}
		}

		public GraphAnnotation()
		{
			_content = new GraphAnnotationVisual();
			_content.GraphAnnotation = this;
		}

		public GraphAnnotation(IGraph graph)
			: this()
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
		}

		public void Initialise(IGraph graph)
		{
			if (_graph == null)
			{
				_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
			}
			else if (_graph != graph)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot reset annotation's graph after it has been specified.");
			}
		}

		public void SetPosition(Point position, AnnotationRelativeTo type)
		{
			_relativePosition = position;
			_relativeTo = type;
			_positionType = AnnotationPositionType.Relative;
			_refreshRequired = true;
			_positionInfo = new RAnnotationPositionInfo(position, type);
		}

		public void SetPosition(object xValue, object yValue, PlotAxisID xAxisMapping, PlotAxisID yAxisMapping)
		{
			if (_graph.AxesStyle == PlotAxesStyle.RectXStackedY)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot set axis mapping for a stacked Y axis. Use SetPosition(object xValue, object yValue, IDataSeries dataSeries) instead.");
			}
			_dataPosition.X = xValue;
			_dataPosition.Y = yValue;
			_xMapping = xAxisMapping;
			_yMapping = yAxisMapping;
			_positionType = AnnotationPositionType.Data;
			_refreshRequired = true;
			_positionInfo = new RAnnotationPositionInfo(xValue, yValue, xAxisMapping, yAxisMapping);
		}

		public void SetPosition(object xValue, object yValue, IDataSeries dataSeries)
		{
			if (dataSeries == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("dataSeries");
			}
			if (!_graph.DataSeriesCollection.Contains(dataSeries))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Data series not recognised in current graph.", "dataSeries");
			}
			_dataPosition.X = xValue;
			_dataPosition.Y = yValue;
			_dataSeries = dataSeries;
			dataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var _);
			_xMapping = xMapId;
			_yMapping = yMapId;
			_positionType = AnnotationPositionType.Data;
			_refreshRequired = true;
			_positionInfo = new RAnnotationPositionInfo(xValue, yValue, dataSeries);
		}

		public virtual void RenderInitialise()
		{
		}

		public virtual void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (PositionType != AnnotationPositionType.Relative || _relativeTo == AnnotationRelativeTo.Axes)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Invalid AnnotationPositionType or AnnotationRelativeTo value.");
			}
			if (_visible)
			{
				DrawContent(refreshAll);
				if (_content != null)
				{
					DrawRelative(renderArea, renderArea, refreshAll);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, ref Rect axisArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (!_visible)
			{
				return;
			}
			DrawContent(refreshAll);
			if (_content != null)
			{
				Rect renderArea2 = renderArea;
				if (_relativeTo == AnnotationRelativeTo.Axes)
				{
					renderArea2 = axisArea;
				}
				if (_positionType == AnnotationPositionType.Relative)
				{
					DrawRelative(renderArea2, renderArea, refreshAll);
				}
				else if (_positionType == AnnotationPositionType.Data)
				{
					DrawToData(axisArea, refreshAll);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DrawContent(bool refreshAll)
		{
		}

		private void DrawToData(Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_xMapping), _dataSeries);
			IGraphAxis axisForSeries2 = MultiGraphAxis.GetAxisForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapping), _dataSeries);
			if (axisForSeries == null || axisForSeries2 == null)
			{
				_relativePosition = new Point(double.NaN, double.NaN);
			}
			else
			{
				renderArea = StackedGraphAxis.GetRenderAreaForSeries(_graph.Axes.GetAxis(_yMapping), _dataSeries, renderArea);
				if (renderArea == Rect.Empty)
				{
					return;
				}
				bool valid;
				double x = axisForSeries.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axisForSeries, _dataPosition.X, out valid);
				if (!valid)
				{
					return;
				}
				double y = axisForSeries2.AxisAnnotator.AxisFractionMapValue(axisForSeries2, _dataPosition.Y, out valid);
				if (!valid)
				{
					return;
				}
				_relativePosition = new Point(x, y);
			}
			DrawRelative(renderArea, renderArea, refreshAll);
		}

		private void DrawRelative(Rect renderArea, Rect clipArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			Rect contentBounds = _content.ContentBounds;
			double num = 0.0 - contentBounds.X + _relativePosition.X * renderArea.Width + renderArea.X;
			double num2 = 0.0 - contentBounds.Y + (1.0 - _relativePosition.Y) * renderArea.Height + renderArea.Y;
			switch (_contentAnchor & GraphElementPosition.HorizontalMask)
			{
			case GraphElementPosition.Centre:
				num -= contentBounds.Width / 2.0;
				break;
			case GraphElementPosition.Right:
				num -= contentBounds.Width;
				break;
			}
			switch (_contentAnchor & GraphElementPosition.VerticalMask)
			{
			case GraphElementPosition.Middle:
				num2 -= contentBounds.Height / 2.0;
				break;
			case GraphElementPosition.Bottom:
				num2 -= contentBounds.Height;
				break;
			}
			_content.RenderArea = new Rect(num, num2, contentBounds.Width, contentBounds.Height);
		}
	}
	public class GraphTextAnnotation : GraphAnnotation, IGraphTextAnnotation, IGraphAnnotation
	{
		private string _text = string.Empty;

		private FontFamily _typeface;

		private double _pointSize = 8.5;

		private Color _colour = Colors.Black;

		private Brush _background = PlotSupport.TransparentBrush;

		private FontStyle? _fontStyle;

		private FontWeight? _fontWeight;

		private TextDecorationCollection _textDecorations;

		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				return _text;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_text != value)
				{
					_text = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public FontFamily FontFamily
		{
			get
			{
				if (_typeface != null)
				{
					return _typeface;
				}
				return PlotSupport.SystemMessageTypeface;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_typeface != value)
				{
					_typeface = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public double PointSize
		{
			get
			{
				return _pointSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_pointSize != value)
				{
					_pointSize = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public Color Colour
		{
			get
			{
				return _colour;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_colour != value)
				{
					_colour = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public FontStyle? FontStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _fontStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_fontStyle != value)
				{
					_fontStyle = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public FontWeight? FontWeight
		{
			get
			{
				return _fontWeight;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_fontWeight != value)
				{
					_fontWeight = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public TextDecorationCollection TextDecorations
		{
			get
			{
				return _textDecorations;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_textDecorations != value)
				{
					_textDecorations = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public Brush BackgroundColour
		{
			get
			{
				return _background;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_background != value)
				{
					_background = value;
					base.RefreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public GraphTextAnnotation()
		{
		}

		public GraphTextAnnotation(IGraph graph)
			: base(graph)
		{
		}

		protected override void DrawContent(bool refreshAll)
		{
			if (refreshAll || base.RefreshRequired)
			{
				GraphAnnotationVisual content = base.Content;
				content.Children.Clear();
				content.AddText(_text, FontFamily, _pointSize, _colour, _background, new Point(1.0, 1.0), FontStyle, FontWeight, TextDecorations);
				base.RefreshRequired = false;
			}
		}

		public string Save()
		{
			XElement xElement = new XElement("GraphTextAnnotation");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Text))
			{
				xElement.SetAttributeValue("Text", Text);
			}
			xElement.SetAttributeValue("FontFamily", FontFamily.Source);
			xElement.SetAttributeValue("PointSize", PointSize);
			if (FontStyle.HasValue)
			{
				xElement.SetAttributeValue("FontStyle", PlotSupport.ConvertToString(FontStyle));
			}
			if (FontWeight.HasValue)
			{
				xElement.SetAttributeValue("FontWeight", PlotSupport.ConvertToString(FontWeight));
			}
			if (TextDecorations != null)
			{
				xElement.SetAttributeValue("TextDecorations", PlotSupport.ConvertToString(TextDecorations));
			}
			xElement.SetAttributeValue("Color", Colour.ToString());
			xElement.SetAttributeValue("BackgroundColour", BackgroundColour.ToString());
			return xElement.ToString(SaveOptions.DisableFormatting);
		}

		public static GraphTextAnnotation Parse(string xml)
		{
			GraphTextAnnotation graphTextAnnotation = new GraphTextAnnotation();
			try
			{
				XElement xElement = XElement.Parse(xml);
				graphTextAnnotation.Text = (string)xElement.Attribute("Text");
				string text = (string)xElement.Attribute("FontFamily");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					graphTextAnnotation.FontFamily = new FontFamily(text);
				}
				double num = (double)xElement.Attribute("PointSize");
				if (!double.IsNaN(num))
				{
					graphTextAnnotation.PointSize = num;
				}
				string text2 = (string)xElement.Attribute("FontStyle");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					graphTextAnnotation.FontStyle = PlotSupport.CreateFontStyle(text2);
				}
				string text3 = (string)xElement.Attribute("FontWeight");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
				{
					graphTextAnnotation.FontWeight = PlotSupport.CreateFontWeight(text3);
				}
				string text4 = (string)xElement.Attribute("TextDecorations");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
				{
					graphTextAnnotation.TextDecorations = PlotSupport.CreateTextDecorations(text4);
				}
				string value = (string)xElement.Attribute("Color");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					graphTextAnnotation.Colour = (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(value);
				}
				string text5 = (string)xElement.Attribute("BackgroundColour");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					graphTextAnnotation.BackgroundColour = (Brush)new BrushConverter().ConvertFromString(text5);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return graphTextAnnotation;
		}
	}
	public class GraphSliceCollection : List<IGraphSlice>, IGraphSliceCollection, IList<IGraphSlice>, ICollection<IGraphSlice>, IEnumerable<IGraphSlice>, IEnumerable
	{
		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private Graph _graph
		{
			get

				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as Graph;
			}
		}

		private GraphSliceCollection()
		{
		}

		public GraphSliceCollection(Graph graph)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
		}

		{
			base.Add(item);
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
		}

		public new bool Remove(IGraphSlice item)
		{
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
			return base.Remove(item);
		}

		public new void Clear()

			base.Clear();
			_graph.RefreshRequired = true;
		}
	}
	public class GraphSlice : IGraphSlice, IPlotRender
	{
		private WeakReference _graphWR;

		private SliceScooter _startScooter;

		private SliceScooter _endScooter;

		private PlotAxisID _linkedAxisId;

		private IDataSeries _linkedDataSeries;

		private Color _lineColour = Colors.Black;

		private LineStyle _lineStyle = LineStyle.Dot;

		private double _lineWidth = 2.0;

		private bool _isVisible = true;

		private object _tag;

		private string _id;

		private bool _refreshRequired;

		private PlotPen _fillPen;

		private GraphSliceVisual _visual;

		private Graph _graph
		{
			get
			{
				if (_graphWR == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _graphWR.Target as Graph;
			}
		}

		protected bool RefreshRequired
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_refreshRequired && !StartScooter.RefreshRequired)
				{
					return EndScooter.RefreshRequired;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal SliceScooter StartScooter
		{
			get
			{
				if (_startScooter == null)
				{
					_startScooter = CreateScooter();
				}
				return _startScooter;
			}
		}

		internal SliceScooter EndScooter
		{
			get
			{
				if (_endScooter == null)
				{
					_endScooter = CreateScooter();
				}
				return _endScooter;
			}
		}

		public object StartValue
		{
			get
			{
				return StartScooter.ScooterPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				StartScooter.ScooterPosition = value;
			}
		}

		public object EndValue
		{
			get
			{
				return EndScooter.ScooterPosition;
			}
			set
			{
				EndScooter.ScooterPosition = value;
			}
		}

		public PlotAxisID LinkedAxisId
		{
			get
			{
				return _linkedAxisId;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_linkedAxisId != value)
				{
					_linkedAxisId = value;
					StartScooter.LinkedAxisId = _linkedAxisId;
					EndScooter.LinkedAxisId = _linkedAxisId;
				}
			}
		}

		public IGraphAxis LinkedAxis
		{
			get
			{
				return _graph.Axes.GetAxis(_linkedAxisId);
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != null)
				{
					LinkedAxisId = value.Id;
				}
				else
				{
					LinkedAxisId = PlotAxisID.None;
				}
			}
		}

		public IDataSeries LinkedDataSeries
		{
			get
			{
				return _linkedDataSeries;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_linkedDataSeries != value)
				{
					_linkedDataSeries = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public IPlotRender Renderer => this;

		public Brush Fill
		{
			get
			{
				return FillPen.Fill;
			}
			set
			{
				FillPen.Fill = value;
			}
		}

		public PlotPen FillPen
		{
			get
			{
				if (_fillPen == null)
				{
					_fillPen = new PlotPen(Colors.Transparent, 0.0, LineStyle.None, WpfSilverlightSupport.Brushes.Transparent)
					{
						IsStrokeVisible = false
					};
				}
				return _fillPen;
			}
		}

		public object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return _tag;
			}
			set
			{
				_tag = value;
			}
		}

		public Color LineColour
		{
			get
			{
				return _lineColour;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_lineColour != value)
				{
					_lineColour = value;
					StartScooter.LineColour = _lineColour;
					EndScooter.LineColour = _lineColour;
				}
			}
		}

		public LineStyle LineStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return _lineStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_lineStyle != value)
				{
					_lineStyle = value;
					StartScooter.LineStyle = _lineStyle;
					EndScooter.LineStyle = _lineStyle;
				}
			}
		}

		public double LineWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return _lineWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_lineWidth != value)
				{
					_lineWidth = value;
					StartScooter.LineWidth = _lineWidth;
					EndScooter.LineWidth = _lineWidth;
				}
			}
		}

		public PlotPen LinePen => StartScooter.LinePen;

		public bool IsVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return _isVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isVisible != value)
				{
					_isVisible = value;
					_refreshRequired = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public string Id
		{
			get
			{
				return _id;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_id != value)
				{
					_id = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public Canvas Visual
		{
			get
			{
				if (_visual == null)
				{
					_visual = new GraphSliceVisual();
					_visual.GraphSlice = this;
				}
				return _visual;
			}
		}

		private GraphSlice()
		{
		}

		public GraphSlice(Graph graph)
		{
			_graphWR = new WeakReference(graph);
		}

		internal void ScooterMoved(SliceScooter scooter)
		{
			if (scooter == StartScooter)
			{
				EndScooter.MinPosition = scooter.ScooterPosition;
			}
			else if (scooter == EndScooter)
			{
				StartScooter.MaxPosition = scooter.ScooterPosition;
			}
		}

		private SliceScooter CreateScooter()
		{
			SliceScooter obj = new SliceScooter(_graph, this)
			{
				Id = Guid.NewGuid().ToString(),
				LineStyle = _lineStyle,
				LineWidth = _lineWidth,
				LineColour = _lineColour,
				ScooterType = ScooterType.Data,
				LinkedAxisId = _linkedAxisId,
				UserMoveable = true
			};
			bool showIntersectValues = (obj.ShowIntersectTicks = false);
			obj.ShowIntersectValues = showIntersectValues;
			return obj;
		}

		public void RenderInitialise()
		{
			StartScooter.RenderInitialise();
			EndScooter.RenderInitialise();
		}

		public void Draw(ref Rect renderArea, bool refreshAll)
		{
			refreshAll = refreshAll || RefreshRequired;
			_refreshRequired = false;
			if (!refreshAll)
			{
				return;
			}
			Visual.Children.Clear();
			if (!_isVisible)
			{
				return;
			}
			Rect rect = (_visual.RenderArea = renderArea);
			Rect renderArea2 = rect;
			if (_linkedDataSeries != null)
			{
				PlotAxisID plotAxisID = PlotAxisID.None;
				_linkedDataSeries.GetAxisMapping(out var xMapId, out var yMapId, out var _);
				if (_linkedAxisId == xMapId)
				{
					plotAxisID = yMapId;
				}
				else if (_linkedAxisId == yMapId)
				{
					plotAxisID = xMapId;
				}
				if (plotAxisID != PlotAxisID.None)
				{
					if (_graph.Axes.GetAxis(plotAxisID) is IStackedGraphAxis stackedGraphAxis)
					{
						renderArea2 = stackedGraphAxis.GetAxisRenderArea(_linkedDataSeries);
					}
					if (renderArea2 == Rect.Empty)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			StartScooter.Draw(ref renderArea2, refreshAll);
			EndScooter.Draw(ref renderArea2, refreshAll);
			GraphScooterVisual graphScooterVisual = StartScooter.Visual as GraphScooterVisual;
			GraphScooterVisual graphScooterVisual2 = EndScooter.Visual as GraphScooterVisual;
			Rect rect3 = new Rect(new Point(Math.Min(Math.Min(graphScooterVisual.Start.X, graphScooterVisual.Finish.X), Math.Min(graphScooterVisual2.Start.X, graphScooterVisual2.Finish.X)), Math.Min(Math.Min(graphScooterVisual.Start.Y, graphScooterVisual.Finish.Y), Math.Min(graphScooterVisual2.Start.Y, graphScooterVisual2.Finish.Y))), new Point(Math.Max(Math.Max(graphScooterVisual.Start.X, graphScooterVisual.Finish.X), Math.Max(graphScooterVisual2.Start.X, graphScooterVisual2.Finish.X)), Math.Max(Math.Max(graphScooterVisual.Start.Y, graphScooterVisual.Finish.Y), Math.Max(graphScooterVisual2.Start.Y, graphScooterVisual2.Finish.Y))));
			_visual.AddRectangle(FillPen, rect3);
			_visual.AddChild(graphScooterVisual, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			_visual.AddChild(graphScooterVisual2, new Point(0.0, 0.0));
		}
	}

	public interface IDataPoint
	{
		bool HideSymbol { get; set; }

		bool IsEmptyDataPoint { get; }

		string Label { get; set; }

		bool NewSegment { get; set; }

		object StyleKey { get; set; }

		object X { get; set; }

		object Y { get; set; }

		object Z { get; set; }
	}
	public class RDataPoint : IDataPoint
	{
		public object X { get; set; }

		public object Y { get; set; }

		public object Z { get; set; }

		public string Label { get; set; }

		public bool NewSegment { get; set; }

		public object StyleKey { get; set; }

		public bool HideSymbol { get; set; }

		public static IDataPoint EmptyDataPoint
		{
			get
			{
				RDataPoint rDataPoint = new RDataPoint();
				object obj = (rDataPoint.Z = null);
				object x = (rDataPoint.Y = obj);
				rDataPoint.X = x;
				return rDataPoint;
			}
		}

		public bool IsEmptyDataPoint
		{
			get
			{
				if (X == null)
				{
					return (Y == null) & (Z == null);
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static bool operator ==(RDataPoint p1, RDataPoint p2)
		{
			if (((p1.X == null && p2.X == null) || (p1.X != null && p1.X.Equals(p2.X))) && ((p1.Y == null && p2.Y == null) || (p1.Y != null && p1.Y.Equals(p2.Y))) && ((p1.Z == null && p2.Z == null) || (p1.Z != null && p1.Z.Equals(p2.Z))) && p1.Label == p2.Label && p1.NewSegment == p2.NewSegment && p1.StyleKey == p2.StyleKey)
			{
				return p1.HideSymbol == p2.HideSymbol;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool operator !=(RDataPoint p1, RDataPoint p2)
		{
			return !(p1 == p2);
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is RDataPoint)
			{
				return this == (RDataPoint)obj;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return ((X != null) ? X.GetHashCode() : 0) ^ ((Y != null) ? Y.GetHashCode() : 0) ^ ((Z != null) ? Z.GetHashCode() : 0);
		}
	}

	public struct R3DCuboid
	{
		private Point3D _point;

		private Vector3D _vector;

		public Point3D Point
		{
			get
			{
				return _point;
			}
			set
			{
				_point = value;
			}
		}

		public Vector3D Vector
		{
			get
			{
				return _vector;
			}
			set
			{
				_vector = value;
			}
		}

		public double X
		{
			get
			{
				return Point.X;
			}
			set
			{
				_point.X = value;
			}
		}

		public double Y
		{
			get
			{
				return Point.Y;
			}
			set
			{
				_point.Y = value;
			}
		}

		public double Z
		{
			get
			{
				return Point.Z;
			}
			set
			{
				_point.Z = value;
			}
		}

		public double Width
		{
			get
			{
				return Vector.X;
			}
			set
			{
				_vector.X = value;
			}
		}

		public double Height
		{
			get
			{
				return Vector.Y;
			}
			set
			{
				_vector.Y = value;
			}
		}

		public double Depth
		{
			get
			{
				return Vector.Z;
			}
			set
			{
				_vector.Z = value;
			}
		}

		public R3DCuboid(double x, double y, double z, double width, double height, double depth)
		{
			_point = new Point3D(x, y, z);
			_vector = new Vector3D(width, height, depth);
		}

		public R3DCuboid(Point3D point, Vector3D vector)
		{
			_point = point;
			_vector = vector;
		}
	}

	public struct RGraphArea
	{
		public double Top;

		public double Left;

		public double Bottom;

		public double Right;

		public static RGraphArea Undefined = new RGraphArea(double.NaN, double.NaN, double.NaN, double.NaN);

		public RGraphArea(double top, double left, double bottom, double right)
		{
			Top = top;
			Left = left;
			Bottom = bottom;
			Right = right;
		}

		public static bool operator ==(RGraphArea r1, RGraphArea r2)
		{
			if (r1.Top == r2.Top && r1.Left == r2.Left && r1.Bottom == r2.Bottom)
			{
				return r1.Right == r2.Right;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool operator !=(RGraphArea r1, RGraphArea r2)
		{
			return !(r1 == r2);
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is RGraphArea)
			{
				return this == (RGraphArea)obj;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return Top.GetHashCode() ^ Left.GetHashCode() ^ Bottom.GetHashCode() ^ Right.GetHashCode();
		}
	}

	public struct RAnnotationPositionInfo
	{
		private readonly Point _relativePos;

		private readonly AnnotationRelativeTo _relativeTo;

		private readonly object _xValue;

		private readonly object _yValue;

		private readonly PlotAxisID _xMapping;

		private readonly PlotAxisID _yMapping;

		private readonly IDataSeries _dataSeries;

		public Point RelativePos => _relativePos;

		public AnnotationRelativeTo RelativeTo => _relativeTo;

		public object XValue => _xValue;

		public object YValue => _yValue;

		public PlotAxisID XMapping => _xMapping;

		public PlotAxisID YMapping => _yMapping;

		public IDataSeries DataSeries => _dataSeries;

		public RAnnotationPositionInfo(Point relativePos, AnnotationRelativeTo relativeTo)
		{
			_relativePos = relativePos;
			_relativeTo = relativeTo;
			_xValue = null;
			_yValue = null;
			_xMapping = PlotAxisID.None;
			_yMapping = PlotAxisID.None;
			_dataSeries = null;
		}

		public RAnnotationPositionInfo(object xValue, object yValue, PlotAxisID xMapping, PlotAxisID yMapping)
		{
			_xValue = xValue;
			_yValue = yValue;
			_xMapping = xMapping;
			_yMapping = yMapping;
			_relativePos = default(Point);
			_relativeTo = AnnotationRelativeTo.Axes;
			_dataSeries = null;
		}

		public RAnnotationPositionInfo(object xValue, object yValue, IDataSeries dataSeries)
		{
			_xValue = xValue;
			_yValue = yValue;
			_dataSeries = dataSeries;
			_relativePos = default(Point);
			_relativeTo = AnnotationRelativeTo.Axes;
			_xMapping = PlotAxisID.None;
			_yMapping = PlotAxisID.None;
		}
	}
